### [1] [SALSA: Semi-Autonomous Literature Summarization Assistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22210)
*William Schertzer,Sonakshi Gupta,Rampi Ramprasad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SALSA 是一个开源的、人在回路（human-in-the-loop）平台，用于从多模态文献中抽取结构化科学数据集，融合文档解析、大语言模型、OCR、计算机视觉与图表数字化等技术，在自动化文献整编的同时保留专家监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学文献数据整编（literature curation）存在大量重复性工作，而信息分散于文本、表格、图表与图注等多模态载体中。SALSA 旨在自动化这些重复任务，同时保留专家判断介入的空间。

Method: 该软件集成文档解析（document parsing）、大语言模型（large language models）、光学字符识别（optical character recognition）、计算机视觉（computer vision）、图表数字化（figure digitization）及用户引导校正工具，支持配置抽取阶段、定义数据集模式（dataset schemas）、对数字化图表实施人工干预，并导出经核验的数据。

Result: SALSA 可从文本、表格、图表和图注中恢复结构化信息，并将核验后的数据导出以供下游分析与机器学习（machine learning）使用；其可定制抽取工作流支持多种输入类型，为材料研究及其他潜在学科提供可扩展、可靠的数据整编框架。

Conclusion: SALSA 提供了面向可扩展且可靠数据整编的灵活框架，适用于材料研究并可能推广至多个学科；使用者需自行确保输入、抽取工作流及下游用途符合出版商协议、版权与许可条款、机构政策及数据使用要求。

Abstract: SALSA (Semi-Autonomous Literature Summarization Assistant) is an open- source, human-in-the-loop platform for extracting structured scientific datasets from multimodal literature sources. The software combines document parsing, large language models, optical character recognition, computer vision, figure digitization, and user-guided correction tools to recover structured information from text, tables, figures, and captions. Users can configure extraction stages, define dataset schemas, perform interventions on digitized figures, and export verified data for downstream analysis and machine learning. SALSA is designed to automate repetitive literature curation tasks while preserving oversight where expert judgment is required. By supporting customizable extraction workflows across diverse input types, the software provides a flexible framework for scalable, reliable data curation for materials research, and potentially across several disciplines. Users are responsible for ensuring that all inputs, extraction workflows, and downstream uses comply with applicable publisher agreements, copyright and licensing terms, institutional policies, and data-use requirements.

</details>

### [2] [Natural coordinates for constrained correlation functions: Partial autocorrelations and the geometry of positive power spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24500)
*Thomas Erben*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 该文指出，两点相关函数（two-point correlation function）的容许取值序列受功率谱非负性约束，构成有界凸区域；作者将 Schneider & Hartlap (2009) 引入的仿射变量 $x_n$ 等同于对应正 Toeplitz 相关矩阵的偏自相关系数（partial autocorrelation coefficient）$\alpha_n$，从而用时间序列分析中的经典工具刻画该区域，并给出 $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$ 的递推算法与统计解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙剪切（cosmic shear）与大尺度结构分析的相关系数取值并非自由，功率谱非负性将其限制在特定凸区域内；原行列式形式在较高阶获取容许区间较繁琐，需更自然、可计算的坐标描述。

Method: 识别 Schneider & Hartlap (2009) 的仿射变量 $x_n$ 与正 Toeplitz 相关矩阵的偏自相关系数 $\alpha_n$ 的等价关系，使偏自相关工具箱可直接应用；利用该结构导出任意阶容许区间的 $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$ 递推，并分析 Wilking & Schneider (2013) 拟高斯似然中的反双曲正切变换。

Result: 偏自相关系数构成容许区域上的自然坐标，每个 $\alpha_n$ 在 $[-1,+1]$ 内独立变化；原形式下高阶难以获得的 $r_n$ 容许区间现可通过 $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$ 递推得到；拟高斯似然中的反双曲正切被解释为偏自相关的 Fisher $z$ 变换（Fisher's $z$-transformation），为其经验性高斯化效果提供经典统计解释。数值实验验证了这些坐标在所述一维情形中的实用性。

Conclusion: 该识别将宇宙学两点相关约束与时间序列偏自相关理论相连接，提供了更高效的计算途径和统计解释；但更高维各向同性约束需额外几何信息，超出该识别的直接适用范围。

Abstract: Two-point correlation functions are a standard summary statistic in cosmic shear and large-scale-structure analyses. Their values are, however, constrained: non-negativity of the underlying power spectrum restricts any admissible sequence of correlation coefficients $(r_1,\ldots,r_N)$ to a bounded convex region, described in the one-dimensional case by the recursive interval geometry of Schneider & Hartlap (2009). Their formalism introduces an affine variable $x_n$ that maps the admissible interval for $r_n$, given $r_1,\ldots,r_{n-1}$, to $[-1,+1]$. We identify $x_n$ with the partial autocorrelation coefficient $α_n$ of the associated positive Toeplitz correlation matrix, a classical quantity in time-series analysis. The partial autocorrelations thus provide natural coordinates on the admissible region, each $α_n$ varying independently in $[-1,+1]$.
  This identification makes the standard partial-autocorrelation toolbox directly applicable. The admissible intervals for $r_n$, cumbersome to obtain at higher order in the original determinant-based formalism, now follow from an $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$ recursion at arbitrary order. The inverse hyperbolic tangent used in the quasi-Gaussian likelihood construction of Wilking & Schneider (2013) becomes Fisher's $z$-transformation of partial autocorrelations, providing a classical statistical interpretation of its empirical Gaussianising effect. Numerical experiments illustrate the practical use of these natural coordinates in the tested one-dimensional settings. Higher-dimensional isotropic constraints require additional geometric input and lie outside the direct scope of this identification.

</details>

### [3] [Vanadium doping induced valley asymmetries in WS$_2$ monolayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22580)
*Frederico B. Sousa,Boyang Zheng,Elizabeth Grace Houser,Paulo E. Faria Junior,Zhuohang Yu,Alessandra Ames,Gabriel A. D. Souza,Mingzu Liu,Gilmar Eugenio Marques,Leandro M. Malard,Mauricio Terrones,Vincent H. Crespi,Marcio D. Teodoro*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作报道了钒掺杂（vanadium-doped）WS₂ 单层中的能谷不对称性：在给定磁极化下，一个能谷表现出更大的塞曼斜率（Zeeman slope）与圆偏振度（degree of circular polarization），且掺杂样品的总圆偏振度约为本征 WS₂ 的两倍；DFT 计算将其归因于自旋相关杂化（spin-dependent hybridization）引起的导带边能量移动差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过渡金属硫族化物（transition metal dichalcogenide, TMD）单层可借能谷自由度（valley degree of freedom）编码与操控信息，但实际应用仍需对能谷极化效率与能谷塞曼效应（valley Zeeman effect）实现更高级别的调控。

Method: 本文研究钒掺杂 WS₂ 单层的能谷不对称性，并结合密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）计算，分析掺杂结构中导带边（conduction band edges）的能量移动。

Result: 在给定磁极化下，一个能谷的塞曼斜率与圆偏振度均大于另一能谷；掺杂样品的总圆偏振度约为本征 WS₂ 单层的两倍。DFT 计算显示，自旋相关杂化导致导带边能量移动不同，进而使两能谷间激子能量（exciton energies）不同，与实验观测一致。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于缺陷工程（defect-engineered）二维材料的能谷电子学（valleytronic）技术具有可行路径。

Abstract: Transition metal dichalcogenide (TMD) monolayers offer an innovative platform for encoding and manipulating information through the valley degree of freedom. While unique valley-related physical phenomena have been reported so far, practical applications still require advanced control over the valley polarization efficiency and the valley Zeeman effect. Recently, the introduction of spin-polarized metal atoms as substitutional defects was reported to break the time-reversal symmetry in TMD monolayers, consequently inducing a room-temperature ferromagnetic ordering and enhancing the valley-dependent optical responses. Here, we report valley asymmetries for vanadium-doped WS$_2$ monolayers. With a given magnetic polarization, one valley exhibits larger Zeeman slope and degree of circular polarization than the other valley. Additionally, the overall degree of circular polarization in the doped samples is approximately twice that of the pristine WS$_2$ monolayer. Density functional theory calculations in the doped structure show that different energy shifts in conduction band edges due to spin-dependent hybridization lead to different exciton energies between valleys, which is consistent with the experimental observations. Our results pave the way for valleytronic technologies based on defect-engineered two-dimensional materials.

</details>


### [4] [Electrical Probing of Dark Excitons through Microwave Permittivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22710)
*Alex Stram,Zhida Liu,Ziheng Zhang,Yangchen He,Xuejian Ma,Kyoung Pyo Lee,Lisa Frammolino,Fuxiang Chen,Robert J. Boyd,Sirapas Tangton,Anand Swain,Kan Yao,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Yuebing Zheng,Chih-Kang Shih,Daniel Rhodes,Li Yang,Xiaoqin Li,Keji Lai*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作证明单层二硫化钨（tungsten disulfide, WS₂）中的光激发激子（exciton）可在吉赫兹（gigahertz, GHz）频段产生大幅可光调介电响应，并借此实现暗激子（dark exciton）的纳米尺度无接触电学成像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子级薄半导体中的激子通常仅通过光学信号探测，因其瞬态寿命短而被普遍认为难以电学检测；本文旨在突破这一限制，寻找激子的电学探测途径。

Method: 采用激光照明的微波阻抗显微镜（microwave impedance microscopy）；对比高纯度封装薄片与缺陷丰富样品的响应性质；通过扩散的空间成像与亚线性功率依赖分析辨识主导贡献者，并以第一性原理（first-principles）激子极化率建模复现实测磁化率。

Result: 高纯度封装薄片呈现与激子谱共振的纯介电响应，缺陷丰富样品则由常规光电导（photoconductivity）主导；扩散成像与亚线性功率依赖表明长寿命暗激子是主要贡献者；第一性原理激子极化率模型与实测磁化率吻合。

Conclusion: 研究确立激子可作为光可调介电元件，并将微波显微镜确立为暗激子输运的直接电学探针，空间分辨率优于 100 nm。

Abstract: Excitons in atomically thin semiconductors are almost always probed through their optical signatures, because the short lifetimes of these transient quasiparticles are generally assumed to preclude electrical detection. Here we show that photoexcited excitons in monolayer tungsten disulfide produce a large, optically tunable permittivity at gigahertz frequencies, and that the effect provides a contact-free electrical route to imaging dark excitons at the nanoscale. Using laser-illuminated microwave impedance microscopy, we find that high-purity encapsulated flakes exhibit a purely dielectric response resonant with the exciton spectrum, whereas defect-rich samples are governed by conventional photoconductivity. Spatial mapping of diffusion and sublinear power dependence identify long-lived dark excitons as the dominant contributors, and first-principles modelling of exciton polarizability reproduces the measured susceptibility. Our results establish excitons as optically tunable dielectric elements and introduce microwave microscopy as a direct electrical probe of dark-exciton transport with sub-100 nm resolution.

</details>


### [5] [Non-Hermitian Topology from Edge Transport in Hermitian Quantum Anomalous Hall Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23099)
*Humian Zhou,Ming Lu,Chui-Zhen Chen,X. C. Xie*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作表明，非厄米物理（non-Hermitian physics）可作为全局厄米量子反常霍尔系统（quantum anomalous Hall system）的内禀输运响应自然涌现，无需外部增益/损耗工程，其表现为由连续 Hatano-Nelson 模型描述的非互易输运及可实验观测的非厄米趋肤效应（non-Hermitian skin effect）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米物理的奇特现象（如奇异点 exceptional points 与趋肤效应）此前主要在依赖受控增益与损耗的工程化系统中实现，其是否能在天然厄米体系中内禀涌现尚不明确。本文旨在证明无需外部非厄米工程，非厄米输运响应亦可自然产生。

Method: 通过分析全局厄米量子反常霍尔系统中单向手性边缘模式（unidirectional chiral edge modes）与扩散型普通边缘模式（diffusive normal edge modes）的相互作用，导出了由连续 Hatano-Nelson 模型刻画的非互易输运；并采用 Landauer–Büttiker 模拟验证输运特征。

Result: 非厄米趋肤效应直接编码于实验可及的霍尔条（Hall-bar）观测量中：电化学势（electrochemical potential）与局域热耗散呈现依赖手性的指数空间分布，纵向电导随系统尺寸指数衰减，而霍尔电导保持量子化；并确定磁性拓扑绝缘体（magnetic topological insulators）为实现该内禀响应的现实平台。

Conclusion: 该结果将非厄米拓扑（non-Hermitian topology）与介观输运（mesoscopic transport）联系起来，为固态系统中的非厄米拓扑器件开辟了路径。

Abstract: Non-Hermitian physics, known for exotic phenomena like exceptional points and the skin effect, has been most prominently realized in engineered systems relying on controlled gain and loss. Here we show that it can also arise naturally as an intrinsic transport response of a globally Hermitian quantum anomalous Hall system, without the need for external non-Hermitian engineering. We show that the interplay between unidirectional chiral edge modes and diffusive normal edge modes induces intrinsic non-reciprocal transport described by a continuum Hatano-Nelson model. Consequently, the non-Hermitian skin effect is encoded directly in experimentally accessible Hall-bar observables: the electrochemical potential and local heat dissipation acquire chirality-dependent exponential spatial profiles, while the longitudinal conductance decays exponentially with system size and the Hall conductance remains quantized. Using Landauer--B{"u}ttiker simulations, we confirm these transport signatures and identify magnetic topological insulators as a realistic platform for an intrinsic non-Hermitian transport response. Our results bridge non-Hermitian topology with mesoscopic transport, opening a pathway toward non-Hermitian topological devices in solid-state systems.

</details>


### [6] [Magnetic Quadrupole Lens and Current-Induced Quadrupolar Dynamics of Antiskyrmion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23129)
*Linfeng Du,Yang Liu,Peng Yan,Ying Su*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作证明反斯格明子（antiskyrmion）可作为磁四极透镜（magnetic quadrupole lens），对穿过其中的电流进行聚焦或发散，从而产生各向异性电流分布并显著改变其自身动力学行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反斯格明子赛道存储器（racetrack memory）面临的关键挑战之一，是其霍尔效应（Hall effect）导致的横向漂移限制器件性能；同时，电流与拓扑磁结构之间的耦合动力学尚未被自洽地刻画。

Method: 将量子输运（quantum transport）与微磁学模拟（micromagnetic simulations）迭代结合，自洽地捕获传导电子与反斯格明子的耦合动力学，并分析自旋转移矩（spin-transfer torque）下的电流诱导动力学。

Result: 反斯格明子核心可成为电子束汇聚的焦点，产生各向异性且非均匀的电流分布；由此揭示出对电流方向高度各向异性、并可由自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）有效调控的四极反斯格明子霍尔效应（quadrupolar antiskyrmion Hall effect）。电流聚焦（发散）显著提升（抑制）反斯格明子速度，同时减小其霍尔角。

Conclusion: 本征电流透镜效应（intrinsic current lensing）被确立为操控拓扑磁结构（topological magnetic textures）的有效机制，并为基于反斯格明子的自旋电子学器件（spintronic devices）开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We demonstrate that antiskyrmions can serve as magnetic quadrupole lenses that focus or defocus electric currents traversing them. We showcase the antiskyrmion core as a focal point where electron beams converge for specific injection directions, leading to anisotropic and nonuniform current distributions. Thus the current-induced antiskyrmion dynamics is profoundly modified by the magnetic quadrupole lensing via the spin-transfer torque. By incorporating quantum transport and micromagnetic simulations iteratively, we self-consistently capture the coupled dynamics of conduction electrons and antiskyrmions. Our results unveil a quadrupolar antiskyrmion Hall effect that is highly anisotropic with respect to the current direction and can be effectively tuned by the spin-orbit coupling. In particular, the current focusing (defocusing) dramatically enhances (suppresses) the antiskyrmion speed while simultaneously reducing its Hall angle, thereby overcoming a key challenge for antiskyrmion racetrack memories. Our work establishes the intrinsic current lensing as a powerful mechanism for manipulating topological magnetic textures and opens new avenues for antiskyrmion-based spintronic devices.

</details>


### [7] [Collective depinning of dipolar skyrmion chains: thermal and athermal rounding of an elastic polymer in a quenched landscape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23181)
*R. L. Silva,R. C. Silva,R. L. Stamps*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过数值求解随机Thiele方程（stochastic Thiele equation），研究弱钉扎势场中相互作用偶极斯格明子链（dipole skyrmion chain）的退钉扎动力学（depinning dynamics），识别出临界自旋电流阈值，并揭示链长无关性、热致展宽的部分热起源及磁场对转变形状的调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究多关注孤立斯格明子织构的钉扎与退钉扎，而实际器件中信息载体可能为斯格明子链而非孤立织构，因此需要理解链状集合体在淬火钉扎势（quenched pinning）中的集体退钉扎行为及其热效应。

Method: 采用随机Thiele方程（stochastic Thiele equation）的数值解，在不同磁场下驱动不同长度的周期性边界斯格明子链穿越淬火吸引钉扎位点，以临界退钉扎力对应的阈值自旋电流（threshold spin current）为判据进行分析。

Result: （i）链长≥50个斯格明子时退钉扎阈值与链长无关，约为孤立斯格明子阈值的1/3；（ii）转变展宽仅部分源于热效应，宽度从T=0时w=0.334±0.004 decades增至300 K时0.633±0.002，而本征Larkin关联长度（Larkin correlation length）在kBT~V0/25以下保持约2.6 bonds；（iii）宽度在kBT~0.14 V0以下几乎不变，至kBT~0.23 V0增大约四倍，主要来自蠕变区（creep regime）展宽，且与链长无关；磁场由30 mT增至40 mT使转变核心加宽26%并缩短蠕变尾部。约200个斯格明子的链通过内部形变序列实现退钉扎。

Conclusion: 斯格明子链的退钉扎受集体效应支配，表现为Larkin长度增长与粗糙度、瞬态键拉伸及迁移率与霍尔角（Hall angle）恢复等内部形变序列；这些集体效应对于以链而非孤立织构作为信息载体的斯格明子器件具有相关性。

Abstract: The dynamics of interacting dipole skyrmion chains in a weak pinning landscape is studied through numerical solutions of the stochastic Thiele equation. Chains of different lengths with periodic boundary conditions are driven across quenched attractive pinning sites under different magnetic fields. A clear threshold spin current is identified as the critical depinning force. Three main results emerge: (i) for chains of 50 skyrmions or more, the depinning threshold is independent of chain length and remains close to one third of that of an isolated skyrmion. (iii) The width is nearly unchanged up to kBT ~ 0.14 V0, but increases almost fourfold by kBT ~ 0.23 V0, mainly through broadening of the creep regime. It is independent of chain length, ruling out finite-size rounding. Increasing the field from 30 to 40 mT changes the transition shape, widening its core by 26% while shortening the creep tails. For chains of about 200 skyrmions, depinning proceeds through a sequence of internal deformations - growth of the Larkin length and roughness, transient bond stretching, and subsequent recovery of mobility and Hall angle. Abstract: Starting from the unitary evolution of a closed quantum system, we rigorously demonstrate that the projection of the global wavefunction onto an arbitrary local subsystem is governed by an exact, time-dependent non-Hermitian Schrödinger equation. Crucially, the derivation does not rely on conventional approximations such as the Born approximation, the Markov approximation, or the wide-band limit. The central quantity is the \textit{instantaneous self-energy}, a time-dependent and generally non-Hermitian operator that encodes environmental backaction and, together with the subsystem Hamiltonian, forms the exact time-local generator of the projected dynamics. By benchmarking the conventional non-Hermitian approximation against this exact framework, we systematically expose its limitations. These results provide a rigorous microscopic foundation for the emergence of effective non-Hermitian dynamics in quantum systems.

</details>

Abstract: Overcoming the approximately 60~mV/dec Boltzmann limit of the subthreshold swing remains a central challenge for low-power nanoelectronics. Negative-capacitance field-effect transistors address it by amplifying the channel potential, but their effectiveness is constrained by capacitance matching and by the quantum capacitance of conventional channels. Here we model a negative-capacitance bandgap-change field-effect transistor (NC-BCFET), in which the ferroelectric gate stack instead amplifies the electric field that opens the bandgap of the channel, using experimentally proven materials: bilayer graphene as a channel with an electrically tunable bandgap and Al$_{0.55}$Sc$_{0.45}$N as the negative-capacitance ferroelectric. Our self-consistent framework couples a four-band tight-binding Hamiltonian with GW-corrected screening, a quasistatic Landau--Devonshire ferroelectric response, and ballistic Landauer--Büttiker transport. At 300~K, the optimized NC-BCFET achieves a subthreshold swing of 15~mV/dec, four times below the Boltzmann limit, improving to 2.5~mV/dec at 100~K. The concept extends directly to materials with larger field-tunable bandgaps, where deep subthermionic switching and high on/off ratios can be achieved together at room temperature.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有的一维 GBZ 条件 $|\beta_p| = |\beta_{p+1}|$ 在二维失效，但该失效随系统从一维向二维过渡时如何发生尚不清楚；探究此问题反过来揭示一维 GBZ 理论本身仍不完整。

Method: 作为系列工作的起点，本文系统回顾一维谱方法（spectral approach），厘清其内在困难所在，而非直接构建新理论。

Result: 识别出推动后续论文（Paper II）发展波函数方法（wavefunction approach）的关键挑战，即一维谱方法的症结所在。

Conclusion: 本文为一维 GBZ 理论提供系统的批判性梳理，并为该系列后续以波函数方法为基础的研究奠定问题框架。

Abstract: This series of papers aims to establish a universal generalized Brillouin zone (GBZ) theory for higher-dimensional non-Hermitian systems. As the starting point of this series, we emphasize a fundamental question: while the conventional one-dimensional (1D) GBZ condition, $|β_p| = |β_{p+1}|$, is well known to fail in two dimensions, how does this breakdown actually occur as a system gradually crosses over from 1D to 2D? Investigating this question reveals that the existing 1D GBZ theory itself remains incomplete. In this first paper, we therefore systematically review the 1D spectral approach and clarify where the underlying difficulties lie. Our work identifies the key challenges that motivate the wavefunction approach developed in Paper~II.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 在 NP+LEMC 框架（结合 Nernst–Planck 方程与局部平衡蒙特卡洛 Local Equilibrium Monte Carlo，以纳入超越平均场近似 mean-field approximation 的离子关联）内，将径向变化的有效扩散系数剖面拟合至显式水 MD 模拟所得的径向电导率剖面，并应用于带负电硅纳米孔中的 NaCl、CaCl₂ 及其混合物。

Abstract: We develop a multiscale approach for incorporating molecular-scale transport information into computationally efficient models of ion transport through nanopores. A radially varying effective diffusion coefficient profile is fitted to radial conductivity profiles obtained from explicit-water molecular dynamics (MD) simulations. The fitting is performed within the NP+LEMC framework, which combines the Nernst--Planck equation with Local Equilibrium Monte Carlo to account for ion correlations beyond mean-field approximation. We apply the approach to NaCl, CaCl$_2$, and their mixtures in a negatively charged silica nanopore. The resulting diffusion coefficient profiles reproduce the radial current distributions of the MD simulations, including the strong suppression of ionic mobility near the pore wall that cannot be captured by a spatially constant diffusion coefficient inside the pore. NaCl and CaCl$_2$ exhibit qualitatively different transport behavior: the former is cation selective due to enhanced near-wall Na$^+$ conduction, whereas the latter shows weak anion selectivity because strongly bound Ca$^{2+}$ ions have strongly suppressed mobility near the surface. For NaCl--CaCl$_2$ mixtures, preferential Ca$^{2+}$ binding leads to nonlinear changes in ionic conductance. The approach establishes a bridge between experimentally relevant device behavior and computationally efficient reduced models, with explicit-water MD providing the molecular-scale information that can be incorporated into the effective transport coefficients.

Conclusion: 非共线螺旋反铁磁体结合自旋轨道耦合可实现多方向自旋极化，其中长程跃迁对自旋过滤具有显著增强作用，为多轴自旋过滤提供可能。

We further show that the spin polarization is modified significantly in the presence of spin-orbit coupling and it has strong dependence on the parameters associated with the helical system. In particular, long-range hopping significantly enhances the spin-filtering effect, leading to large spin polarization components along all three spatial directions.

Abstract: We present a scalable electron-counting current cell based on a floating gate architecture operated as a Multiple Electron Transport device (MET). Experimental results confirm noise reduction following the square root of the number of independent measurements and demonstrate stable, programmable output current. This approach provides a compact and scalable platform for electron-counting current sources and precision quantum metrology applications.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探究近邻化库珀对在弹道二维电子气中的相干传播所导致的量子自成像行为，并克服输运测量中的空间分辨率限制，以实现对实空间量子干涉的高分辨探测。

Method: 方法上，将周期性超导引线（superconducting leads）配置为具有可编程相位差的相位阵列约瑟夫森结（phased-array Josephson junction），以主动调控超导塔尔博特地毯（superconducting Talbot carpet）；并设计游标式收集器阵列（Vernier-scale collector array）对分数塔尔博特图样（fractional Talbot pattern）进行亚波长采样。采用方晶格紧束缚数值计算（tight-binding numerical calculations）验证实空间干涉图样。

Result: 结果显示，该方法能够以对无序（disorder）高度鲁棒的方式绘制实空间量子干涉，并可直接提取螺旋型（helical）、自旋简并（spin-degenerate）与自旋轨道劈裂（spin-orbit-split）费米面（Fermi surfaces）对应的费米波长（Fermi wavelengths）。

Conclusion: 结论表明，该研究为超导光学（superconducting optics）中的相干波前工程（coherent wavefront engineering）与量子材料诊断（quantum materials diagnostics）建立了一个通用框架。

Abstract: We introduce the superconducting Talbot effect---a macroscopic quantum self-imaging phenomenon occurring when proximitized Cooper pairs propagate through a ballistic two-dimensional electron gas. By configuring periodic superconducting leads into a phased-array Josephson junction with programmable phase differences, we demonstrate active steering of the resulting superconducting Talbot carpet. To overcome transport resolution limits, we design a Vernier-scale collector array that performs sub-wavelength sampling of the fractional Talbot pattern. This approach maps real-space quantum interference with high robustness to disorder, enabling direct extraction of Fermi wavelengths across helical, spin-degenerate, and spin-orbit-split Fermi surfaces. Tight-binding numerical calculations on a square lattice validate the real-space interference patterns. Our results establish a versatile framework for coherent wavefront engineering and quantum materials diagnostics in superconducting optics.

</details>

Abstract: The collective coordinate dynamics of antiferromagnetic and ferrimagnetic textures emerges as a geodesic equation governed by the quantum geometry of the CP$^1$ state associated with the Néel vector, rendering an effective Riemannian gravity and a Lorentz force in the texture motion. </details>

Abstract: Transient pressure diffusion in heterogeneous porous media becomes difficult to resolve efficiently when permeability is discontinuous and spans several orders of magnitude. We develop a domain-decomposed random-feature evolutional deep neural network (DD RF-EDNN) that separates spatial approximation from temporal evolution. Permeability-informed random features are compressed into an orthonormal pressure space, and a conservative finite-volume operator is projected onto this space so that only the reduced coordinates are advanced in time. This formulation retains the dissipative structure of the discrete flow problem without repeated neural-network optimization. The analysis quantifies dictionary and singular-value truncation errors, proves contractivity under an energy-consistent dissipativity correction, and derives a conditional grid-level estimate for homogeneous dynamics and time-independent data admitting a steady lifting. Experiments on low-permeability inclusions, high-conductivity channels and blocks, and three-dimensional grids demonstrate consistent accuracy across distinct coefficient structures. The method reaches a final-time relative $L^2$ error of $9.54\times10^{-4}$ in the principal benchmark and maintains errors on the order of $10^{-2}$ at a permeability contrast of $10^3$. Numerical diagnostics further show that the observed error balance and temporal convergence are consistent with the analysis. These results support DD RF-EDNN as a structure-preserving and interpretable reduced formulation for transient simulations on prescribed heterogeneous media.

</details>


### [22] [An Implementation-Friendly SDG Scheme based on Cartesian Grids for Stokes Equations with Pressure Robustness and Superconvergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24232)
*Bohan Yang,Eric Chung*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于笛卡尔网格（Cartesian grids）的交错间断伽辽金（staggered discontinuous Galerkin, SDG）格式，用于求解 Stokes 方程，具备实现简单、本质压力鲁棒（pressure-robust）以及对所有变量超收敛（superconvergent）的特性，并进一步推广至 Navier-Stokes 方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 SDG 所采用的复合网格（composite meshes）构造较为复杂，本文旨在以笛卡尔网格为基础简化构造，同时保持压力鲁棒性与超收敛性，并将方法拓展至非线性流动问题。

Conclusion: 该 SDG 格式实现简便、理论性质严格，可自然推广至 Navier-Stokes 方程，兼具无条件能量稳定性与二阶精度，适用于一般非均匀笛卡尔网格。

Abstract: This paper develops a staggered discontinuous Galerkin (SDG) scheme based on Cartesian grids for Stokes equations that is simple to implement, intrinsically pressure-robust, and superconvergent for all variables. Instead of the composite meshes used in standard SDG, we construct staggered quadrilateral meshes from Cartesian grids. The scheme takes velocity, pressure, and velocity gradient as unknowns, and employs piecewise-constant spaces with carefully designed staggered continuity. The additional gradient unknowns are locally eliminated via static condensation and can be further removed by mass lumping without loss of accuracy. An explicit pointwise formulation of the scheme is derived, which facilitates implementation and enables a detailed pointwise analysis. We rigorously prove pressure robustness and second-order superconvergence, which holds on general non-uniform Cartesian grids. The scheme is further extended to Navier-Stokes equations by introducing a novel discrete convection term with second-order consistency. Combined with the scalar auxiliary variable (SAV) approach and the Crank-Nicolson (CN) scheme, this yields an unconditionally energy-stable and second-order accurate scheme. Numerical experiments validate the theory and demonstrate accuracy and robustness.

</details>


### [23] [Fourth-Order Energy-Consistent Average Vector Field Discrete Gradient Methods for Dissipative Hamiltonian Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24692)
*Lucas Lautwein,Nicole Marheineke,Håkon Noren Myhr,Kevin Schäfers*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该文将原用于泊松系统（Poisson systems）的微扰原理（perturbation principle）推广至耗散情形，构造了四阶平均向量场离散梯度（average vector field discrete gradient）格式，并通过保持耗散矩阵半正定性的补全（completion）策略保证无条件能量耗散性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散梯度方法（discrete gradient methods）虽能实现耗散哈密顿系统（dissipative Hamiltonian systems）的能量一致时间积分，但传统形式至多达到二阶收敛，精度受限。作者旨在突破该阶数瓶颈，同时保持结构保持（structure-preserving）与能量一致特性。

Method: 基于同余（congruence）的补全方式保持耗散矩阵的正半定性（positive semi-definiteness），从而对所有正步长保证无条件能量耗散性；对纯耗散系统另行推导指数补全（exponential completion）与结构保持有理逼近，并刻画可保持正半定性、可嵌入能量关联分裂格式（energy-associated splitting schemes）的帕德逼近（Padé approximants）。

Result: 在阻尼物理摆（damped physical pendulum）与耗散 Fermi–Pasta–Ulam 系统上的数值实验表明，正半定补全对避免非物理数值不稳定、刻画正确定性动力学至关重要；所提方法达到预期精度与能量一致性，计算代价与现有伽辽金型（Galerkin-type）方法相当，且四阶格式在考察精度范围内效率优于传统二阶离散梯度方法。

Conclusion: 该补全概念为构造高阶能量一致格式提供了系统化途径；四阶格式虽单步成本更高，但整体计算效率仍优于二阶离散梯度方法。

Abstract: Discrete gradient methods provide a structure-preserving approach to energy-consistent time integration of dissipative Hamiltonian systems. In their conventional formulation, however, they generally attain at most second-order convergence. Extending the perturbation principle previously developed for Poisson systems to the dissipative setting, we derive fourth-order average vector field discrete gradient schemes. A congruence-based completion preserves the positive semi-definiteness of the dissipation matrix, thereby guaranteeing unconditional energy dissipativity for all positive step sizes. This completion concept provides a systematic route to higher-order energy-consistent schemes. For purely dissipative systems, an alternative exponential completion is derived, together with structure-preserving rational approximations. In particular, we characterize Padé approximants that preserve positive semi-definiteness and can be incorporated into energy-associated splitting schemes. Numerical experiments on a damped physical pendulum and a dissipative Fermi--Pasta--Ulam system demonstrate the importance of the positive semi-definite completion for avoiding nonphysical numerical instabilities and capturing the correct qualitative dynamics. They further show that the proposed methods achieve the desired accuracy and energy consistency at a computational cost competitive with existing Galerkin-type approaches. Moreover, the fourth-order schemes outperform the conventional second-order discrete gradient method in computational efficiency over the considered accuracy range, despite their higher per-step cost.

</details>

### [24] [Bootstrap certification of string order in quantum spin chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22420)
*Sagnik Banerjee,Haoyu Guo,Debanjan Chowdhury*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作通过将弦算符（string operators）视为原初对象并利用其算子代数闭合性，将多体自举（many-body bootstrap）中弦关联函数的计算代价从指数级降至多项式级，从而无需波函数即可严格认证拓扑相中的非局域弦序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体自举仅依靠正定性约束、无需变分波函数即可认证基态性质，但其计算成本随算符规模指数增长，使得用于诊断拓扑相的长程弦关联（string correlators）无法处理。因此需要一种能在多项式代价下获取任意长度弦关联的方法。

Method: 把裸弦与端点修饰弦（endpoint-dressed strings）视为原初对象，构造其与局部算符词共同满足的闭合算子代数（closed operator algebra），由此得到一个代价随弦长多项式增长半定规划（semidefinite program）。单次计算即可给出所有长度弦关联的估计，并在基态能量被限制于一个窗口内时对每个目标弦给出严格的双侧界。

Result: 在簇伊辛链（cluster Ising）与自旋-1海森堡链（spin-1 Heisenberg）上与密度矩阵重整化群（density-matrix renormalization group, DMRG）结果进行基准比较，该方法直接由哈密顿量认证了簇相与Haldane相的非局域弦序。严格的弦序参数认证显示，界的锐度取决于进入计算的算符种类，而不仅取决于其长度。

Conclusion: 通过利用弦与局部算符词的代数闭合性，该框架将空间延展的可观测量提升为多项式的自举变量，开辟了无需波函数即可认证量子多体系统非局域序的途径。

Abstract: The many-body bootstrap certifies ground-state properties by minimizing energy over correlators constrained only by positivity, without any variational wavefunction. Its cost, however, grows exponentially with the size of the operators involved, placing the long-range string correlators that diagnose topological phases out of reach. We overcome this by treating string operators as primary objects: bare and endpoint-dressed strings satisfy a closed operator algebra with one another and with local words, yielding a semidefinite program whose cost grows only polynomially with string length. A single computation then yields estimates of string correlators of all lengths. Rigorous two-sided bounds are obtained for each target string once the ground-state energy is pinned within a window. When benchmarked against density-matrix renormalization group computations on the cluster Ising and spin$-1$ Heisenberg chains, the method certifies the nonlocal string order of the cluster and Haldane phases directly from the Hamiltonian. Our rigorous certification of string order parameters reveal that the sharpness of the bounds is set by which operators enter the calculation, not by their length alone. By exploiting algebraic closure of strings and local words, our framework lifts spatially extended observables into polynomially tractable bootstrap variables, opening up a route to wavefunction-free certification of nonlocal order in quantum many-body systems.

</details>


### [25] [Approaching the Inverse Neutron Scattering Problem with Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22535)
*Jingyi Luo,Fletcher Williams,Ang-Kun Wu,David Dahlbom,Cristian D. Batista,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究证明神经网络（neural networks）可高效求解从非弹性中子散射（inelastic neutron scattering）谱反演量子磁体哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）参数的逆问题，在蜂窝晶格（honeycomb lattice）海森堡模型（Heisenberg model）基准上实现高精度且抗噪的参数重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从非弹性中子散射测量中确定量子磁体的微观哈密顿量是凝聚态物理的核心难题；动力学自旋结构因子（dynamical spin structure factor）理论上包含相互作用信息，但因参数空间庞大与实验噪声，从谱中提取哈密顿量参数是高度非平庸的逆问题（inverse problem）。

Method: 以处于全极化相（fully polarized phase）的八参数蜂窝晶格海森堡自旋模型为基准，其动力学自旋结构因子可在线性自旋波理论（linear spin-wave theory）下精确计算；利用大规模合成中子散射谱数据集训练神经网络，直接从动力学响应推断哈密顿量参数，并比较全连接神经网络（FCNNs）、一维卷积神经网络（CNN1Ds）与二维卷积神经网络（CNN2Ds）三种架构。

Result: 三种架构均达到高预测精度；训练模型在显著实验不确定性下保持稳健，即使输入谱被幅度达信号强度10%的随机噪声污染，仍能可靠复原哈密顿量参数。

Conclusion: 结果表明机器学习（machine learning）可作为从中子散射数据定量重建哈密顿量的有力框架，并为量子磁性材料的自动化表征提供实用途径。

Abstract: Determining the microscopic Hamiltonian of a quantum magnet from inelastic neutron scattering measurements remains a central challenge in condensed matter physics. While the dynamical spin structure factor contains, in principle, sufficient information about the underlying interactions, extracting Hamiltonian parameters from experimental spectra constitutes a highly nontrivial inverse problem due to the large parameter space and the presence of experimental noise. Here we demonstrate that neural networks can efficiently solve this inverse problem for a broad class of quantum magnets. As a benchmark, we consider an eight-parameter family of honeycomb lattice Heisenberg spin models in the fully polarized phase, where the dynamical spin structure factor can be computed exactly within linear spin-wave theory. Using a large synthetic dataset of neutron scattering spectra, we train neural networks to infer the underlying Hamiltonian parameters directly from the dynamical response. We compare the performance of three different architectures---fully connected neural networks (FCNNs), one-dimensional convolutional neural networks (CNN1Ds), and two-dimensional convolutional neural networks (CNN2Ds)---and find that all achieve high predictive accuracy. Remarkably, the trained models remain robust in the presence of substantial experimental uncertainty, reliably recovering the Hamiltonian parameters even when the input spectra are contaminated by random noise with amplitudes reaching $10\%$ of the signal intensity. Our results establish machine learning as a powerful framework for quantitative Hamiltonian reconstruction from neutron scattering data and provide a practical route toward automated characterization of quantum magnetic materials.

</details>

Abstract: We investigated antiferromagnetic metal TbCuAs$_2$, which exhibits an anomalous resistivity upturn at low temperatures. Here, we present a combined single crystal neutron diffraction, X-ray resonant magnetic scattering, and single crystal X-ray diffraction study of TbCuAs$_2$. These measurements unambiguously determine the Tb magnetic moment direction, the antiferromagnetic arrangement along high symmetry directions, the interplay between structure and magnetism, and their temperature dependence. Our observations are compared with previous studies on sister compounds in the rare-earth copper arsenide family ($R$CuAs$_2$, $R$ = Sm and Gd), which also displays resistivity anomalies. We suggest a common underlying physics governing the structural and magentic properties of rare-earth copper arsenides that exhibit low-temperature resistivity anomalies.

*Xiao Liang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 基于张量回流（tensor-backflow）方法并结合 Lanczos 步骤，研究了二维 t-t'-U Hubbard 模型中 d 波配对，能量精度与最先进神经量子态（neural quantum states）相当，并观察到有限负 t' 增强的 d 波配对涨落，但未出现 ODLRO 所需的广延标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期发展的张量回流方法已在 Fermi-Hubbard 模型中准确描述能量与条纹态（stripe states），本文据此进一步考察二维 t-t'-U Hubbard 模型中 d 波配对的增强现象，聚焦于此前报道存在增强 d 波配对的参数区间。

Method: 对 8×8 晶格，在波函数上引入对称性投影（symmetry projections）后执行一步 Lanczos；对 12×12 与 16×16 晶格，则仅执行一步 Lanczos 而不做对称性投影。主要参数为填充 n=0.875、在位排斥 U=8、次近邻跃迁 t'=-0.2t，并分析实空间配对关联与配对密度矩阵（pair-density matrix）谱权重。

Result: 能量与最先进神经量子态具有竞争力：在 16×16 周期边界条件下，经一步 Lanczos 且无显式对称性强制的张量回流波函数，与保持对称性的神经量子态结果相对能量差为 4.4×10⁻³。t'=-0.2t 时观察到更强的 d 波配对关联及水平与竖直键对分量间的特征相对符号；相较 t'=0，有限负 t' 提升了配对密度矩阵的谱权重。8×8 晶格上，无论是否投影，主导本征值均被增强；12×12 与 16×16 上主导谱权重分散于多个近简并本征模，而非集中于单一模式。

Conclusion: 有限负 t' 增强了 d 波配对涨落，且在大晶格中涨落分布更为广泛，但没有任何单一本征值展现出 ODLRO 所需的广延标度（extensive scaling），因此未给出长程 d 波超导序的直接证据。

Abstract: Building on the recently developed tensor-backflow method, which accurately describes energies and stripe states in the Fermi-Hubbard model, we investigate $d$-wave pairing in the two-dimensional $t$-$t'$-$U$ Hubbard model. For the $8\times8$ lattice, we improve the wave function using symmetry projections followed by one Lanczos step, whereas for the $12\times 12$ and $16\times 16$ lattices, we apply one Lanczos step without symmetry projection. We focus primarily on the parameter regime with filling $n=0.875$, on-site repulsion $U=8$ and next-nearest-neighbor hopping $t'=-0.2t$, a regime in which enhanced $d$-wave pairing has been reported. The resulting energies are competitive with those obtained using state-of-the-art neural quantum states. For example, on a $16\times 16$ lattice with periodic boundary conditions, the tensor-backflow wave function after one Lanczos step without explicit symmetry enforcement achieves a relative energy difference of $4.4\times 10^{-3}$ from the symmetry-preserving neural quantum state result. From the real space pair correlations, for $t'=-0.2t$, we observe stronger $d$-wave pair correlations together with the characteristic relative sign between horizontal and vertical bond-pair components. Compared with $t'=0$, finite negative $t'$ increases the spectral weight of the corresponding pair-density matrix, which indicates the enhanced overall $d$-wave pair fluctuations. The leading eigenvalue is enhanced on the $8\times8$ lattice for both with and without symmetry projections. For larger lattices such as $12\times 12$ and $16\times 16$, the leading pair-density matrix spectral weight is distributed among several nearly degenerate eigenmodes rather than being concentrated in a single dominant mode. This indicates enhanced and more broadly distributed $d$-wave pair fluctuations, but no single eigenvalue exhibits the extensive scaling required for ODLRO.

</details>


### [28] [On the precise boundary of the Néel antiferromagnetic phase in the Shastry-Sutherland model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22907)
*Rongyi Lv,Xiangjian Qian,Mingpu Qin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 基于大规模密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）计算，测量 Shastry-Sutherland 模型中的体交错磁化强度，发现在争议区间 0.78 ≲ J1/J2 ≤ 0.81 内交错磁化强度保持有限，从而支持 PVBS 与 Néel AFM 之间存在直接相变，而非中间量子自旋液体相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shastry-Sutherland 模型的基态相图仍存争议，尤其是 plaquette 价键固体（PVBS）相与 Néel 反铁磁（AFM）相之间相变的性质未定：部分研究主张两相直接相变，另一些则提出存在狭窄的中间量子自旋液体（quantum spin liquid）区间。

Method: 采用大规模密度矩阵重整化群（Density Matrix Renormalization Group, DMRG）计算确定 Néel AFM 相边界；施加交错边界钉扎场（staggered boundary pinning fields）并直接测量体交错磁化强度（bulk staggered magnetization），同时进行关于截断误差（truncation error）的系统外推与细致的有限尺寸标度（finite-size scaling）分析。

Result: 在争议区间 0.78 ≲ J1/J2 ≤ 0.81 内，交错磁化强度始终保持有限（非零），该区间即部分研究认为可能存在量子自旋液体相的区域。

Conclusion: 结合此前已确立的 PVBS 相边界约位于 J1/J2 ≈ 0.78 这一共识，上述结果提供了反对中间量子自旋液体相存在的证据，并支持 Shastry-Sutherland 模型中 PVBS 与 Néel AFM 之间的直接相变。

Abstract: The ground state phase diagram of the Shastry-Sutherland model remains controversial. In particular, the nature of the transition between the plaquette valence bond solid (PVBS) phase and the Néel antiferromagnetic (AFM) phase is still under debate. While some studies suggest a direct transition between the two phases, others propose the existence of a narrow intermediate quantum spin liquid regime. To address this issue, we perform large-scale Density Matrix Renormalization Group calculations to determine the Néel AFM phase boundary in the Shastry-Sutherland model. By applying staggered boundary pinning fields and directly measuring the bulk staggered magnetization, and through systematic extrapolations with respect to truncation error as well as careful finite-size scaling, we find that the staggered magnetization remains finite across the controversial region $0.78 \lesssim J_{1}/J_{2} \leq 0.81$, which was suggested to host a quantum spin liquid phase in some studies. Together with the previously established consensus placing the PVBS phase boundary at $J_{1}/J_{2} \approx 0.78$, these results provide evidence against the existence of an intermediate quantum spin liquid phase and support a direct PVBS--Néel AFM transition in the Shastry-Sutherland model.

</details>


### [29] [Correlation-assisted spin-selective metallicity in strained and bilayer altermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23150)
*Marnin J. Di Nunzio,Frank Lechermann,Ilya M. Eremin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作在单层与双层最小 Hubbard 型模型中系统研究了电子关联、掺杂、单轴应变与层间耦合对交错磁（altermagnetism）的调控，发现交错磁序在准粒子重整化下依然稳健，并可实现完全自旋极化的费米面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交错磁（altermagnets）兼具零净磁化与动量依赖的自旋劈裂能带结构，可在无铁磁性的条件下提供自旋极化载流子，因而成为潜在的自旋电子学材料平台；其电子关联效应与外部调控手段（掺杂、应变、堆叠）的作用机制尚需厘清。

Result: 交错磁序对准粒子重整化表现出稳健性，且在强耦合区出现磁序起始与准粒子相干性丧失之间的分离。单层中掺杂产生显著粒子—空穴不对称，与单轴应变结合可在近半满处形成完全自旋极化的费米面。双层中有利于层间交错磁序参量铁性对齐的堆叠可使动量依赖自旋劈裂相长叠加，弱非对称掺杂还诱导晶胞内电荷序，产生完全自旋极化的低能载流子；在四轨道五电子填充下观察到连续顺磁—交错磁相变，并在更强耦合下向准粒子权重被强烈抑制的类 Mott 区演化。

Conclusion: 关联效应、应变、掺杂与堆叠可作为互补手段用于调控交错磁金属并产生完全自旋极化的费米面。

Abstract: Altermagnets combine vanishing net magnetization with a momentum-dependent spin-split electronic structure, providing a route to spin-polarized carriers without ferromagnetism. Here, we investigate how electronic correlations, doping, uniaxial strain, and interlayer coupling control altermagnetism within a minimal Hubbard-type model for mono- and bilayer systems. Comparing Hartree--Fock theory with the rotationally invariant slave-boson (RISB) approach, we demonstrate the robustness of altermagnetic order against quasiparticle renormalization and reveal a separation between the onset of magnetic order and the loss of quasiparticle coherence at stronger coupling. In the monolayer, doping produces a pronounced particle--hole asymmetry, while its combination with uniaxial strain generates a fully spin-polarized Fermi surface close to half-filling. In the bilayer, stacking that favors ferroic alignment of the layer altermagnetic order parameters allows their momentum-dependent spin splittings to combine constructively. Weak asymmetric doping additionally induces intra-unit-cell charge order, resulting in fully spin-polarized low-energy carriers. At a bilayer filling of five electrons in four orbitals, we find a continuous paramagnet-to-altermagnet transition followed, at stronger coupling, by an evolution toward a Mott-like regime with strongly suppressed quasiparticle weight. Our results establish correlations, strain, doping, and stacking as complementary means of controlling altermagnetic metals and generating fully spin-polarized Fermi surfaces.

</details>


### [30] [Hund-driven local-itinerant duality of Eu-4$f$ electrons in infinite-layer nickelates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23645)
*Yingying Cao,Yi-feng Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 结合密度泛函理论（DFT）与动力学平均场理论（DMFT），在同时处理 Ni-3$d$ 与 Eu-4$f$ 库仑排斥的前提下，揭示 Eu-4$f$ 组态随洪特耦合 $J_{\rm H}$ 变化的三区间演化，解释了实验中观测到的混合价与局域磁矩行为，并预测可与 Ni-3$d$ 电子杂化的低能 4$f$ 准粒子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Eu 取代无限层镍酸盐中出现的重入超导（reentrant superconductivity）与 $T_c$ 升高，引发对 Eu-4$f$ 态性质及其与 Ni-$d$ 电子相互作用的疑问。理解该体系的价态与磁性，是解释其非常规铁磁与超导性质的前提。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论结合动力学平均场理论（DFT+DMFT）计算，研究镍酸盐 112 结构中 Eu 离子的价态与磁性，并显式处理 Ni-3$d$ 与 Eu-4$f$ 壳层的库仑排斥（Coulomb repulsion）。

Result: 发现 Eu-4$f$ 组态随洪特耦合（Hund's rule coupling）$J_{\rm H}$ 呈三区间演化：小 $J_{\rm H}$ 为非磁态，大 $J_{\rm H}$ 为完全自旋极化的二价态，仅中等 $J_{\rm H}$ 出现实验推断的磁性混合价（mixed-valence）Eu 离子态，其中局域磁矩与强关联 $j=7/2$ 准粒子共存。

Conclusion: 这种局域-巡游二重性（local-itinerant duality）源于 $j=5/2$ 与 $7/2$ 流形间 4$f$ 内电荷转移所诱导的有效空穴掺杂轨道选择性莫特态（orbital-selective Mott state）。该结果不仅解释实验观测的 Eu 混合价与局域磁矩起源，还预测可与 Ni-3$d$ 电子杂化的低能 4$f$ 准粒子，为理解 Eu 取代无限层镍酸盐的非常规铁磁与超导性质提供基础。

Abstract: Recent discovery of reentrant superconductivity and elevated $T_c$ in Eu-substituted infinite-layer nickelates raises a critical question concerning the nature of Eu-4$f$ states and their potential interaction with Ni-$d$ electrons. Here we combine density functional theory with the dynamical mean-field theory (DFT+DMFT) calculations to investigate the valence and magnetic properties of Eu-ions in the nickelate 112 structure, explicitly treating the Coulomb repulsion in both Ni-3$d$ and Eu-4$f$ shells. We find a three-regime evolution of the Eu-4$f$ configuration driven by its Hund's rule coupling $J_{\rm H}$. The magnetic and mixed-valence Eu ionic state inferred by experiments only occurs for moderate $J_{\rm H}$, with coexisting local moments and strongly correlated $j=7/2$ quasiparticles, in contrast to the nonmagnetic state at small $J_{\rm H}$ and the fully spin-polarized divalent state at large $J_{\rm H}$. This local-itinerant duality arises from an effectively hole-doped orbital-selective Mott state induced by the intra-4$f$ charge transfer between $j=5/2$ and $7/2$ manifolds. It not only explains the origin of the mixed-valence and local moment behaviors of the Eu-ions observed experimentally, but also predicts low-energy 4$f$ quasiparticles that may hybridize with the Ni-3$d$ electrons and contribute to the elevated $T_c$. Our work provides a basis for understanding the unusual ferromagnetic and superconducting properties of Eu-substituted infinite-layer nickelates.

</details>

*Debasmita Swain,Mohit Mudgal Suman Nandi,Aradhana Kumari,Yogendra Kumar,Vivek Kumar Malik,Anup Pradhan Sakhya,Masahiro Sawada,Shin-ichiro Ideta,Kenya Shimada,Jayita Nayak*

TL;DR: 该研究综合磁性测量、磁输运、XMCD、ARPES 与第一性原理计算，系统表征了笼目铁磁体（kagome ferromagnet）MgMn6Sn6 的磁各向异性、各向异性磁电阻及笼目衍生电子结构，建立三者之间的关联。

The finite orbital-to-spin moment ratio revealed by XMCD suggests a significant role of spin-orbit coupling (SOC) in MgMn6Sn6. Abstract: The Stochastic Series Expansion (SSE) quantum Monte Carlo method with loop updates is among the most powerful approaches for quantum spin and boson systems. In the standard formulation, the magnetic field term is routinely absorbed into the Heisenberg interactions---a strategy that has proven highly efficient across a wide range of field strengths. In this work, we propose a more general SSE framework in which the magnetic field operator is treated as an independent operator class, enabling it to participate in Monte Carlo updates on an equal footing with all other operators. Importantly, the field operator and other interaction operators can transform into one another during the update process. We demonstrate this algorithm using the two-dimensional antiferromagnetic Heisenberg model in a magnetic field as a concrete example. The simulation results agree with those of the conventional algorithm, confirming the correctness of the new formulation. A comparison of the integrated autocorrelation time shows that the new algorithm is competitive. This flexibility not only facilitates measurements of observables associated with operators but also offers potential computational advantages for a broader class of problems.

For the Hubbard quench, TP captures strong-coupling dynamics missed by Hartree--Fock (HF). For the Kitaev quenches, TP accurately reproduces the early-time flux dynamics in several regimes and provides a substantial improvement over HF when two-particle correlations are important. Abstract: The convergence of highly mobile Dirac fermions and flat-band heavy electrons offers a paradigm for emergent quantum phenomena. However, experimental realization of such intriguing state remains elusive. The interaction between Dirac and HOVHS-derived carriers can be captured by a minimal two-pocket model, manifesting as sublinear resistivity above ~100 K and field-induced non-Fermi-liquid behavior at low temperatures. Abstract: The Mott-Hubbard transition is a paradigmatic phenomenon driven by strong electronic correlations. We investigate this transition in a three-orbital modified periodic Anderson model using slave-rotor mean-field theory. In comparison to the single-site solution, short-range spatial correlations reveal a shift of the transition point. Going beyond the slave-rotor analysis, we also evaluate the dynamical self-energy and map it onto the boundary Green's function of a non-interacting semi-infinite auxiliary chain. For the metallic phase, the resulting hopping pattern corresponds to a topologically trivial generalized Su--Schrieffer--Heeger chain. This construction provides a framework for exploring the possible topological character of the Mott transition in this model.

Abstract: Repulsively bound doublons are two-particle composites formed by strong interactions and are usually separated from the scattering continuum. These results demonstrate that spin-orbit coupling can turn selected doublons into interaction-induced BICs, deepening the understanding of continuum physics for interaction-bound pairs.

Second, for a finite-range fermionic family with explicit quartet transfer and sufficiently large onsite penalty, we rigorously prove quartet ODLRO without charge-\(2e\) ODLRO at half quartet filling, both at the hypercubic XY point for \(d\geq2\) and throughout a finite XXZ interval on the square lattice. With local gap \(U_0\), pair hopping \(K\) generates quartet motion at order \(K^2/U_0\), whereas electron hopping \(t\) first contributes at order \(t^4/U_0^3\); charge-\(2e\) excitations remain gapped at \(O(U_0)\). Abstract: Carrier-induced helical magnetism can arise in interacting one-dimensional systems coupled to localized moments. Here, we investigate the coupled-wire sliding-Luttinger-liquid counterparts with and without valley degrees of freedom, using isolated Tomonaga-Luttinger liquids as reference limits. The carrier spin susceptibility mediates a Ruderman-Kittel-Kasuya-Yosida interaction that selects the helical ordering wave vector, while interchannel correlations lock the relative helix phases and stabilize a collective quasi-two-dimensional texture. Using a variational treatment, we estimate the helix-induced partial gap and analyze the reconstruction of the remaining gapless carrier modes. To probe the spatial texture through transport, we incorporate the helix into an anisotropic superconductor-normal-metal-superconductor Josephson junction motivated by the coupled-channel structure of twisted bilayer WTe$_2$. We exploit flux focusing near the junction interfaces to make an applied in-plane field a controlled probe of the spin helix. Relative to junctions without the helix, the helical exchange field modifies the central-lobe width and relative lobe weights. In the transverse-channel geometry, it also produces field-dependent side-lobe asymmetry and central-peak displacement, which remain negligible in the matched references for the parameters considered. The reconstructed carrier sector also exhibits modified local-density-of-states exponents, spin-relaxation behavior, and competing correlations, with distinct signatures in isolated and coupled-wire systems. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We define a natural orientation on a spanning-tree-decorated planar map whereby, roughly speaking, each directed edge in the map is oriented to match the direction of the contour exploration of the spanning tree. Using a strong coupling argument, we deduce analogous statements for directed distances in other random planar maps in the $\sqrt 2$-Liouville quantum gravity (LQG) universality class, including uniform meandric systems and mated-CRT maps for $γ=\sqrt 2$. These results give the scaling dimension for a hypothetical directed version of the $\sqrt 2$-LQG metric.

### [46] [Poisson statistics of one-dimensional random band matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23389)
*Jiaqi Fan,Guangyi Zou*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该论文证明在几乎尖锐的带宽条件 $W^2\ll N/\log N$（$N\times N$ 随机带矩阵（random band matrix）$H$，带宽 $W$）下，$H$ 的体局部统计（bulk local statistics）渐近为泊松点过程（Poisson point process），从而确立随机带矩阵局部统计的泊松-RMT 转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作旨在刻画随机带矩阵局部统计从泊松行为到随机矩阵理论（RMT）行为的转变，与前期关于体特征向量（bulk eigenvectors）局域化-退局域化转变（localization-delocalization transition）的结果形成互补。

  <summary>Details</summary>

In this work, we establish a framework of transformation rules that describe both the angles in the 2 points of view, and the Doppler effect that comes from the source's motion around the lens. We also provide the framework to produce consistent AGN-lensed waveforms from any currently available GW waveform. These AGN-CBC systems give 2 unique points of view on a binary merger thus opening up a new window into the merger's properties unlike any other lens system.

The second weak phase occurs as the secondary's orbit becomes nearly coplanar with the circumbinary disk, reducing the gas and magnetic flux reaching the primary. Result: 在维度为一、二、三的景观中均发现存在相当比例的多阶段暴胀轨迹；在场空间维度增加而典型景观曲率固定时，多阶段暴胀轨迹所占比例上升。多阶段暴胀会产生原初特征（primordial features）以及偏离最简单慢滚模型行为的其他表现。

Conclusion: 多阶段暴胀在暴胀景观中并非罕见情形，其占比可观且随场空间维度增加而提高，因此推导原初密度扰动时默认单阶段慢滚暴胀的做法可能需要重新审视；论文另给出若干相关量的定义与轨迹性质的分类，为后续分析提供基础。

Abstract: Generic potentials in an inflationary landscape are typically too steep to support a prolonged period of cosmic inflation. The occurrence of such regions leads broadly to two possibilities: a single prolonged period of slow-roll inflation (which we call single-stage inflation), or multiple shorter periods of inflation separated by transient departures from slow roll (which we call multi-stage inflation), giving rise to primordial features and other departures from the behavior of the simplest slow-roll model. We find a substantial fraction of multi-stage inflationary trajectories in landscapes with field-space dimensions one, two, and three, and this fraction increases with dimensionality at fixed typical landscape curvature. In constructing the inflationary landscapes and identifying successful inflationary trajectories, we also define several relevant quantities and classify detailed properties of both multi-stage and multi-field inflationary trajectories.

Abstract: We study a five-dimensional realization of endothermic dark matter motivated by the recent high-recoil LUX-ZEPLIN event. The same localization that suppresses a distant-brane Majorana operator and generates the inelastic splitting also controls the couplings to the Kaluza--Klein (KK) dark-photon tower, producing a localization-filtered mediator propagator. The latter is used only to compare terrestrial and solar kinematics; the published IceCube bound is Higgsino-specific and is not directly imposed on our singlet KK model. At the LZ benchmark with $R^{-1}=0.30~\GeV$ and $m_0=0.10~\GeV$, the KK tower enhances the high-recoil xenon rate by a factor of about four relative to a single dark photon at fixed microscopic coupling. The LZ point lies just below the terrestrial halo speed ceiling, whereas the $566~\keV$ comparison point is inaccessible to ordinary xenon scattering and instead lies in a kinematic regime accessible to solar capture; any neutrino constraint on the present singlet model remains dependent on its capture and annihilation dynamics. The characteristic prediction of the extra-dimensional construction is therefore a correlated interplay of localization, KK spectral distortion, and inelastic kinematics.

Abstract: We investigate the spectroscopic and decay properties of the $B_c$ meson within a nonrelativistic quark model. The model predicts an inverted fine-structure ordering in the $P$- and $D$-wave multiplets, reflecting the competition between the one-gluon-exchange (OGE) and confinement contributions to the spin--orbit interaction. The allowed M1 widths are strongly suppressed by the small predicted hyperfine splittings, highlighting the importance of a direct $B_c^*$ mass measurement. The radial Regge trajectories are nearly linear across all considered families, with slopes decreasing as the orbital angular momentum increases.   <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 以硅和锗为靶核，通过非相对论有效场论（non-relativistic effective field theory, NREFT）形式引入一整套核形状因子（nuclear form factors），其不确定性由大规模壳模型（large-scale shell model）计算获得；同时考察高能物理内容（粒子物理系数）对核不确定性幅度的影响。

Result: 核结构不确定性会改变核形状因子，进而影响类SuperCDMS（SuperCDMS-like）实验的排除限（exclusion limits）；在若干WIMP-核散射响应（scattering responses）中，核不确定性达到不可忽略的量级。

Conclusion: 直接探测的多个核响应存在不可忽略的核结构不确定性，表明在实验分析与解释中必须纳入核结构考量。

Abstract: Dark matter (DM) direct detection searches look for nuclear recoils as a result of weakly interacting massive particles (WIMPs) scattering off nuclei. Calculating the rate associated with this WIMP-nucleus elastic scattering process involves input from several components, which include the direct detection experimental response functions, the high energy physics content employed, the DM halo velocity distribution, and the target nuclear structure information. Uncertainties in any of these components can impact the interpretation of experimental signals, and affect analysis of rate predictions. In this work, we focus on the uncertainties present due to the nuclear structure modelling of silicon and germanium targets, where a comprehensive set of nuclear form factors are employed through a non-relativistic effective field theory (NREFT) formalism. We show that these uncertainties, obtained from large-scale shell model calculations, impact the nuclear form factors and thus experimental exclusion limits for a SuperCDMS-like experiment. The impact of high energy physics content (through particle physics coefficients) on the magnitude of the nuclear uncertainties is also explored for several WIMP-nucleus scattering responses. Non-negligible nuclear uncertainties are present for several direct detection nuclear responses, indicating that nuclear structure must be accounted for in experimental analysis and interpretation.

</details>

TL;DR: 该文针对 LUX-ZEPLIN（LZ）报告的核反冲候选事件，提出用复合非弹性暗物质（composite inelastic dark matter）解释，并给出与 LZ 90% 置信区间相符的基准参数。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LZ 合作组观测到一个重建能量为 248±23_stat±23_sys keV 的核反冲候选事件，需要理论解释其可能的暗物质起源。作者据此探索复合非弹性暗物质框架对该事件的适用性。

Method: 采用限制性 SU(3)_D 理论，用十维算符（dimension-ten operator）将最轻重子分裂为两个马约拉纳态（Majorana states）；通过耦合到重子数的轻子惰性矢量（leptophobic vector）介导吸热散射（endothermic scattering）。

Result: 取暗重子质量 1 TeV、能级劈裂 300 keV 时，参考核子截面 6.5×10^-43 cm² 落在 LZ 已发表的双侧 90% 置信区间内；该基准确定 M_V/g_B≃9.9 TeV，且与太阳中微子和双喷注（dijet）限制相容。

Abstract: The LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) Collaboration has reported a nuclear-recoil candidate with reconstructed energy $248\pm23_{\rm stat}\pm23_{\rm sys}\,\mathrm{keV}$. We investigate an interpretation in terms of composite inelastic dark matter, in which a dimension-ten operator splits the lightest baryon of a confining $SU(3)_{\rm D}$ theory into two Majorana states. Endothermic scattering is mediated by a leptophobic vector coupled to baryon number. For a dark-baryon mass of $1\,\mathrm{TeV}$ and a splitting of $300\,\mathrm{keV}$, a reference nucleon cross section of $6.5\times10^{-43}\,\mathrm{cm^2}$ lies within LZ's published two-sided 90\% confidence interval. This benchmark fixes $M_V/g_B\simeq9.9\,\mathrm{TeV}$, where $M_V$ and $g_B$ are the mediator mass and baryon-number gauge coupling, and is compatible with solar neutrino and dijet constraints.

</details>


### [66] [Resonant Gravitational Production of Chiral Dark Photons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22423)
*Leah Jenks,Marc Kamionkowski,Edward W. Kolb*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 具有手性宇称破缺耦合的矢量场在暴胀时期可通过共振机制被有效产生，其横向模式可主导晚期丰度，从而拓宽暗光子暗物质参数空间并可能产生宇称破坏信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究已知具手性、宇称奇（parity-odd）耦合的矢量场会发生共振放大，但该现象在早期宇宙暴胀期间宇宙学引力粒子产生中的作用尚待考察。本文旨在将这一共振放大机制应用于暗光子暗物质产生的研究。

Result: 由于该耦合，横向模式（在最小与非最小耦合理论中均处于次主导）能够主导总晚期丰度，从而拓宽暗光子暗物质的可行参数空间。在快子情形中，产生由单一螺旋度模式（helicity mode）主导，形成手性暗光子族，可能带来宇称破坏的可观测信号。此外，产生过程依赖再加热历史（reheating history），再加热的信息编码于数密度谱（number density spectrum）中。

Conclusion: 手性宇称奇耦合显著改变暗光子的产生与丰度结构，扩大暗光子暗物质参数空间，并在快子情形下产生手性种群及潜在宇称破坏信号；同时数密度谱可编码再加热历史信息。

Abstract: Vector fields with a chiral, parity-odd coupling to a pseudoscalar field are known to undergo resonant amplification. In this work, we study this phenomenon in the context of cosmological gravitational particle production during early-universe inflation. We consider a massive vector (dark photon) field with a parity-odd coupling to the inflaton. There are two regimes of production, delineated by the relative size of the coupling and the dark photon mass, arising from a tachyonic instability, and a parametric resonance, respectively. As a result of the coupling, the transverse modes, subdominant in both the minimally and nonminimally coupled theories, can dominate the total late-time abundance. The viable parameter space for dark-photon dark matter is thus widened. Furthermore, in the tachyonic case, the production is dominated by a single helicity mode, leading to a population of chiral dark photons that can lead to potential parity-violating observational signatures. We further explore the dependence of the production on the reheating history, and find that information about reheating is encoded in the number density spectrum.

</details>

Abstract: We construct the Yukawa couplings of the quark sector as overlap integrals of Gaussian wave functions in extra spatial dimensions. Assuming that the wave function of each chiral fermion is localised at a point in the extra dimensions, while that of the Higgs doublet is flat, the Yukawa matrix elements are determined by the relative positions of the three left-handed quark doublets, $q_1$, $q_2$, $q_3$, and the six right-handed quark singlets, $u_1$, $u_2$, $u_3$, $d_1$, $d_2$, $d_3$. We employ numerical optimisation techniques from machine learning to identify field configurations that satisfy all current experimental constraints on the quark Yukawa sector. In this framework, the large hierarchy of quark masses arises naturally, since the overlap integrals of Gaussian wave functions are exponentially sensitive to ${\mathcal O}(1)$ separations in the extra dimensions.

### [68] [Exact Amplitude Reconstruction in the Real Common-Phase Sector of Small-x Diffractive Energy Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22899)

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小-$x$ 衍射振幅的角向结构通常难以直接测量，而能量流分布提供了可能的实验通道，因此需要建立二者间的定量关系。

Method: 在领头梯近似（leading-eikonal approximation）下研究相干衍射双喷注（diffractive dijet）产生，建立偶极振幅角向多极矩、胶子 Wigner 分布（gluon Wigner distribution）与衍射散射振幅之间的关系，并显式保留各自不同的径向变换。

Abstract: We develop an operator-level framework for reconstructing the angular structure of small-$x$ diffractive amplitudes from energy-flow measurements. Focusing on coherent diffractive dijet production in the leading-eikonal approximation, we establish the relation between the angular multipoles of the dipole amplitude, the gluon Wigner distribution, and the diffractive scattering amplitude, while keeping their distinct radial transforms explicit. We show that, in the real common-phase sector and away from diffractive zeros, the normalized scattering amplitude can be reconstructed directly, up to a global sign convention, from the square root of the normalized energy-flow distribution. For generic complex amplitudes, the measured intensity instead determines autocorrelations of the amplitude spectrum and phase retrieval is not unique without additional information. We further discuss corrections arising from harmonic-dependent phases, practical conditions for an EIC extraction, and effects beyond the leading-eikonal approximation.

Conclusion: 所提出的混合近似策略在缺乏完整解析解的情况下可给出可靠的尖点反常维度估计，且与先前结果的一致性支持该方法的可靠性。

# math.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [70] [Dry Ten Martini Problem in the Subcritical Type I Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23467)
*Xianzhe Li*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 该文通过建立双算子（dual operators）双曲投影（hyperbolic projection）的预解式分解（resolvent factorization），在次临界（subcritical）情形下验证了 Ge–Jitomirskaya–You 的谱隙猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在解决 Ge–Jitomirskaya–You（[GJY, You]）提出的关于亚临界能量是否构成开放谱隙端点的猜想。该问题涉及无理频率与解析势下的谱隙结构。

Method: 方法与核心工具为：对有限程双算子（finite-range dual operators）对应的双曲投影（hyperbolic projection），利用具有非对角指数衰减（exponential off-diagonal decay）的核进行预解式分解（resolvent factorization）。

Result: 作者证明：对任意无理频率（irrational frequency）与任意解析势（analytic potential），每个满足谱隙标记条件（gap-labelling condition）的次临界 Type I 能量均为某个开放谱隙（open spectral gap）的端点。

Conclusion: 该结果在次临界情形（subcritical regime）下证实了 Ge–Jitomirskaya–You 的猜想，表明该猜想在次临界范围内成立。

Abstract: We establish a resolvent factorization of the hyperbolic projection associated with the finite-range dual operators, by a kernel with exponential off-diagonal decay. As an application, we prove that, for every irrational frequency and every analytic potential, each subcritical Type I energy satisfying the gap-labelling condition is an endpoint of an open spectral gap. This confirms the conjecture of Ge--Jitomirskaya--You [GJY,You] in the subcritical regime.

</details>

Zalambani,J. A. Zamora Saa,F. Zangari,M. Zavertyaev,M. Zdybal,F. Zenesini,C. Zeng,M. Zeng,S. H Zeng,C. Zhang,C. Zhang,D. Zhang,J. Zhang,L. Zhang,Q. Z. Zhang,R. Zhang,S. Zhang,S. L. Zhang,Y. Zhang,Z. Zhang,J. Zhao,M. Zhao,Y. Zhao,A. Zhelezov,S. Z. Zheng,X. Z. Zheng,Y. Zheng,T. Zhou,X. Zhou,V. Zhovkovska,L. Z. Zhu,X. Zhu,X. Zhu,Y. Zhu,V. Zhukov,J. Zhuo,T. Zies,D. Zuliani,X. Zuo*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 在 LHCb 实验 13 TeV 质子-质子碰撞数据中，测量了 $W$ 玻色子横向螺旋度分数（transverse helicity fractions）随快度（rapidity）$y_W$ 的变化，结果呈现强烈快度依赖并与次领头阶（next-to-leading-order, NLO）标准模型预言一致，为前向区域首次测定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $W$ 玻色子极化（polarization）相关的螺旋度分数是检验电弱理论（electroweak theory）与量子色动力学（QCD）描述的重要观测量；前向区域此前缺乏实验测定，而该区域对质子部分子分布（parton distribution functions）及理论计算敏感。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在前向区域（forward region）测定 $W$ 玻色子的横向螺旋度分数，为极化相关理论描述提供了新的实验约束与检验。

Abstract: The transverse helicity fractions of $W$ bosons are measured as a function of the $W$ boson rapidity, $y_{W}$, in the range $0 \leq y_{W} \leq 5$ using $W\toμν_μ$ decays in $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV recorded by the LHCb experiment and corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $5.1$ fb$^{-1}$. The fractions are extracted from a template fit to the muon transverse momentum and pseudorapidity. The results show a strong rapidity dependence and agree with next-to-leading-order Standard Model predictions, providing the first determination of the transverse helicity fractions of $W$ bosons in the forward region.

Method: 利用中微子事件产生器 GENIE 的模拟，其中包含 Geant4 Bertini Cascade 与 INCL++ 两种强子散射模型实现，针对中微子实验中常见的强子（核子与 π 介子）及常用靶核，考察模型预测的核子产额。

Abstract: In neutrino scattering experiments, the emitted final state particles are essential for identifying interaction channels and reconstructing neutrino energies. During intranuclear propagation, neutrino-induced hadrons can interact with surrounding nucleons, altering both the number of final state particles and detectable energy depositions. As a result, neutrino experiments rely on hadron-nucleus scattering models to simulate these nuclear effects. This paper explores nucleon production predicted by hadron scattering models for common hadrons in neutrino experiments, specifically nucleons and pions. We investigate these models with simulations from the \textsc{GENIE} neutrino event generator, which includes implementations of \textsc{Geant4} Bertini Cascade and \textsc{INCL++}. For popular target nuclei, we find that the number of emitted nucleons for mesonless hadron interactions can be parameterized by simple Gaussian and exponential decay functions. We present these relationships and provide reweighting tools for addressing the hadron scattering model spread in both visible energy and final state nucleon multiplicities.

</details>


### [73] [Mixture Density Networks for Neutrino Reconstruction at Hadron Colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22979)
*Seungjin Yang,Jason S. H. Lee,Junghwan Goh*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强子对撞机（hadron collider）中中微子纵向动量无法直接观测，导致中微子动量重建（neutrino momentum reconstruction）本质上存在多解歧义。该研究旨在为半轻子 $t\bar{t}$ 事例中的这一歧义问题提供更优的求解方法。

Method: 采用 \monster{}，即一种混合密度网络（mixture density network）：以基于 Transformer 的事件编码器（event encoder）从重建事例对象中提取表示，预测中微子动量条件分布的多元正态混合（multivariate normal mixture），并由解析密度（closed-form density）直接给出无采样的点估计（sampling-free point estimate）。

Result: 在 \nuflows{} 引入的公开基准（benchmark）上，\monster{} 相较经验众数（empirical-mode）\nuflows{} 基线将三动量残差（three-momentum residual）的第 68 百分位降低 5\%、第 95 百分位降低 6\%；在偏置（bias）相当的情况下，逐变量均方根误差（root-mean-square error, RMSE）降低 2\%–9\%；推理速度依设备与批量大小提升 2–8.5 倍。

Conclusion: 结果表明，对于中微子重建任务，混合密度网络是归一化流（normalizing flow）的一个具竞争力的替代方案。

Abstract: Neutrino momentum reconstruction at hadron colliders is intrinsically ambiguous because the longitudinal momentum is not directly observed. We study this problem in semileptonic $t\bar{t}$ events using \monster{} (Mixture of Neutrino Solutions with Transformer Event Representation), a mixture density network that predicts a multivariate normal mixture for the conditional distribution of neutrino momentum from reconstructed event objects. The model uses a Transformer-based event encoder and yields a sampling-free point estimate of the neutrino momentum from the closed-form density. On the public benchmark introduced with \nuflows, \monster{} reduces the 68th percentile of the three-momentum residual by 5\% and the 95th percentile by 6\% relative to the empirical-mode \nuflows{} baseline, with per-variable root-mean-square errors smaller by 2 to 9\% at comparable bias, while running 2 to 8.5 times faster at inference, depending on the device and batch size. These results indicate that mixture density networks are a competitive alternative to normalizing flows for neutrino reconstruction.

</details>


### [74] [Detailed investigation on the Geant4 simulation of alpha particles in liquid scintillator detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23793)
*Shiwen Xu,Shubing Liu,Zeyuan Yu*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: α 粒子源日益用于大型液体闪烁体探测器（liquid scintillator detector）的标定，但其能量沉积的 Geant4 模拟尚需系统验证。

Method: 在 4 m 立方 LAB 基液闪中，使用 Geant4 11.4.2 逐步施加 Birks 猝灭（Birks quenching），考察步长、产生阈值、Birks 常数与电磁模型依赖性，并用 Geant4-DNA 分析 keV 尺度电子径迹结构。

Result: δ 电子相关涨落主导模拟响应；所测显式 δ 设置下，次级径迹猝灭响应贡献的方差为总方差的 1.01--1.12 倍，负的主--次协方差降低总方差。模拟猝灭曲线形状与 SNO+ 数据在当前不确定度内相符（$k_B=0.007$--$0.010$ g cm$^{-2}$ MeV$^{-1}$ 时 $χ^2/\mathrm{ndf}\simeq0.14$--$0.17$）。Geant4-DNA 显示强烈的电子径迹弯曲，曲折度（tortuosity）由 200 eV 的约 6 降至 10 keV 的 1.6。

Conclusion: 在所测配置中 δ 电子涨落是内在分辨率的主要贡献。产生阈值的影响大于电磁模型：Penelope 与 Livermore 在 L/E 上约 3% 内一致，而 Standard 给出更高的 L/E，在低阈值时尤为明显。

Abstract: Purpose: Alpha particles from $^{214}$Po, $^{212}$Po, and $^{210}$Po are increasingly used to calibrate large liquid scintillator detectors, but Geant4 simulations of their energy deposition require detailed validation.
  Methods: We simulate alpha particles in a 4 m cube of LAB-based liquid scintillator with Geant4 11.4.2 and apply Birks quenching per step. We study step-size, production-threshold, Birks-constant, and electromagnetic-model dependence, and use Geant4-DNA to examine keV-scale electron track structure.
  Results: Delta-electron-associated fluctuations dominate the simulated response. Across the tested explicit-delta settings, the variance of the quenched-response contribution from secondary tracks is 1.01--1.12 times the total variance; a negative primary--secondary covariance reduces the total. The simulated quenching-curve shape is compatible with SNO+ data within current uncertainties, with $χ^2/\mathrm{ndf}\simeq0.14$--$0.17$ for $k_B=0.007$--$0.010$ g cm$^{-2}$ MeV$^{-1}$. Geant4-DNA gives strong electron-track curvature, with tortuosity decreasing from about 6 at 200 eV to 1.6 at 10 keV.
  Conclusion: Delta-electron fluctuations are the dominant contribution to the intrinsic resolution in the tested configurations. The production threshold has a larger effect than the electromagnetic model: Penelope and Livermore agree within about 3% in L/E, whereas Standard gives higher L/E, especially at low thresholds.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用快速生成模型（generative models）进行模拟，以提升模拟生成效率；并从概念层面讨论杰文斯悖论（Jevons' paradox）在该领域的表现。

Result: 快速生成模型可产出效率高约100倍的模拟；文中指出需借助现实的（realistic）评价指标才能实现"真正的"效率，但摘要未给出具体量化结果或实验数据。

Conclusion: 文章讨论了领域内的杰文斯悖论（Jevons' paradox），并强调现实指标（realistic metrics）对于实现"真实"效率的重要性。

Abstract: Simulation is a major computational expense in HEP, and calorimeter simulation in particular drives the overall energy cost of our physics analyses. Future detectors will contain more finely grained calorimeters than ever, and their data analyses will demand unprecedented simulated statistics. Fast generative models redefine what is possible, producing simulations 100 times more efficiently. This article addresses Jevons' paradox in our field and considers the importance of realistic metrics in achieving "true" efficiency.

</details>

Abstract: The SHiP/NA67 experiment aims to search for feebly interacting GeV-scale new particles and to perform all-flavour neutrino-physics measurements at the HI-ECN3 beam facility at the CERN SPS. The collaboration is currently optimising the experiment's initial configuration for the commissioning and first physics runs of 2032-2033. The detector subsystems comprise a few large-area instruments: for new-particle searches, highly sophisticated veto detectors, high timing-resolution detectors, a lightweight straw tracker system, and high-spatial-precision calorimeters; and, for neutrino reconstruction, small transverse-size, high-granularity calorimeters.

</details>

*Leonardo Giannini,Emmanouil Vourliotis*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
