Method: 将原本用于径迹构建、基于卡尔曼滤波并利用线程级与数据级并行性的 mkFit 算法扩展至径迹拟合任务；评估基于现实的 Phase-2 模拟，针对 CMS 高能级触发器中的径迹拟合开展研究。

Result: 给出 mkFit 用于 CMS 高能级触发器径迹拟合的初步结果，涵盖物理性能与计算性能；摘要未提供具体数值。

Conclusion: mkFit 已被 CMS 采纳为 Run 3 径迹构建主算法并在保持或改善物理性能的同时实现约 3.5 倍平均加速；其在径迹拟合任务的扩展已获得初步结果，具有应对 HL-LHC 更高需求的潜力。

Abstract: The mkFit algorithm provides an implementation of the Kalman filter-based track reconstruction algorithm that exploits both thread- and data-level parallelism. It has been adopted by the CMS Collaboration as the primary track building algorithm during the Run 3 of the LHC for both the online and offline track reconstruction sequences. Thanks to mkFit, an average speedup of a factor 3.5 is achieved in track building while retaining or improving physics performance. As a consequence of the speedup provided by mkFit in track building, track fitting has become a comparably significant component of the overall tracking time. Given the increased demands of the High-Luminosity Large Hadron Collider, further speedup can be achieved by extending mkFit to the track fitting task. We present preliminary results for track fitting in the CMS High-Level Trigger using mkFit, covering both the physics and the computational performance, based on realistic Phase-2 simulations.

</details>


### [78] [Goodness-of-fit for multi-distribution neutrino cross-section measurements with shared events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24796)
*Francisco Martinez Lopez,London Cooper-Troendle,Steven Gardiner,Patrick Green,Tanaz Mohayai*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 当多个微分截面（differential cross-section）测量取自同一样本时，事件共享（event-sharing）结构会在各分布的分箱计数（bin counts）间引入精确线性约束，使协方差矩阵（covariance matrix）秩亏（rank deficiency），导致全局拟合优度检验中 χ² 值被非物理地放大。该工作提出范围投影 χ²（range-projected χ²），将检验限制在承载独立统计信息的子空间，自由度（degrees of freedom）为 N_bins − N_null。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多微分截面测量共享同一事件样本，使同一事件同时贡献于多个分布，从而在分箱计数之间产生精确线性约束，降低测量的有效维度。既有做法忽略此结构：数值精度可能意外恢复协方差矩阵的可逆性，但受约束维度上的 χ² 贡献是任意的，使全局拟合优度检验得到偏大的 χ² 值。

Method: 作者提出范围投影 χ²（range-projected χ²）作为检验统计量，把拟合优度检验限制在携带独立统计信息的子空间内，其自由度为 N_bins − N_null，其中 N_null 为独立约束数目。分析表明秩亏是事件共享结构的可预测结果，且 N_null 可分解为两部分：由分箱几何（binning geometry）先验确定的结构性贡献，以及依赖相空间占据（phase-space occupancy）的运动学贡献。

Result: 该方法通过一个解析玩具模型（analytical toy model）以及一个模拟的中微子—氩（neutrino–argon）截面测量进行验证，后者包含解卷（unfolding）与多源系统不确定性（systematic uncertainties）。

Conclusion: 事件共享所导致的协方差矩阵秩亏是可预测的结构性效应，而非数值噪声。范围投影 χ² 通过在独立统计信息子空间内进行检验，可避免 χ² 被人为放大，且 N_null 可按结构性贡献与运动学贡献加以分解。

Abstract: When multiple differential cross-section measurements are extracted from a common event sample, the same events contribute simultaneously to multiple distributions. This event-sharing structure imposes exact linear constraints among the bin counts, reducing the effective dimensionality of the measurement below the total number of bins. Since the covariance matrix obeys these inter-distribution constraints, it contains a rank deficiency. While limited numerical precision may inadvertently restore invertibility, the $χ^{2}$ contributions along constrained dimensions will be arbitrary, yielding unphysically inflated $χ^{2}$ values in global goodness-of-fit tests. We present the range-projected $χ^{2}$, a test statistic that restricts the goodness-of-fit test to the subspace carrying independent statistical information, yielding a $χ^{2}$ with $N_\text{bins} - N_\text{null}$ degrees of freedom, where $N_\text{null}$ is the number of independent constraints. We show that this rank deficiency is a predictable consequence of the event-sharing structure, and that $N_\text{null}$ decomposes into a structural contribution determined a priori from the binning geometry and a kinematic contribution that depends on the phase-space occupancy. The method is validated with an analytical toy model and a simulated neutrino--argon cross-section measurement including unfolding and multi-source systematic uncertainties.

</details>


### [79] [Search for a top-philic heavy resonance in association with top quarks in $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s} = $ 13 TeV and 13.6 TeV with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24950)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: ATLAS 实验搜索与顶夸克伴随产生并衰变为顶夸克对的新顶亲和质量矢量玻色子（top-philic vector boson，$Z'$），使用 13 TeV 与 13.6 TeV $pp$ 碰撞数据。基于 Transformer 的分类器用于优化信号与本底区分。在基准场景（$c_t=1$，$\theta=0$）下未观测到显著超出，排除 1150 GeV 以下的 $Z'$ 质量，期望上限为 1250 GeV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究针对顶夸克与其他粒子可能存在的新型相互作用，搜索一种仅与顶夸克耦合的矢量玻色子（top-philic vector boson，$Z'$），作为标准模型（Standard Model）之外新物理的候选信号。

Method: 利用大型强子对撞机（Large Hadron Collider，LHC）上 ATLAS 探测器收集的 $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV 与 13.6 TeV $pp$ 碰撞数据，对应积分亮度分别为 140 fb$^{-1}$ 与 56 fb$^{-1}$。选取恰好两个同号轻子（same-sign leptons）或至少三个轻子（电子或缪子）的事例，并采用以 $Z'$ 质量参数化的 Transformer 分类器优化信号与本底区分。

Result: 结果在色单态矢量玻色子（colour-singlet vector boson）与顶夸克以耦合强度 $c_t$ 和手征角 $\theta$ 耦合的简化模型中进行解释。未观测到高于标准模型预期本底的显著超出。

Conclusion: 在 $c_t=1$、$\theta=0$ 的基准场景下，质量低于 1150 GeV 的 $Z'$ 玻色子被以 95% 置信水平（confidence level）排除，相应期望上限为 1250 GeV。

Abstract: A search for a new top-philic vector boson ($Z'$) produced in association with a top-quark or a pair of top quarks, and decaying into a top quark pair is presented. The search uses $pp$ collision data collected by the ATLAS detector at the Large Hadron Collider at centre-of-mass energies of $\sqrt{s} =$ 13 TeV and 13.6 TeV, corresponding to integrated luminosities of 140 fb$^{-1}$ and 56 fb$^{-1}$, respectively. Events containing exactly two same-sign leptons or at least three leptons (electrons or muons) are selected. A transformer-based classifier parameterised as a function of the $Z'$ mass is used to optimise the signal-to-background discrimination. The results are interpreted in a simplified model with a colour-singlet vector boson coupled to top quarks with coupling strength $c_t$ and chirality angle $θ$. No significant excess is observed above the expected Standard Model background. An exact trace identity shows that the vector Hamiltonian is not positive definite in the generic branch, even when the kinetic eigenvalues are positive and the propagation speeds are real. The stationary limit reproduces the results of Ref.~\cite{ShirafujiNashed1997}, and a representative parameter scan illustrates vector-mode splitting and kinetic degeneracy.

Abstract: We investigate the dynamics of charged particles and fields in the background of a charged black hole in bumblebee gravity, aiming to better understand the role played by the Lorentz-violating parameter $l$. We first review the derivation of the charged bumblebee black hole and investigate its main physical and geometrical properties. We then obtain the circular and bound trajectories of charged particles and find that the innermost stable circular orbit increases with $l$. By using a numerical approach, we compute the total absorption cross section for arbitrary values of the scalar wave frequency $ω$. Since the spacetime is not asymptotically flat, we construct the total absorption cross section by taking into account the modified asymptotic momentum and flux. Analytical approximations are derived in the low- and high-frequency regimes. The low-frequency expression displays a transition velocity that separates two distinct asymptotic branches and generalizes the corresponding Reissner-Nordström result. Our numerical results for the cross section agree with the analytical approximations in their corresponding limits, and the absorption parameter space can be divided into three regions: Unbounded absorption, bounded absorption, and bounded superradiance. In particular, superradiance occurs whenever $ω<ω_{c}$, where $ω_{c}$ is the threshold for superradiance. Moreover, the superradiance amplification decreases as we increase $l$, and charged bumblebee black holes with $l < 0$ lead to greater superradiant scattering than in the Reissner-Nordström case. We also note that although increasing $l$ lowers the potential barrier, it reduces the total absorption cross section because of the geometrical factor $1/(1+l)$ present in the cross section formula.

Abstract: Recent work has used the method of characteristic gluing to construct spacetimes violating the third law of black hole mechanics. This paper uses numerical methods to investigate two aspects of the gluing construction of solutions describing the gravitational collapse of a charged scalar field to form an extremal Reissner-Nordström black hole. First, for a massless scalar field, we improve significantly the lower bounds on the size of the final black hole for which gluing is possible. Second, for a massive scalar field, it is known that the formation of an extremal Reissner--Nordström black hole is impossible if the mass-to-charge ratio of the scalar field exceeds unity. We present strong evidence that this bound is sharp by constructing gluing solutions with a scalar field mass-to-charge ratio just below unity.

</details>

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将黑洞视为依赖频率、角度与偏振的滤波器（filter），量化其对待定态引力波背景（stationary gravitational-wave background）的响应。结果表明稳态响应属实频散射（real-frequency scattering）问题，不产生额外准正模（quasi-normal-mode）谱线；对Schwarzschild黑洞只有视界吸收（horizon absorption）造成群体级谱畸变，且宇宙学尺度上的光深（optical depth）可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在厘清一个常见混淆：黑洞受扰动时会"振铃"（ringdown），但面对定态背景时其响应本质上是散射而非辐射源。作者希望把这一标准区分定量化，并评估黑洞群体是否会在引力波背景中留下可观测的累积谱特征。

Method: 将黑洞建模为依赖频率、角度与偏振的滤波器，先对Schwarzschild黑洞详细计算其转移函数（transfer function），识别吸收与相位延迟（phase-delay）特征，并把定态响应与有限波包（finite wave packet）激发的因果铃宕（causal ringdown）相联系；随后将单黑洞结果推广为宇宙学群体的角度与偏振输运核（transport kernel），并进一步扩展到Kerr黑洞。

Result: 对Schwarzschild黑洞，各向同性定态背景下弹性散射不产生净单极（monopole）信号，只有视界吸收造成群体级谱畸变。推广到宇宙学群体后得到的光深对现实黑洞群体可忽略，即使小行星质量原初黑洞（primordial black holes）构成全部暗物质亦如此。对Kerr黑洞，超辐射（superradiance）可在特定共转模式中产生真实放大，但各向同性入射与随机自旋取向会强烈稀释漫射信号。

Conclusion: 可观测效应更可能出现在稀有的、邻近的、取向对齐的、快速自旋或瞬时光照的系统中，而非通过累积的宇宙学传播产生。稳态背景不应被误认为额外准正模谱线的来源。

Abstract: Black holes ring when perturbed, whereas their response to a stationary gravitational-wave background is a real-frequency scattering problem, not a source of additional quasi-normal-mode lines. We make this standard distinction quantitative by treating a black hole as a frequency-, angle-, and polarization-dependent filter. For an isotropic stationary background around Schwarzschild holes, elastic scattering produces no net monopole signal, so horizon absorption is the only population-level spectral distortion. We calculate this transfer function for Schwarzschild holes in detail, identify its absorptive and phase-delay signatures, and connect the stationary response to the causal ringdown excited by a finite wave packet. We then promote the single-hole result to an angular and polarization transport kernel for a cosmological population. The resulting optical depth is negligible for realistic black-hole populations, including asteroid-mass primordial black holes comprising all dark matter. We then extend the analysis to Kerr holes, for which superradiance allows genuine amplification in selected co-rotating modes, but isotropic incidence and random spin orientations strongly dilute the diffuse signal. Observable effects are therefore more likely in rare, nearby, aligned, rapidly spinning, or transiently illuminated systems than through cumulative cosmological propagation.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文在 Teo 型稳态轴对称假设下，构造了零红移 Morris–Thorne 虫洞的类 Kerr 旋转推广。该解渐近平坦、具有零 ADM 质量，其喉部为准局域定义的零平均曲率面，且为类时而非视界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在将零红移 Morris–Thorne 虫洞推广到旋转情形，并获得一个可解析处理、具有 Kerr 型拖曳特征的精确解。同时澄清 ADM 质量为零时转动参数与角动量的关系问题。

Conclusion: 该解为渐近平坦、零 ADM 质量、零红移旋转虫洞的精确推广，其喉部是准局域类时面而非视界，相关性质独立于 J_ADM=ar₀ 的选择。所支持的应力张量（stress tensor）为有效唯象源，作者不主张微观物质拉氏量或真实物态方程。

Abstract: We construct an exact Kerr-like rotating extension of the zero-redshift Morris--Thorne wormhole in a Teo-type stationary, axisymmetric ansatz. The Einstein equations for an anisotropic source comoving with zero angular momentum observers fix $\mathcal{K}(r)=r^2+a^2$ and reduce frame dragging to a radial quadrature. For $b(r)=r_0^2/r$, this is evaluated using incomplete elliptic integrals and normalised by the ADM angular momentum. The spacetime is asymptotically flat with zero ADM mass, so $a=J_{\rm ADM}/M_{\rm ADM}$ does not apply. The oblateness length $a$ and angular momentum $J_{\rm ADM}$ remain independent, yielding a family $(r_0,a,J_{\rm ADM})$. The choice $J_{\rm ADM}=ar_0$ selects only a one-parameter slice for numerical ergoregion and equatorial-capture illustrations; it is not a field-equation constraint. We find $r_{\rm th}=\sqrt{r_0^2-a^2}$, with reality bound $|a|\leq r_0$ and regular range $0\leq |a|<r_0$. In the canonical stationary foliation, the throat is defined quasi-locally as the unique closed surface $S_0$ in the family $S_\ell$ with zero mean curvature and positive area second variation. Moreover, $r=r_{\rm th}$ and $\ell=0$ are coordinate representations. A signed throat-adapted coordinate reveals two asymptotically flat ends, excludes closed timelike curves, and establishes that the throat is timelike, not a horizon. These conclusions hold independently of $J_{\rm ADM}=ar_0$. The supporting stress tensor, reconstructed from the Einstein tensor, is an effective phenomenological source. We do not claim any microscopic matter Lagrangian or realistic equation of state. We also determine the ergoregion onset. Since the full Hamilton--Jacobi equation is nonseparable, we limit ourselves to derive the optical result for a one-dimensional equatorial capture interval.

</details>


### [86] [Covariant kinetic theory of photons coupled to massive particles in curved spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22837)
*Long Cui,Yi-fan Cai,Xin Hao,Bin Wu*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强引力场中的非平衡辐射过程（non-equilibrium radiative processes）缺乏从第一性原理出发的协变处理框架，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构建光子发射-吸收、康普顿散射（Compton scattering）及有质量粒子间散射的碰撞积分（collision integrals）；通过要求H定理（H-theorem）成立确定吸收与发射跃迁率之间的关系，并将碰撞积分推广至涉及零质量与有质量粒子的任意多组分过程。

Result: 吸收与发射跃迁率之关系在非相对论极限下退化为爱因斯坦关系（Einstein relation）；平衡态下熵产生为零，给出细致平衡条件（detailed balance condition），并由此得到光子气体的协变热力学关系。以Kerr时空吸积盘热辐射为例，分别讨论了全局平衡与局部热力学平衡（local thermodynamic equilibrium）下的Keplerian盘。

Conclusion: 该表述从第一性原理出发处理强引力场中的非平衡辐射过程，并推广至任意多组分（multi-species）过程。

Abstract: This paper develops the covariant Boltzmann equation governing the coupled evolution of photons and massive particles in general curved spacetimes. We construct the collision integrals for photon emission-absorption, Compton scattering, and scattering between massive particles, and show the conservation laws. Requiring the H-theorem to hold determines the relation between the absorption and emission transition rates, which reduces to the Einstein relation in the non-relativistic limit. At equilibrium the entropy production vanishes, yielding the detailed balance condition, from which we obtain the covariant thermodynamic relations of the photon gas. We illustrate these results with the thermal radiation from an accretion disk in Kerr spacetime, first in global equilibrium and then in a Keplerian disk in local thermodynamic equilibrium. We further extend the collision integrals to arbitrary multi-species processes involving both null and massive particles. The formulation treats non-equilibrium radiative processes in strong gravitational fields from first principles.

</details>


### [87] [Charged Klein-Gordon modes on an Ellis wormhole: magnetic confinement and conditional Heun solvability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22860)
*Abdullah Guvendi,Omar Mustafa*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We study charged Klein-Gordon modes on a $(2+1)$-dimensional Ellis traversable wormhole in an external magnetic field defined as uniform in an auxiliary Euclidean embedding space. Pullback of the ambient potential yields a regular azimuthal gauge field whose associated intrinsic magnetic scalar reverses sign across the throat. Separation of the Klein-Gordon equation leads to a radial problem that is unitarily equivalent to a one-dimensional Schrödinger operator with a regular effective potential. For nonzero magnetic coupling, the quadratic asymptotic term produces radial confinement on the complete wormhole. The radial equation can be reduced to the confluent-Heun class. Polynomial truncation is possible only on constrained parameter submanifolds determined by simultaneous termination conditions and therefore does not represent the generic confined spectrum. The horizon structure reveals the two parameters acting in opposite directions: strengthening $K$ draws the inner and outer horizons together until they merge into a single extremal horizon, while a larger $α$ restores the Schwarzschild limit; the same opposing trend extends to the effective potential and the characteristic circular-orbit radii -- the innermost stable circular orbit, the marginally bound orbit, and the marginal circular orbit -- which shift inward with $K$ and outward with $α$, and to the Keplerian and radial epicyclic frequencies of massive test particles. From these frequencies we build three twin-peak quasi-periodic oscillation models -- relativistic precession, warped-disk, and epicyclic-resonance -- along with their $3{:}2$, $4{:}3$, and $5{:}4$ resonance radii, all following the same $K$-$α$ trend. Testing all three models against twin-peak QPO data from two stellar-mass (GRO~J1655$-$40, XTE~J1550$-$564) and two intermediate-mass (M82~X$-$1, NGC~1313~X$-$1) sources through a Markov Chain Monte Carlo analysis places best-fit constraints, at the $2σ$ level, of $K\in(0,0.5)$ and $α\in(0.3,0.6)$ across all sources and models.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者认为将时间问题单纯视作“把量子理论应用于广义相对论”的后果过于狭窄，需采取更广阔的视角。动机在于揭示时间的概念困难在构建量子引力所依据的各理论中已经存在，并将该问题从普朗克尺度（Planck scale）扩展至弱引力区域。

Conclusion: 时间问题不能仅归因于普朗克尺度的引力量化，其在为近平坦几何附近时空提供可预测且具操作意义的量子描述时即已出现；因此需在量子基础（quantum foundations）与相对论量子信息（relativistic quantum information）等议题框架下，从更广泛的理论与概念层面加以审视。

Abstract: The problem of time in quantum gravity is often presented as a consequence of applying quantum theory to general relativity. These notes adopt a broader perspective. We first examine how time enters classical mechanics, relativity, quantum theory, and quantum field theory, distinguishing three principal aspects: causal order, temporal duration, and the present. This analysis shows that the conceptual difficulties associated with time do not arise only when gravity is quantised. Tensions between causal structure, clocks, observables, and measurement are already present in the theories from which quantum gravity is constructed. We then survey responses to the problem of time across the principal approaches to quantum gravity. These include canonical and Wheeler-DeWitt quantisation, path-integral and histories formulations, perturbative and background-dependent approaches, causal sets, and twistor theory. The programmes are compared according to whether they retain an external time, render time problematic through quantisation, or incorporate causal or temporal structure into their foundations. Particular emphasis is placed on questions arising from quantum foundations and relativistic quantum information. We conclude by examining linearised gravity and weak-gravity quantum systems. This regime shows that the problem of time is not confined to Planck-scale physics, but arises already in attempts to provide a predictive and operationally meaningful quantum description of spacetime near flat geometry.

</details>

Method: 在 Einstein-Hilbert 作用量中引入暴胀子 φ 与能动张量迹 T 的非最小耦合，形成 f(φ)T 框架，并在此理论中采用 Woods-Saxon 势进行慢滚（slow-roll）动力学分析。

Conclusion: 该工作在本研究所考察的暴胀观测量范围内，确立了一个可行的暴胀情景，并强调 f(φ)T 耦合是其观测可行性的关键。

Abstract: We investigate an inflationary scenario within a modified gravity theory that extends the Einstein-Hilbert action through a non-minimal coupling between the inflaton $φ$ and the trace of the energy-momentum tensor $T$. This $f(φ)T$ framework is designed to naturally recover the standard general relativity in the post-inflationary universe. Within this theory, we apply the Woods-Saxon potential. The slow-roll dynamics yields a luminal sound speed $c_s^2=1$ for our specific choice of the coupling function. For the model parameters $a \lesssim 1$ and the modest coupling $β=0.1$, we obtain a scalar spectral index $n_s\approx0.959$--$0.966$ and a tensor-to-scalar ratio $r<0.01$, which reveal a good agreement with the latest Planck and BICEP/Keck data on the $(n_s, r)$ constraints. Crucially, we shall demonstrate that the $f(φ)T$ coupling is essential for the observational viability of the model with respect to the CMB two-point statistics. This work establishes a viable inflationary scenario within the scope of the inflationary observables which are considered here.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We explore the quasinormal modes of a massless test scalar field in the gravitational background of a non-extremal dilatonic dyonic black hole. The dyon-like black hole solution is considered within a four-dimensional gravitational model that includes two scalar fields and two 2-forms. It is governed by two dilatonic coupling vectors $\vecλ_s$ (with $s=1,2$) that satisfy $\vecλ_1 = \vecλ_2 = \vecλ$ and $\vecλ^2 = 1/2$. We derive and examine the quasinormal modes of a massless scalar test field in the eikonal limit. These modes are governed by the solution parameters $μ> 0$, $P_1 > 0$, and $P_2 > 0$. In the two limiting scenarios -- namely $P_1 = P_2 = 0$ and $P_1 = P_2 = P > 0$ -- our results reduce exactly to the known (eikonal) quasinormal spectra for the Schwarzschild and Reissner-Nordström black holes, respectively. Abstract: We investigate the equilibrium structure of electrically charged quark stars within the framework of energy-momentum-squared gravity (EMSG), adopting a unified interacting quark-matter equation of state that incorporates perturbative QCD corrections, color superconductivity, and finite-strange-quark-mass effects. By solving the modified Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff equations over a broad range of EMSG coupling strengths and interaction parameters, we show that the inclusion of electric charge substantially increases both the maximum mass and radius compared with neutral configurations. For strong negative couplings and pronounced interaction strengths, the resulting stellar models reach masses close to $3,M_{\odot}$ with compactness ratios of order $0.34$, placing them within the mass range inferred for the secondary component of GW190814, whose nature remains observationally undetermined. The presence of charge reduces the central density at maximum mass by approximately $10$--$15\%$ while maintaining full compliance with causality requirements. Furthermore, comparisons with observational constraints from PSR J1614$-$2230, J0348$+$0432, J0740$+$6620, J0952$-$0607, and recent NICER radius measurements indicate that charged EMSG configurations remain consistent with current astrophysical bounds over a wide parameter space. These results demonstrate that a moderate electric charge, when combined with nonlinear matter-geometry coupling, can support quark stars that are significantly more massive than those predicted in general relativity, underscoring the potential observational relevance of charged compact objects in modified gravity and multimessenger astrophysics.

</details>


### [93] [Null Geodesics in the Reissner-Nordström Black Hole Spacetime Pierced by a Cosmic String](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23025)

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 通过分析零测地线运动方程对其进行分类，并获得三类情形各自的解析解（analytical solutions）；在此基础上数值模拟光轨迹，并针对赤道面（equatorial plane）内的光线推导偏折角（deflection angle）表达式。

Result: 零测地线可归为三大类，分类完全由 |Q|/M 决定。数值模拟显示宇宙弦参数 α 在三类情形中均增强零测地线的缠绕（winding）行为；偏折角结果显示电荷 Q 使偏折角减小，而宇宙弦参数 α 使其增大。

Conclusion: 在被宇宙弦穿透的 Reissner-Nordström 黑洞时空中，零测地线的类别由 |Q|/M 唯一确定，且 α 与 Q 对光线缠绕与偏折表现出方向相反的效应：α 增强缠绕与偏折，Q 减弱偏折。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate null geodesics in the spacetime of a Reissner-Nordström black hole pierced by a cosmic string. Analysis of the equations of motion reveals that null geodesics in this conical spacetime can be classified into three main categories, with the classification entirely determined by the ratio $|Q|/M$ (where $Q$ and $M$ denote the black hole charge and mass, respectively). Analytical solutions to the null geodesic equations are obtained for each category. Based on these solutions, we numerically simulate light trajectories and analyze in detail the effects of the black hole charge $Q$ and the cosmic string parameter $α$. It is found that the cosmic string parameter $α$ enhances the winding behavior of null geodesics across all three main categories. Finally, we consider light deflection in the equatorial plane and derive an expression for the deflection angle, revealing that the charge $Q$ decreases the deflection angle, whereas the cosmic string parameter $α$ increases it.

</details>


### [94] [LQG-inspired black hole solutions in (2+1)-dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23044)
*Zijian Shi,Xiangdong Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文首次构建了三种独立的自旋网络量子引力（loop quantum gravity, LQG）启发式框架，用于（2+1）维黑洞，并发现三者给出相同的定性图像：加入量子修正后，原本在经典情形下仅存在于反德西特（anti-de Sitter, AdS）情形的黑洞，可出现渐近平坦、AdS 与德西特（de Sitter, dS）三类解。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典（2+1）维广义相对论中黑洞解仅存在于反德西特（AdS）情形（即 BTZ 黑洞），这一限制构成经典（2+1）维与（3+1）维解之间的不对称。该文旨在通过引入有效量子引力效应考察这种不对称是否可被消除。

Method: 构建三种相互独立的 LQG 启发式框架：（1）$\bar\mu$-scheme holonomy corrections（$\bar\mu$ 格式的完整移动修正）；（2）量子 Oppenheimer-Snyder 塌缩（quantum Oppenheimer-Snyder collapse）；（3）协变有效模型（covariant effective model）。三者在纳入 LQG 启发式修正后分别求解（2+1）维黑洞解。

Result: 三种方法均给出相同的定性图像：在包含 LQG 启发式修正时，出现三类不同的解，分别对应渐近平坦（asymptotically flat）、反德西特（AdS）与德西特（dS）几何。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在纳入有效量子引力效应后，经典（2+1）维与（3+1）维解之间的不对称被消除。

Abstract: It is well known that in (2+1)-dimensional general relativity, black hole solutions exist only in the anti-de Sitter case--the so-called Banados-Teitelboim-Zanelli (BTZ) black hole. In this letter, we construct, for the first time, three independent LQG-inspired frameworks for (2+1)-dimensional black holes: the $\barμ$-scheme holonomy corrections, the quantum Oppenheimer-Snyder collapse, and a covariant effective model. Remarkably, all three approaches yield the same qualitative picture: when LQG-inspired corrections are included, we find three distinct types of solutions corresponding to asymptotically flat, anti-de Sitter, and de Sitter geometries. Our results demonstrate that the asymmetry between classical (2+1)-dimensional and (3+1)-dimensional solutions is eliminated upon incorporating effective quantum gravity effects.

</details>


### [95] [Charged particles and QPO constraints in tidal charge black hole magnetosphere sourced by a current loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23187)
*Ozodbek Abdurakhmonov,Javlon Rayimbaev,Inomjon Ibragimov,Sherzod Djumanov,Nuraliev Farhod,lkhomjon Makhmudov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在Randall–Sundrum膜世界（braneworld）模型中，研究潮汐荷黑洞（tidal charged black hole）赤道面电流环产生的偶极电磁场（dipolar electromagnetic field）对带电粒子动力学的影响，并用双峰准周期振荡（twin-peak QPO）观测数据约束膜世界参数 $(b,\omega)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潮汐荷参数 $b$ 与磁耦合参数 $\omega$ 是膜世界模型的自由度，但缺乏天文观测层面的约束；同时静止非旋转背景下是否存在非零节点进动尚待探究。

Method: 将电流环置于最内稳定圆轨道（ISCO），求解弯曲背景下的Maxwell方程得到方位角电磁四势的精确解析解；由Hamilton–Jacobi方程导出环内、环外区域带电粒子的有效势与轨道参数（ISCO半径、角动量、能量、轨道频率），计算三个基本频率，构建五个双峰QPO模型（RP、WD、ER2–ER4），并对六个黑洞源的观测频率做MCMC拟合。

Result: 偶极环磁场使静态非旋转背景下重新出现非零节点进动频率；以ER4为代表性模型，拟合得到中等且不可忽略的潮汐荷 $b\simeq0.3$–$1.0$，而磁耦合与零一致（$|\omega|\lesssim0.05$）。

Conclusion: 基于天体物理QPO数据给出了潮汐荷与磁耦合强度的上限约束，表明膜世界效应可能以中等强度存在于所研究的黑洞源中。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamics of charged particles in the dipolar electromagnetic field generated by a stationary current loop around a static black hole in the Randall--Sundrum braneworld model. The current loop is located at the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO) of the tidal charged black hole, and the azimuthal electromagnetic four-potential is obtained as an exact analytical solution of the Maxwell equations in the curved background. Starting from the Hamilton--Jacobi equation, we derive the effective potential for charged particle motion in both the interior and exterior regions of the current loop, and analyze the dependence of the ISCO radius, angular momentum, energy, and orbital frequency on the tidal charge parameter $b$ and the magnetic coupling parameter $ω$. We further compute three fundamental frequencies and show that the dipolar loop field revives a nonzero nodal precession frequency even though the background is static and non-rotating. Using these frequencies we construct five twin-peak QPO models (RP, WD, and ER2--ER4) and perform a Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) analysis to constrain the braneworld parameters $(b,ω)$ from the observed twin-peak QPO frequencies of six black hole sources spanning three mass regimes: GRO~J1655--40, XTE~J1550--564, GRS~1915$+$105, H~1743$+$322, M82~X-1, and Sgr~A$^{*}$. Adopting the epicyclic-resonance ER4 model as the representative framework, we find moderate, non-negligible tidal charges $b\simeq0.3$--$1.0$, while the magnetic coupling remains consistent with zero, $|ω|\lesssim0.05$. Our results therefore place upper bounds on the tidal charge and on the magnetic coupling strength from astrophysical QPO data.

</details>


### [96] [Renormalized Dynamical Friction in Black-Hole Spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23192)
*Jordan Wilson-Gerow*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了致密天体穿越黑洞时空中物质时所受引力自力（gravitational self-force）的重整化局域运动方程，通过在Schwinger–Keldysh闭合时间路径上积掉环境与度规扰动，得到由曲时空费曼图组成的因果有效作用量，并给出可用于数值自力计算的完整重整化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 致密天体在介质中运动时会激发环境尾迹（wake），其引力反作用（backreaction）影响世界线运动，但相关计算存在紫外发散，需要在自力框架内建立一致的重整化处理，以便数值自力计算可用。

Method: 在Schwinger–Keldysh围道（Schwinger–Keldysh contour）上积掉环境与度规扰动，获得因果有效作用量，并以质量比与环境密度的幂次组织为曲时空费曼图；采用局域大动量展开（large-momentum expansion）分离发散，并将奇异性翻译为Barack–Ori模求和（mode-sum）方案，导出理想流体环境的解析穿刺（puncture）与正则化参数；对无碰撞尘埃（collisionless dust），通过与黑洞穿越介质的精确近区（near-zone）计算匹配来确定有限Wilson系数。

Result: 领头阶图中环境尾迹对世界线产生引力反作用，该图含对数紫外发散（logarithmic ultraviolet divergence），重整化了领头耗散流体–粒子耦合；模求和减除固定了发散部分，但不足以确定有限Wilson系数，该系数由无碰撞尘埃情形下的精确近区计算匹配得到。

Conclusion: 所提方法提供了一套适用于数值自力计算的完整重整化方案（renormalization prescription），将环境介质的引力反作用纳入致密天体在黑洞时空中运动的局域运动方程。

Abstract: We derive the renormalized local equation of motion for the gravitational self-force on a compact object moving through matter in a black-hole spacetime. We integrate out the environment and metric perturbations on a Schwinger--Keldysh contour, obtaining a causal effective action given by a sum of curved-spacetime Feynman diagrams organized by powers of the mass ratio and environmental density. At leading order, an environmental wake gravitationally backreacts on the worldline. The corresponding diagram contains a logarithmic ultraviolet divergence that renormalizes the leading dissipative fluid--particle coupling. We isolate the divergence with a local large-momentum expansion, in which the curved-spacetime retarded Green's function reduces to its flat tangent-space form. We translate the resulting singularity into a Barack--Ori mode-sum scheme, deriving an analytic puncture and regularization parameters for an ideal-fluid environment. Mode subtraction fixes the divergent part but not the finite Wilson coefficient. For collisionless dust, we determine this coefficient by matching to an exact near-zone calculation for a black hole moving through the medium. This gives a complete renormalization prescription suitable for numerical self-force calculations.

</details>


### [97] [Instability of naked singularities of perfect fluid under $C^{1,α}$ gravitational perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23357)
*Junbin Li,Tingting Li,Xi-Ping Zhu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该研究探讨 Einstein–Euler 系统球对称自相似坍缩中裸奇点（naked singularity）在引力扰动下的不稳定性，证明初始共形度规的无对称性小扰动会导致捕获面（trapped surface）形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关注的是球对称自相似坍缩中出现的裸奇点是否稳定这一问题；裸奇点的存在涉及宇宙监督假设（cosmic censorship）的讨论，而此前分析多依赖对称性假设。

Method: 对 Einstein–Euler 系统球对称自相似坍缩的初始共形度规（conformal metric）施加小 $C^{1,\alpha}$ 扰动，且不预设任何对称性，进而分析引力扰动下裸奇点的演化行为。

Result: 小 $C^{1,\alpha}$ 扰动导致捕获面形成。关键机制在于声速（speed of sound）严格小于光速，使引力辐射（gravitational radiation）能够充分聚集，在流体发展出任何奇点之前即形成捕获面。

Conclusion: 在无对称性的一般扰动下，该球对称自相似坍缩中的裸奇点是不稳定的，倾向形成捕获面而非直接暴露奇点；这一结论为相关宇宙监督问题的讨论提供了依据。

Abstract: We study the instability of the naked singularities arising in the spherically symmetric self-similar collapsing of the Einstein--Euler system under gravitational perturbations. We show that small $C^{1,α}$ perturbations (without any symmetries) of the initial conformal metric lead to trapped surface formation. One key point is that the speed of sound is strictly slower the the speed of light, so the gravitational radiation can become sufficiently concentrated to form a trapped surface before the fluid develops any singularity.

</details>


### [98] [Optimal all-angle reconstruction of the Hellings-Downs curve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23400)
*Jing-Hong Han,Zhi-Chao Zhao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文提出对脉冲星计时阵列（PTA）角相关曲线进行全角度联合重建的方法，利用完整脉冲星对协方差在每个角分箱保留自由幅度，构造最佳线性无偏估计量（BLUE），在 NANOGrav 15 yr 数据上将分箱标准差最多降低 12.9%，对未来 SKAO 类阵列预测可降低达 36.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量 Hellings–Downs（HD）角相关模式是检验纳赫兹引力波信号起源的关键手段，但传统逐分箱（bin-by-bin）重建在各角分箱内独立优化权重，未充分利用脉冲星对之间的整体协方差信息。因此需要在保持各分箱自由幅度的前提下，改进估计以提升测量精度。

Method: 方法为全角度联合重建：使用完整的脉冲星对协方差（full pulsar-pair covariance），并在每个角分箱中保留一个自由幅度，从而得到 all-angle 最佳线性无偏估计量（best linear unbiased estimator, BLUE）。该估计量最小化每个分箱取值及任意分箱线性组合的方差。随后将该方法应用于公开的 NANOGrav 15 yr 数据产品，并对未来 SKAO 类 PTA 进行预测。

Result: 在 NANOGrav 15 yr 公开数据上，该方法使分箱标准差（bin standard deviations）最多降低 12.9%，中位降低 9.5%。对于未来 Square Kilometre Array Observatory（SKAO）类 PTA，预测降低幅度可达 36.4%，中位为 29.6%。

Conclusion: 全角度联合重建可在不引入额外模型假设的情况下提升 HD 曲线各分箱的测量精度，并随 PTA 灵敏度提升（如 SKAO 类阵列）带来更大收益，从而实现对引力波背景（gravitational-wave background）更精确的测量。

Abstract: Pulsar timing arrays (PTAs) detect nanohertz gravitational waves through spatial correlations between the timing residuals of different pulsars. For an isotropic, unpolarized stochastic background in general relativity, the ensemble-mean correlation follows the Hellings--Downs (HD) curve; measuring this angular pattern tests the gravitational-wave origin of the signal. Standard bin-by-bin reconstructions optimize the weights within each angular bin separately. We reconstruct the curve jointly using the full pulsar-pair covariance, retaining a free amplitude in every bin. The resulting all-angle best linear unbiased estimator minimizes the variance of every bin value and every linear combination of bins. Applied to the public NANOGrav 15 yr data products, our method reduces the bin standard deviations by up to 12.9\%, with a median reduction of 9.5\%. For a future Square Kilometre Array Observatory (SKAO)-like PTA, the predicted reduction reaches 36.4\%, with a median of 29.6\%, enabling significantly more precise measurements of the gravitational-wave background.

</details>


### [99] [Grey-body factor from correspondence with quasinormal mode for gravitational perturbation of higher-dimensional Reissner-Nordström black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23434)
*Hyewon Han,Bogeun Gwak*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究高维 Reissner-Nordström 黑洞的准正规模（quasinormal modes, QNMs）与灰体因子（grey-body factors, GBFs）之间的对应关系，系统计算标量、矢量、张量扰动（含标量/矢量的 (+) 与 (-) 类型）下的 QNMs 与 GBFs，并检验该对应关系的准确性与适用范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QNMs 与 GBFs 之间存在对应关系，但其在高维带电黑洞及不同扰动类型下的有效性尚需系统检验，尤其是势函数出现双峰结构等偏离 WKB 假设的情形。

Method: 采用连分数法（continued fraction method）计算 QNMs；依据各扰动类型与黑洞参数的 Frobenius 级数奇点结构，必要时使用中点积分法（integration-through-midpoints method）计算 GBFs，并将对应关系给出的 GBFs 与数值积分结果进行比对。

Result: 对应关系对 scalar(+)、vector(+)、vector(-) 与 tensor 扰动给出良好近似；scalar(-) 类型在 D > 6 的低至中等电荷下出现显著偏差，但在高电荷下恢复合理精度。与其他类型随 D 增大而变差不同，scalar(-) 在 D=8 时比 D=7 更精确；vector(-) 扰动在近极端黑洞中表现良好。

Conclusion: 即使有效势出现与 WKB 推导假设不符的双峰结构，该对应关系仍可适用，表明其适用范围比 WKB 近似假设所预期的更广。

Abstract: We investigate the correspondence between quasinormal modes (QNMs) and grey-body factors (GBFs) of higher-dimensional Reissner-Nordström black holes. Considering gravitational perturbations of these black holes, we systematically compute the QNMs and GBFs for scalar, vector, and tensor perturbations, including the $(+)$ and $(-)$ types of scalar and vector perturbations, to examine the validity of the correspondence. The QNMs are computed using the continued fraction method, and the integration-through-midpoints method is applied when required by the singularity structure of the Frobenius series for each perturbation type and set of black-hole parameters. We evaluate the accuracy of the correspondence for each spacetime dimension $D$ and black-hole charge by comparing the GBFs obtained from the correspondence with those obtained by numerical integration. The correspondence provides a good approximation to the GBFs for the scalar($+$), vector($+$), vector($-$), and tensor perturbations. However, for the scalar($-$) type, the correspondence exhibits pronounced deviations at low to intermediate charges in $D > 6$. At higher charges, it recovers reasonable accuracy despite the effective potential exhibiting a double-peak structure inconsistent with the assumptions underlying the Wentzel-Kramers-Brillouin (WKB)-based derivation of the correspondence. In contrast to the other types, for which the correspondence deteriorates as $D$ increases, the scalar($-$) type is more accurate in $D=8$ than in $D=7$. Furthermore, the correspondence performs well for the vector($-$) perturbation of near-extreme black holes, even though the potential develops a double-peak structure in $D>6$. These results indicate that the correspondence is applicable over a broader range than expected from the assumptions of the WKB approximation.

</details>


### [100] [ACT-compatible Inflation in Scalar Field Coupled $f\left(Q, \tilde R\right)$ Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23511)
*Shin'ichi Nojiri,S. D. Odintsov,V. K. Oikonomou*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在 \(f(Q,\tilde R)\) 引力框架下，构建了与 ACT 对暴胀（inflation）的观测约束相符的模型，并通过重建技术给出两种满足约束的哈勃率（Hubble rate）\(H\) 形式，同时讨论暴胀后的再加热（reheating）阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于将暴胀模型与 ACT（Atacama Cosmology Telescope）给出的观测约束保持一致，并在 \(f(Q,\tilde R)\) 引力及耦合标量场的 \(f(R)\) 引力框架中实现可行的暴胀行为。

Method: 采用与 \(f(Q,\tilde R)\) 理论相容的重建技术（reconstruction techniques），提出两种满足约束的哈勃率 \(H\) 函数形式，并在 \(f(Q,\tilde R)\) 引力与耦合标量场的 \(f(R)\) 引力中构造实现这些 \(H\) 行为的模型。文中指出 \(f(Q,\tilde R)\) 等价于 \(f(Q,B)\) 或 \(f(Q,C)\) 引力，其中 \(B=Q-\tilde R\)、\(C=\tilde R-Q\)。

Result: 构造出两类满足 ACT 约束的哈勃率 \(H\) 函数行为，并在 \(f(Q,\tilde R)\) 引力及耦合标量场的 \(f(R)\) 引力中实现了相应模型；同时讨论了暴胀后的再加热阶段。

Conclusion: 耦合标量场的 \(f(R)\) 引力情景较为成熟，可改写为爱因斯坦引力耦合两个标量场的形式；而 \(f(Q,\tilde R)\) 引力情形因 \(f(Q)\) 引力的动力学自由度（dynamical degrees of freedom）数目尚未确定，存在非平凡困难。

Abstract: In this work, we construct models compatible with ACT constraints on inflation in the framework of $f\left(Q, \tilde R\right)$ gravity, with $f(R)$ gravity coupled to a scalar field. The $f\left(Q, \tilde R\right)$ theory is equivalent to $f(Q, B)$ gravity models or $f(Q, C)$ gravity where $B$ or $C$ is the difference between $Q$ and the scalar curvature $\tilde R$ in Einstein's gravity, so, $B=Q-\tilde R$ or $C=\tilde R - Q$. Using appropriate reconstruction techniques compatible with $f\left(Q, \tilde R\right)$ theory, we propose two functional behaviors of the Hubble rate $H$ that satisfy the constraints, and we construct models that realize these behaviors of the Hubble rate $H$ using $f\left(Q, \tilde R\right)$ gravity and $f(R)$ gravity coupled with a scalar field. We also discuss the reheating stage after inflation. Although the scenario of $f(R)$ gravity coupled with a scalar field is well-known, since the model can be rewritten in the form of Einstein's gravity coupled with two scalar fields, the non-trivial problem occurs in the $f\left(Q, \tilde R\right)$ gravity case, because the number of dynamical degrees of freedom in $f(Q)$ gravity is not settled.

</details>


### [101] [Bi-scalar-tensor theory: a spatially covariant gravity approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23704)
*Jia-Jun Chen,Xian Gao,Tsutomu Kobayashi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一种构造无鬼（ghost-free）多场高阶导数标量-张量理论的新方法：从空间协变引力（spatially covariant gravity）出发，耦合额外标量场，通过哈密顿约束分析（Hamiltonian constraint analysis）导出消除多余自由度的简并条件与一致性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶导数标量-张量理论通常引入不期望的鬼场（ghost）自由度，需寻找系统性方法以在保持高阶导数的同时消除这类多余模式。本文旨在为双场与多场情形提供一种系统化的构造途径。

Method: 从空间协变引力出发耦合一个额外标量场，其结果可理解为一个标量在幺正规范（unitary gauge）下的双标量-张量理论。在一类较广的框架中——度规速度通过外曲率（extrinsic curvature）进入、额外标量以最高二阶时间导数进入——实施哈密顿约束分析，并推导简并条件（degeneracy condition）与额外一致性条件（consistency condition）；同时研究双场非共形变换（two-field disformal transformation）及其可逆性条件。

Result: 一般情况下该理论传播五个物理自由度（degrees of freedom, DOFs），即两个张量模式与三个标量模式，其中包含一个与额外标量相关的非期望模式。当简并条件与一致性条件同时满足时，该非期望模式被消除，理论仅传播四个自由度。所得理论依三级约束（tertiary constraint）的保持是否闭合、或是否产生四级约束（quaternary constraint）而分为两类。此外给出了双场非共形变换的可逆性条件及其在幺正规范下的表达。

Conclusion: 该构造为建立具有高阶导数的双标量-张量与多标量-张量理论提供了系统化方法，并通过约束分析明确了消除多余自由度的条件与理论分类。

Abstract: We propose a novel method for constructing ghost-free multi-field higher-derivative scalar-tensor theories. The idea is to start from spatially covariant gravity and couple it to an additional scalar field. The resulting spatially covariant scalar field theory can be understood as a bi-scalar-tensor theory in the unitary gauge of one scalar. Within a broad class in which metric velocities enter through the extrinsic curvature and the additional scalar enters through temporal derivatives up to second order, we perform a Hamiltonian constraint analysis. We show that the theory generally propagates five physical degrees of freedom (DOFs), i.e., two tensor modes and three scalar modes. One of the scalar modes is the unwanted mode associated with the additional scalar. We then derive a degeneracy condition and an additional consistency condition. When both conditions hold, the unwanted mode is eliminated and the theory propagates four degrees of freedom. The resulting theories fall into two cases, depending on whether the preservation of the tertiary constraint closes without a new constraint or generates a quaternary constraint. We also study the two-field disformal transformation, derive its invertibility condition, and express it in the unitary gauge. Our construction provides a systematic approach to constructing bi- and multi-scalar-tensor theories with higher derivatives.

</details>


### [102] [Black holes in depleted Dehnen dark matter halos with physical inner edges and strong-field observables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23750)
*Hassan Hassanabadi,Soroush Zare*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了一种由"耗尽型"Dehnen暗物质晕（dark matter halo）包围的静态球对称黑洞（black hole, BH）时空，描述最终平衡态而非动力学吸积过程，并导出了度规函数、红移因子、光子球与阴影等强场可观测量的解析结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有黑洞—暗物质晕解析几何多针对完整晕分布，缺乏对晕内部被黑洞吸收后形成"真空间隙"这一平衡构型的系统刻画。本文旨在给出此类耗尽晕包围黑洞的一般性构造，并统一已研究的若干解析解。

Method: 采用解析方法构造静态球对称度规：将预设内边缘以内的初始晕质量并入中心黑洞，使修饰视界（dressed horizon）与残余晕之间留出真空间隙；由此推导度规函数与红移相的一般表达式，并对Dehnen尖点指数（cusp index）γ=0,1,2,5/2给出显式解，进而分析能量条件、光子球、阴影及允许参数空间。

Result: 视界半径依赖尖点指数γ与被吸收晕质量，去除晕时精确还原Schwarzschild结果。主能量条件（dominant energy condition）给出普适局域下界κ≥5/4；全局可容许性还取决于尖点指数与晕致密度。若在晕边缘施加稳定圆轨道运动并要求字面意义上的稳定爱因斯坦团簇（Einstein-cluster）解释，则需更强条件κ≥3。

Conclusion: 该构造表明晕尖点与耗尽尺度显著影响强场观测特征，并将此前研究的解析黑洞—晕几何及Schwarzschild、弱晕极限作为特例纳入统一框架。

Abstract: We construct a static and spherically symmetric black hole (BH) spacetime surrounded by a depleted Dehnen dark matter (DM) halo. The model describes a final equilibrium configuration rather than dynamical accretion process: the initial halo mass inside a prescribed inner edge is incorporated into the central BH, leaving a vacuum gap between the dressed horizon and the surviving halo. Consequently, the horizon radius depends on the Dehnen cusp index $γ$ and the absorbed halo mass, while the Schwarzschild result is recovered exactly when the halo is removed. We derive general expressions for the metric functions and redshift phase and obtain explicit solutions for $γ=0,1,2,$ and $5/2$. The dominant energy condition imposes the universal local bound $κ\geq5/4$, whereas global admissibility depends additionally on the cusp index and halo compactness. A literal stable Einstein-cluster interpretation requires the stronger condition $κ\geq3$ when stable circular motion is imposed at the halo edge. We also analyze the photon sphere, shadow, and admissible parameter space, demonstrating how the halo cusp and depletion scale affect strong-field observables. Previously studied analytic BH--halo geometries and the Schwarzschild and weak-halo limits follow as special cases of the general construction.

</details>


### [103] [Equivalence of the wave function of the universe in the Einstein and Jordan frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23785)
*Vikramaditya Mondal,Sumanta Chakraborty,Soumitra SenGupta*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在微超空间量子宇宙学框架下考察爱因斯坦框架（Einstein frame）与约当框架（Jordan frame）的等价性，指出该等价性依赖于量子哈密顿量的算符排序方案；在点正则变换协变的情形下两框架量子等价，但引入流体钟后除辐射流体（w=1/3）外等价性被破坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 爱因斯坦框架与约当框架在经典层面通常被认为等价，但在量子层面这一等价性是否成立尚存疑问。作者希望在微超空间量子宇宙学中，对一般标量-张量（scalar-tensor）与 f(R) 引力理论，不依赖具体模型地澄清两框架的量子等价条件。

Method: 采用均匀各向同性宇宙的 Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker 度规所对应的微超空间（minisuperspace），并在正则（canonical）与路径积分（path integral）两种形式下作形式化论证，且不预设任何特定模型。

Result: 两框架的等价性取决于量子哈密顿量的算符排序（operator ordering）方案：若量子理论在微超空间的点正则变换（point canonical transformations）下协变，则等价性成立。对包括微超空间 Laplace-Beltrami 算符在内的一大类算符排序，两框架所得宇宙波函数由连接两框架的同一变换相联系，即量子力学意义上等价。此外，引入具有任意状态方程参数 w 的流体钟（fluid clock）会破坏等价性，但辐射流体（w=1/3）除外。

Conclusion: 在一般标量-张量与 f(R) 引力理论中，爱因斯坦框架与约当框架的量子等价性并非普遍成立，而是由算符排序方案决定；只有在点正则变换协变的排序下，以及无流体钟或流体钟为辐射时，量子等价才得以保持。

Abstract: We investigate the question of equivalence between the Einstein and Jordan frames for generic scalar-tensor and $f(R)$ gravity theories in the context of minisuperspace quantum cosmology. We consider, particularly, the minisuperspace of a homogeneous and isotropic universe with the Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker metric. We show that the equivalence between the frames depends on the operator ordering scheme of the quantum Hamiltonian: if the quantum theory is covariant under point canonical transformations in the minisuperspace, then the equivalence between the two frames holds. We find that for a large class of operator orderings, including the Laplace-Beltrami operator in the minisuperspace, the wave functions of the universe obtained in the two frames are related by the same transformations that connect the two frames, \textit{i.e.}, the frames are quantum mechanically equivalent. Further, we show that adding a fluid clock with an arbitrary equation of state parameter $w$ to the system breaks the equivalence, except for the case of a radiation fluid ($w=1/3$). All our arguments are presented at a formal level in both canonical and path integral approaches, and for any scalar-tensor, $f(R)$ theories without assuming any particular model.

</details>


### [104] [Signatures in the local interstellar medium of electromagnetic radiation produced by neutron star gravitational waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23865)
*Preston Jones,Pragati Pradhan,Douglas Singleton*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 旅行者号（Voyager）探测器持续40余年探测到数kHz的等离子体振荡（plasma oscillations），其天体物理来源至今仍无公认假说，即使进入局地星际介质（local interstellar medium）后该现象依然存在。该文探讨近期提出的“引力波背景中电磁辐射协同产生（co-production）”理论作为这些低频振荡的可能来源，并检验中子星（neutron star）是否为其天体物理来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旅行者号探测到的数kHz等离子体振荡已持续40余年，学界始终缺乏被广泛接受的来源假说；探测器进入局地星际介质后探测依然持续，进一步增加了溯源难度。中子星虽为最早被假设的候选源之一，但此前没有理论机制能解释其振荡频率，因此需要引入新的理论框架来重新审视这一问题。

Method: 该研究采用近期提出的引力波背景中电磁辐射协同产生理论，将其作为低频振荡的可能来源加以考察，并在此基础上评估中子星能否构成对应的天体物理来源。分析属于理论探讨与候选源检验性质，而非基于摘要所示的新观测数据。

Result: 摘要未给出具体数值结果或验证结论，仅说明研究探索了协同产生理论作为低频振荡来源的可能性，并考察中子星是否可充当其天体物理来源。所涉机制与频率的对应关系为核心待检验问题，因以往理论无法解释观测到的振荡频率。

Conclusion: 该工作将引力波背景下电磁辐射协同产生理论引入旅行者号等离子体振荡来源问题的讨论，并重新评估中子星作为候选源的可能性。其意义在于为长期未解的来源之谜提供新的理论切入角度，但摘要未给出明确的证实或证伪结论。

Abstract: The Voyager spacecraft have been detecting plasma oscillations of a few kHz for over 40 years. However, there is still no widely accepted hypothesis for the astrophysical source of the oscillations. Having entered the local interstellar medium, these detections have persisted, adding to the challenge of identifying the astrophysical source. Neutron stars were one of the earliest hypothesized candidates of the electromagnetic radiation responsible for the detected plasma oscillations, but no theoretical mechanism could be identified to explain the frequency of the oscillations. We explore the recently developed theory of co-production of electromagnetic radiation in gravitational wave backgrounds as a possible source of these low-frequency oscillations and investigate whether neutron stars could be the astrophysical source.

</details>


### [105] [Exact horizon response and charge ensembles of Majumdar--Papapetrou black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23921)
*Jongheon Baek*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文严格确定任意四维 Majumdar--Papapetrou 时空的静电 Dirichlet-to-Neumann 映射，证明分离极端视界的势可独立指定，通量电荷由精确电容矩阵关联；该矩阵可分解为对角项与完全图加权拉普拉斯量，并给出共模/差模能量分解与固定电荷格林函数，最后推广至 D=n+3 维。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多中心极端黑洞时空的视界间静电响应、通量分配与自能修正缺乏统一的精确表述，已有文献（如 Frolov 与 Zelnikov 的多中心格林函数）所用对角补全并未保持各视界电荷。该文旨在给出无需额外紫外减除的精确响应矩阵与固定电荷格林函数，并澄清电荷转移问题。

Method: 对最小耦合旁观 Maxwell 场（spectator Maxwell field）求解四维 Majumdar--Papapetrou 时空的 Dirichlet-to-Neumann 映射，构造接地 Dirichlet 格林函数（Dirichlet Green function），并与 Frolov--Zelnikov 多中心格林函数作直接对照，比较其对角补全与保持各视界电荷的对称格林函数。进一步将响应矩阵、通量分配与固定电荷格林函数推广到 D=n+3 维。

Result: 电容矩阵元为 C_{AA}=M_A+Σ_{B≠A}M_AM_B/R_{AB}，C_{AB}=-M_AM_B/R_{AB}，即正对角项加视界完全图的加权拉普拉斯量，给出精确的共模与差模场能分解。接地格林函数表明点电荷按初等调和测度（harmonic measure）在无穷远与各视界间分配通量。Frolov--Zelnikov 式(68)的对角补全虽保持总视界电荷，但在视界间转移电荷；保持各视界电荷的对称格林函数则含完整电容矩阵之逆。对双中心情形，二者之差给出有限、负半定的静电自能移动及相应精确自作用力（self-force）差，无需额外紫外减除。D=n+3 维下推广结果含普适因子 π^{n/2}/Γ(n/2)。

Conclusion: 静电 Dirichlet-to-Neumann 映射为多中心极端黑洞时空提供了精确且结构清晰的电容矩阵表述，统一解释了通量分配、能量共模/差模分解与自作用力修正；区分了保持总电荷与保持各视界电荷两类格林函数，并在任意 D=n+3 维给出带普适系数的推广。

Abstract: We determine the electrostatic Dirichlet-to-Neumann map of an arbitrary four-dimensional Majumdar--Papapetrou spacetime. For a minimally coupled spectator Maxwell field, the potentials of the disconnected extremal horizons may be prescribed independently, and the resulting flux charges are related by an exact capacitance matrix. If the centers have masses $M_A$ and coordinate separations $R_{AB}$, its entries are $C_{AA}=M_A+\sum_{B\ne A}M_AM_B/R_{AB}$ and $C_{AB}=-M_AM_B/R_{AB}$. The matrix is a positive diagonal term plus the weighted Laplacian of the complete graph of horizons, giving an exact common-mode and differential-mode decomposition of the field energy. The grounded Dirichlet Green function shows that a point charge partitions its flux among infinity and the horizons according to elementary harmonic measures. A direct translation of the multicenter Green function of Frolov and Zelnikov gives the diagonal completion used in their published Eq.~(68), whose component-resolved Gauss flux preserves the total horizon charge but generally transfers charge between horizons. The symmetric Green function that preserves every horizon charge instead contains the inverse of the full capacitance matrix. For a binary, the difference gives a finite, negative-semidefinite shift of the electrostatic self-energy and a corresponding exact self-force difference without an additional ultraviolet subtraction. We finally extend the response matrix, flux partition, and fixed-charge Green function to $D=n+3$ dimensions, where the graph weights acquire the universal factor $π^{n/2}/Γ(n/2)$.

</details>


### [106] [Soliton solution of a gravitating chiral-quark soliton model: dynamical fermion and Dirac-sea in general relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24159)
*Ryoma Mizutani,Daiki Niiyama,Nobuyuki Sawado*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在静态球对称时空（static spherically symmetric spacetime）中研究由狄拉克费米子（Dirac fermion）与非线性和手征场（chiral field）耦合构成的 Einstein-Dirac 系统，将手征夸克孤子模型（chiral-quark soliton model, CQSM）推广至弯曲时空并与爱因斯坦引力耦合，自洽求解后得到费米子能谱与 ADM 质量的谱流（spectral flow），发现狄拉克海（Dirac-sea）效应可导致负 ADM 质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于考察引力对费米子动力学质量（dynamical mass）的影响，并将原本用于描述大 N_c QCD 低能区强子物理的 CQSM 拓展至广义相对论与弯曲时空背景。该工作试图把包含完整狄拉克海贡献的局域化费米子图像与爱因斯坦引力结合，以探索引力与量子真空效应（狄拉克海）的相互作用。

Method: 方法上，将基于大 N_c QCD 低能区启发、由正则化单费米子圈（regularized one-fermion loop）导出的 CQSM，推广到球对称弯曲时空并与爱因斯坦引力耦合。随后自洽求解 CQSM 与爱因斯坦方程（Einstein equation），并计算费米子能量与本征质量的谱流。

Result: 结果上，成功实现 CQSM 与爱因斯坦方程的自洽求解，获得费米子能级以及 ADM 质量（ADM mass）的谱流。狄拉克海会稀释能动张量（energy-momentum tensor）的效应，从而即便存在局域化物质也会出现一个引力影响不活跃的“非激活点”。当动力学质量较大（M ∼ ⟨ψ̄ψ⟩）时，狄拉克海效应占主导，导致负 ADM 质量的出现。

Conclusion: 结论指出，在弯曲时空中耦合引力的 CQSM 框架可以自洽描述局域化费米子与引力的相互作用，狄拉克海显著改变能动张量效应并主导大动力学质量情形，使系统出现负 ADM 质量。该结果凸显量子真空（狄拉克海）在引力-物质耦合体系中的关键作用。

Abstract: In this paper, we study Einstein-Dirac system based on a Dirac fermion coupled with a nonlinear chiral field on static spherically symmetric spacetime. Gravitational effects on the dynamical mass of fermions are examined. The chiral-quark soliton model (CQSM) originally was a model of hadron inspired by the low-energy regime of large-$N_\textrm{c}$ QCD, realizing localized fermions with the full inclusion of the Dirac-sea, which is derived from a regularized one-fermion loop. We extend the CQSM in spherically symmetric curved spacetime and coupled with the Einstein gravity. We successfully solve the CQSM and the Einstein equation self-consistently, and obtain the spectral flow of the fermion energies and also of the ADM mass. The Dirac-sea dilutes the effect of the energy-momentum tensor, which induces an inactive point for the impact of gravity even when the localizing matter exists. The Dirac-sea effect becomes dominant for larger dynamical mass $M\sim \langle\barψψ\rangle$, leading to the emergence of the negative ADM mass.

</details>


### [107] [Signatures of Novikov-Thorne accretion disks around rotating black holes in semiclassical gravity with trace anomaly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24273)
*Yuxiang Zuo,Shiyang Hu,Dan Li,Chen Deng,Guansheng He,Wenbin Lin*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在半经典引力（semiclassical gravity）框架下，针对含A型迹反常（type-A trace anomaly）的旋转黑洞，研究了Novikov-Thorne吸积盘的热辐射特征。结果表明耦合参数（coupling parameter）会抑制盘辐射，且在高自旋时差异显著，可作为区分半经典引力与广义相对论（general relativity）的观测窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半经典引力通过量子反作用（quantum backreaction）为解决黑洞奇点问题提供了有前景的框架，而该框架由耦合参数刻画，因此有必要深入探究此类天体的吸积性质。当前缺乏对半经典引力下吸积盘热辐射特征的细致认识，故本文针对这一空白展开研究。

Method: 在含A型迹反常的半经典引力框架内，针对不同参数空间中的旋转黑洞，研究Novikov-Thorne吸积盘的热辐射特征，重点关注能量通量密度（energy flux density）、温度分布（temperature profile）与热光度（thermal luminosity），并与广义相对论中的Kerr黑洞进行对比。

Result: 耦合参数的引入抑制了盘的辐射，使能量通量密度与温度的峰值降低，热光度的峰值及其对应峰值频率亦低于广义相对论中的Kerr对应情形；当黑洞具有高自旋（high spin）时，这一差异尤为显著。

Conclusion: 机制上，增大耦合参数会增强中心致密天体的有效引力场强度，使赤道稳定圆轨道（equatorial stable circular orbits）外移并提高轨道物质的比能（specific energy），从而降低辐射效率并削弱整体盘辐射。该抑制机制为区分半经典引力与广义相对论、以及通过热盘谱对耦合参数施加观测约束提供了实际的天体物理途径。

Abstract: Semiclassical gravity provides a promising framework to address the black hole singularity problem through quantum backreaction characterized by a coupling parameter, making it essential to thoroughly explore the accretion properties around such objects. In this paper, within the framework of semiclassical gravity with type-A trace anomaly, we investigate the thermal radiative signatures of Novikov-Thorne accretion disks around rotating black holes across diverse parameter spaces, focusing on the energy flux density, temperature profile, and thermal luminosity. Our findings demonstrate that the introduction of the coupling parameter suppresses disk emission, leading to lower peak values of both energy flux density and temperature, as well as lower peak values and corresponding peak frequencies in thermal luminosity compared to their Kerr counterparts in general relativity. In particular, this discrepancy becomes remarkably pronounced when the black hole possesses a high spin. Mechanistically, increasing the coupling parameter reinforces the effective gravitational field strength of the central compact object, which pushes the equatorial stable circular orbits outward and increases the specific energy of the orbiting matter, thereby reducing the radiative efficiency and weakening the overall disk emission. This suppression mechanism offers a practical astrophysical window to distinguish semiclassical gravity from general relativity and to place observational constraints on the coupling parameter via thermal disk spectra.

</details>


### [108] [A novel phenomenological parametrization of dynamical dark energy via matter-gravity coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24361)
*Hrishikesh Chakrabarty,Daniele Malafarina*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 提出基于 Markov-Mukhanov 作用量、含非最小物质-引力耦合的 $\\Lambda$CDM 偏离参数化方案，使 $\\Lambda$ 与 $G$ 动力学化，并用于构建可约束、可解释 DESI "phantom crossing" 的唯象模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为描述对标准 $\\Lambda$CDM 模型的一般性偏离，需要一个更普适的参数化框架，以处理暗能量与引力耦合可能的动力学行为。

Method: 采用含非最小物质-引力耦合（non-minimal matter-gravity coupling）的 Markov-Mukhanov 作用量（Markov-Mukhanov action），将宇宙学常数 $\\Lambda$ 与引力耦合常数 $G$ 视为动力学量，其数值由物质-引力耦合的具体形式决定；并据此构建低密度下偏离经典广义相对论（General Relativity）的简单唯象模型。

Result: 在该框架下，动力学暗能量（dynamical dark energy）自然出现，源于低密度区域理论对经典广义相对论的偏离；模型自由参数可由观测数据（observational data）进行约束。

Conclusion: 所提形式体系为约束模型参数提供了途径，并可用于解释 DESI 合作组关于 "phantom crossing" 的初步观测结果。

Abstract: We develop a new parametrization for generic departures from the $Λ$CDM model based on the Markov-Mukhanov action with non-minimal matter-gravity coupling. In this approach the cosmological constant $Λ$ and the gravitational coupling $G$ become dynamical with their values determined by the explicit form of the matter-gravity coupling. We use the framework to build a simple phenomenological model where dynamical dark energy is naturally obtained as a consequence of the departure of the theory from classical General Relativity at low densities. We show how the model's free parameters can be constrained from observational data and interpret the tentative observations of `phantom crossing' by the DESI collaboration in view of the proposed formalism.

</details>


### [109] [Joule Thomson expansion of symmergent black holes in extended phase space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24400)
*Saheb Soroushfar,Sayyed Mehrab Ramezani*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在扩展相空间（extended phase-space）框架下研究 symmergent 黑洞的 Joule-Thomson 膨胀，发现 Joule-Thomson 系数仅依赖组合 8πP r_+²，反转曲线 P_i=(π/2)I_i² 与 α-hat 无关，而等焓曲线仍保留对 α-hat 的参数依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: symmergent 黑洞的熵含有参数 α-hat，会改变焓与热力学体积的标准形式，因此需要在扩展相空间（extended phase-space formalism）中对 Joule-Thomson 膨胀进行系统刻画，以厘清 symmergent 参数在该过程中的作用并建立一致的热力学框架。

Method: 采用扩展相空间形式，将热力学压力（thermodynamic pressure）作为独立变量引入；基于含 α-hat 的 symmergent 熵（symmergent entropy），计算修正后的焓（enthalpy）与热力学体积（thermodynamic volume），并推导 Joule-Thomson 系数、反转曲线（inversion curve）以及等焓曲线（isenthalpic curves）。

Result: Joule-Thomson 系数仅由 8πP r_+² 这一组合决定，反转曲线为 P_i=(π/2)I_i²，与 α-hat 无关；等焓曲线则仍参数化依赖于 α-hat，表明 symmergent 部分影响热力学轨迹但不改变反转边界本身。文中显式给出了反转压力与温度，并区分冷却区（cooling regime）P > P_i 与加热区（heating regime）P < P_i。

Conclusion: 在正压下有效几何呈渐近 AdS 形式（asymptotically AdS-like），因此结果可作全息解读（holographic reading），即将 Joule-Thomson 膨胀对应于对偶场论（dual field theory）中的等焓过程。该工作为 symmergent 黑洞提供了自洽的热力学框架，并明确了 symmergent 参数在 Joule-Thomson 过程中的角色。

Abstract: We investigate the Joule Thomson expansion of a symmergent black hole within an extended phase-space formalism in which the thermodynamic pressure is introduced as an independent variable. The symmergent entropy contains the parameter alpha-hat, which modifies the enthalpy and thermodynamic volume relative to their standard forms. We show that the Joule Thomson coefficient depends only on the combination 8piP*r_+^2, leading to an inversion curve P_i = (pi/2) * I_i^2 that is independent of alpha-hat. The isenthalpic curves, however, retain a parametric dependence on alpha-hat, indicating that the symmergent sector affects the thermodynamic trajectories but not the inversion boundary itself. The inversion pressure and temperature are derived explicitly, and the cooling regime P > P_i is distinguished from the heating regime P < P_i. Since the effective geometry is asymptotically AdS-like for positive pressure, the results also admit a holographic reading, in which the Joule Thomson expansion corresponds to an isenthalpic process in the dual field theory. The results provide a consistent thermodynamic framework for symmergent black holes and clarify the role of the symmergent parameters in the Joule Thomson process.

</details>


### [110] [Stationary Rotating Geometries Associated with Nonsingular Pulsating Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28850)
*A. D. Souza,F. A. Brito,J. Spinelly*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 基于 Gao–Lu–Shen–Faraoni 的静态外部几何，通过 Newman–Janis 算法（Azreg-Aïnou 表述）构造其旋转稳态延拓，所得时空在量子/膜世界修正趋于零时回到 Kerr 与 Schwarzschild 极限，并进一步分析其热力学性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义相对论（General Relativity, GR）的奇点定理表明引力坍缩会导致奇异性，而该区域预期需要超越经典 GR 的物理。受圈量子宇宙学（Loop Quantum Cosmology, LQC）与膜世界（braneworld）情景启发的高密度修正方案，可望避免奇点，因此有必要研究相应外部几何及其旋转推广。

Method: 采用 Azreg-Aïnou 表述下的 Newman–Janis 算法，对 Gao、Lu、Shen 与 Faraoni 提出的静态外部几何（对应经坍缩与反弹阶段避免奇点的共动内部模型）构造稳态旋转外部延拓。

Result: 所得线元描述一个稳态轴对称旋转时空；其视界几何、角速度、表面重力与霍金温度（Hawking temperature）在量子/膜世界修正可忽略（l→0）时恢复 Kerr 与 Schwarzschild 极限。此外，在固定转动状态空间参数下分析了热响应，识别出 Davies 型奇点，并给出扩展的热力学状态空间关系。

Conclusion: 该工作为含高密度修正的静态外部几何提供了旋转稳态推广，并显示其在经典极限下与 Kerr/Schwarzschild 一致；其热力学结构包含 Davies 型奇点与扩展状态空间关系，可作为进一步检验此类量子/膜世界启发模型的基础。

Abstract: Since the first exact solutions of General Relativity (GR) were obtained, it became clear that the theory predicts a new class of compact objects: black holes. The Hawking--Penrose singularity theorems show that, under appropriate assumptions, gravitational collapse in GR leads to singular behavior. Motivated by the expectation that such regimes require physics beyond classical GR, effective approaches inspired by Loop Quantum Cosmology (LQC) and braneworld scenarios introduce high-density corrections. In this work we consider the static exterior geometry introduced by Gao, Lu, Shen, and Faraoni, associated with a comoving interior model in which the singularity is avoided through a sequence of collapse and bounce phases. Using the Newman--Janis algorithm in the Azreg-Aïnou formulation, we construct a stationary rotating exterior extension of this geometry. The resulting line element describes a stationary axisymmetric rotating spacetime whose horizon geometry, angular velocity, surface gravity, and Hawking temperature recover the Kerr and Schwarzschild limits when the quantum/braneworld correction becomes negligible ($l\to 0$). We further analyze the thermal response at fixed rotational state-space parameter, identify Davies-type singular points, and formulate an extended thermodynamic state-space relation.

</details>


### [111] [Do gravitational waves assist the genuine tripartite entanglement harvesting?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24428)
*Xiang-Yue Yu,Xiao-Li Huang,Shu-Min Wu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究三个线性排列的 Unruh-DeWitt 探测器在引力波背景下提取真三体纠缠，发现引力波对纠缠提取具增强或抑制的双重作用，且真三体纠缠对引力波扰动比二体纠缠更敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究引力波背景对量子纠缠提取的影响，特别是真三体纠缠（genuine tripartite entanglement）在弯曲时空扰动下的行为，并评估其作为时空扰动量子探针的潜力。

Method: 将三个线性排列的 Unruh-DeWitt 探测器局域耦合至引力波背景中的无质量标量场（massless scalar field），分析其所提取的真三体纠缠。

Result: 引力波对纠缠提取呈双重作用，取决于系统参数；真三体纠缠对引力波频率呈显著非单调响应，经历抑制、类共振增强、再次抑制，并在高频区趋近闵可夫斯基时空（Minkowski spacetime）对应值；其对探测器间距的依赖亦为非单调，出现二体纠缠所不具备的局域增强峰。

Conclusion: 相比二体纠缠，真三体纠缠在频率与空间域均对引力波扰动更敏感，可能成为探测引力波所致时空扰动的灵敏量子探针（quantum probe）。

Abstract: We investigate the harvesting of genuine tripartite entanglement by three linearly arranged Unruh-DeWitt detectors locally coupled to a massless scalar field in a gravitational wave background. We show that gravitational waves play a dual role in entanglement harvesting, either enhancing or suppressing the harvested entanglement depending on the system parameters. In particular, genuine tripartite entanglement exhibits a pronounced nonmonotonic response to the gravitational wave frequency, characterized by successive suppression, enhancement resembling resonance, and renewed suppression, while approaching its corresponding value in Minkowski spacetime in the regime of high frequencies. Its dependence on the detector separation is also nonmonotonic, displaying local enhancement peaks that are absent in the corresponding bipartite entanglement behavior. Compared with bipartite entanglement, genuine tripartite entanglement therefore exhibits a higher sensitivity to gravitational wave perturbations in both the frequency and spatial domains. These results suggest that genuine tripartite entanglement may provide a sensitive quantum probe of spacetime perturbations induced by gravitational waves.

</details>


### [112] [How Universal Is the Black Hole Zeroth Law?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24648)
*Rajes Ghosh,Sudipta Sarkar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文指出黑洞第零定律（zeroth law）在超越广义相对论的引力理论中并非由普适机制保证，而是由场方程不同的视界投影分量各自实现，并据此给出高阶曲率引力中的首个精确证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞第零定律断言稳态Killing视界（Killing horizon）处于热平衡，但其在广义相对论（general relativity）之外的适用性仍有限。既有认识多局限于特定理论或依赖对广义相对论的微扰展开，缺乏对定律成立机制的普遍理解。

Method: 通过分析不同引力理论中场方程在视界上的投影结构来判别定律的成立机制：在广义相对论、$f(R)$引力与Lovelock理论中，定律源自混合（零—横向，null-transverse）视界方程；在非Lovelock理论中则机制不同，例如广义二次引力（generalized quadratic gravity）由场方程的零—零（null-null）分量决定。作者将这一投影结构差异作为新的诊断工具。

Result: 在更广泛的高阶曲率引力（higher-curvature gravity）框架下，首次给出了第零定律的精确证明，且不依赖围绕广义相对论的微扰展开（perturbative expansion）。

Conclusion: 第零定律可作为引力内在热力学结构（thermodynamic structure）的敏锐探针，并指向依据其成立机制对修正引力理论（modified theories）进行分类的可能性。

Abstract: The black hole zeroth law asserts the thermal equilibrium of a stationary Killing horizon, yet its status beyond general relativity remains limited. We demonstrate that its validity is not governed by a universal mechanism: distinct gravitational theories enforce constancy of the surface gravity through different horizon-projections of their field equations. In general relativity, $f(R)$-gravity, and Lovelock theories, the zeroth law is known to follow from the mixed (null-transverse) horizon equations. By contrast, we show that in non-Lovelock theories the zeroth law is enforced by qualitatively different mechanisms. In generalized quadratic gravity, for instance, the null-null component of the field equations dictates its validity. Exploiting this novel diagnostic, we obtain the first exact proof of the zeroth law in the broader landscape of higher-curvature gravity, without relying on a perturbative expansion around general relativity. Our result establishes the zeroth law as a sharp probe of the underlying thermodynamic structure of gravity, and points toward a classification of modified theories according to the mechanism responsible for its validity.

</details>


### [113] [Formalizing a unified dynamical system framework for $f(R)$ and $f(Q)$ gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24733)
*Saikat Chakraborty*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出统一动力学系统（dynamical system）框架，突破传统哈勃归一化（Hubble-normalized）表述，用三类无量纲变量覆盖 $f(R)$ 与协和规范 $f(Q)$ 引力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统哈勃归一化形式化方法在部分 $f(R)$ 与 $f(Q)$ 模型（如 Hu-Sawicki $f(R)$、$f(Q)=-2\Lambda+Q+\beta\sqrt{-Q}$）中失效，需要更具普适性的动力学系统表述。

Method: 采用三组无量纲变量：运动学宇宙学参数（cosmographic parameters）、暗能量状态方程参数 $w$（及其 $N=\ln a$ 导数）与物质密度丰度 $\Omega_m$、以及刻画理论函数形状的理论空间参数 $\{m_i\}$；系统不含辅助变量（auxiliary variables），并通过三种等价方式闭合：指定理论、指定宇宙演化或指定暗能量状态方程演化。

Result: 理论闭合中，$f(Q)$ 普遍存在从广义相对论—物质主导时期到晚期德西特（de-Sitter）时期的异宿轨道（heteroclinic trajectory），而 $f(R)$ 对初始条件敏感；宇宙学与状态方程闭合下，$f(R)$ 与 $f(Q)$ 可分别运动学等价于 $j(z)=1$、动力学等价于 $w(z)=-1$ 的 $\Lambda$CDM，且 GR 对 $f(Q)$ 为宇宙学过去吸引子（past attractor），对 $f(R)$ 则不然。

Conclusion: 该统一表述为 $f(R)$ 与 $f(Q)$ 引力提供了无需辅助变量、可通过三种等价途径闭合的动力学分析框架，并揭示了二者在吸引子结构与初始条件敏感性上的实质差异。

Abstract: Going beyond the traditional Hubble-normalized framework, we formalize a unified dynamical system formulation for $f(R)$ and coincident gauge $f(Q)$ gravity using three distinct sets of dimensionless variables: (1) kinematical cosmographic parameters, (2) the dark energy equation of state parameter $w$ (with its $N=\ln a$ derivatives) and matter density abundance $Ω_m$, and (3) theory space parameters $\{m_i\}$ characterizing the shape of the theory function. Our formulation is free of auxiliary variables, closing via three equivalent ways: (1) specifying a theory, (2) requiring a specific cosmological evolution, or (3) requiring a specific dark energy equation of state evolution.
  Each closure strategy is illustrated with explicit examples. The theory closure approach studies a case of Hu-Sawicki $f(R)$ and $f(Q)=-2Λ+Q+β\sqrt{-Q}$ models, where traditional formalisms fail. A heteroclinic trajectory from a GR-matter-dominated epoch to a late-time de-Sitter epoch is general for $f(Q)$, but sensitive to initial conditions for $f(R)$. The cosmographic and equation-of-state closure approaches compare $f(R)$ and $f(Q)$ gravities kinematically equivalent to $Λ$CDM ($j(z)=1$) and dynamically equivalent to $Λ$CDM ($w(z)=-1$). In both, GR acts as a cosmological past attractor for $f(Q)$ gravity, but not for $f(R)$.

</details>


### [114] [Constraining the geometry of rotating black holes with eikonal QNMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24824)
*Ciro De Simone,Sebastian H. Völkel,Kostas D. Kokkotas*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该工作将校准的程函方法（eikonal method）与旋转黑洞度规（rotating black hole metric）相连接，并通过贝叶斯推断（Bayesian inference）比较度规特异性模型与度规不可知（metric-agnostic）模型，评估其在环降（ringdown）阶段推断中的鲁棒性与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞准正规模（quasinormal modes）是检验广义相对论（general relativity）与引力波（gravitational-wave）观测的重要途径，但从环降信号推断黑洞性质或背景时空需要依赖理论的输入，因此需要评估不同建模方式的可靠性。

Method: 采用校准的程函方法，将准正规模偏移与光球（light ring）附近的局部度规性质相联系；使用贝叶斯推断，对比基于全局 Konoplya-Rezzolla-Zhidenko 参数化的度规特异性模型，与基于光球处度规函数局部值及径向导数的度规不可知方法；对每种模型开展单参数与多参数同时分析，并量化先验如何映射到相同的局部量。

Result: 发现度规特异性系数的均匀先验可在局部度规量上诱导强烈非均匀且偏斜的先验；对与广义相对论一致的注入，两模型均与 Kerr 一致，且对轨道频率（orbital frequency）与李雅普诺夫指数（Lyapunov exponent）给出相似后验；对偏离广义相对论的注入，单参数分析可能无法恢复注入的局部偏离，且在度规特异性情形下可能偏差全局度规重建；多参数分析给出更一致约束但引入显著简并（degeneracies）与边缘化参数差异；应用于 GW250114 环降近似后验信息时，两模型均与广义相对论中的 Kerr 度规一致。

Conclusion: 基于程函方法的度规特异与度规不可知建模在广义相对论情形下表现一致，但在偏离情形下单参数分析可能失效或产生偏差；多参数分析虽更稳健却带来简并与参数差异，表明在更灵活模型下存在实际局限；对 GW250114 的应用结果与广义相对论中的 Kerr 一致。

Abstract: Quasinormal modes of black holes provide a central avenue for confronting general relativity with gravitational-wave observations from the ringdown stage of compact binary coalescence. However, inferring black-hole properties or the background spacetime requires theory-dependent input. In this work, we connect a calibrated eikonal method, which relates quasinormal mode shifts to local metric properties near the light ring, with the underlying rotating black hole metric. Using Bayesian inference, we compare a metric-specific model based on the global Konoplya-Rezzolla-Zhidenko parametrization with a metric-agnostic approach based on local values and radial derivatives of the metric functions at the light ring. For each model, we perform both one-parameter and simultaneous multi-parameter analyses and quantify how their priors map onto the same local quantities. We find that uniform priors on the metric-specific coefficients can induce strongly nonuniform and skewed priors on these local metric quantities. For an injection compatible with general relativity, both models remain consistent with Kerr and yield similar posteriors for the orbital frequency and Lyapunov exponent despite their different induced priors. For injections representing deviations from general relativity, one-parameter analyses can fail to recover the injected local deviations and, in the metric-specific case, can bias the global metric reconstruction. The multi-parameter analyses of both models yield consistent constraints, suggesting sufficient robustness. However, they introduce substantial degeneracies and differences in marginalized parameters, demonstrating practical limitations when considering more flexible models. Finally, we apply the framework to approximate posterior information from the ringdown analysis of GW250114 and find that both models are consistent with the Kerr metric in general relativity.

</details>


### [115] [Boson Star Factory: Past, Present, and Future](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24913)
*Romain Gervalle,Jorge Filipe Mónico Delgado,Etevaldo dos Santos Costa Filho,Carlos Herdeiro,Víctor Jaramillo,Eugen Radu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文对旋转玻色星与静态偶极玻色星两类非球对称构型，采用有限差分、有限元与谱方法三种独立代码进行系统性方法比较，三者对全局可观测量一致到约八位有效数字，并给出精确基准值与运行时间对比，另验证了物理信息神经网络（PINN）求解旋转Q-ball的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻色星（boson stars）是非球对称情形下需数值求解非线性椭圆偏微分方程（nonlinear elliptic PDEs）的紧凑天体，而各研究组通常使用自有的数值实现，缺乏对同一物理模型的系统性方法比较。

Method: 针对旋转玻色星（rotating boson stars）与静态偶极玻色星（static dipolar boson stars）两种最简非球对称构型，使用三种独立代码：有限差分（finite differences, FIDISOL/CADSOL）、有限元（finite elements, FreeFem）与谱方法（spectral methods, Kadath），并通过多种诊断量评估精度，同时在相同硬件上比较运行时间。

Result: 三种求解器对全局可观测量的结果一致到约八位有效数字；论文给出相关物理量的精确基准值并比较了各代码在相同硬件上的运行时间。此外，作为概念验证，展示了用物理信息神经网络（physics-informed neural networks）构造旋转Q-ball解。

Conclusion: 该工作为玻色星的数值研究提供了跨方法一致性与参考基准数据，并初步表明物理信息神经网络可扩展至旋转Q-ball等构型的求解。

Abstract: Boson stars are self-gravitating solitons of the Einstein-Klein-Gordon equations for a massive complex scalar field, and arguably the simplest horizonless compact objects arising in General Relativity. Beyond spherical symmetry, their construction requires solving a system of nonlinear elliptic partial differential equations, a difficult task per se and that each group has traditionally addressed with its own numerical implementation. A systematic comparison of different methods applied to the same physical model has so far been lacking. In this work, we carry out such a comparison for the two simplest non-spherical configurations: rotating boson stars and static dipolar boson stars. We employ three independent codes, based respectively on finite differences (FIDISOL/CADSOL), finite elements (FreeFem), and spectral methods (Kadath), and assess their accuracy through different diagnostics. The three solvers agree on the global observables to typically eight significant digits. We further compare the runtime of each code on identical hardware and provide precise benchmark values of the relevant physical quantities, intended to serve as reference data for future numerical studies of boson stars. As a further proof of concept, we also present the use of physics-informed neural networks to construct a rotating Q-ball solution.

</details>


### [116] [Observational Features of Nonsingular Rotating Black Holes in the Dark-Energy Dominated Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24926)
*Ramon Torres*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文研究宇宙学项随曲率跑动（running cosmological term）的非奇异旋转黑洞（nonsingular rotating black hole）在相空间中的零测地线（null geodesic）阴影现象学，指出阴影面积与垂直直径可作为该跑动效应的清晰特征，并可用于约束有效正则化尺度（regularization scale）并区分非奇异几何与 Kerr/Kerr-dS 解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期 DESI 重子声学振荡（baryon acoustic oscillation, BAO）测量结合宇宙微波背景（cosmic microwave background, CMB）与 Ia 型超新星（type-Ia supernova）数据，暗示暗能量可能随宇宙时间演化。在宇宙学背景上，演化暗能量密度可局部表达为单调曲率不变量（curvature invariant）的函数，从而引出有效曲率依赖宇宙学项；弯曲时空量子场论（quantum field theory in curved spacetime）与引力耦合跑动的量子引力（quantum gravity）路径亦提供互补动机。

Method: 将问题直接在相空间（phase space）中表述，采用零测地线超哈密顿量（null geodesic super-Hamiltonian）与 Mino 型参数（Mino-type parameter）；通过从零角动量观测者（zero-angular-momentum observer）进行反向光线追踪（backward ray tracing）计算黑洞阴影，并定义与分析一组涵盖阴影大小、形状与位移的标准阴影可观测量（shadow observables）。

Result: 在暗能量主导宇宙中，非奇异旋转黑洞的现象学可与 Kerr 及 Kerr–de Sitter（Kerr–dS）几何显著不同；阴影面积及其垂直直径（vertical diameter）对跑动宇宙学项尤为敏感，构成特别清晰的特征信号。

Conclusion: 对多个强场可观测量（strong-field observables）与多个不同质量、自旋黑洞的联合分析，可为紧密约束乃至测量宇宙学项跑动背后的有效正则化尺度、并在统计上区分非奇异黑洞几何与 Kerr/Kerr-dS 解提供现实途径。

Abstract: Recent DESI baryon acoustic oscillation measurements, combined with cosmic microwave background and type-Ia supernova data, suggest that dark energy may evolve with cosmic time. Along a cosmological background, an evolving dark-energy density can be expressed locally as a function of a monotonic curvature invariant, motivating an effective curvature-dependent cosmological term. Moreover, quantum field theory in curved spacetime and approaches to quantum gravity with running gravitational couplings provide complementary motivations for exploring such a term.
  Here we study how the phenomenology of nonsingular rotating black holes embedded in a Universe dominated by dark energy can differ substantially from that of the Kerr and Kerr--de Sitter geometries. The problem is directly formulated in phase space using a null geodesic super-Hamiltonian and a Mino-type parameter. The shadows of the black holes are calculated by backward ray tracing from a zero-angular-momentum observer. We define and analyze a comprehensive set of standard shadow observables, characterizing the size, shape, and displacement of the shadow. We show that the area of the shadow and its vertical diameter provide particularly clear signatures of a running cosmological term. In this way, joint analyses of multiple strong-field observables and of several black holes with different masses and spins could provide a realistic route to tightly constraining, or potentially measuring, the effective regularization scale behind the running of the cosmological term and statistically distinguishing the non-singular black hole geometry from Kerr/Kerr-dS solutions.

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Spin-Boson Mappings in the Formalism of $f$-Deformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22502)
*Vladimir A. Orlov,Liubov A. Markovich,Alexey V. Mikheenkov,Vladimir I. Man'ko*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于 $f$-变形振子（$f$-deformed oscillators）形式提出自旋-玻色子变换（spin-boson transformations）的统一代数方法，将已知映射解释为同一代数结构的因子化实现，并给出二模情形的新表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自旋-玻色子变换（如 Holstein-Primakoff、Dyson-Maleev 及插值 $\alpha$-族）通常被分别处理，缺乏统一框架，难以区分精确代数内容与非厄米性（non-Hermiticity）、度规选择（choice of metric）等附加效应。

Method: 采用 $f$-变形振子形式，在单模情形将 Holstein-Primakoff 与 Dyson-Maleev 变换及插值 $\alpha$-族表述为同一代数确定对象的不同因子化；在二模情形将同一方法应用于 Jordan-Schwinger 变换的变形版本及新的精确二模实现。

Result: 单模情形表明标准自旋-玻色子映射可视为共同结构的实现，从而可将物理子空间上的精确代数内容与非厄米性、度规选择及超出物理子空间的扩展效应清晰分离；二模情形同时得到 Jordan-Schwinger 变换的变形版本和新的精确二模实现。

Conclusion: 该工作给出已知自旋-玻色子变换的统一描述，并自然导出新的玻色子表示（bosonic representations）。

Abstract: We develop a unified algebraic approach to spin-boson transformations based on the formalism of $f$-deformed oscillators. In the single-mode case, we show that the Holstein-Primakoff and Dyson-Maleev transformations, together with the interpolating $α$-family, arise as different factorizations of the same algebraically determined object. The standard spin-boson mappings can thus be interpreted as realizations of a common structure, which makes it possible to clearly separate the exact algebraic content on the physical subspace from effects associated with non-Hermiticity, the choice of metric, and extensions beyond the physical subspace. In the two-mode case, the same approach yields both deformed versions of the Jordan-Schwinger transformation and new exact two-mode realizations. Our results provide a unified description of known spin-boson transformations and naturally lead to new bosonic representations.

</details>


### [118] [Exact Realization of Deep ReLU Computation in a Microscopic Statistical-Mechanical System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22965)
*Junxu Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作为任意深度 ReLU 网络构造了一个精确的统计力学实现，证明其分段线性计算源自微观系统的相变，从而为深度神经计算提供物理基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度整流线性单元（ReLU）网络中分段线性计算的物理起源仍是一个基本未解问题。作者试图从微观物理层面为该计算行为寻找精确对应。

Method: 从微观构型空间及其状态多重度出发，在不预设神经网络激活函数的前提下构造配分函数；所得系统可用带后选择（post selection）的级联量子操作或费米子输运模型等价描述。

Result: 严格证明在 β→+∞ 极限下，该系统的热力学可观测量精确重现任意深度 ReLU 网络的隐藏状态、输出与损失函数；ReLU 的分段线性行为源于微观系统的一级相变，其不连续点恰与 ReLU 网络线性区域边界重合。
