Conclusion: 该工作为深度神经计算建立了精确的物理基础，并为神经架构设计提供了新的统计力学视角。

Abstract: Understanding the physical origin of the piecewise-linear computation in deep rectified linear unit (ReLU) networks remains a fundamental challenge. Here we establish an exact statistical-mechanical realization of arbitrary ReLU networks. Starting from a microscopic configuration space and its state multiplicities, we construct a partition function without prescribing a neural-network activation function. The resulting system admits equivalent descriptions in terms of cascaded quantum operations with post selection, or fermionic transport model. We rigorously prove that in $β\to+\infty$ limit, the thermodynamic observables of this system exactly reproduce the hidden states, outputs, and loss function of an arbitrary deep ReLU network. Crucially, we demonstrate that the piecewise-linear behavior of the ReLU network emerges from a first-order phase transition in the microscopic system, where the discontinuities precisely coincide with the boundaries of linear regions of ReLU network. This work establishes an exact physical foundation for deep neural computation and provides a novel statistical-mechanical perspective on neural architecture design.

</details>


### [119] [Entangled measurements are necessary for optimal tomography of mixed fermionic Gaussian states and of bosonic Gaussian states near the vacuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23189)
*Ron Rubin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作证明：在无量子存储（quantum memory）的单拷贝测量模型下，学习 m 模混合费米子高斯态（mixed fermionic Gaussian state）到迹距离 ε 需 Ω(m³/ε²) 个拷贝，与集体测量（collective measurement）已知速率 Θ(m²/ε²) 形成分离，并给出匹配的上界与近似被动玻色子情形的类似分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对 Chen 等人提出的混合态测量资源问题（mixed-state measurement-resource question），即有限量子存储如何影响高斯态学习复杂度。目标是将单拷贝（single-copy）模型与集体模型区分开来，明确量子存储作为测量资源的必要性。

Method: 基于统一的单拷贝费雪信息预算（uniform single-copy Fisher-information budget），并建立与维数无关的迹距离—高斯参数比较。对算子范数不超过 1−c 的协方差矩阵（covariance matrix），证明 Frobenius 范数到迹范数的连续界，常数取 [2c(2−c)]^{−1/2}；并结合 matchgate shadows 构造上界。玻色子情形通过归约到有界块 qudit 层析（bounded-block qudit tomography）得到下界。

Result: 费米子情形得到单拷贝下界 Ω(m³/ε²)（适用于 0&lt;ε≤1/3600），且 matchgate shadows 达到 O(m³/(cε²))；对总平均光子数不超过一的混合被动玻色子高斯态，下界为 Ω(m³/(ε²√log(m/ε)))，集体复杂度为 Θ(m²/ε²)。另有：双模例子的最优双拷贝费雪迹为 5，可分上限为 4；IBM 复现给出预注册见证下界 4.12（单侧 95% 散粒噪声置信度）。

Conclusion: 结果确立了单拷贝与集体测量模型之间的量子存储分离，为热自由费米子态（thermal free-fermion state）提供显式误差证书。IBM 实验的解释依赖预设制备与共同测量信道，不约束制备系统误差。

Abstract: We prove that learning an unknown mixed fermionic Gaussian state on $m$ modes to trace distance $ε$ requires $Ω(m^3/ε^2)$ copies when measurements act on one copy at a time, even with arbitrary POVMs, fresh ancillas and classical adaptivity, but without quantum memory between copies. The bound holds for $0<ε\le1/3600$ and separates this model from the known collective rate $Θ(m^2/ε^2)$. It follows from a uniform single-copy Fisher-information budget and a dimension-independent comparison between trace distance and Gaussian parameters. On covariance matrices of operator norm at most $1-c$, we prove a Frobenius-to-trace-norm continuity bound with constant $[2c(2-c)]^{-1/2}$; matchgate shadows then attain $O(m^3/(cε^2))$ copies. This also gives explicit error certificates for thermal free-fermion states. For a class of mixed passive bosonic Gaussian states with total mean photon number at most one, a reduction to bounded-block qudit tomography gives a single-copy lower bound $Ω(m^3/(ε^2\sqrt{\log(m/ε)}))$, versus collective complexity $Θ(m^2/ε^2)$. These separations answer the mixed-state measurement-resource question posed by Chen et al. A two-mode example has optimal two-copy Fisher trace $5$, versus the separable ceiling $4$. An IBM replication gave a pre-registered witness lower bound $4.12$ at one-sided $95\%$ shot-noise confidence; its interpretation assumes the prescribed preparations and a common measurement channel and does not bound preparation systematics.

</details>


### [120] [Localized charges, reset noise, and boundary memory in matrix-product-conserving quantum chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23195)
*Ron Rubin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在具有守恒矩阵标签有序乘积的量子链中构造了能保留有限时间记忆的边界可观测量；借助随机矩阵乘积的投影收缩与一条有限时间不等式，将读出重叠与重置泄漏转化为关联上界，并给出指数记忆窗口及显式多项式证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子链中的边界信息在噪声与重置作用下通常迅速丢失，如何在有限时间窗内保证守恒荷（conserved charge）的边界可读性缺乏定量刻画。论文旨在为噪声区域附近的记忆保持提供可证明的时间窗口与定量修正。

Method: 在标签矩阵满足强不可约性（strong-irreducibility）与近邻性（proximality）假设下，利用随机矩阵乘积的投影收缩（projective contraction）在均匀基组态上的均方根意义下局域化守恒荷；再通过有限时间不等式将读出重叠与重置泄漏（reset leakage）转化为关联界 Φ 通道序列不依赖门平均。对十三态基本矩阵模型给出精确多项式证书（polynomial certificates）计算。

Result: 得到噪声区域距离上的指数记忆窗口，并对空间分布噪声与不完美守恒给出定量修正；十三态模型中荷与其末 r 位点限制之间的均方根局域化误差为 e^{-r/1400}，有理荷的极限方差至少 1/19；相关归一化 Gram 荷给出八位点证书，在两轮边界重置后保留超过一半的指定读出关联；均匀退极化（uniform depolarization）给出寿命上界随噪声率倒数变化。两比特 IBM 实验仅示例重置不等式，未实现矩阵乘积链。

Conclusion: 论文建立了基于矩阵乘积收缩与守恒通道的边界记忆定量框架，并以精确证书与独立复核的实验结果支撑该界；其适用性受强不可约性与近邻性假设限制，实验部分不构成对矩阵乘积链实现。

Abstract: We construct boundary observables that retain memory for a quantified time window in quantum chains whose basis configurations carry a conserved ordered product of matrix labels. Under strong-irreducibility and proximality hypotheses on the label matrices, projective contraction of random matrix products localizes a conserved charge in the root-mean-square over uniform basis configurations. A finite-time inequality then converts readout overlap and reset leakage into a correlation bound: for a conserved reference observable $H$, a readout $F$, accumulated squared leakage $\mathcal B$, and the normalized Hilbert-Schmidt inner product, $\langle F,Φ(F)\rangle\ge 2\langle H,F\rangle^2/(\|H\|_2^2+\mathcal B)-\|F\|_2^2$. Here $Φ$ is a sequence of conserving channels and partial resets. The bound holds for each such circuit, without averaging its gates. It yields an exponential memory window in the distance from the noisy region, with quantitative corrections for spatially distributed noise and imperfect conservation. For a thirteen-state elementary-matrix model, exact polynomial certificates give root-mean-square localization error $e^{-r/1400}$ between the charge and its restriction to the last $r$ sites, and limiting variance at least $1/19$ for a rational charge. A related normalized-Gram charge gives an eight-site certificate retaining more than half the specified readout correlation through two boundary-reset rounds. Uniform depolarization imposes an inverse-noise-rate lifetime ceiling. A two-qubit IBM experiment illustrates the general reset inequality; it does not realize the matrix-product chain. We provide proofs, exact certificate programs, and an independent recount of the archived experimental outcomes.

</details>


### [121] [Field-Modified Quantum Potentials from Tridiagonal Representations: Analytical Spectra and Galerkin Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23348)
*Tunde Joseph Osunmusanmi,Berihu Teklu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文构建了一个有效径向框架，用于分析共线电场与磁场中带电无自旋粒子的场修正势函数，并借助三对角表示法（tridiagonal representation approach）的间接模式获得解析谱与波函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共线电磁场中带电粒子的势函数在弱磁场与角度局域化条件下缺乏简便可解的径向模型，需要一个既能解析处理又便于数值验证的有效框架。

Method: 在弱磁场近似（weak-magnetic field approximation）下忽略抗磁项（diamagnetic term），以角度因子替换 C=cosθ 并在小量条件 ≪ΔE 下化简为沿场方向的径向模型；随后在 Laguerre 基与 Jacobi 基中应用三对角表示法（tridiagonal representation approach）的间接模式（indirect mode/search mode）。

Result: Laguerre 基下三对角约束生成线性加倒数势（linear-plus-inverse）与线性加二次势（linear-plus-quadratic），系数递推联系 Meixner–Pollaczek 与 Meixner 多项式结构，给出解析谱与展开波函数；Jacobi 基下径向半线映射为有限区间，产生奇异束缚势，三项递推通过截断广义本征值问题（generalized eigenvalue problem）处理。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了可解析且可数值计算的基准模型，明确列出假设、参数限制、端点行为与收敛检验；所得能谱公式均为新结果，且分别在 Laguerre 基与 Jacobi 基下与磁场进行解析和数值耦合。

Abstract: We develop an effective radial framework for analyzing new field-modified potential function of a charged spinless particle in collinear electric and magnetic fields. Using the weak- magnetic field approximation, we neglect the diamagnetic term and other angular projections under an explicit smallness condition $\ll ΔE$, where $E$ is the electric field, and the replacement of the angular factor by $C=\cosθ$ in the spherical coordinates. The resulting equation is therefore a field-aligned radial model whose validity is limited to angularly localized states. Within this setting, we apply the indirect mode (or search mode) of Tridiagonal representation approach in the Laguerre and Jacobi bases. In the Laguerre basis, the tridiagonal constraint generates linear-plus-inverse and linear-plus-quadratic radial potentials, and the coefficient recurrences are connected with Meixner--Pollaczek and Meixner polynomial type structures, therefore yielding the analytic spectra and expansion wavefunctions. In the Jacobi basis, the mapping of the radial half-line to a finite interval produces a singular confining effective potential and a three-term recurrence that is treated through a truncated generalized eigenvalue problem. The formulation provides analytically and numerically tractable benchmark models for field-modified potential functions, while keeping the assumptions, parameter restrictions, endpoint behavior, and convergence tests explicit. All energy spectra formulae obtained under the present models are new and are coupled with magnetic fields analytically and numerically when using the Laguerre bases and the Jacobi bases respectively.

</details>


### [122] [Physics-Informed Classical and Quantum Neural Networks for One-Dimensional Schrodinger Eigenvalue Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22189)
*Tariq Mahmood,Waqas Arshad,Bilal Naseer,Alfredo Raya*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究构建物理信息神经网络（PINNs）与物理信息量子神经网络（PIQNNs）求解一维定态薛定谔方程，在谐振子、无限深方势阱与有限深方势阱三个基准上，无监督地重现了本征值与本征函数，并与多种经典数值方法对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维定态薛定谔方程仅有少量可精确求解的势能模型，这些模型构成检验新本征值求解器的天然基准，论文借此评估神经网络类求解方法的有效性与精度。

Method: 构建 PINNs 与 PIQNNs 两类求解器，复合损失（composite loss）同时编码微分方程残差、归一化条件、边界行为与本征态间正交性，从而无需监督数据即可将试探波函数（trial wave function）驱动趋近真实本征函数；量子线路采用分层角度嵌入（angle-embedding）拟设与强纠缠块（strongly entangling blocks）。

Result: 两种神经求解器在平滑谐振子上将最低四个本征值复现至百万分率（parts per million）量级，在势能不连续的方势阱中亦以相当精度恢复解析能级；结果与精确谱及矩阵 Numerov 法、有限差分法、打靶法三种经典参考方法对比。量子线路在较高激发态上比经典网络收敛更可靠，后者损失景观（loss landscape）更难以导航。

Conclusion: 物理信息神经方法可在无监督条件下以高精度求解一维定态薛定谔方程，且在势能不连续情形下依然有效；量子线路拟设在高层激发态求解上相较经典网络表现出更稳定的收敛性。

Abstract: The Schrodinger equation in one spatial dimension admits a small set of exactly solvable potentials that serve as natural proving grounds for any new eigenvalue solver. We formulate Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) and Physics-Informed Quantum Neural Networks (PIQNNs) for the time-independent Schrodinger equation and apply them to three of these benchmarks: the harmonic oscillator, the infinite square well, and the finite square well. In each case a composite loss encodes the differential-equation residual, the normalization condition, the boundary behavior, and the orthogonality between eigenstates, so that the trial wave function is driven toward a genuine eigenfunction without supervised data. The eigenvalues and wave functions returned by both methods are compared against the exact spectra and against three classical references: the matrix Numerov method, the finite difference method, and the shooting method. For the smooth oscillator the two neural solvers reproduce the lowest four eigenvalues to parts per million, while for the square wells they recover the analytic levels with comparable fidelity even where the potential is discontinuous. The quantum circuit, built as a layered angle-embedding ansatz with strongly entangling blocks, converges more reliably than its classical counterpart on the higher excited states, where the loss landscape of the classical network becomes harder to navigate.

</details>


### [123] [Global zero-excitation state preparation through subsystem cooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23960)
*Kerstin Beer,Daniel Burgarth*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在有限子集上施加局域耗散（localized dissipation）的激发数守恒量子系统，利用 GKLS 主方程与零强制集（zero forcing set）概念，给出系统被驱动至唯一全局吸引零激发态的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子模拟与量子信息处理中，将相互作用量子系统制备至低能态或基态是基本任务；但实际中耗散与主动冷却往往仅能作用于系统的有限子集，因此需明确局域冷却何时足以实现全局态制备。

Method: 采用 GKLS 主方程（GKLS master equation）描述激发数守恒系统的耗散动力学，跳变算符（jump operators）仅作用于部分量子比特；通过图论分析，将哈密顿量诱导的激发转移刻画为图，并考察耗散子系统是否构成零强制集（zero forcing set）。

Result: 证明若激发转移图满足耗散子系统为零强制集，则零激发态是唯一的全局吸引定态（globally attractive stationary state）；当该态与哈密顿量基态重合时，同一机制即实现基态冷却；并以最近邻海森堡自旋链（nearest-neighbor Heisenberg spin chain）为例，单激发子空间归约给出随链长的标度估计，表明冷却效率良好。

Conclusion: 研究提供了基于图论的判据，用以判断局域耗散能否实现全局态制备，并给出局域冷却有效性的充分条件与效率标度依据。

Abstract: Preparing interacting quantum systems in low-energy or ground states is a fundamental task in quantum simulation and quantum information processing. In realistic settings, dissipation and active cooling can typically be engineered only on a limited subset of the system. We study the dissipative dynamics of excitation-number conserving quantum systems governed by a GKLS master equation with local jump operators acting on a subset of qubits. We establish sufficient conditions under which such localized dissipation drives the full system to a unique globally attractive zero-excitation state. In particular, we prove that if the Hamiltonian generates excitation transfer described by a graph for which the dissipative subsystem forms a zero forcing set, then the zero-excitation state is the unique globally attractive stationary state. When this state coincides with a ground state of the Hamiltonian, the same mechanism realizes ground-state cooling. Our results provide a graph-theoretic criterion for global state preparation from localized dissipation, which we illustrate using a nearest-neighbor Heisenberg spin chain. For this model, a reduction to the single-excitation sector further yields a scaling estimate with the length of the chain which indicates efficient cooling.

</details>


### [124] [Gravitationally Induced Entanglement of Matter in Quadratic Curvature Gravity and Constraints on Ghost Mass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22501)
*Linda M. van Manen,Tim Blankenstein,Anupam Mazumdar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究二次（Stelle）引力在双量子谐振子中诱导的引力纠缠，推导含 1.5 后牛顿阶修正的有效二体哈密顿量，并给出自旋-2 鬼模与自旋-0 模的质量约束及实验可探测预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子与引力交叉领域，引力能否诱导纠缠是检验引力量子性的重要探针，而牛顿引力之外的高阶修正可能改变这一效应。本文动机在于将分析推广至二次引力，考察其额外自旋-2 与自旋-0 自由度对引力诱导纠缠的影响及可约束性。

Method: 从二次引力作用量出发，为两个谐振子束缚质量推导有效二体哈密顿量，纳入大质量自旋-2 鬼模（ghost, m_2）与自旋-0 模（m_0）的贡献，并计入最高至 1.5 后牛顿阶（post-Newtonian order）的修正。对处于基态的两量子谐振子计算约化态的 von Neumann 熵与 Rényi 熵，并在非高斯（non-Gaussian）设置下计算引力诱导的 concurrence。

Result: 发现一个使引力诱导纠缠消失的频率，其源于相对论动量压缩（momentum squeezing）与量子离域引起的位形压缩（position squeezing）之间的抵消；由此导出近似约束 m_0 < ∛4 m_2。在非高斯设置中，concurrence 在特定质量、空间叠加、粒子间距及自旋模参数下可接近 O(1)，并在特定间距偏离牛顿引力，例如自旋模低至 0.0197 eV 时可在 d ~ 40 μm 处与牛顿引力区分。

Conclusion: 二次引力通过自旋-2 与自旋-0 模显著修正引力诱导纠缠，其可探测偏差为在实验中约束这两种自旋模质量提供了途径。

Abstract: We investigate gravitationally generated entanglement in two quantum harmonic oscillators induced by quadratic (Stelle) gravity, including corrections up to $1.5$ post-Newtonian order. Starting from the quadratic action, we derive the effective two-body Hamiltonian for two harmonically trapped masses, incorporating the contributions of the massive spin-$2$ ghost ($m_2$) and massive spin-$0$ ($m_0$) degrees of freedom of the gravitational field. For two quantised oscillators prepared in their ground state, we compute the von Neumann and Rényi entropies of the reduced state and identify a frequency at which the gravitationally-induced entanglement vanishes due to cancellation between relativistic momentum squeezing and quantum-delocalisation-induced position squeezing. We further analyze the cancellation frequency and derive the approximate constraint $m_0 < \sqrt[3]{4}\, m_2$ for the spin-$2$ and spin-$0$ modes. This relation follows from demanding a stable harmonic oscillator description. Finally, we study gravitationally-induced concurrence in a non-Gaussian setup and show how quadratic gravity modifies the entanglement generated between two spatial superpositions. The concurrence can approach $\mathcal{O}(1)$ for certain choices of mass, spatial superposition, particle distance, and spin-$2$ and spin-$0$ modes. The concurrence will deviate from Newtonian gravity at certain particle separations, depending on the energy of the spin-$2$ and spin-$0$ modes. For example, spin modes as low as $0.0197$ eV become distinguishable from Newtonian gravity at a distance $d \sim 40 μ$m. This allows us to constrain the spin-$2$ and spin-$0$ masses in experiments.

</details>


### [125] [Unified spatiotemporal quantum states and spatiotemporal entanglement from Kirkwood-Dirac phase space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24680)
*Zhian Jia,Mei-Hui Xiao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用时空 Kirkwood-Dirac 相空间（spatiotemporal Kirkwood-Dirac phase space）对时空量子态（spatiotemporal quantum state）给出统一刻画，揭示其非厄米、非正规特性，并构建基于（准）概率混合的统一框架，扩展至多重关联函数、OTOC、KMS 条件及时空纠缠分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子态概念限于空间域，而时空量子态（spatiotemporal quantum state）需刻画分布于时空中的量子事件关联，现有表述分散、缺乏统一刻画，故本文旨在提供统一框架。

Method: 采用时空 Kirkwood-Dirac 相空间（spatiotemporal Kirkwood-Dirac phase space）表征时空量子态，通过 Kirkwood-Dirac 时空态的（准）概率混合（quasi)probabilistic mixture）进行统一，并扩展至 s-参数化时空态框架。

Result: 由 Kirkwood-Dirac 分布得到的时空态一般为非厄米（non-Hermitian）且非正规（nonnormal），而 Margenau-Hill 分布构造的为厄米（Hermitian）；推导了递归表达式，给出 Kirkwood-Dirac 非经典性与时间性关系，并连接 OTOC、Petz 时间反演及双时 KMS 条件，应用于时空纠缠的熵度量分析。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了几乎所有现有的时空量子态表述，并为时空量子纠缠（spatiotemporal entanglement）及其非经典性的刻画提供了通用工具。

Abstract: The notion of a spatiotemporal quantum state extends the conventional concept of a spatial quantum state to the spatiotemporal domain. Such states are represented by unit-trace operators that encode correlations among quantum events distributed across space and time. In this work, we use the spatiotemporal Kirkwood-Dirac phase space to provide a unified characterization of spatiotemporal quantum states. Spatiotemporal states obtained from Kirkwood-Dirac distributions are generally non-Hermitian and nonnormal; whereas those constructed from Margenau-Hill distributions are Hermitian. We provide a unification via (quasi)probabilistic mixture of Kirkwwod-Dirac spatiotemporal states which encompasses almost all existing formulations of spatiotemporal quantum states. We derive recursive expressions for spatiotemporal states and elucidate the relation between Kirkwood-Dirac nonclassicality and the temporality of spatiotemporal states. We further extend the construction to many-fold correlation functions and establish its connection with out-of-time-ordered correlators (OTOCs). We also develop a more general unifying framework based on (quasi)probabilistic mixture of $s$-parametrized spatiotemporal states and establish their Petz time reversal and application in studying Kubo-Martin-Schwinger (KMS) condition in two-time setting. Finally, we apply this framework to characterize quantum entanglement in spacetime and analyze spatiotemporal entanglement using several complementary entropy measures.

</details>


### [126] [Strong Symmetry from Two-Point Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23591)
*Han Yan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明混合态的两点关联函数（two-point correlation functions）完整决定连续在位酉作用（continuous onsite unitary actions）的强对称性（strong symmetry），并给出从两点关联中提取最大连通强对称子李群及其特征标（character）的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合态中强对称性与弱对称性及其自发破缺区分了不同的对称性与有序形式，但如何由可观测的关联数据确定强对称性尚不明确。

Method: 利用完整的一点与两点关联函数（等价于所有两格点约化密度矩阵，two-site reduced density matrices），分析其在连通群（connected groups）连续在位酉作用下的性质，并设计算法从两点关联确定最大连通强对称子李群及其特征标；同时识别可用更少关联量判定的群与表示特定情形。

Result: 具有相同关联的态拥有相同的强对称性与特征标，因此两点关联可精确决定强对称性；所提算法能从两点关联中确定最大连通强对称子李群与特征标。

Conclusion: 由于两点关联比全态层析（full-state tomography）更易获取，该算法可直接应用于凝聚态系统（condensed-matter systems）到量子模拟器与量子线路（quantum simulators and circuits）等实验场景。

Abstract: Strong and weak symmetries and their spontaneous breaking distinguish different forms of symmetry and order in mixed states. We show that complete one- and two-point correlation functions, equivalently all two-site reduced density matrices, determine exact strong symmetry for continuous onsite unitary actions of connected groups: states with identical correlations have the same strong symmetry and character. We also present an algorithm to determine the maximal connected strong-symmetry sub-Lie group and its character from two-point correlations. Because such correlations are more accessible than full-state tomography, the algorithm is readily applicable to experiments ranging from condensed-matter systems to quantum simulators and circuits. We also identify group and representation-specific cases in which fewer correlations suffice.

</details>


### [127] [Quantum Computing Solution of the Bethe-Salpeter Equation for Relativistic Scalar Bound States via Tensor-Network VQE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24282)
*Gerhard Hellstern*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作将齐次Bethe-Salpeter方程（homogeneous Bethe-Salpeter equation, hBSE）约化为量子比特编码下的对称矩阵本征值问题，并用基于矩阵乘积态（Matrix Product State, MPS）拟设的变分量子本征求解器（Variational Quantum Eigensolver, VQE）求解。结果显示在N=16（4量子比特）时，VQE以优于1%的平均相对误差复现最大本征值，但纠缠分析表明该问题仍处于经典可处理区，构成一个负面结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是探索门基量子计算（gate-based quantum computing）能否有效求解两质量标量粒子束缚态的齐次Bethe-Salpeter方程。作者希望通过量子算法处理该本征值问题，并厘清其是否可能带来量子优势（quantum advantage）。

Method: 方法为：在梯近似（ladder-approximation）标量交换下，经Wick旋转至欧氏空间并做O(4) S波分波投影，将hBSE化为维度N=2^n的对称矩阵本征值问题；随后把哈密顿量分解为n量子比特泡利算符（Pauli operators）之和，采用MPS张量网络拟设（tensor-network ansatz）的VQE求解。

Result: 在N=16（n=4量子比特）时，VQE相对经典对角化（classical diagonalization）以优于1%的平均相对误差恢复最大本征值，该本征值编码了发生束缚所需的最小耦合常数。BSE振幅的纠缠分析显示在所测尺寸上纠缠度低且近似满足面积律（area-law-like），这为MPS拟设提供了依据。

Conclusion: 对三重独立障碍（泡利开销指数增长、近似尺寸无关的低纠缠、VQE梯度随规模下降）的批判性分析表明，所研究问题处于经典可处理区域，可被低键维张量网络良好描述并由MPS/Lanczos方法高效处理。该负面结果首次给出了量子比特编码下BSE振幅的纠缠量化，建立了泡利哈密顿量框架，并指出二维Minkowski空间BSE、N体束缚态与非梯核等可能产生真正量子优势的扩展方向。

Abstract: We present a gate-based quantum computing solution of the homogeneous Bethe-Salpeter equation (hBSE) for the bound state of two massive relativistic scalar particles interacting via ladder-approximation scalar exchange. After Wick rotation to Euclidean space and O(4) S-wave partial-wave projection, the hBSE is reduced to a symmetric matrix eigenvalue problem of dimension N = 2^n. We decompose the resulting Hamiltonian into a sum of n-qubit Pauli operators and solve it with the Variational Quantum Eigensolver (VQE) using a Matrix Product State (MPS) tensor-network ansatz. For N=16 (n=4 qubits), the VQE recovers the maximum eigenvalue - which encodes the minimum coupling constant for binding - to better than 1 % mean relative error compared to classical diagonalization. Entanglement analysis of the BSE amplitude shows low, area-law-like entanglement over the tested sizes, which motivates the MPS ansatz. A critical analysis of three independent barriers - exponential Pauli overhead, approximately size-independent low entanglement, and decreasing VQE gradient scales for the tested ansatz, consistent with generic barren-plateau concerns at larger n - reveals that the specific problem studied here lies in a classically tractable regime: over the tested range it is well described by low-bond-dimension tensor networks and efficiently handled by MPS/Lanczos methods. This negative result provides, to our knowledge, the first entanglement quantification of the BSE amplitude in qubit encoding, establishes the Pauli Hamiltonian framework for future BSE variants, and identifies 2D Minkowski-space BSE, N-body bound states, and non-ladder kernels as physically motivated extensions where genuine quantum advantage may become plausible.

</details>


### [128] [Non-Markovian Quantum Decay in Complex Environments: A Hyperstatistical Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22190)
*Nicola Fabiano*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文运用"超统计（hyperstatistics）"框架研究复杂环境中的量子衰变问题，通过假设局域衰减率服从γ分布，推导出由q指数函数（q-exponential function）支配的宏观存活概率，并借助q广义Gamma函数计算衰减时间分布的矩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准马尔可夫理论（Markovian theories，如费米黄金规则 Fermi's Golden Rule）假设环境无结构，而具有无序、长程相互作用或强涨落的复杂环境会导致局域衰减率涨落、非马尔可夫动力学（non-Markovian dynamics）及幂律长时尾（long-time tails），该问题尚待系统求解。

Method: 采用近期提出的超统计框架，设定介观区域（mesoscopic domains）内的局域衰减率服从γ分布（γ-distribution），据此导出宏观存活概率；并利用通过q指数函数的Mellin变换定义的q广义Gamma函数（q-generalized Gamma function）计算衰减时间分布的矩。

Result: 平均量子寿命在q<2时有限；二阶矩收敛需更严格的条件q<3/2。当1<q<3/2时平均寿命与方差均有限；当3/2≤q<2时平均寿命有限但寿命涨落无限宽；当q≥2时平均寿命本身发散。

Conclusion: 结果给出了将极端环境复杂性同安德森局域化（Anderson localization）及类Griffiths相（Griffiths-like phases）相联系的物理解释。

Abstract: The exponential decay of an unstable quantum state, as described by standard Markovian theories such as Fermi's Golden Rule, assumes a simple, structureless environment. However, in complex environments characterized by disorder, long-range interactions, or strong fluctuations, local decay rates fluctuate, leading to non-Markovian dynamics and power-law ``long-time tails.'' In this paper, we apply the recently proposed \textit{hyperstatistics} framework to solve the problem of quantum decay in such complex environments. By considering a $γ$-distribution of local decay rates across mesoscopic domains, we derive a macroscopic survival probability governed by a $q$-exponential function. We then use the $q$-generalized Gamma function, defined via the Mellin transform of the $q$-exponential, to calculate the moments of the decay-time distribution. We show that the mean quantum lifetime is finite for $q<2$. The convergence of the second moment instead requires the stricter condition $q<3/2$. For $1<q<3/2$ both the mean lifetime and its variance are finite, for $3/2\le q<2$ the mean lifetime remains finite but lifetime fluctuations become infinitely broad, and for $q\ge2$ the mean lifetime itself diverges. This result provides a physical interpretation linking extreme environmental complexity to Anderson localization and Griffiths-like phases.

</details>


### [129] [Efficient creation of shallow NV$^-$ ensembles by high-angle ion implantation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22937)
*Kento Sasaki,Hideyuki Watanabe,Tokuyuki Teraji,Takashi Taniguchi,Kenji Watanabe,Kensuke Kobayashi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过大角度（>60°）离子注入（ion implantation）可显著提升浅层负电氮-空位色心（NV$^-$ centers）的制备效率，产率接近10%且有效深度小于10 nm，并用于探测范德华材料（van der Waals materials）中的核自旋。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 位于金刚石表面下数纳米的浅层NV$^-$色心是纳米尺度外部自旋传感的关键量子缺陷，但高产率地制备浅层NV$^-$色心仍是材料难题。

Method: 以超过60°的高角度注入$^{15}$N离子，并分析近表面区域空位与氮的比例变化，结合退火（annealing）过程解释NV$^-$形成机制。

Result: 实现接近10%的高NV$^-$产率，有效深度低于10 nm，明显高于浅层NV$^-$制备的常见报道值；所制备NV$^-$系综（ensembles）的相干性（coherence）与同深度单个浅层NV$^-$色心相当，并可探测附着于金刚石表面的范德华材料中的核自旋。

Conclusion: 离子注入的几何控制（geometric control）是一种简单且具广泛适用性的途径，可用于工程化浅层空位相关量子缺陷（vacancy-related quantum defects）。

Abstract: Negatively charged nitrogen-vacancy (NV$^-$) centers located a few nanometers below the diamond surface are key quantum defects for nanoscale sensing of external spins. However, the creation of shallow NV$^-$ centers with high yield remains a materials challenge. Here, we demonstrate that high-angle ion implantation enhances the creation efficiency of shallow NV$^-$ centers. By implanting $^{15}$N ions at angles exceeding 60$^\circ$, we achieve high NV$^-$ yields approaching 10% with effective NV$^-$ depths below 10 nm. These yields are significantly higher than those typically reported for shallow NV$^-$ creation. The enhanced NV$^-$ yield is consistent with an increased vacancy-to-nitrogen ratio in the near-surface region, which is expected to promote NV formation during annealing. The created NV$^-$ ensembles show coherence properties comparable to those of single shallow NV$^-$ centers at similar depths, and allow detection of nuclear spins in van der Waals materials attached to the diamond surface. Our results establish geometric control of ion implantation as a simple and broadly applicable approach to engineer shallow vacancy-related quantum defects.

</details>


### [130] [Performance optimization of cascaded traveling wave Josephson parametric amplifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23126)
*Ilari Lilja,Ekaterina Mukhanova,Stanislav Khaldeev,Ilya Golokolenov,Visa Vesterinen,Pertti Hakonen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出并实现了一种级联行波参量放大器（cascaded TWPA）架构：以短的低损耗超导非线性非对称电感元件（SNAIL）TWPA 作为第一级，后接传统长 TWPA 提供额外增益，从而兼顾高增益与低噪声，在约 1 GHz 可调带宽内实现近 30 dB 总增益且附加噪声接近量子极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于约瑟夫森超材料（Josephson metamaterials）的行波参量放大器（TWPA）可提供宽带增益与近量子极限的附加噪声，但长非线性器件虽能实现高增益，却易受耗散、泵浦耗尽（pump depletion）与内部驻波影响而退化噪声性能；短阵列则损耗更小但增益有限。

Method: 采用级联 TWPA 架构：第一级为短（736 个元件）、低耗散的超导非线性非对称电感元件（SNAIL）基 TWPA，第二级为传统长（1632 个元件）TWPA 以提供额外增益。

Result: 该级联放大器在约 1 GHz 的可调带宽内实现近 30 dB 的总增益，同时附加噪声保持在接近量子极限的水平。

Conclusion: 结果表明，TWPA 级联是一种实现高增益、宽带量子放大的可行方案，可应用于量子信息处理（quantum information processing）、多模纠缠（multi-mode entanglement）以及微波频段的量子传感（quantum sensing）。

Abstract: Traveling-wave parametric amplifiers (TWPAs) based on Josephson metamaterials provide broadband gain with near-quantum-limited added noise. Whereas long nonlinear metamaterial devices can deliver high gain, short arrays suffer less from dissipation, pump depletion, and internal standing waves which can degrade noise performance. Here, we demonstrate a cascaded TWPA architecture that combines the advantages of both approaches by employing a short (736-element), low-dissipation Superconducting Nonlinear Asymmetric Inductive eLement (SNAIL)-based TWPA as the first amplification stage, followed by a conventional long (1632-element) TWPA that provides additional gain. The resulting amplifier cascade achieves nearly 30 dB of total gain over a tunable bandwidth of approximately 1 GHz while maintaining added noise close to the quantum limit. Our results establish a cascade of TWPAs as a practical approach for high-gain, broadband, quantum amplification with applications in quantum information processing, multi-mode entanglement, and quantum sensing applications at microwave frequencies.

</details>


### [131] [Feedback-Induced Dynamical Phases in a Self-Adaptive Quantum Kicked Rotor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23420)
*Pan Gao,Zheng-Wei Zhou,Guang-Can Guo,Xi-Wang Luo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于量子受击转子（quantum kicked rotor）的自适应 Floquet 系统（self-adaptive Floquet system），其中踢力强度由腔介导反馈（cavity-mediated feedback）自洽地成为动力学变量。该反馈机制统一刻画了对称与反对称两条失稳通道的竞争，并产生共振弹道输运、反共振以及非公度驱动下的周期倍增局域化等新相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规受击转子中踢力强度为外部固定参数，无法体现驱动与系统之间的自洽反馈。作者希望引入腔介导的动力学反馈，使踢力本身成为动力学变量，从而为非平衡 Floquet 相（nonequilibrium Floquet phases）提供统一组织原理。

Method: 构建基于量子受击转子的自适应 Floquet 系统，使踢力强度通过腔介导反馈自洽生成。超辐射转变（superradiant transition）产生腔介导踢力，并给出对称与反对称两条竞争失稳通道；分别在共振踢力与非公度踢力（incommensurate kicking）条件下分析其动力学行为。

Result: 共振踢力下，两通道竞争产生双踢动力学（double-kick dynamics），支持共振弹道输运（resonant ballistic transport），并出现周期四倍转子演化的涌现反共振。非公度踢力下，反对称失稳稳定出一个稳健的周期倍增局域化相（period-doubled localized phase），具有持续次谐波动力学，即常规受击转子所不具备的局域时间序（localized temporal order）。随反馈强度增大，关联时间涨落逐渐抑制量子干涉，导致由周期倍增局域化向不规则局域化、最终向亚扩散输运（subdiffusive transport）的跨接。

Conclusion: 结果建立了自适应量子混沌动力学（self-adaptive quantum-chaotic dynamics）的一般框架，表明动力学反馈可从根本上重塑驱动量子系统中的输运、局域化与时间序。

Abstract: We introduce a self-adaptive Floquet system based on a quantum kicked rotor, in which the kicking strength itself becomes a dynamical variable generated self-consistently through cavity-mediated feedback. A superradiant transition gives rise to cavity-mediated kicking and two competing instability channels, symmetric and antisymmetric, which provide a unified organizing principle for the nonequilibrium Floquet phases. For resonant kicking, their competition produces double-kick dynamics that support resonant ballistic transport and an emergent antiresonance with period-quadrupled rotor evolution, arising from a balance between the two instability channels. Remarkably, for incommensurate kicking, the antisymmetric instability stabilizes a robust period-doubled localized phase with persistent subharmonic dynamics despite the underlying incommensurate driving, revealing localized temporal order absent in conventional kicked rotors. As the feedback strength increases, correlated temporal fluctuations progressively suppress quantum interference, driving crossovers from period-doubled localization to irregular localization and eventually to subdiffusive transport. Our results establish a general framework for self-adaptive quantum-chaotic dynamics and demonstrate how dynamical feedback can fundamentally reshape transport, localization, and temporal order in driven quantum systems.

</details>


### [132] [Casimir force in a water nanolayer confined by graphene sheets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23609)
*Wijnand Broer,Jure Dobnikar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文理论上研究了以纳米薄层液态水（liquid water）分隔的两片石墨烯（graphene）之间的卡西米尔力（Casimir force），揭示纳米限域（nanoscale confinement）与界面效应（interface effect）对卡西米尔力的非单调修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卡西米尔力源于量子涨落与热涨落，强烈依赖相互作用材料的电磁极化率（electromagnetic susceptibility）。现有理解多基于体相（bulk）材料假设，而纳米限域下液态水与石墨烯界面处的力行为尚不明确，故需考察受限几何中的偏离程度。

Method: 采用理论计算，构建两片石墨烯被纳米薄层液态水分隔的体系，分别评估液态水层的纳米限域效应，以及石墨烯电子响应（electronic response）受纳米限域影响后对卡西米尔力的贡献，并与体相模型（bulk model）对比。

Result: 厚度不超过 3.4 nm 时，限域使卡西米尔力相对体相情形最多降低 26%；厚度更大时，因界面效应，体相模型反而最多低估 42%。该效应随厚度非单调变化：在 70 nm 处达到最大，直至 2 μm 才缓慢回落至体相极限。石墨烯电子响应的纳米限域效应对卡西米尔力无影响。

Conclusion: 纳米薄层液态水的限域与界面效应可显著且非单调地改变石墨烯间的卡西米尔力，体相模型在纳米至微米尺度均可能失效；相比之下，石墨烯电子响应的限域修正可忽略。

Abstract: Casimir forces, arising from quantum and thermal fluctuations, depend strongly on the electromagnetic susceptibilities of the interacting materials. Here, we theoretically investigate the Casimir force between two graphene sheets separated by a nanometer-thin layer of liquid water. We find that, at thicknesses up to 3.4 nm, the confinement reduces the Casimir force by up to 26 % compared to the bulk case. At larger thicknesses, the bulk model underestimates the Casimir force by up to 42 % due to the interface effect. This effect does not decrease monotonically as a function of the layer thickness: it increases up to a maximum at 70 nm and it slowly declines to the bulk limit only at 2 microns. We separately assess the effect of nanoscale confinement on the electronic response of graphene, which has no effect on the Casimir force.

</details>


### [133] [Non-Hermitian quantum phase transitions in the XY model induced by staggered imaginary Dzyaloshinskii--Moriya interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23721)
*Hong Jiang,Xiang-Ping Jiang,Yan-Chao Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了横向场 XY 链中由交错虚 Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya（DM）相互作用驱动的非厄米量子相变，并通过交错非酉变换实现精确对角化，进而提出可探测 RT 对称性破缺的新相干度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有量子信息探针（如纠缠熵、量子失协、量子相干）在刻画非厄米体系中的 RT 对称性破缺相变时失效，需要新的判据来可靠识别该类相变及其磁相边界。

Method: 采用交错非酉变换（staggered nonunitary transformation）对含交错虚 DM 相互作用的横向场 XY 链哈密顿量进行精确对角化；当 D<1 时映射为标准厄米 XY 链并解析推导相边界，同时分析 D=1 与 D>1 区域的准粒子本征值与本征矢量性质，并构造基于 RT 对称性与复共轭本征对关联的相干度量。

Result: D<1 时模型等价于厄米 XY 链，可解析给出全部相边界；D=1 构成具有准粒子本征值与本征矢量合并的例外边界（exceptional boundary）；D>1 全部落入 RT 对称性破缺相并包含两个不同的 z-铁磁相。常规探针无法捕捉 RT 对称性破缺，而所提度量 \widetilde{\mathrm{QC}}_{\max}^{\rm LR} 能可靠识别该相变并准确分辨所有磁相边界。

Conclusion: 基于左右本征态（left and right eigenstates）的内禀非厄米信息构建的相干度量 \widetilde{\mathrm{QC}}_{\max}^{\rm LR}，可作为识别 RT 对称性破缺相变与全部磁相边界的高效量子信息探针。

Abstract: We investigate non-Hermitian quantum phase transitions driven by staggered imaginary Dzyaloshinskii--Moriya (DM) interactions in a transverse-field $XY$ chain. By virtue of a staggered nonunitary transformation, we exactly diagonalize the Hamiltonian. For $D<1$, this procedure maps the non-Hermitian model onto a standard Hermitian $XY$ chain, allowing analytical derivation of the full phase boundaries. The parameter line $D=1$ forms an exceptional boundary with coalesced quasiparticle eigenvalues and eigenvectors, while the entire $D>1$ regime falls into the $\mathcal{RT}$-symmetry-broken phase containing two distinct $z$-ferromagnetic phases. We examine common quantum-information probes and show that conventional measures, such as entanglement entropy, quantum discord, and quantum coherence, fail to capture the $\mathcal{RT}$ symmetry-breaking transition. To address this deficiency, we propose a novel coherence measure $\widetilde{\mathrm{QC}}_{\max}^{\rm LR}$ based on $\mathcal{RT}$ symmetry and complex-conjugate eigenpair correlations. Embedding intrinsic non-Hermitian information of left and right eigenstates, $\widetilde{\mathrm{QC}}_{\max}^{\rm LR}$ reliably identifies the $\mathcal{RT}$ symmetry-breaking transition and accurately resolves all magnetic phase boundaries.

</details>


### [134] [Lee-Yang theorem for fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23942)
*Chaithanya Rayudu,Takahiro Misawa,Andrew Zhao,Jun Takahashi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作为一大类相互作用费米子模型（interacting fermion models）的配分函数（partition function）证明了 Lee-Yang 无零点定理（Lee-Yang zero-freeness theorem），并据此给出可证明高效的地基态能量估计量子算法，同时严格证明这些模型在非零局域外场下不存在相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lee-Yang 定理是研究多体系统（many-body systems）的强力工具，已应用于相变分析与经典及量子算法效率的证明。作者希望将这一工具推广至相互作用费米子体系，以同时获得算法效率保证与相变判据。

Method: 作者针对一大类相互作用费米子模型的配分函数证明 Lee-Yang 无零点定理（即配分函数在相关复参数域内无零点）。该模型类涵盖吸引型 Hubbard 模型（attractive Hubbard model）、二分图上的排斥型 Hubbard 模型（repulsive Hubbard model on bipartite graphs）以及相互作用 Hofstadter 模型（interacting Hofstadter model）。证明仅基于解析与零点结构分析，摘要未给出具体技术细节。

Result: 由无零点定理推出一个可证明高效的量子算法（quantum algorithm），用于估计该模型类的地基态能量（ground-state energies）；同时严格证明在存在非零局域外场（nonzero local external field）时，这些模型中不存在相变（phase transitions）。

Conclusion: Lee-Yang 无零点框架可推广至广泛的相互作用费米子模型，兼具算法与物理两方面的推论：既提供高效基态能量估计量子算法，又给出局域外场下无相变的严格结论。摘要未讨论该结果的适用边界与潜在限制。

Abstract: Lee-Yang theorems are a powerful tool for studying many-body systems, with applications ranging from analyzing phase transitions to proving the efficiency of certain classical and quantum algorithms. In this work, we prove a Lee-Yang zero-freeness theorem for the partition function of a broad class of interacting fermion models, implying the existence of a provably efficient quantum algorithm for estimating their ground-state energies. This class includes several well-known models such as the attractive Hubbard model, repulsive Hubbard model on bipartite graphs, and the interacting Hofstadter model. Our results also rigorously establish the nonexistence of phase transitions in these models in the presence of a nonzero local external field.

</details>


### [135] [Collective advantage from a minimal record in a quantum information engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24558)
*Kangqiao Liu,Jie Gu,Deyou Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在量子信息引擎（quantum information engine）中，让恶魔（demon）仅测量最左粒子的位置序统计量（order statistic）而非全部粒子位置，可获得与粒子数无关的能量上界，并使集体引擎每记录 nat 的功比独立单粒子引擎高 44%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多粒子体系下，若恶魔（demon）分辨每个粒子的位置，其记录熵（record entropy）会随粒子数增长，然而这些额外区分并不改变势垒（barrier）放置位置，因此存在测量冗余。研究旨在确认仅测量决定势垒位置的最左粒子序统计量（order statistic）能否提供更优的信息—能量转换效率。

Method: 通过让恶魔仅测量最左粒子的位置序统计量来设定势垒，并给出该粗粒化测量（coarse measurement）所供给能量的粒子数无关上界；据此推导记录熵（record entropy）随填充（filling）增大而下降的上限，并与逐粒分辨及独立单粒子引擎进行比较；同时评估粗测量遗留的相干性（coherence）与势垒放置不精确的代价。

Result: 粗粒化测量（coarse measurement）提供的能量具有独立于粒子数的上界；该上界给出随填充增加而下降的记录熵（record entropy）上限。相同倾斜（tilt）下，集体引擎（collective engine）每记录 nat 的输出功比各自优化循环时间、每粒子功率相当或更高的独立单粒子引擎多 44%；当势垒上方可达区域被填满后，泡利阻塞（Pauli blocking）终止该优势。

Conclusion: 仅依赖决定势垒位置的单一位序统计量（order statistic）的粗粒化测量（coarse measurement），可在多粒子量子信息引擎中实现粒子数无关的能量收益与更高的信息效率；该优势受泡利阻塞（Pauli blocking）限制，并需权衡测量遗留的相干性（coherence）与势垒放置精度。

Abstract: For a single particle, a quantum information engine can turn measurement fluctuations into transport by raising a barrier behind the particle each time its position is measured. When many particles are present, the barrier still depends on just one number, the position of the leftmost particle, so we let the demon measure that order statistic and nothing else. A demon that resolves every position instead pays a record entropy that grows with particle number, even though the extra distinctions never change where the barrier is placed. We show that the coarser measurement supplies an energy that is bounded independently of particle number, and that this bound yields a ceiling on the record entropy that falls as the filling increases. At the same tilt, the collective engine then delivers $44\%$ more work per recorded nat than independent single-particle engines, each with its own optimized cycle time and each running at equal or greater power per particle. Pauli blocking ends this advantage once the accessible region just above the wall fills. We also evaluate the coherence that the coarse measurement leaves behind, and the cost of placing the barrier imprecisely.

</details>


### [136] [Superconducting qubit based on altermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24759)
*Xue-Feng Pan,Xin-Lei Hei,Franco Nori,Peng-Bo Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作利用超导体-交替磁体-超导体（superconductor-altermagnet-superconductor）结中的约瑟夫森效应（Josephson effect），通过器件设计实现可编程电流-相位关系（current-phase relation），并据此提出一类结合大非谐性（anharmonicity）与抗退相干能力的新型 transmon 类超导量子比特。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交替磁体（altermagnets）具有净磁化为零与动量依赖自旋劈裂（momentum-dependent spin splitting）的特性，被视为下一代约瑟夫森器件的潜在平台。作者旨在探索如何借助交替磁性实现约瑟夫森势（Josephson potential）的工程化调控，进而提升超导量子比特的性能。

Method: 基于超导体-交替磁体-超导体结的约瑟夫森效应，通过器件设计工程化预设的电流-相位关系，构建可利用交替磁性编程的约瑟夫森势；并在此基础上设计 transmon 类超导量子比特，分析其 $2φ$ 结区特性、电荷噪声（charge noise）与磁通噪声（flux noise）响应，以及利用磁通进行调控的方案。

Result: 所提出的量子比特同时具备大非谐性与通过相干双库珀对隧穿（coherent two-Cooper-pair tunneling）增强的抗退相干能力；在 $2φ$ 结区，凭借宇称保护（parity protection）获得对电荷噪声的固有防护；磁通可精确调控量子比特，并在适当偏置下进一步抑制电荷噪声与磁通噪声。

Conclusion: 结果确立了交替磁体作为约瑟夫森势工程通用平台的地位，并为兼具高相干性、大非谐性与宽可调性的高性能超导量子比特开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Altermagnets, characterized by vanishing net magnetization and momentum-dependent spin splitting, provide a promising platform for next-generation Josephson devices. Here, we exploit the Josephson effect in superconductor-altermagnet-superconductor junctions and show how to engineer prescribed current-phase relations by device design. Based on these programmable Josephson potentials utilizing altermagnetism, we propose a new class of transmon-like superconducting qubits that combine large anharmonicity with enhanced robustness against decoherence via coherent two-Cooper-pair tunneling. We show that in the $2φ$-junction regime, this kind of qubit provides intrinsic protection against charge noise due to parity protection. Magnetic flux can be used to precisely control the qubit and, under appropriate bias, this architecture further suppresses charge and flux noise. Our results establish altermagnets as a versatile platform for Josephson-potential engineering and open a new route toward high-performance superconducting qubits combining high coherence, large anharmonicity, and broad tunability.

</details>


### [137] [Overcoming the Hong-Ou-Mandel interference limitation for the second photon from a two-photon cascade](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24858)
*Raphael Joos,Michal Vyvlecka,Tim Strobel,Benjamin Breiholz,Furkan Aglarci,Ponraj Vijayan,Hans-Georg Babin,Arne Ludwig,Michael Jetter,Simone L. Portalupi,Peter Michler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本工作针对级联三能级系统（three-level ladder system）中双光子间时间关联（temporal correlation）对双光子干涉（two-photon interference）的限制，证明该限制在第二跃迁辐射光子情形下仅源于干涉分束器处的时域重叠度下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体量子点（semiconductor quantum dots）等量子发射器可提供源自级联态（cascaded states）的纠缠光子对，但三能级系统中的时间关联被指会限制可达到的双光子干涉，需澄清该限制的物理来源。

Method: 采用理论分析与实验相结合的方式，通过测量两个先后发射的光子对之间的时间分辨四光子符合（time-resolved four-photon coincidences）来检验该限制机制。

Result: 结果表明，第二跃迁所发射光子的干涉限制仅由干涉分束器处时域重叠度衰减所致；发射后同步（post-emission synchronization）可使干涉可见度（interference visibility）恢复至最大值。

Conclusion: 该限制并非不可克服，可通过后同步手段恢复最大干涉可见度；在未来量子网络（quantum networks）中，此过程可借助确定性量子存储器（deterministic quantum memories）实现。

Abstract: Quantum emitters such as semiconductor quantum dots can provide entangled photon pairs emitting from a system of cascaded states. However, the resulting temporal correlation between the two photons from the three-level ladder system has been shown to set a limit on the achievable two-photon interference. In this work, we show that such limitation, for photons emitted by the second transition, is only due to reduced temporal overlap at the interference beam splitter. We investigate this theoretically and experimentally by determining time-resolved four-photon coincidences between two successively emitted photon pairs. Post-emission synchronization shows the recovery of maximum interference visibility; in prospective quantum networks this could be achieved via deterministic quantum memories.

</details>

### [138] [Boundary-condition identification from a scale-invariant frequency ratio in shear-deformable and nonlocal beams and plates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22567)
*Akın Oktav*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出利用结构前两阶固有频率之比（frequency ratio $r=\omega_2/\omega_1$）仅取决于边界条件、而与尺寸、密度及绝对刚度无关的特性，通过两次比值测量反推小尺度梁/板的边界条件，无需知道确切尺寸或材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小尺度梁或板的尺寸与材料参数往往难以准确获知，但其边界条件对结构行为至关重要。由于频率比值可使尺寸、密度与绝对刚度在商中相互抵消，因此存在从比值测量识别边界条件的可能。

Method: 建立比值 $r$ 及其在边界条件改变时的分数偏移量 $\Delta r$ 对小尺度两类效应（横向剪切变形，经 Timoshenko 梁与 Mindlin 板理论；小尺度弹性，经 Eringen 非局部 Timoshenko 模型）的响应关系，并采用无锁定（locking-free）Rayleigh-Ritz 梁求解器与有限元 Mindlin 板求解器，均经基准验证。

Result: 发现不对称性：绝对比值对厚度（剪切可变形范围内 12%–14%）与正交异性（orthotropy，6%–9%）较敏感，对非局部性（nonlocality）仅弱敏感；而偏移量 $\Delta r$ 在完整非局部范围内变化小于 0.2%，在厚度变化下仅变化百分之几。方法并推广至用两个弹簧连续体估计支座柔度（support compliance），并明确指出端部支承情形的非唯一性。

Conclusion: 因 $\Delta r$ 基本不依赖厚度与材料细节，实测偏移量可识别支承变更所对应的边界条件转变，其分辨率经量化并与分子动力学（molecular dynamics）数据验证。该方法无需小尺度参数或材料常数的取值。

Abstract: The ratio of a structure's first two natural frequencies depends on its boundary conditions but not on its size, material density, or absolute stiffness, which multiply every frequency equally and cancel in the quotient. We use this to identify the boundary conditions of a small-scale beam or plate from two ratio measurements, without knowing its exact dimensions or material. The paper first establishes how the ratio $r=ω_2/ω_1$, and the fractional shift $Δr$ it undergoes when a boundary condition changes, respond to the two effects that matter at small scale: transverse shear deformation, through Timoshenko beam and Mindlin plate theory, and small-scale elasticity, through Eringen's nonlocal Timoshenko model. Using a locking-free Rayleigh-Ritz beam solver and a finite-element Mindlin plate solver, each checked against benchmarks, we find an asymmetry. The absolute ratio moves appreciably with thickness, by 12 to 14 per cent over the shear-deformable range, and with orthotropy, by 6 to 9 per cent, but only weakly with nonlocality; the shift $Δr$, by contrast, varies by less than 0.2 per cent over the full nonlocal range and by a few per cent under thickness change. Because $Δr$ is largely independent of these details, a measured shift identifies which boundary-condition transition a deliberate change of support has produced, with a resolution we quantify and verify against molecular-dynamics data. Two spring continua then extend the method to estimating support compliance, with the non-uniqueness of the tip-support case stated explicitly. The method needs no value of the small-scale parameter or the material constants.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.soft'></div>

# cond-mat.soft [[Back]](#toc)

### [139] [Existence and Uniqueness of Nearest Stealthy Hyperuniform Configurations from Random Initial Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22365)
*Fausto Martelli*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该论文为二维周期性区域中隐身超均匀（stealthy hyperuniform）构型建立了严格的几何唯一性理论，并用梯度型生成网络验证了随机构型可被驱动至无序超均匀态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐身超均匀态的生成通常依赖数值优化，缺乏关于其投影唯一性与残余自由度（floppy modes）的严格几何刻画。作者希望在"受约束模比例 χ=m/(dN) 使系统保持无序"的参数区间内，为这一构型流形提供理论基础，并说明如何从一般随机构型到达该流形。

Method: 理论上：对二维周期方域内 N 个粒子的均匀随机构型，在约束 m 个低 k 傅里叶模（Fourier modes）精确为零时，证明其最近隐身超均匀构型在软模位移（floppy-mode displacements）意义下唯一；在显式满秩雅可比（full-rank Jacobian）假设下，分析垂直于有限模超均匀流形切空间的分量唯一性，以及切空间残余自由度对应的一阶软模。计算上：构建基于梯度的生成器网络（gradient-based generator network），迭代位移粒子以最小化"超均匀性 + 短程排斥 + 平滑性"的复合损失泛函。

Result: 在满秩雅可比假设下，正交分量唯一确定，残余切空间自由度为一阶软模，可保持受约束的低 k 密度涨落不变。生成网络成功将一般随机构型推向无序超均匀代表，逐步抑制长波密度涨落；结构因子（structure factor）与数方差指数（number-variance exponent）显示抑制效应出现于远超显式约束模的倒空间窗口，且受结构因子求和规则（sum rule）界定。局部键取向分析与布拉格峰（Bragg peaks）缺失证实所得态保持无序。

Conclusion: 该研究将解析几何论证与生成式计算相结合，为理解隐身超均匀性提供了严格的几何基础，并给出了从一般随机构型生成其无序代表态的可操作途径。

Abstract: We prove that, for almost every uniformly random configuration of $N$ particles in a two-dimensional periodic square domain, there exists a unique nearest stealthy hyperuniform configuration, up to floppy-mode displacements, when a finite set of $m$ low-$k$ Fourier modes is constrained to vanish exactly. Under an explicit full-rank Jacobian assumption, the displacement component orthogonal to the tangent space of the finite-mode hyperuniform manifold is uniquely determined, while residual tangent-space degrees of freedom correspond to first-order floppy modes that preserve the constrained low-$k$ density fluctuations. We restrict attention to the regime of constrained-mode fraction $χ=m/(dN)$ in which such configurations are known to remain disordered rather than crystallize. To corroborate this framework, we implement a gradient-based generator network that iteratively displaces particles to minimize a combined loss functional of hyperuniformity, short-range repulsion, and smoothness, and whose dynamics is expected to approximate the theory's minimal orthogonal projection onto the hyperuniform manifold. The network drives generic random configurations toward disordered hyperuniform representatives, progressively suppressing long-wavelength density fluctuations as measured by the structure factor and number-variance exponent\textcolor{black}{, over a reciprocal-space window that extends well beyond the explicitly constrained modes and is bounded, as we show, by the structure-factor sum rule. Local bond-orientational analysis and the absence of Bragg peaks confirm that the resulting states remain disordered}. This integrated analytical and computational approach provides a rigorous geometric foundation for understanding stealthy hyperuniformity and for generating its disordered representatives from generic random configurations.

</details>


### [140] [On the ratio between longitudinal and transverse Ioffe-Regel frequencies in glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22386)
*Licun Fu,Mingyu Zhu,Xinyu Chang,Lijin Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该工作通过二维与三维模型玻璃（model glasses）系统，揭示了纵向与横向 Ioffe-Regel（IR）频率比值与低声波衰减系数比值之间的关系，并发现该比值由玻璃稳定性（glass stability）强烈调控：稳定性越高，纵横 IR 频率比越小，在足够稳定的玻璃中趋近于 1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻璃中热导率的温度依赖性与晶体显著不同，且主要由声波的衰减方式决定；IR 频率界定了良定义声波的高频上限。横向 IR 频率已被确认与玻色峰（boson peak）频率一致并远低于纵向 IR 频率，但纵、横 IR 频率之间的定量关系仍属未知。

Method: 对具有显著不同稳定性的二维（2D）与三维（3D）模型玻璃进行考察，分析其纵向与横向 IR 频率之比，并将其与低频率下横向与纵向声衰减系数之比进行对照。

Result: 在 3D 玻璃中，纵向与横向 IR 频率之比近似等于低频横向与纵向声衰减系数之比；而在 2D 玻璃中未观察到这一对应关系。此外，玻璃稳定性在 2D 与 3D 玻璃中均强烈控制该频率比：比值随稳定性提高而显著下降，并在所研究的最稳定玻璃中趋近于 1。

Conclusion: 在足够稳定的玻璃中，纵向声衰减应与横向声衰减相当，因而不可视为可忽略，这对理论研究中常采用的假设构成挑战。该工作再次表明玻璃稳定性是研究玻璃性质的关键参数，并提示不应将退火不足玻璃（poorly annealed glasses）的观察结果简单外推至稳定玻璃。

Abstract: The temperature dependence of thermal conductivity in glasses differs characteristically from that in crystals, and is largely controlled by how sound waves are damped. The Ioffe-Regel (IR) frequency sets the high-frequency limit of well-defined sound waves. Although the transverse IR frequency has been established to coincide with the boson peak frequency and reported to be far below the longitudinal IR frequency, the quantitative relation between longitudinal and transverse IR frequencies has remained unknown. Here we examine this relation in two- (2D) and three-dimensional (3D) model glasses with vastly different stability. We observe that, in 3D glasses, the longitudinal-to-transverse IR frequency ratio is approximately equal to the ratio of the low-frequency transverse to longitudinal sound attenuation coefficient, whereas this correspondence is not observed in 2D glasses. Moreover, we find that the glass stability strongly controls the longitudinal-to-transverse IR frequency ratio in both 2D and 3D glasses: the ratio decreases significantly with increasing glass stability and approaches unity for the most stable glasses under study. Accordingly, in sufficiently stable glasses, the longitudinal sound attenuation should be comparable to the transverse attenuation, and therefore cannot be treated as negligible, challenging common assumptions adopted especially in theoretical studies. Our work is another demonstration of the glass stability as a key parameter for investigating glass properties, and cautions against simply extrapolating observations from poorly annealed glasses to stable glasses.

</details>


### [141] [Acoustic dip in the effective temperature of hot Brownian motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22515)
*Mayank Srivastava,Dipanjan Chakraborty*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文研究非均匀加热溶剂中热布朗粒子（hot Brownian particle）的涨落-耗散关系（fluctuation-dissipation relation），指出在动力学区（kinetic regime）有限声速会定性改变频率依赖的有效温度：当声波波长与粒子半径相当时，有效噪声温度出现显著“声学凹陷（acoustic dip）”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有理论假设溶剂不可压缩（incompressible），忽略了有限声速的影响，因此需要检验可压缩性对热布朗运动中有效温度的作用。

Method: 求解受热球体的可压缩涨落流体力学（compressible fluctuating-hydrodynamic）问题，分析声波波长与粒子半径可比时的有效噪声温度行为。

Result: 有效噪声温度出现明显的声学凹陷；其位置由 aω/c=O(1) 决定，深度由无量纲黏性-声衰减长度（viscous-acoustic attenuation length）a_c/a 控制。机制为弱衰减纵模在被耗散前将机械能从受热表面带走，使耗散被温度场中较冷区域加权。

Conclusion: 结果揭示热布朗运动中的一个声学窗口（acoustic window），在该窗口内可压缩性降低而非升高动力学噪声温度。

Abstract: A hot Brownian particle is driven by thermal fluctuations from a
  nonuniformly heated solvent and therefore obeys a
  fluctuation-dissipation relation with a frequency-dependent
  effective temperature. Existing theories assume an incompressible
  solvent. We show that finite sound speed qualitatively changes this
  effective temperature in the kinetic regime. Solving the
  compressible fluctuating-hydrodynamic problem for a heated sphere,
  we find that the effective noise temperature develops a pronounced
  acoustic dip when the sound wavelength becomes comparable to the
  particle radius. The dip occurs because weakly attenuated
  longitudinal modes carry mechanical energy away from the heated
  surface before it is dissipated, so that the dissipation is weighted
  by colder regions of the temperature field. Its position is set by
  $aω/c=\mathcal{O}(1)$, while its depth is controlled by the
  dimensionless viscous-acoustic attenuation length $a_c/a$. The
  result identifies an acoustic window in hot Brownian motion in which
  compressibility lowers, rather than raises, the kinetic noise
  temperature.

</details>


### [142] [Two-Stage Ordering Kinetics in Binary Mixtures of Ellipsoidal Particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22533)
*Parameshwaran A,Sanjay Puri,Bhaskar Sen Gupta*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 采用大规模分子动力学（molecular dynamics）模拟，研究各向异性 Gay-Berne 势下单轴椭球粒子二元混合物的非平衡相序动力学（nonequilibrium phase-ordering kinetics），揭示取向序与组分序在两个时间尺度上依次发生的分离机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 各向异性粒子体系中的相序动力学涉及取向与组分两类序参量的耦合，其守恒律与演化规律尚需统一刻画。该研究旨在厘清椭球粒子混合物在淬火后取向序与组分相分离过程的时间尺度关系及其动力学标度。

Method: 对单轴椭球粒子二元混合物（相互作用为各向异性 Gay-Berne 势）进行大规模分子动力学模拟，从各向同性且均匀相（isotropic and homogeneous phase）淬火（temperature quench）至共存区；随后分析取向序与组分序的域粗化（domain coarsening）、动力学标度（dynamical scaling）、Porod 定律行为及生长律。

Result: 系统呈现两个不同时间尺度的有序化过程：先发生快速取向有序化，形成并粗化向列相（nematic）域，表现出动力学标度、广义 Porod 定律行为以及与非守恒序参量动力学一致的生长律 ℓ(t) ∼ t^{1/2}；随后由组分涨落驱动二元相分离，生长律从扩散型 ℓ(t) ∼ t^{1/3} 过渡到流体力学（hydrodynamic）区域，有限尺寸标度分析（finite-size scaling）表明渐近黏性生长为 ℓ(t) ∼ t。尽管存在全局取向序，相分离动力学仍保持有效各向同性。

Conclusion: 结果明确了取向有序化与组份有序化动力学之间的清晰分离，二者由不同守恒律支配，并为理解各向异性粒子体系中的相序行为提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We investigate the nonequilibrium phase-ordering kinetics of a binary mixture of uniaxial ellipsoidal particles interacting via the anisotropic Gay-Berne potential using large-scale molecular dynamics simulations. Following a temperature quench from the isotropic and homogeneous phase into the coexistence region, the system exhibits two distinct ordering processes occurring on different time scales. At first, rapid orientational ordering leads to the formation and coarsening of nematic domains, characterized by dynamical scaling, generalized Porod-law behavior, and a growth law $\ell(t) \sim t^{1/2}$ consistent with nonconserved order-parameter dynamics. Later, the system undergoes binary phase separation driven by compositional fluctuations. This compositional ordering process shows a transition from diffusive growth $\ell(t) \sim t^{1/3}$ to hydrodynamic regimes, with finite-size scaling analysis indicating asymptotic viscous growth $\ell(t) \sim t$. Despite the presence of global orientational order, the phase-separation kinetics remains effectively isotropic. These results demonstrate a clear separation between orientational and compositional ordering dynamics, governed by distinct conservation laws, and provide a unified framework for understanding phase ordering in anisotropic particle systems.

</details>


### [143] [Apparent Breakdown of the Stress-Optic Rule in Rigid-Rod Suspensions: A Stress-Partitioning Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23080)
*William Kai Alexander Worby,Yuto Yokoyama,Misa Kawaguchi,Yoshiyuki Tagawa*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究以纤维素纳米晶悬浮液为刚性棒模型体系，结合流变光学测量、Fokker-Planck取向模型与应力分解，揭示应力-光学规则的表观成立源于应力分配而非唯一材料系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 各向异性大分子材料领域的一个基本问题在于，流动诱导的取向有序（orientational order）如何将微观结构同宏观流变应力与光学各向异性联系起来。采用纤维素纳米晶悬浮液作刚性棒模型体系，旨在将取向效应与链拉伸效应隔离。

Method: 同步开展双折射（birefringence）、取向角与剪切应力的流变光学（rheo-optical）测量，并结合 Fokker-Planck 取向模型与应力分解（stress decomposition）进行分析。

Result: 在低佩克莱特数（Peclet number）下，双折射与总应力呈类似应力-光学规则（stress-optic rule）的比例关系；但该比例随浓度变化，表明其反映应力分配（stress partitioning）而非唯一材料系数。随流动强度增大，取向饱和伴随相对布朗应力（Brownian stress）贡献下降，总应力关系相应改变。将光学响应参照估算的布朗应力贡献后，不同浓度与流动条件下的响应显著趋于统一。

Conclusion: 结果为表观应力-光学规则失效提供了应力分配解释，支持将光学响应与布朗应力贡献相关联的解读。

Abstract: A fundamental problem in the field of anisotropic macromolecular materials concerns how the flow-induced orientational order connects microscopic structure to macroscopic rheological stress and optical anisotropy. This study considered cellulose nanocrystal suspensions as rigid-rod model systems for the purpose of isolating orientational effects from chain stretching. Simultaneous rheo-optical measurements of birefringence, orientation angle, and shear stress were combined with a Fokker-Planck orientation model and stress decomposition. Our results show that, at low values of the Peclet number, birefringence and total stress exhibit proportionality similar to the stress-optic rule. However, the concentration dependence indicates that this proportionality reflects stress partitioning rather than a unique material coefficient. With increasing flow strength, orientational saturation is accompanied by a reduced relative Brownian stress contribution and a corresponding change in the total stress-based relation. Referencing the optical response to an estimated Brownian stress contribution yields a substantially more unified response across concentrations and flow conditions, supporting a stress-partitioning interpretation of apparent stress-optic rule breakdown.

</details>


### [144] [An active soft glassy rheology model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23168)
*Raffaele Mendozza,Tobias Müller,Peter Sollich*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文在软玻璃态流变学（Soft Glassy Rheology, SGR）框架中引入介观尺度的持续随机应变率作为活动性，发现活动性开启长时松弛通道，使低频流变从小子幂律行为（power-law rheology）转变为麦克斯韦型行为（Maxwell-like behavior），并刻画了其流化时间尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物材料（如细胞骨架与汇合细胞单层）是受内部应力与应变持续作用的活性稠密体系，其流变表征至关重要；然而软物质中的活动性可跨多个长度尺度建模，其力学后果高度依赖模型，尚无统一理论框架。

Method: 采用软玻璃态流变学（SGR）模型研究稠密活性非晶材料，在局部元素的介观尺度上引入活动性，将其表示为在某一时间尺度 τ_p 上持续的随机应变率；结合极限区间的解析论证与数值模拟进行表征。

Result: 活动性开启了一条长时松弛通道，导致从 SGR 型幂律流变向最低频下麦克斯韦型行为的转变；由此表征了流化时间尺度及其对活动性参数的依赖，并与别处引入的有效温度（effective temperatures）表现出强类比性。

Conclusion: 所提出的活性 SGR 模型为理解活动性驱动如何改变稠密非晶材料的流变学提供了一条最小化的介观路径。

Abstract: Biological materials such as the cytoskeleton and confluent cell monolayers are active, dense systems continuously subjected to internal stresses and strains, making their rheological characterization essential. While activity in soft matter can be modeled across multiple length scales, its mechanical consequences remain strongly model dependent and no unified theoretical framework has yet emerged. Here, we study the rheology of dense active amorphous materials using the Soft Glassy Rheology (SGR) model, incorporating activity at the mesoscopic scale of local elements as a stochastic strain rate that is persistent on some timescale $τ_p$. We show that activity opens a long-time relaxation channel, driving a crossover from SGR-like power-law rheology to Maxwell-like behavior at the lowest frequencies. Combining analytical arguments in limiting regimes with numerical simulations, we characterize the resulting fluidisation time scale and its dependence on the activity parameters, which shows strong analogies with effective temperatures introduced elsewhere that similarly encode activity-induced fluidisation. Our active SGR model provides a minimal mesoscopic route to understanding how driving by activity modifies the rheology of dense amorphous materials.

</details>


### [145] [Tumor-induced lipid loss physically accelerates breast cancer invasion into mammary adipose tissue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23229)
*Garrett F. Beeghly,Dong Wang,Bo Ri Seo,Yitong Zheng,Joseph E. Druso,Benjamin D. Hopkins,Linda T. Vahdat,Neil M. Iyengar,Mark D. Shattuck,Corey S. O'Hern,Claudia Fischbach*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究结合小鼠模型、人乳腺切除样本与离散元方法（discrete element method, DEM）模拟，揭示肥胖通过改变白色脂肪组织（white adipose tissue, WAT）物理性质（脂肪细胞增大及非球形度升高）影响乳腺癌侵袭。模拟表明组织压力在较大脂质丢失速率下加速癌症侵袭，提示脂质丢失物理性重塑WAT并促进侵袭，肥胖可能加剧该效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肥胖是乳腺癌的重要风险因素，但肥胖相关的白色脂肪组织（WAT）物理性质变化如何影响肿瘤侵袭仍不清楚。研究旨在探究肥胖诱导的细胞与组织性质改变对乳腺癌侵袭的作用机制。

Method: 结合实验与计算方法：利用小鼠模型及人乳腺切除样本观察脂肪细胞大小与非球形度（asphericity）变化；在体外与体内检测肿瘤条件培养基及肿瘤诱导的脂肪细胞脂质丢失与去分化；并构建离散元方法（DEM）模拟，将脂肪细胞建模为可变形多边形与多面体，癌细胞建模为二维软黏附圆盘与三维球体。

Result: 肥胖不仅增大脂肪细胞尺寸，也提高其非球形度；肿瘤条件培养基驱动脂肪细胞脂质丢失并向肌成纤维细胞样细胞（myofibroblast-like cells）去分化，高脂饮食小鼠脂肪细胞响应肿瘤时脂质丢失更快。DEM模拟显示肥胖相关组织压力升高引起脂肪细胞变形，与实验观察到的非球形度升高一致；在小脂质丢失速率下侵袭受组织压力影响较弱，但在足够大的脂质丢失速率下组织压力加速癌症侵袭。

Conclusion: 脂质丢失通过物理方式重塑白色脂肪组织（WAT）从而促进乳腺癌侵袭，肥胖可能加剧这一效应。研究提示肥胖相关组织物理性质变化是肿瘤侵袭的重要调控因素。

Abstract: Obesity is a major risk factor for breast cancer, yet how obesity-associated changes in the physical properties of white adipose tissue (WAT) influence tumor invasion remains poorly understood. Here, we combine experimental and computational approaches to investigate this question. Using mouse models and human mastectomy samples, we show that obesity not only increases adipocyte size, but also asphericity. We observe that tumor-conditioned media drives lipid loss and dedifferentiation of adipocytes into myofibroblast-like cells in vitro and that adipocytes in high-fat diet-fed mice lose lipid at a faster rate than those in normal diet-fed mice in response to mammary tumors in vivo. We then develop discrete element method (DEM) simulations to understand how these obesity-induced changes in cell and tissue properties alter breast cancer invasion under high-fat and normal diet conditions. In DEM simulations, adipocytes are modeled as deformable polygons and polyhedra, and cancer cells are modeled as soft, adhesive disks and spheres in two and three dimensions, respectively. Invasion is driven by cancer cell proliferation and tumor-induced lipid loss is modeled as corresponding decreases in adipocyte size. DEM simulations indicate that obesity-associated increases in tissue pressure induce adipocyte deformation, consistent with elevated asphericity in experimental data. At small lipid loss rates, the degree of cancer invasion is only weakly affected by tissue pressure. However, at sufficiently large lipid loss rates, we find that tissue pressure accelerates cancer invasion. Together, these results suggest that lipid loss physically remodels WAT to facilitate breast cancer invasion, and that obesity may exacerbate this effect.

</details>


### [146] [Uni-Macro-FRPN: Full-Resolution and Cross-Scale Learning for Polymers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23611)
*Jintao Wu,Yiran Shan,Rui Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 提出全分辨率聚合物网络 FRPN（Full-Resolution Polymer Network），在统一框架内同时保留原子级、单体级特征与显式聚合物结构信息，在嵌段共聚物分类与拓扑丰富基准上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合物性质源于跨尺度相互作用，但受计算约束（聚合物常含数万原子）限制，现有模型或保留精细单体化学结构而缺乏显式聚合物图，或保留连接性而简化单体表示，二者难以兼顾。

Method: FRPN 采用两个 Transformer（Transformer）联合学习由 BigSMILES 派生表示中的原子信息单体语义（monomer semantics）、序列顺序与链拓扑（chain topology）。

Result: 在嵌段共聚物数据库（Block Copolymer Database, BCDB）层状/非层状分类任务上达到 86.4% 准确率与 90.6% ROC-AUC，为当前最优；消融显示增益并非仅由参数量增加解释。在线性均聚物基准上，以单体为中心的学习仍具竞争力，提示聚合物尺度组织简单时的边界情形。另构建含 1640 个数据点、覆盖多种单体化学、序列顺序、链拓扑与物理性质的全原子分子动力学（all-atom molecular dynamics）基准，FRPN 总体表现最强，诊断结果支持联合建模单体化学与聚合物结构的收益。

Conclusion: FRPN 为聚合物表示学习超越以单体为中心的表示（monomer-centric representation）提供了可行路径；未来聚合物预测模型应将聚合物视为完整的多尺度化学与拓扑对象，而非仅单体描述符的集合。

Abstract: Polymer properties emerge from interactions across scales, yet existing polymer models typically preserve either detailed monomer chemistry without an explicit polymer graph or polymer connectivity with simplified monomer representations, due to computational constraints, as polymers typically contain tens of thousands of atoms. We present Uni-Macro-FRPN (FRPN), a Full-Resolution Polymer Network that retains both detailed atom-level and monomer-level features and explicit polymer structure information within a unified framework. Two Transformers jointly learn atom-informed monomer semantics, sequence order, and chain topology from BigSMILES-derived representations. On the Block Copolymer Database (BCDB) lamellar-versus-non-lamellar classification task, FRPN achieves 86.4% accuracy and 90.6% ROC-AUC, establishing state-of-the-art performance. Ablation results indicate that the gain is not explained solely by increased parameter count. On a linear homopolymer benchmark, monomer-centric learning remains competitive, highlighting a boundary case where polymer-scale organization is simple. To test generalization beyond linear polymers, we further construct an all-atom molecular-dynamics benchmark of 1640 datapoints spanning diverse monomer chemistries, sequence orderings, chain topologies, and physical properties. FRPN achieves the strongest overall performance on this topology-rich benchmark, with diagnostics supporting the benefit of jointly modeling monomer chemistry and polymer structure. Taken together, FRPN provides a practical route for moving polymer representation learning beyond monomer-centric representations. The leading performance of FRPN also suggests a promising direction for polymer informatics: future polymer prediction models should treat polymers not only as collections of monomer descriptors, but as complete multiscale chemical and topological objects.

</details>


### [147] [Molecular dynamics simulations of DMSO-MeOH liquid mixtures. Effects of force fields on mixing properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23620)
*M. Cruz-Sánchez,O. Pizio*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 采用分子动力学（molecular dynamics）模拟，考察液态二甲基亚砜（DMSO）-甲醇（MeOH）混合物的组成依赖性，涵盖密度、超额混合体积与焓、自扩散、剪切黏度、介电常数、表面张力及微观氢键结构，并对多种力场组合与实验数据进行对比评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究 DMSO-甲醇二元体系在不同组成下的宏观性质与微观结构变化规律，并检验现有非极化半柔性分子模型（non-polarizable semi-flexible models）组合对该体系的适用性与准确性。

Method: 使用分子动力学计算机模拟（molecular dynamics computer simulations）；采用一组 DMSO 非极化半柔性模型与甲醇模型的组合；分析径向分布函数（radial distribution functions）及平均氢键（hydrogen-bonded）分子数等微观结构量。

Result: 评估了密度、超额混合体积（excess mixing volume）与超额混合焓（excess mixing enthalpy）的组成趋势，并研究了物种自扩散（self-diffusion）、剪切黏度（shear viscosity）、静态介电常数（static dielectric constant）与表面张力（surface tension）的组成依赖性。

Conclusion: 通过与实验数据的比较，说明并批判性评估了若干模型组合的表现质量，为后续模型选择与改进提供依据。

Abstract: We explore composition dependence of the principal properties of liquid dimethylsulfoxide (DMSO)-methanol (MeOH) liquid mixtures by using molecular dynamics computer simulations. A set of non-polarizable semi-flexible models for the DMSO molecule combined with methanol models is investigated. Composition trends of density, excess mixing volume and excess mixing enthalpy are evaluated. Besides, we study the composition dependence of self-diffusion of species and shear viscosity ofthe static dielectric constant and the surface tension.Certain aspects of the microscopic structure are analyzedin terms of radial distribution functions and of the average number of hydrogen-bonded molecules. The quality of several combinations of the models is illustrated and critically evaluated by comparisons with experimental data.

</details>


### [148] [The effect of homopolymer adding on aggregation behavior in amphiphilic triblock copolymers containing rigid blocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23626)
*X. -G. Han,H. Zhang,Z. H. Sun,M. Y. Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文采用格子自洽场理论（lattice self-consistent field theory）研究均聚物B的加入对两亲性coil/rod/coil BAB三嵌段共聚物聚集行为的影响，发现该效应取决于疏水棒状嵌段长度与共聚物浓度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目的在于揭示刚性（rigid）棒状嵌段核胶束的生长机理，并厘清均聚物添加对两亲性coil/rod/coil三嵌段共聚物聚集行为的调控作用。

Method: 采用格子自洽场理论（lattice self-consistent field theory），系统考察均聚物B添加量、疏水棒状嵌段长度与共聚物浓度对coil/rod/coil BAB三嵌段共聚物聚集行为的影响。

Result: 在低浓度下，均聚物添加有利于立方相与大片层状胶束（lamellar micelles）出现，虽丰富了结构行为但抑制了重排（rearrangement）；随棒状嵌段增长，重排与取向依赖扩散（orientation-dependent diffusion）及逆扩散过程相关。在较高浓度下，均聚物添加促进重排，长棒嵌段体系出现与逆层化（inverse laminarization）及协同生长相关的排列。在高浓度下，短棒嵌段体系中均聚物添加有利于与有序/无序转变（order/disorder transition）相关的胶束重排，而中等长度与长棒嵌段体系的重排受到抑制。

Conclusion: 均聚物添加对聚集行为的影响高度依赖棒状嵌段长度与浓度条件；该工作有助于理解以刚性嵌段为核的胶束的生长机理。

Abstract: The effect of B homopolymer adding on the aggregation behavior of amphiphilic coil/rod/coil BAB triblock copolymers was studied using lattice self-consistent field theory. It depends on the length of the hydrophobic rod block and copolymer concentration. Compared with the solutions, at low concentrations, homopolymer addition is favorable to the emergence of cubic and large lamellar micelles. Although it enriches the structural behavior, the rearrangement is suppressed. As rod blocks increase, the rearrangement is related to the orientation-dependent diffusion and inverse diffusion processes. At relatively high concentrations, adding homopolymers promotes rearrangement. For long rod block case, the arrangements related to inverse laminarization and the cooperative growth emerges. At high concentrations, for short rod block system, homopolymer addition is advantageous to micelle rearrangement concerned with order/disorder transition, and the rearrangements in the system of intermediate length and long rod blocks are suppressed. This work aids in understanding of the growth mechanism of micelles with rigid block cores.

</details>


### [149] [Competition between uniform ferromagnetism and finite-wave-vector instabilities in square-lattice skyrmion crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23647)
*S. Hayami*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究通过退火模拟，在具有动量依赖磁性相互作用（momentum-dependent magnetic interactions）的中心对称自旋模型（centrosymmetric spin model）中，确定了方格子（square-lattice）斯格明子晶体（skyrmion crystal）的低温磁相图，并阐明均匀铁磁相互作用（uniform ferromagnetic interaction）对其稳定性的调控机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中心对称磁体（centrosymmetric magnets）中方格子斯格明子的形成机制尚需澄清，本文旨在考察动量依赖相互作用与均匀铁磁相互作用对斯格明子晶体相稳定性的影响。

Method: 构建包含均匀铁磁相互作用、两个正交有限波矢处的易轴各向异性相互作用（easy-axis anisotropic interactions）、高次谐波波矢相互作用（higher-harmonic wave-vector interactions）及外磁场的中心对称自旋模型，并采用模拟退火（simulated annealing）计算低温磁相图。

Result: 当均匀铁磁相互作用较弱时，斯格明子晶体相在中间磁场区间稳定，表现为非共面双-$Q$自旋结构（noncoplanar double-$Q$ spin texture）、有限标量自旋手性（scalar spin chirality）及高次谐波分量；增强该相互作用会通过一阶相变（first-order transitions）直接抑制乃至消除该相，减弱则扩大其稳定区域并在高场稳定单-$Q$锥形螺旋相（single-$Q$ conical spiral phase）。

Conclusion: 结果澄清了中心对称磁体中方格子斯格明子形成的调控机制，表明均匀铁磁相互作用的强弱是决定斯格明子晶体稳定性的关键因素。

Abstract: We investigate square-lattice skyrmion crystal states in a centrosymmetric spin model with momentum-dependent magnetic interactions, including a uniform ferromagnetic interaction, easy-axis anisotropic interactions at two orthogonal finite wave vectors, higher-harmonic wave-vector interactions, and an external magnetic field. Using simulated annealing calculations, we determine the low-temperature magnetic phase diagram as functions of magnetic field and uniform ferromagnetic interaction strength. We find that the skyrmion crystal phase is stabilized at intermediate fields when the uniform ferromagnetic interaction is weak, exhibiting a noncoplanar double-$Q$ spin texture, finite scalar spin chirality, and higher-harmonic components. Increasing the uniform ferromagnetic interaction suppresses and eventually eliminates the skyrmion crystal phase through direct first-order transitions to the fully polarized state, whereas its weakening expands the skyrmion stability region and stabilizes a single-$Q$ conical spiral phase at high fields. These results clarify the mechanisms governing the square-lattice skyrmion formation in centrosymmetric magnets.

</details>


### [150] [Light absorption and scattering of an assembly of spheroidal metal nanoparticles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23649)
*N. I. Pavlyshche,A. V. Korotun,V. P. Kurbatsky*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该论文研究由金属长椭球与扁椭球纳米颗粒（nanospheroids）构成的集合体的光学特性，计算了平均吸收与散射截面（absorption and scattering cross-sections）以及吸收、散射和反射系数的频率依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注金属椭球纳米颗粒集合体的光学响应，动机在于比较长椭球（prolate）与扁椭球（oblate）两种几何形态对光学特性的影响，并考察不同金属材料的作用。

Method: 采用计算方式，对不同金属的椭球颗粒集合体求解平均吸收与散射截面以及吸收、散射、反射系数的频率依赖曲线，并针对长椭球与扁椭球两类形状分别计算。

Result: 长椭球与扁椭球集合体在吸收与散射截面的频率特性上存在差异，表现为极值（maxima）位置与数值不同；吸收与散射系数的频率依赖呈定性相似（qualitative similarity）。不同金属的椭球集合体频率曲线在可见光至紫外（ultraviolet）波段内极值位置差异显著。

Conclusion: 椭球颗粒形状（长椭球或扁椭球）与金属种类均显著影响集合体光学频率特性的极值分布，其中金属种类的影响主要体现在可见光到紫外区的极值位置上。

Abstract: The paper studies the optical properties of assemblies of metallic prolate and oblate nanospheroids. The frequency dependences of averaged absorption and scattering cross-sections, absorption, scattering and reflection coefficients are obtained. The calculations were performed for assemblies of spheroidal particles of various metals. The difference in the results of calculating the frequency characteristics of the absorption and scattering cross-sections for assemblies of prolate and oblate spheroids manifested itself in different location and value of the maxima. A qualitative similarity of the frequency dependences of the absorption and scattering coefficients was established. It is shown that the frequency curves of assemblies of spheroids of different metals differ significantly in the location of the extrema within the range from the visible to the ultraviolet region of the spectrum

</details>


### [151] [Phase diagram morphology shapes droplet propulsion in chemical gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24529)
*Stefan Köstler,Malcolm Steen,David Zwicker*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究基于薄界面近似（thin-interface approximation）推导出液滴在成分梯度中的速度解析式，证明即使没有表面活性剂（surfactant），液滴亦可产生马兰戈尼型（Marangoni）自推进，且运动方向指向相图中稳定性较低的区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂环境中的液滴常因成分梯度（compositional gradient）而推进，已有认识多归因于表面活性剂梯度诱导的表面张力驱动。本文意在探究此类推进是否在无表面活性剂条件下仍具普遍性。

Method: 采用薄界面近似（thin-interface approximation），将液滴速度表示为液滴尺寸、粘度（viscosity）、表面张力对调控组分的敏感度以及该组分化学势梯度（chemical potential gradient）的紧凑表达式；并结合数值模拟（numerical simulations）验证理论结果。

Result: 理论给出液滴速度的紧凑解析表达式，并揭示液滴朝稳定性较低区域运动，该方向由相图（phase diagram）形貌编码，在临界点（critical point）附近尤为显著；数值模拟证实上述结论。

Conclusion: 液滴在成分梯度中的推进是一种普遍现象，可由复杂流体（complex fluids）的相行为（phase behavior）预测与设计液滴运动性（droplet motility），为无需表面活性剂的驱动机制提供一般性路径。

Abstract: Droplets in complex environments often encounter compositional gradients that drive their propulsion. For example, surfactant gradients induce surface-tension-driven propulsion via the Marangoni effect. Here, we show that such propulsion emerges generically, even in the absence of surfactants. Employing a thin-interface approximation, we derive a compact expression for the droplet velocity in terms of droplet size, viscosity, the sensitivity of surface tension to a regulating component, and the chemical potential gradient of that component. Our theory reveals that droplets move toward regions of lower stability, as encoded in the morphology of the phase diagram, particularly near critical points. Numerical simulations confirm these results, which establish a general route to predicting and designing droplet motility from the phase behavior of complex fluids.

</details>


### [152] [Nucleosome simulations suggest mechanisms of electrostatically-driven mesoscale chromatin evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24907)
*Siddhartha G. Jena*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究通过挖掘蛋白质数据库，为跨越15亿年进化的3241个物种构建了硅学核小体，结合静电计算与粗粒化分子动力学模拟，揭示核小体单元在生物物理层面的广泛多样化，并证明其导致染色质宏观相行为的显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核小体由组蛋白结合并压缩DNA，驱动染色质聚合物的介观尺度组织；尽管组蛋白在进化中已多样化，但其对染色质结构相应多样化的贡献尚不清楚。

Method: 从蛋白质数据库挖掘组蛋白序列，为3241个物种构建in silico核小体，采用静电计算（electrostatic calculations）与粗粒化分子动力学模拟（coarse-grained molecular dynamics simulations）相结合的方法，并对进化与生物物理上分化的核小体子集开展粗粒化寡核小体模拟。

Result: 揭示核小体单元存在广泛的生物物理多样化；在进化与生物物理分化的核小体子集上进行的寡核小体模拟显示，染色质整体相行为（bulk phase behavior）出现显著差异。

Conclusion: 组蛋白可能通过进化促进特定类型的介观尺度染色质行为，提示一种新的范式。

Abstract: Nucleosomes are structures made up of proteins called histones that bind and compact DNA, driving the mesoscale organization of the chromatin polymer. Although histone proteins have diversified over evolutionary time, their contributions to the corresponding diversification of chromatin structure are poorly understood. Here, we mine protein databases for histones and create \emph{in silico} nucleosomes for 3241 organisms spanning $>$1.5B years of evolution. Using a combination of electrostatic calculations and coarse-grained molecular dynamics simulations, we reveal extensive biophysical diversification of the nucleosome unit. Finally, we perform coarse-grained oligonucleosomal simulations on a subset of evolutionarily and biophysically divergent nucleosomes, demonstrating dramatic differences in bulk phase behavior of chromatin. Taken together, our results suggest a paradigm in which histones may have evolved to facilitate particular types of mesoscale chromatin behavior.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [153] [AutoRASOR: Autonomous Rapid Scanning Electron Microscope Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22411)
*Kevin Zhang,Mohammad Taha,Rafael Espinosa Castañeda,Yutong Liu,Yin Zhu,Lotan Portal,Robert A. Mcleod,Vahid Attari,Jane Y. Howe,Jason Hattrick-Simpers*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: AutoRASOR 是一个任务无关（task-agnostic）的自主扫描电子显微镜（SEM）管线，利用视觉基础模型（VFM）DINOv3 实时嵌入显微图像，并通过 LFPS 与形态模糊度主动学习两种策略选取感兴趣区域，在无需领域预训练或先验材料假设下实现未知样品的多尺度形态调查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自驱动实验室（SDL）实现大批量样品合成，需要同样高通量的自主表征能力。现有自动化电镜管线均为任务特定（task-specific），只能检测预定义特征、优化已知属性或依赖样品先验知识，导致每个管线仅限于其原设计所针对的材料体系。

Method: AutoRASOR 在实时条件下以视觉基础模型 DINOv3 对显微图像进行嵌入，并采用两种互补的感兴趣区域（ROI）选取策略：潜在最远点采样（Latent Farthest Point Sampling, LFPS）与基于形态模糊度（morphological ambiguity，即给定低倍形貌下未解析细尺度特征的条件方差）的主动学习。方法无需领域特定预训练、微调或人工提示。

Result: 在真实 SEM 显微图像与合成相场（phase-field）图像上测试，基于模糊度的主动学习相较随机 ROI 选取能持续捕获更多样、更稀有的形态；LFPS 则能在有限采集预算内可靠地恢复样品的形态分布。

Conclusion: 该工作将自主表征从预定义、任务特定的特征定位转向形态调查（morphological survey），可生成信息丰富的多尺度数据集以支持多种下游任务。由于无需领域特定预训练或材料先验假设，该框架可开箱即用地部署于表征由 SDL 制备的新材料。

Abstract: Scanning Electron Microscopy (SEM) is a foundational technique for characterizing material microstructure, which dictates many fundamental physical and chemical properties.
