With the rise of Self-Driving Labs (SDLs), samples are now synthesized in large batches, demanding equally high-throughput, autonomous characterization. Existing automated electron microscopy pipelines are task-specific: they detect pre-defined features, optimize known properties, or require prior knowledge of the sample, restricting each pipeline to the material system it was built for. We introduce AutoRASOR, a task-agnostic autonomous SEM pipeline that captures the multi-scale morphology of an unknown sample without domain-specific pre-training, fine-tuning, or human prompting. AutoRASOR embeds micrographs in real time with a vision foundation model (VFM), DINOv3, and selects regions of interest (ROIs) through two complementary policies: Latent Farthest Point Sampling (LFPS) and active learning on morphological ambiguity, which is the conditional variance of unresolved fine-scale features given lower magnification appearance. Active learning on ambiguity consistently captures more diverse and rare morphologies than random ROI selection, while LFPS reliably recovers the specimen's morphological distribution within a limited capture budget, tested both on real SEM micrographs and synthetic phase-field images. By shifting autonomous characterization from pre-defined, task-specific targeting of features to morphological survey, AutoRASOR generates information-rich multi-scale datasets for diverse downstream tasks. Without the need for domain-specific pre-training or prior material assumptions, this framework can be deployed out-of-the-box to characterize novel materials produced by SDLs.

</details>


<div id='nucl-th'></div>

# nucl-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [Flow plus coalescence explain hadron nuclear modification and high-$p_T$ anisotropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24762)
*Wilke van der Schee,Isobel Kolbé*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 该论文针对相对论 pPb 碰撞中核修正因子（nuclear modification factor）接近 1 与高横动量（high-$p_T$）椭圆流（elliptic flow）显著为正这一长期矛盾，提出硬部分子（hard parton）与受推挤的热介质部分子（thermal medium parton）发生聚并（coalescence）的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来存在一个困惑：pPb 碰撞的核修正因子与 1 一致（暗示能量损失为零），而高 $p_T$ 椭圆流却显著为正（传统解释为与路径长度相关的能量损失）。二者在传统框架下相互矛盾。

Method: 论文提出，即使在高 $p_T$，硬部分子也会与一个受推挤的热介质部分子发生聚并，后者的能量与依赖角度的径向流（radial flow）相关联。作者对比了 pp、pPb、pO、OO 和 PbPb 等碰撞系统中的该流动。

Result: 分析表明 pPb 中的流动较大且具有各向异性；集体介质将硬部分子向外推挤，且该效应在 pPb 中比在 pp 中更强、在平面内（in-plane）比在平面外（out-of-plane）更强。该机制在定性上与实验一致。

Conclusion: 该机制可定性解释实验现象，并预言喷注（jets）的椭圆流信号将显著小于强子（hadrons）的椭圆流信号。

Abstract: It has long been a puzzle why the nuclear modification factor in relativistic $p$Pb collisions is consistent with unity, while the high-$p_T$ elliptic flow is significantly positive. The latter is traditionally interpreted as path-length-dependent energy loss, whereas the former is consistent with zero energy loss. In this Letter, we propose that even at high $p_T$ a hard parton coalesces with a boosted thermal medium parton whose energy is correlated with the angle-dependent radial flow. We compare this flow for $pp$, $p$Pb, $p$O, OO and PbPb collision systems and conclude in particular that the $p$Pb flow is large and anisotropic. The collective medium pushes out the hard partons, and more so in $p$Pb, or in-plane, than in $pp$ collisions, or out-of-plane. The mechanism qualitatively agrees with experiment and predicts a significantly smaller elliptic flow signal for jets than for hadrons.

</details>


### [155] [Accessing zero-point fluctuations in high-energy nuclear collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24977)
*Jean-Paul Blaizot,Jean-Yves Ollitrault*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 该文将 LHC 上 $^{208}$Pb 超中心碰撞（ultracentral collisions）中每粒子动量涨落的测量，与铅核半径零点涨落（zero-point fluctuations）的幅度相联系，发现数据提取值符合核结构计算预言，但被经典 Glauber 型模型高估，并强调泡利原理（Pauli principle）对涨落的压制作用及其带来的 $A$ 依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超中心重离子碰撞对碰撞瞬间核子冻结构型（frozen configuration）的尺寸与形状高度敏感，该信息编码于碰撞核的基态波函数（ground state wave functions）中。近期已从 LHC 铅核碰撞的逐事件每粒子动量涨落中推断出碰撞体系尺寸的相对标准偏差，因而有必要将这一可观测量与核半径零点涨落的理论幅度建立联系。

Method: 将实验测得的碰撞体系尺寸相对标准偏差与铅核半径零点涨落的幅度进行关联比对，并与核结构计算（nuclear-structure calculations）的预言及经典 Glauber 型（Glauber-type）高能碰撞模型的结果进行比较；同时分析泡利原理（Pauli principle）在压制涨落中所扮演的角色。

Result: 从数据中提取的数值与核结构计算的预言相符，但被经典 Glauber 型建模所高估。泡利原理对涨落具有压制作用，并由此导致一种特征性的质量数 $A$ 依赖关系。

Conclusion: 超中心碰撞的逐事件涨落可作为探测核基态零点涨落的精密手段。泡利原理导致的特征性 $A$ 依赖，可通过更小规模碰撞体系的实验加以检验。

Abstract: Ultracentral high-energy collisions between identical nuclei are very sensitive to the size and shape of the frozen configuration of nucleons at the instant of the collision, an information that is encoded in the ground state wave functions of the colliding nuclei. The relative standard deviation of the size of the colliding system soon after the collision has recently been inferred from event-by-event fluctuations of the momentum per particle in collisions of $^{208}$Pb nuclei at the LHC, and since a single collision event produces thousands of particles, this information is very precise. We connect this measurement to the magnitude of the zero-point fluctuations of the radius of the lead nucleus. The value that is extracted from the data is compatible with predictions from nuclear-structure calculations, but is overestimated by classical Glauber-type modeling of high-energy collisions. The role of the Pauli principle in suppressing the fluctuations is underlined. It leads to a distinctive $A$ dependence that could be checked in collisions of smaller systems.

</details>


### [156] [Spin-One Secondary Pairing in Two-Flavor Color Superconductivity: Spinful Relativistic Superfluidity and Anomaly Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24472)
*Naoki Yamamoto*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 本文研究双味色超导（2SC）相中的次级配对（secondary pairing）：未成隙夸克在瞬子诱导吸引相互作用下形成同手征 $J^P=1^+$ 凝聚。工作确定其对称性实现、准粒子结构与反常匹配（anomaly matching），并给出低能有效理论；该态为带节点费米子的自旋性相对论超流，且其节点费米子由反常匹配所要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在2SC相中仍有残余未成隙夸克，其可能的次级配对及其对称性、低能自由度与反常实现尚未被系统刻画。研究动机在于弄清这种额外凝聚如何重组对称性破缺模式与反常匹配，并寻找带节点费米子的自旋性相对论超流在稠密QCD中的具体实现。

Method: 采用有效场论与对称性分析路径：考虑瞬子诱导的吸引相互作用所驱动的同手征 $J^P=1^+$ 凝聚，依次确定其对称性实现、准粒子结构、反常匹配，并构造对应的低能有效理论。分析中包含Berry项（Berry term）、Mermin-Ho关系及Nambu-Goldstone模式计数。

Result: 配对能隙必然为节点型（nodal）；复轴矢态（complex axial state）具有两个点节点（point nodes），实现自旋性相对论超流。该态保持完整手征对称性 ${\rm SU}(2)_{\rm L}\times{\rm SU}(2)_{\rm R}$，同时破缺修正重子数对称性 ${\rm U}(1)_{\tilde{\rm B}}$ 与空间旋转；绕节点轴的旋转与凝聚相位锁定，由此产生Berry项、Mermin-Ho关系，以及除超流声子外的B型取向Nambu-Goldstone模式。反常匹配被重组：微扰混合反常 ${\rm SU}(2)_{\rm L,R}^2{\rm U}(1)_{\tilde{\rm B}}$ 由超流声子的Wess-Zumino耦合实现，而 ${\rm SU}(2)_{\rm L}$ 与 ${\rm SU}(2)_{\rm R}$ 的Witten反常由点节点Bogoliubov-de Gennes味二重态承载。

Conclusion: 所得理论为稠密QCD提供了一个具体的自旋性相对论超流实现，其中节点费米子是反常匹配所必需的。该框架将次级配对的对称性破缺、拓扑/取向自由度与反常实现统一描述。

Abstract: We study secondary pairing in the two-flavor color-superconducting (2SC) phase, where the residual ungapped quarks form a same-chirality $J^P=1^+$ condensate driven by an attractive instanton-induced interaction. We determine its symmetry realization, quasiparticle structure, anomaly matching, and low-energy effective theory. The pairing gap is necessarily nodal; in particular, the complex axial state has two point nodes and realizes a spinful relativistic superfluid. This state preserves the full chiral symmetry ${\rm SU}(2)_{\rm L}\times{\rm SU}(2)_{\rm R}$ while breaking the modified baryon-number symmetry ${\rm U}(1)_{\tilde{\rm B}}$ and spatial rotations, with rotations about the nodal axis locked to the condensate phase. This locking gives rise to a Berry term, the Mermin-Ho relation, and a type-B orientational Nambu-Goldstone mode in addition to the superfluid phonon. We also show how anomaly matching is reorganized by secondary pairing: the perturbative mixed ${\rm SU}(2)_{\rm L,R}^2{\rm U}(1)_{\tilde{\rm B}}$ anomaly is realized by a Wess-Zumino coupling of the superfluid phonon, whereas the ${\rm SU}(2)_{\rm L}$ and ${\rm SU}(2)_{\rm R}$ Witten anomalies are carried by the point-node Bogoliubov-de Gennes flavor doublets. The resulting theory provides a concrete dense-QCD realization of a spinful relativistic superfluid with nodal fermions required by anomaly matching.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.dis-nn'></div>

# cond-mat.dis-nn [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [Testing Two-State Access in Brain and Language Model: Human EEG Reproduction, Simulation Audit, and a Proposed Model Assay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22300)
*Pieter van Rooyen*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文以全局神经工作空间理论（global neuronal workspace theory）的一项预测——接近阈值时单试次反应分布为两状态混合而非连续谱——为切入点，考察人工系统组织能否支持意识。已完成两项分析：复现既有竞争模型检验，以及在模拟中校准一个拟用于语言模型表征的留出族比较。拟议的模型研究已设计但尚未运行，全文不涉及任何关于主观经验的断言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大脑与人工神经网络硬件同为普通物质，人工系统的组织形式能否支持意识仍属未决问题。本文不直接判定意识，而是选取“容量受限的全局工作空间（capacity-limited global workspace）”这一被提出的机制，通过该理论的一个可检验预测来推进讨论。

Method: 第一项分析基于二十名被试的公开脑电（EEG）数据，使用原作者代码复现已发表的竞争模型检验；第二项分析在模拟中于单一层上校准一个留出族比较（held-out family comparison），该比较拟将检验迁移至语言模型表征。拟议的模型研究包含自然文本证据剂量、目标桥接与状态条件因果检验，但尚未运行。

Result: 复现结果显示，在主动会话中两状态模型的保护性超越概率（protected exceedance probability）首次超过 0.95 的窗口同样位于 315 ms，预测优势约 0.003 nat/试次，且区间边界对优化器敏感；被动会话中另一分析下分级比较器（graded comparator）排名最高，且无跨窗口判定。校准结果显示，该族比较在十二种分级零假设下的 12,000 个数据集中未出现虚假两状态判定，但其概念簇区间在六种设定下覆盖不足，最低降至 0.22。

Conclusion: 两状态预测在主动会话中获得部分复现支持，但效应量小且结果对分析选择敏感；语言模型迁移检验的区间估计需替换并验证后方可用于确证性研究。本文仅评估机制的预测表现，不对意识或经验作任何主张。

Abstract: Brains and the hardware of artificial neural networks are both organized ordinary matter, and whether an artificial system's organization can support consciousness remains open. This paper examines one proposed mechanism, access to a capacity-limited global workspace, through one prediction of the global neuronal workspace theory: near threshold, the single-trial response distribution is a mixture of two states rather than a continuum. Two completed analyses are reported. First, the published competing-model test of that prediction is reproduced from the authors' code on the open EEG data of twenty participants. In the active session the two-state model's protected exceedance probability first exceeds 0.95 at the same 315 ms window, with a modest predictive advantage of about 0.003 nat per trial and optimizer-sensitive interval edges; in the passive session, an additional analysis, a graded comparator ranks highest without an across-window decision. Second, a held-out family comparison proposed for transferring the test to language-model representations is calibrated in simulation at one layer: it made no false two-state call in 12,000 datasets under twelve graded nulls, but its concept-cluster interval under-covered in six settings, down to 0.22, so a replacement interval must be validated before any confirmatory use. The proposed model study, with a natural-text evidence dose, a target bridge and a state-conditional causal test, is specified but not run. No claim about experience is made.

</details>


### [158] [Towards the spin-glass transition in finite dimensions via blue percolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22301)
*Yan Ru Pei*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该论文在高维零场 Edwards-Anderson 自旋玻璃（spin glass）的 Chayes-Machta-Redner 表示中证明了蓝色渗流（blue percolation）。首个渗流起始点（first percolation onset）在周期联合极限下渐近为 $(2dm)^{-1}$，并给出 $\pm1$ 耦合的 22 维计算机辅助证明，以及指数矩无序下渗流区间的存在性。密度不平衡问题仍开放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于刻画高维零场 Edwards-Anderson 自旋玻璃的渗流结构，以连接渗流相变与自旋玻璃相变（如 Gibbs 态与压力奇异性）。摘要指出，持续的密度不平衡将蕴含不同的自旋翻转相关 Gibbs 态及压力尖点（pressure cusp）。

Method: 方法采用 Chayes-Machta-Redner 表示框架下的蓝色渗流分析，针对固定对称独立同分布耦合（$0<m=\mathbb{E}|J|<\infty$）研究周期联合极限；对对称 $\pm1$ 耦合给出计算机辅助的 22 维验证；对指数矩无序建立渗流区间并与自旋玻璃磁化率（susceptibility）结合讨论。

Result: 首个渗流起始点在周期联合极限下渐近为 $(2dm)^{-1}$。对对称 $\pm1$ 耦合给出 22 维计算机辅助证明；对指数矩无序，建立了具有完全相等的无限蓝区密度（infinite-blue-sector densities）且自旋玻璃磁化率有限的渗流区间。

Conclusion: 在这些周期极限下，若存在持续密度不平衡，则意味着不同的自旋翻转相关 Gibbs 态以及适当有限局部复制耦合（replica coupling）下的压力尖点，且无需有限蓝抵消（finite-blue cancellation）。证明不平衡（imbalance）仍为开放问题。

Abstract: We prove blue percolation in the Chayes-Machta-Redner representation of the zero-field Edwards-Anderson spin glass in high finite dimensions. For fixed symmetric iid couplings with $0<m=\mathbb{E}|J|<\infty$, the first percolation onset is asymptotic to $(2dm)^{-1}$ in periodic joint limits. For symmetric $\pm1$ couplings, we give a computer-assisted dimension-22 certificate. For exponential-moment disorder, we also establish a percolating regime with exactly equal infinite-blue-sector densities and finite spin-glass susceptibility. In these periodic limits, persistent density imbalance would imply distinct spin-flip-related Gibbs states and a pressure cusp for a suitable finite local replica coupling, without requiring finite-blue cancellation. Proving imbalance remains open.

</details>


### [159] [A rank-two spherical transform for eigenvector and singular-vector overlaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22334)
*Pierre Bousseyroux*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 定义球面 $\mathcal{H}$-变换（spherical $\mathcal{H}$-transform）的二阶秩扩展 $\mathcal{H}^{(2)}$ 及其伴随变换 $\mathcal{R}^{(2)}$，并用于计算两厄米化预解式乘积的平均值，从而得到非对角本征向量重叠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有单秩 $\mathcal{H}$-变换框架仅处理单预解式量，无法直接刻画两个（厄米化）预解式乘积的平均行为，因此需要引入秩二阶扩展以获得非对角本征向量重叠等二阶谱信息。

Method: 定义球面 $\mathcal{H}$-变换的秩二阶扩展 $\mathcal{H}^{(2)}$ 及伴随变换 $\mathcal{R}^{(2)}$；两变换对求和均具可加性；由 $\mathcal{R}^{(2)}$ 导出两厄米化预解式（hermitized resolvents）平均乘积的 $4\times4$ 公式；并推广至相关矩阵对。

Result: $\mathcal{R}^{(2)}$ 给出两厄米化预解式平均乘积的 $4\times4$ 公式，据此得到非对角本征向量重叠，并重现 Ginibre、椭圆（elliptic）与双不变（bi-invariant）公式；对相关矩阵对，亦得到其本征向量或奇异向量（singular vectors）之间的重叠。

Conclusion: 秩二阶变换扩展为二阶谱统计提供统一工具，可覆盖多类随机矩阵系综的本征向量重叠并推广至相关矩阵对情形。

Abstract: We define a rank-two extension $\mathcal{H}^{(2)}$ of the spherical $\mathcal{H}$-transform, together with its associated transform $\mathcal{R}^{(2)}$. For sums, both transforms are additive. The transform $\mathcal{R}^{(2)}$ yields a $4\times4$ formula for the average product of two hermitized resolvents, and hence for off-diagonal eigenvector overlaps, recovering the Ginibre, elliptic, and bi-invariant formulas. We also treat correlated pairs of matrices, and obtain overlaps between their eigenvectors or singular vectors.

</details>


### [160] [Gradient-estimator design overcomes trainability barriers in neural-network-based variational optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22342)
*Yi-Ran Xue,Rui Wang,Baigeng Wang,Chenan Wei*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该工作提出无偏直接梯度估计器（unbiased direct gradient estimator）与自适应最小方差相位估计器（adaptive minimum-variance phase, AMVP），以提升弱梯度（weak-gradient）条件下的信噪比，从而在量子多体物理与从头算量子化学（ab initio quantum chemistry）中实现可靠的神经网络变分优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络为科学计算提供强表达力，但即便网络表达力充分，在弱梯度情形下仍会出现训练失败，制约其在量子多体物理与从头算量子化学中的实际应用。

Method: 推导无偏直接梯度估计器，并引入自适应最小方差相位（AMVP）估计器用于神经网络变分优化；核心机制是改善弱梯度的信噪比（signal-to-noise ratio）。

Result: 在关联通量模型（correlated flux models）上，紧凑网络以低一个数量级以上的 GPU 时间超越更大且经微调的默认标准估计器模型，并最终超过密度矩阵重整化群（density matrix renormalization group, DMRG）精度；在 N$_2$ 键断裂中达到化学精度（chemical accuracy），并首次在含显式自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）的重元素 I$_2$ 中达到化学精度。

Conclusion: 梯度估计器设计可拓展神经网络变分方法的能力，支持精确科学计算；在原本训练失败的任务上实现可靠计算并显著降低计算成本。

Abstract: Neural networks provide expressive representations for scientific computing. However, even sufficiently expressive networks can suffer training failure in weak-gradient regimes, limiting their practical use in quantum many-body physics and ab initio quantum chemistry. Here we derive an unbiased direct gradient estimator and introduce the adaptive minimum-variance phase (AMVP) estimator for neural-network variational optimization. By improving the signal-to-noise ratio of weak gradients, these methods enable reliable scientific calculations where training previously failed, while substantially reducing computational cost. The framework enables compact networks to outperform larger and fine-tuned default standard-estimator models with over an order of magnitude less GPU time on correlated flux models, and ultimately exceed the density matrix renormalization group (DMRG) accuracy. It further achieves chemical accuracy in N$_2$ bond breaking and, for the first time, in heavy-element I$_2$ with explicit spin-orbit coupling. These results demonstrate that gradient-estimator design expands the capabilities of neural-network variational methods for accurate scientific computing.

</details>


### [161] [Extreme-Scale Ising Machines with Cluster Mean-Field Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22645)
*Xiuqi Zhang,Shuvro Chowdhury,Shaila Niazi,Christian Z. Pratt,Navid Anjum Aadit,Kerem Y. Camsari*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该论文提出簇平均场理论（cluster mean-field theory, CMFT）框架，将大规模相互作用图（interaction graph）划分为适配硬件的簇，并引入动态划分（dynamic partitioning）以削弱固定簇边界带来的误差，从而在模拟与数字伊辛机（Ising machine）上实现超大规模求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将伊辛机扩展至更大规模问题需克服有限器件容量（finite device capacity）与器件间通信成本这两大瓶颈，而划分图会因固定边界引入平均场近似误差。

Method: CMFT 将大图分割为可容纳于现有硬件的簇，簇内独立进行局部更新（local updates），跨簇相互作用通过由边界自旋平均值计算、周期性更新的有效偏置（effective biases）传入；动态划分则循环使用多种划分方案，使某一阶段以平均场近似的相互作用在另一阶段以瞬时自旋（instantaneous spins）形式发挥作用。另结合自动图划分器（automated graph partitioner）与加权恢复比（weighted recovery ratio）作为选取划分组合的启发式方法。

Result: 在三维自旋玻璃（three-dimensional spin glasses）与植入式 Pegasus 实例（planted Pegasus instances）上，动态 CMFT 的残余能量（residual energy）随扫描预算（sweep budgets）呈幂律衰减（power-law decay），表明解质量随计算投入持续提升；在四块 GPU、约四百万 p-bits 上实现与单 GPU 完整图实现相当的能量密度（energy densities），速度最高快约 15 倍。

Conclusion: 通过可编程有效偏置耦合局部演化的簇，CMFT 为在模拟与数字硬件上构建极端规模伊辛机（extreme-scale Ising machines）提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Scaling analog and digital Ising machines to larger problems requires overcoming finite device capacity and the cost of communication between devices. We present cluster mean-field theory (CMFT), a framework that partitions a large interaction graph into clusters sized to fit available hardware. Each cluster performs local updates independently, while interactions across cluster boundaries enter through periodically updated effective biases computed from boundary-spin averages. To reduce the error introduced by fixed cluster boundaries, we introduce dynamic partitioning, which cycles through multiple partitions so that interactions approximated by mean fields at one stage can act through instantaneous spins at another. On three-dimensional spin glasses and planted Pegasus instances, dynamic CMFT exhibits power-law decay of residual energy over sweep budgets. This shows that solution quality continues to improve with computational effort despite the mean-field approximation. An automated graph partitioner combined with a weighted recovery ratio provides a practical heuristic for selecting partition combinations on graphs without natural cut directions. We demonstrate CMFT on four GPUs with approximately four million p-bits, reaching comparable energy densities up to 15 times faster than a single-GPU implementation of the full graph. By coupling locally evolving clusters through programmable effective biases, CMFT provides a route to extreme-scale Ising machines on both analog and digital hardware.

</details>


### [162] [The disordered logistic map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24402)
*Joseph W. Baron,Tobias Galla*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 将多个逻辑斯蒂映射（logistic map）通过无序耦合（disordered couplings）相互连接，发现极小无序即可消除传统周期倍增级联（period-doubling cascade），转而出现高维混沌（high-dimensional chaos）与振荡不稳定性（oscillatory instability）；足够强的均匀耦合可部分恢复周期倍增级联特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逻辑斯蒂映射是低维混沌（low-dimensional chaos）的典型模型，而高维混沌多见于具有大量相互作用且耦合异质的无序系统；本文旨在探讨无序耦合下逻辑斯蒂映射系统的行为，检验传统动力学现象在无序面前是否脆弱。

Method: 构建由多个逻辑斯蒂映射组成、通过无序耦合相互作用的系统，结合动态平均场理论（dynamic mean-field theory）、随机矩阵理论（random matrix theory）与数值模拟（numerical simulations）进行分析。

Result: 即使无序程度极小，也会消除传统逻辑斯蒂映射中的周期倍增级联；系统转而发生向高维混沌的转变，并以此前在无序系统中未报道的振荡不稳定性为标志；当映射间存在足够强的均匀耦合（homogeneous coupling）时，可恢复传统周期倍增级联的部分要素。

Conclusion: 动力学系统中广为人知的现象在无序作用下可能变得脆弱，同时会涌现出既不见于简单低维动力学、也不见于高维无序系统的新现象。

Abstract: The logistic map is a quintessential model in the study of low-dimensional chaos. High-dimensional chaos, on the other hand, presents itself in disordered systems with many interacting and heterogeneously coupled components. Here, we formulate a system of many logistic maps, interacting through disordered couplings. Using a combination of dynamic mean-field theory, random matrix theory and numerical simulations, we show that even the smallest amount of disorder can remove the period-doubling cascade in the conventional logistic map. Instead we find a transition to high-dimensional chaos, marked by an oscillatory instability not previously reported for disordered systems. We also show that with sufficiently strong homogeneous coupling between the maps one recovers elements of the conventional period-doubling cascade. Our findings indicate that well-known phenomena in dynamical systems can be fragile in the face of disorder. At the same time, new phenomena emerge that are neither found in simple low-dimensional dynamics nor in high-dimensional disordered systems.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [163] [Universal Response Functions in Driven Dissipative Tunneling Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22328)
*Krishna Kingkar Pathak*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究在包含 Floquet 调制与欧姆环境耦合的半经典瞬子框架下，证明周期驱动耗散系统中的势垒穿越过程由一个显式的双参数普适响应函数描述，其隧道指数可分解为系统依赖的静态部分与两无量纲控制参数的普适函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性非平衡系统的普适性通常表现为对微观细节不敏感的标度律。当周期驱动与非局域耗散记忆同时作用时，这种普适性是否仍然成立，是驱动开放动力学中的未解问题。

Method: 采用半经典瞬子（semiclassical instanton）框架，并纳入 Floquet 调制与欧姆（Ohmic）环境耦合。通过单一鞍点轨迹的联合修正实现因子化，并借助受控解析展开、高频区分析及非局域瞬子作用的直接数值评估进行验证。

Result: 隧道指数（tunneling exponent）因子化为系统依赖的静态贡献与两无量纲控制参数（归一化驱动频率、耗散强度）的普适函数，且不引入额外独立标度变量。高频区呈现普适动力学平均，存在连接绝热（adiabatic）与 Floquet 交叉的积分表示；数值结果确认归一化隧道指数在不同模型系统中具有普适依赖。

Conclusion: 驱动耗散势垒穿越被识别为非线性非平衡动力学中的一类独特双参数普适类，并为具有记忆的驱动系统响应现象提供了可预测的函数框架。

Abstract: Universality in nonlinear nonequilibrium systems is typically expressed through scaling laws that render macroscopic behavior insensitive to microscopic details. Whether this universality survives when periodic forcing and nonlocal dissipative memory act simultaneously remains an open question in driven open dynamics. Here, we demonstrate that barrier-crossing processes in periodically driven dissipative systems are governed not merely by modified exponential scaling, but by an explicit two-parameter universal response function.
  Within a semiclassical instanton framework incorporating Floquet modulation and Ohmic environmental coupling, the tunneling exponent factorizes into a system-dependent static contribution and a universal function of two dimensionless control parameters: normalized driving frequency and dissipation strength. This factorization arises from the combined modification of a single saddle-point trajectory and introduces no additional independent scaling variables.
  Weak-to-moderate dissipation acts as a smooth dynamical renormalization of the effective action, preserving the saddle-point structure and enabling controlled analytical expansion. In the high-frequency regime, the response exhibits universal dynamical averaging, while an explicit integral representation establishes a continuous adiabatic to Floquet crossover. Direct numerical evaluation of the nonlocal instanton action confirms that the normalized tunneling exponent exhibits a universal dependence on the driving parameters across different model systems. These results identify driven dissipative barrier crossing as a distinct two-parameter universality class within nonlinear nonequilibrium dynamics and provide a predictive functional framework for response phenomena in driven systems with memory.

</details>


### [164] [Diffusion with stochastic resetting in the presence of a delta killing trap: Drift-controlled survival regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22527)
*Alain Mazzolo*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究一维扩散粒子在随机重置（stochastic resetting）与局域吸收靶（delta-function killing rate）共同作用下的存活问题，给出非归一化传播子与存活概率的精确拉普拉斯空间（Laplace-space）表达式，并区分无漂移与常漂移两种情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是随机重置如何与漂移导致的暂态性（transience）以及靶的有限反应性（finite reactivity）相互竞争。

Method: 对任意扩散系数 D、重置率 r 与杀伤强度 k，先在不含漂移、再在常漂移条件下推导非归一化传播子与存活概率的精确拉普拉斯空间表达式；长时间行为由拉普拉斯变换的主导（最右）极点决定。

Result: 无重置、无漂移扩散呈循环型代数存活律 S(t)∼t^{-1/2}；任何非零漂移使运动相对靶呈暂态并留下非零终极存活概率；而任何 r>0 与 k>0 都会反复更新与靶的相遇并恢复最终吸收，给出指数存活律 S(t)∼A e^{-θt}，归一化后存活密度收敛到显式的准稳态（quasi-stationary）剖面。

Conclusion: 无漂移与完全吸收极限可回归标准重置结果；所给公式统一刻画了循环控制、暂态控制与更新控制三种存活机制之间的交叉（crossover）。

Abstract: We study a one-dimensional diffusive particle subject to stochastic resetting to its initial position, in the presence of an imperfect, localized target that can absorb (kill) the particle, modeled by a delta-function killing rate. The central question is how stochastic resetting competes with drift-induced transience and the target's finite reactivity. Exact Laplace-space expressions are derived for the non-normalized propagator and survival probability, for arbitrary diffusion coefficient $D$, resetting rate $r$, and killing strength $k$, first in the absence of drift and then under a constant drift. The long-time behavior separates into distinct regimes. Without resetting, unbiased diffusion exhibits the recurrent algebraic survival law $S(t)\sim t^{-1/2}$, whereas any nonzero drift renders the motion transient with respect to the target and leaves a nonzero ultimate survival probability. By contrast, any $r>0$ and $k>0$ repeatedly renews encounters with the target and restores eventual absorption, yielding an exponential survival law $S(t)\sim A e^{-θt}$. The decay rate $θ$ is given by the dominant (rightmost) pole of the Laplace transform, and the surviving density converges after normalization to an explicit quasi-stationary profile. The driftless and perfectly absorbing limits recover standard resetting results. These formulas provide a unified description of the crossover between recurrence-controlled, transience-controlled, and renewal-controlled survival.

</details>


### [165] [Intermittency in Wind-Driven Fires](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22541)
*Laurent Hébert-Dufresne,Aanjaneya Kumar,S. Redner*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 构建一维风驱动森林火灾模型（wind-driven forest-fire model），允许火势跳跃间隙点燃下风方向互不相连的森林；火势跳跃能力随火强度变化，火强度随传播增强、随跳跃减弱。模型呈现出准确定态、稳态与间歇性三种动力学区制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统森林火灾模型通常假设火只在相邻树木间传播，未刻画跳跃间隙（gap jumping）及风驱动的下风引燃机制。该研究旨在引入火强度随传播与跳跃动态变化这一要素，探究其对火灾时空动力学的影响。

Method: 在一维（one dimension）系统中建立风驱动森林火灾模型：树木以速率 \(r\) 在空位生长，雷电以速率 \(f\) 击中每个位点；火可跳跃的间隙大小取决于火强度（fire intensity），而火强度随其在树木中传播而增强、随跳跃间隙而减弱。通过调节 \(f\) 与系统长度 \(L\) 的相对关系分析不同区制。

Result: 当 \(f\ll r/L\) 时，雷电足够稀少，出现准确定性动力学（quasi-deterministic dynamics），即一次雷击引发的火灾会耗尽所有树木。当 \(f\gg L^{-\mu}\)（\(\mu\approx 0.8\)）时，雷电足够频繁，系统达到稳态（steady state），但森林与间隙尺寸分布（forest- and gap-size distributions）表现出未预期的行为。两者之间出现间歇性（intermittency），且确定性动力学与混沌动力学（chaotic dynamics）的时间域共存。

Conclusion: 火强度随传播增强、随跳跃减弱的机制使模型产生由雷电频率调控的多种动力学区制，包括准确定性耗尽、带异常分布特征的稳态以及确定性—混沌共存的时间间歇性。

Abstract: We construct a wind-driven forest-fire model in one dimension in which a fire can jump gaps between trees to ignite disjoint downwind forests. The size of a gap that a fire can jump depends on the fire intensity, which increases as the fire propagates through trees and diminishes as the fire jumps gaps. Trees grow on empty sites at rate $r$ and lightning strikes each site with rate $f$. When $f\ll r/L$, where $L$ is the system length, lightning is sufficiently rare that quasi-deterministic dynamics arises where all trees are consumed when a lightning-induced fire occurs. For $f\gg L^{-μ}$ with $μ\approx 0.8$, lightning is sufficiently frequent that a steady state is reached, but with unexpected behaviors for the forest- and gap-size distributions. Intermittency arises in between these regimes, with coexisting temporal domains of deterministic and chaotic dynamics.

</details>


### [166] [Exact Nonperturbative Equilibrium Mode Statistics in Nonlinear Wave and Lattice Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24063)
*Jialin Zhang,Yong Zhang,Hong Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文针对三个代表性非线性系统（MMT色散波模型、FPUT-β非谐链、离散非线性薛定谔晶格），推导出平衡态模态占据数及相关统计量的精确有限尺寸非微扰表示，并由独立数值模拟在弱至强非线性范围内验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性波与晶格模型（nonlinear wave and lattice models）的平衡态模态分布缺乏精确、非微扰的理论描述；微扰方法在何种条件下可靠尚不清楚，尤其在弱耦合准凝聚（quasicondensation）附近。

Method: 推导三类系统的精确有限尺寸非微扰（nonperturbative）表示；对DNLS将有限环模态占据数分解为具有不同关联长度的Rayleigh-Jeans通道之和；并据此评估两种代表性微扰方法（含平均相互作用处理）在不同阶数下的表现。

Result: 理论与独立模拟在弱到强非线性下一致；DNLS理论在弱耦合准凝聚交叉区仍准确，即使裸非线性系数很小，强低模占据与长程相干会放大相互作用效应。精确占据数还决定MMT与DNLS的平均模态频率，即使动力学谱展宽或分裂。微扰方法在适当处理平均相互作用时低阶近似准确（含强非线性），但高阶修正不收敛且逐阶振幅振荡增大，且在弱耦合准凝聚附近均失效。

Conclusion: 低阶吻合或小裸耦合均不能保证微扰描述可靠；结果确立了广泛使用的非线性波与晶格模型的非微扰平衡理论，并为非线性光学、色散波、非谐晶格与冷原子系统中的能量、粒子与光功率模态分布提供定量基础。

Abstract: We derive exact finite-size nonperturbative representations of equilibrium modal occupations and related statistics for three representative nonlinear systems: the Majda-McLaughlin-Tabak dispersive-wave model, the Fermi-Pasta-Ulam-Tsingou beta anharmonic chain, and the discrete nonlinear Schrodinger lattice field. Independent simulations confirm the predictions from weak to strong nonlinearity. For DNLS, the theory remains accurate across the weak-coupling quasicondensation crossover, where large low-mode occupations and long-range coherence amplify interaction effects even when the bare nonlinear coefficient is small. The finite-ring DNLS occupations are further resolved into a positive sum of Rayleigh-Jeans channels with distinct correlation lengths, explaining when a single Rayleigh-Jeans law applies and why it fails near quasicondensation. In MMT and DNLS, the exact occupations also determine the mean modal frequencies even when the dynamical spectra broaden or split. The nonperturbative results allow a direct assessment of two representative perturbative approaches. Treating the mean interaction appropriately yields accurate low-order approximations, including at strong nonlinearity. At higher orders, however, the corrections cease to decrease and successive approximations oscillate with increasing amplitude; both finite-order approaches also fail near weak-coupling quasicondensation. Thus neither low-order agreement nor a small bare coupling guarantees a reliable perturbative description. The results establish nonperturbative equilibrium theory for widely used nonlinear wave and lattice models and provide a quantitative basis for modal distributions of energy, particles, and optical power in nonlinear optics, dispersive waves, anharmonic lattices, and cold-atom systems.

</details>


### [167] [Ageing in the exact correlations of the voter model on a fractal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24408)
*Malte Henkel*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文求解了分形基底（fractal substrate）上最近邻投票者模型（voter model）单时与双时关联函数包络的精确行为，通过空间依赖扩散常数引入谱指数，并借助标度假设导出动态指数与自关联指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分形基底上的投票者模型同时涉及非整数几何分形维度与谱维度，其关联函数包络的精确标度行为尚需解析刻画，尤其是几何维度与输运（扩散）维度分离所带来的影响。

Method: 以非整数几何分形维度 $d_f$ 描述基底几何，以谱维度 $d_s$ 描述拓扑与扩散输运；在关联函数运动方程层面用空间依赖扩散常数 ${\cal D}(r)\sim r^{-\theta}$ 建模，其中谱指数 $\theta$ 为 $d_f$ 与 $d_s$ 的函数，并采用标度假设（scaling ansatz）进行分析。

Result: 确认了普适的老化（ageing）现象，导出动态指数 $z=2+\theta$ 与自关联指数 $\lambda=d_f$；所得动态标度函数仅依赖于谱维度 $d_s$；活跃界面密度包络的衰减由指数 $\alpha=1-d_s/2$（当 $d_s<2$）描述，与既有数值模拟结果一致。

Conclusion: 分形基底上投票者模型关联函数包络的标度行为可由几何维度与谱维度分别控制：动态指数依赖谱指数 $\theta$，而动态标度函数与界面密度衰减仅由谱维度决定，从而验证并解释了先前的数值模拟结论。

Abstract: The exact behaviour of the enveloppes of the single-time and two-time correlators is found for the voter model on a fractal substrate, with nearest-neighbour interactions. Herein the geometry of the fractal substrate is described by its non-integer geometric fractal dimension $d_f$, and its topology and diffusive transport by the distinct spectral dimension $d_s$. On the level of the equations of motion of the correlators this can be modelled by considering a space-dependent diffusion constant ${\cal D}(r)\sim r^{-θ}$ which implies the spectral index $θ$, itself a function of $d_f$ and $d_s$. With a scaling ansatz, the generic phenomenology of ageing is confirmed and the dynamic exponent ${z}=2+θ$ and the autocorrelation exponent $λ=d_f$ are derived. The explicitly found dynamic scaling functions are shown to depend only on the spectral dimension $d_s$. The decay of the enveloppe of the density of active interfaces with time is described by the exponent $α=1-d_s/2$ for $d_s<2$, confirming the results of preexisting numerical simulations.

</details>


### [168] [Long-range self-avoiding walk in one dimension: a Monte Carlo study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23346)
*Jiang Zhou,Ziyu Liu,Pengcheng Hou,Zhijie Fan,Youjin Deng*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用高效不可逆更新方案的大规模蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）模拟，研究一维长程自回避行走（long-range self-avoiding walk）在巨正则系综中的临界行为，确定 σ=1 为长程与短程普适类（universality class）的分界，并验证了长程 O(n) 模型普适相图（self-avoiding walk 对应 n→0 极限）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程相互作用系统中临界行为随衰减指数 σ 的转变机制尚需高精度数值验证，尤其是短程与长程普适类之间的边界位置及临界指数演化规律，且近期提出的 (d,σ) 平面普适相图有待独立检验。

Method: 在一维巨正则系综（grand-canonical ensemble）中考察跳跃长度 r 的统计权重按 r^{-(d+σ)} 代数衰减的长程自回避行走，采用高效不可逆更新方案（irreversible update scheme）进行大规模蒙特卡洛模拟，高精度估计临界逸度（critical fugacity）z_c、普适 Binder 比（Binder ratio）Q_N^c、关联长度指数（correlation-length exponent）ν 与反常维度（anomalous dimension）η。

Result: 当 σ>1 时，临界逸度随 σ 平滑变化，而 Binder 比与临界指数与短程普适类一致；当 σ≤1 时结果明显偏离短程行为，表明 σ=1 为两类区域的分界。在 1/2<σ≤1 的长程 Wilson-Fisher 区域，η 与长程高斯不动点（Gaussian fixed point）预言 η_GFP=2-σ 相符，而 Q_N^c 与 ν 随 σ 呈非平凡变化，并在 σ=1 处出现不连续跳变。

Conclusion: 结果与近期针对长程 O(n) 模型提出的 (d,σ) 平面普适相图相符，其中自回避行走对应 n→0 极限；该相图得到独立数值支持，σ=1 作为长程—短程转变边界的判定得到确认。

Abstract: We study the one-dimensional long-range self-avoiding walk in the grand-canonical ensemble where the statistical weight of a jump of length $r$ decays algebraically as $r^{-(d+σ)}$. Using large-scale Monte Carlo simulations with an efficient irreversible update scheme, we obtain high-precision estimates of the critical fugacity $z_c$, the universal Binder ratio $Q_N^c$, the correlation-length exponent $ν$, and the anomalous dimension $η$. For $σ>1$, the critical fugacity varies smoothly with $σ$, while the Binder ratio and the critical exponents remain consistent with the short-range universality class. For $σ\le 1$, by contrast, the results clearly depart from short-range behavior, identifying $σ=1$ as the boundary between long-range and short-range regimes. In the long-range Wilson-Fisher regime with $1/2 < σ\leq 1$, $η$ agrees with the long-range Gaussian-fixed-point prediction $η_{\mathrm{GFP}}=2-σ$, whereas $Q_N^c$ and $ν$ vary nontrivially with $σ$ and exhibit discontinuous jumps at $σ=1$. These findings are in good agreement with the recently proposed universality diagram in the $(d,σ)$ plane for long-range O$(n)$ models, with the self-avoiding walk corresponding to the $n\to0$ limit.

</details>


### [169] [Lowest-known Energy Configuration of $N=100\,000$ Coulomb Charges in a Disk: Breaking the $10^{5}$ Barrier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24722)
*Georgiy K. Lavrov,Eduard G. Nikonov*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该工作首次给出 $N=100\,000$ 个受限圆盘内经典点电荷（1/r 库仑势，即圆盘 Thomson 问题）的最低已知能量构型，规模为此前记录（$N=40\,886$）的两倍以上，并验证了渐近展开在极端尺度下的外推精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 圆盘约束下的 Thomson 问题在超大 $N$ 时的基态构型与能量此前未被计算，既有记录仅达 $N=40\,886$，缺乏对渐近展开在大尺度外推能力的严格检验，也缺少二维库仑系统的构型基准。

Method: 采用自适应缺陷定位子域优化策略（adaptive defect-targeting subdomain optimization），实现每周期墙钟时间（wall-clock time）随 $N$ 近似线性增长；$N=100\,000$ 时在单台 24 核 CPU 工作站、无 GPU 加速下耗时约 31 小时。

Result: 得到最低能量 $E_{\min}=7.80466624157\times10^{9}$，与基于 $100\le N\le5000$ 数据拟合的渐近展开（asymptotic expansion）仅偏差 $2.525\times10^{-7}$；键取向序（bond-orientational order）分析显示多晶内部（polycrystalline bulk）被径向晶界（radial grain boundaries）贯穿。

Conclusion: 该结果将圆盘 Thomson 问题的可计算规模提升至 $N=100\,000$，为渐近展开的极端尺度外推提供了严格检验，同时为二维库仑系统（two-dimensional Coulomb systems）建立了新的构型基准。

Abstract: We report the first calculation of the lowest-known energy configuration for $N=100\,000$ classical point charges confined to a disk and interacting via the $1/r$ Coulomb potential - a system size never before achieved for the Thomson problem in a disk, more than doubling the previous record of $N=40\,886$ reported by Amore and Zarate. An adaptive defect-targeting subdomain optimization strategy yields an approximately linear growth of the wall-clock time per cycle with $N$; for $N=100\,000$ a single $24$-core CPU workstation without GPU acceleration required $\approx31$ hours. The energy $E_{\min}=7.80466624157\times10^{9}$ deviates from the asymptotic expansion fitted to $100\le N\le5000$ data by only $2.525\times10^{-7}$, providing a stringent test of its extrapolation to unprecedented scales. Bond-orientational order analysis reveals a polycrystalline bulk threaded by radial grain boundaries, establishing a new benchmark for two-dimensional Coulomb systems.

</details>


### [170] [Critical Touching of Temporal Entanglement Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23513)
*Ting-Long Wang,Shi-Xin Zhang,Shuai Yin,Yi-Fan Jiang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 在周期驱动的横向 $J_1$-$J_2$ 伊辛链（transverse $J_1$-$J_2$ Ising chain）中，两个时间纠缠转变（temporal entanglement transition, TET）可合并为一个“临界接触”（critical touching），实现连续相变的时间类比，并被确立为一种独立的 TET 形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平衡态相变通常分为一阶与连续两类，而远离平衡的量子实时演化中会出现新转变，其中已有 TET 均表现为不同对称扇区主导施密特能级（Schmidt levels）的线性交叉，类似平衡一阶相变。由此产生的自然问题是：以纠缠谱（entanglement spectrum）接触为特征的连续 TET 是否可能存在。

Method: 研究采用周期驱动的横向 $J_1$-$J_2$ 伊辛链模型，通过频率扫描考察 TET 的行为，并结合二阶 Floquet 哈密顿量（Floquet Hamiltonian）进行分析。

Result: 研究表明两个 TET 可合并为一个临界接触，主导能级相切而不交换；在临界频率附近，两个 TET 的时间间隔连续消失且其对频率的导数发散，这一非解析特征在单一的一阶 TET 中并不存在。该有限频率接触源于初始乘积态（product initial state）的弱对称保持微扰与 Floquet 修正之间的相互作用。频率扫描进一步揭示在更高频率处存在第二个临界接触，两个临界频率之间形成一个无 TET 的有限窗口，且这些特征在较宽的耦合比范围内持续存在。

Conclusion: 上述特征可由二阶 Floquet 哈密顿量理解，并确立临界接触为一种独特的 TET 形式。

Abstract: Equilibrium phase transitions are conventionally categorized into first-order and continuous phase transitions. Far from equilibrium, many new transitions emerge during the real-time evolution of quantum systems. One such transition is the temporal entanglement transition (TET) characterized by the nonanalyticity of the entanglement spectrum. So far, all TETs occur through a linear crossing of the leading Schmidt levels in different symmetry sectors, resembling a first-order transition in equilibrium. A natural question is whether a continuous TET, featured by entanglement spectrum touching, is possible. In a periodically driven transverse $J_1$-$J_2$ Ising chain, we show that two such TETs can merge into a critical touching, where the leading levels meet tangentially without exchanging, realizing the temporal analog of a continuous phase transition. Near the critical frequency, the temporal separation of the two TETs vanishes continuously and its derivative with respect to frequency diverges, a nonanalytic signature that is absent in a single first-order TET. This finite-frequency touching arises from the interplay between a weak symmetry-preserving perturbation of the product initial state and Floquet corrections. Further extending the frequency scan reveals a second critical touching at a higher frequency, and the two critical frequencies enclose a finite window with no TET. These features can be understood from a second-order Floquet Hamiltonian and persist across a broad range of coupling ratios, establishing the critical touching as a distinct form of TET.

</details>


### [171] [Rank-dependent optimal resetting in multiparticle search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23542)
*Ron Vatash,Eden Goldfarb,Vladimir Yu. Rudyak,Yael Roichman*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究随机重置（stochastic resetting）如何优化多粒子搜索中全部到达秩次的有序首次通过时间（ordered first-passage times），建立非相互作用布朗搜索者的精确有限 $N$ 参考，并指出最优重置率由所需完成任务秩次决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多软物质与生物系统的任务完成依赖多个搜索者的累积到达，而非单个先驱者的速度，因此完成动力学由完整的首次通过时间有序序列决定，而非仅由首次到达决定。研究旨在厘清随机重置对这一有序序列各秩次的优化方式。

Method: 针对非相互作用布朗搜索者（non-interacting Brownian searchers）构建精确的有限 $N$ 参考，求得平均有序首次通过时间 $\langle T_{(k)} \rangle$ 及其最优重置率 $r_k^*$；并将该基线用于对照布朗胶体实验、相互作用活性布朗粒子（active Brownian particles）以及具有持久环境记忆的集体自趋化搜索（autochemotactic search）。

Result: 在搜索者初始条件相同时，$r_k^*$ 随到达秩次单调增加；随种群规模增大，趋于已知的大 $N$ 分位数极限，即仅当秩次比例高于临界值 $\varphi_c \simeq 0.412$ 时才出现有限最优值。空间异质性在无粒子相互作用情况下即可定性重组该序列，使其最大值由晚秩次移到早秩次。在各类系统中，对重置的敏感性随到达秩次显著增强，而与相应非相互作用参考的偏离则揭示了直接相互作用、有限返回开销与环境记忆的影响。

Conclusion: 多粒子搜索中的最优重置由所需的完成秩次决定，评估时必须相对协议与几何匹配的基线进行。该结论将最优重置分析从单一首次到达推广到全部到达秩次。

Abstract: In many soft-matter and biological systems, task completion relies on the cumulative arrival of multiple searchers rather than the speed of a single pioneer. The completion kinetics are therefore set not only by the first arrival, but by the full ordered sequence of first-passage times. Here, we determine how stochastic resetting optimizes these ordered arrivals for all arrival ranks. We construct an exact finite-$N$ reference for non-interacting Brownian searchers and obtain the mean ordered first-passage time $\langle T_{(k)} \rangle$ and its optimal resetting rate $r_k^*$. For searchers with identical initial conditions, $r_k^*$ increases monotonically with arrival rank and, with increasing population size, approaches the known large-$N$ quantile limit where a finite optimum appears only above a critical rank fraction $φ_c \simeq 0.412$. Spatial heterogeneity qualitatively reorganizes this sequence, shifting its maximum from late to early ranks even without particle interactions. We then compare this baseline with Brownian colloid experiments, interacting active Brownian particles, and a collective autochemotactic search with persistent environmental memory. Across these systems, sensitivity to resetting increases strongly with arrival rank, while deviations from appropriate non-interacting references reveal the influence of direct interactions, finite return overhead, and environmental memory. Our results show that optimal resetting in multiparticle search is governed by the required completion rank and must be evaluated relative to protocol- and geometry-matched baselines.

</details>


### [172] [The ferromagnetic diatomic molecules with the mixed spin-1/2 and spin-1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23632)
*E. Albayrak*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 在配位数 q=3、4、6 的贝特晶格（Bethe lattice, BL）上，每个格点放置由自旋-1/2（σ）与自旋-1（S）原子组成的双原子分子，通过磁化强度热变化研究相图。相图在定性与定量上均依赖 q，出现三个铁磁相与重入行为，并包含一阶与二阶相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究关注混合自旋双原子分子模型在贝特晶格上的磁性与相变行为，考察配位数、晶场（crystal field）D 及外加磁场 H 对相图的影响，以揭示几何配位与相互作用参数对相变类型和相区结构的调控作用。

Method: 将各由一个自旋-1/2（σ）与一个自旋-1 原子构成的分子置于配位数 q=3、4、6 的贝特晶格格点上，分子内原子间及最近邻（nearest neighbors, NN）分子间均设铁磁（ferromagnetic, FM）相互作用，采用双线性参数 J 描述，晶场 D 仅作用于自旋-1 格点。在零外磁场（H）下，通过分析磁化强度的热变化获得 (D, T) 平面相图。

Result: 相图在定性与定量上均依赖 q。基态层面观察到三个独立的铁磁相，其 (σ, S) 分别为 (1/2, 1) 与 (1/2, 0)，另在较高温区存在小磁化强度（small magnetization, SM）相区。模型在相间过渡区呈现重入（reentrant）行为，同时产生一阶与二阶相变。此外，考察了 H 对磁化曲线的影响，得到较为有趣的发现。

Conclusion: 该混合自旋双原子分子模型在贝特晶格上的相图结构由配位数 q 显著决定，晶场 D 与磁场 H 共同调节相变类型与磁化行为，为理解此类分子磁体系统的相变多样性提供了依据。

Abstract: Diatomic molecules consisting of one spin-1/2 ($σ$) and one spin-1 ($S$) atom are put at each site of the Bethe lattice (BL) of the coordination number $q=3, 4$, or 6. Alongside the ferromagnetic (FM) interactions of individual atoms within a molecule, the molecules are permitted to engage in ferromagnetic interactions with their nearest neighbors (NN) through diverse bilinear interaction parameters $J$. The spin-1 sites are also under the influence of crystal field $D$. The phase diagrams on the ($D, T$) planes for a given $q$ are obtained by investigating the thermal variations of magnetizations for the case with zero external magnetic field $(H)$. It is discovered that the phase diagrams rely on $q$ both qualitatively and quantitatively. The crystal field is also included which operates only on the spin-1 sites. Three separate FM phases are observed with ($σ,S$) being $(1/2,1), (1/2,0)$ at the ground state level and a phase region with small magnetization (SM) values at a higher temperature range. In addition to the reentrant behavior in the transition zone from one phase to the other, the model produces first- and second-order phase transitions. Additionally, the impact of $H$ on the magnetization curves is examined, yielding quite intriguing findings.

</details>


### [173] [A Spatio-Temporal Generalisation of Green Kubo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23905)
*E. R. Smith,D. Dini,D. M. Heyes*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该工作将 Green-Kubo（GK）方法拓展到空间相关层面，通过将体系总剪切应力分解到相邻子体积网格上，把总应力自相关改写为子体积应力间的互相关，从而在实空间中揭示液体的时空响应结构，并据此提出以短时 MD 拟合长时行为来估计黏度的思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平衡分子动力学（equilibrium molecular dynamics, MD）中基于 Green-Kubo（GK）方法计算剪切黏度通常需要很长的模拟时间才能获得可接受的统计量。同时，液体结构此前主要由液体态理论在傅里叶空间（Fourier space）处理，缺乏实空间的相关图像。

Method: 将 GK 表达式中的总剪切应力拆分为连续体积网格上的分量，使总体系应力的自相关（autocorrelation）可重写为子体积应力之间的互相关（cross-correlation）。随后将所得的时空响应以解析形式拟合，即用一个主导高斯脉冲（Gaussian pulse）叠加一个表示反弹的负高斯项进行描述。

Result: 揭示出一种类似行波（travelling wave）的新结构，可由“主导高斯脉冲加负高斯反弹项”很好地拟合；拟合参数包括波的位置、幅度与宽度。该波在短时间内以声速（speed of sound）传播，随后速度随时间的平方根下降，波包展宽同样随时间的平方根增大；幅度按 $t^{-3/2}$ 形式衰减，该结果在 MD 模拟史上具有重要意义。

Conclusion: 液体的整体时空响应可通过拟合以闭式（closed form）建模：短时部分由 MD 描述，长时与长距离部分用高斯形式近似。将相关限制在局部相互作用可消除基本只贡献噪声的长程贡献。该方法有望通过短时 MD 运行并拟合长时行为来建模黏度。

Abstract: The Green-Kubo (GK) method which is used to obtain the shear viscosity of a model liquid in equilibrium molecular dynamics (MD) often requires long simulation times to obtain acceptable statistics. This work extends the GK approach to include spatial correlations. The total shear stress in the GK expression is split into a grid of its components in contiguous volumes. In doing this, the autocorrelation of the total system stress can be rewritten as the cross-correlation of the subvolume stresses. This provides a novel real-space insight into the liquid structure, something previously only treated in Fourier space by liquid-state theory. A novel travelling wave like structure is exposed that is shown to be well fitted by a leading Gaussian pulse added to a second negative Gaussian for the bounce back. The fitting parameters include the wave position, magnitude and width which show deep insights into the liquid property. The wave moves at the speed of sound over short times before decreasing in speed with the square root of time, while the wave packet spreads out also as the square root of time. The magnitude is decreasing with form $t^{-3/2}$, a result with strong significance in the history of MD simulation. These fitted forms mean the entire spatial temporal response of the liquid can be modelled in closed form by fitting to data, with short term requiring MD and the long time and distances approximated by the Gaussian form. By limiting the correlation to localized interactions the longer range contribution, which essentially only contribute to the noise, can be eliminated. This looks like a promising approach to model viscosity by taking short MD runs and fitting the long time behaviour.

</details>


### [174] [Derivation of the Thiele equation for magnetic skyrmions using the Mori-Zwanzig formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24160)
*Michael te Vrugt,Mathias Kläui*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用 Mori-Zwanzig 形式体系（Mori-Zwanzig formalism）推导出描述磁性斯格明子（magnetic skyrmion）动力学的精确广义朗之万方程（generalized Langevin equation），并在标准近似下将其约化为经典的 Thiele 方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在为磁性斯格明子动力学提供更严谨的微观理论基础，并寻求超越标准 Thiele 方程推导路径的推广方式，以覆盖标准推导未涉及的磁结构（magnetic texture）与自由度。

Method: 采用 Mori-Zwanzig 形式体系（Mori-Zwanzig formalism）推导精确的广义朗之万方程（generalized Langevin equation），并引入标准近似以考察其与 Thiele 方程的关系。

Result: 在标准近似下，所得的广义朗之万方程约化为通常的 Thiele 方程；该框架同时揭示快模（fast modes）对斯格明子 Fokker-Planck 方程中漂移项（drift term）与扩散项（diffusion term）形式的贡献。

Conclusion: 该工作为获得 Thiele 方程的推广提供了一条通用路径，适用于标准推导未覆盖的磁结构与自由度，并为标准 Thiele 方程的微观合理性（特别是快模的作用）提供了洞见。

Abstract: We use the Mori-Zwanzig formalism to derive an exact generalized Langevin equation governing the dynamics of magnetic skyrmions. Using standard approximations, this generalized Langevin equation is found to reduce to the usual Thiele equation. Our approach provides a general route for obtaining extensions of the Thiele equation applicable to magnetic textures and degrees of freedom not covered by the standard derivation route. It also provides some insights into the microscopic justification of the standard Thiele equation, specifically concerning contributions of fast modes to the form of the drift and diffusion terms in the Fokker-Planck equation for skyrmions.

</details>


### [175] [Heterogeneous planar diffusion with axisymmetric power-law decaying diffusion coefficient under stochastic resetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24427)
*Trifce Sandev,Sebastien Fumeron,Malte Henkel,Ervin K. Lenzif*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文在二维各向同性非均匀扩散（heterogeneous diffusion）中引入径向衰减扩散系数 D(ρ)=D₀/ρ² 与泊松随机重置（Poissonian stochastic resetting），统一处理 Itô、Stratonovich 与 Hänggi–Klimontovich 三种随机微积分规定，并进一步以从属化（subordination）方法纳入幂律记忆效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乘性噪声（multiplicative noise）使过程解释依赖微积分规定，而空间非均匀扩散叠加随机重置后的稳态、扩散与首达性质尚缺乏统一分析；同时需考察记忆效应的影响。

Method: 在单一框架下分别处理 Itô、Stratonovich 与 Hänggi–Klimontovich 三种规定；进一步采用从属化（subordination，即连续时间随机游走 continuous-time random walk）配合幂律核，推导分数阶 Fokker–Planck 方程。

Result: 对每种规定均得到精确概率密度函数、重置诱导的非平衡稳态、均方位移与首达性质；非平衡稳态由修正贝塞尔函数（modified Bessel functions）描述，长时均方位移饱和为 ⟨ρ²⟩∼r̄^(-1/2)；首达时间存在使其最小的最优重置率；分数情形下均方位移以三参数 Mittag-Leffler 函数表示，且最优重置率随记忆指数减小而增大。

Conclusion: 非均匀扩散下的重置稳态偏离均匀扩散的拉普拉斯形式，呈现贝塞尔函数结构；存在可最小化平均首达时间的最优重置率，且该最优值对记忆强度敏感，记忆越强（记忆指数越小）最优重置率越大。

Abstract: We analyse two-dimensional isotropic heterogeneous diffusion with a radially decaying diffusion coefficient $D(ρ)=D_0/ρ^2$, subject to Poissonian stochastic resetting to the origin. Since multiplicative noise makes the process interpretation-dependent, we treat the Itô, Stratonovich, and Hänggi--Klimontovich prescriptions within a single approach. For each, we obtain the exact probability density function, the non-equilibrium stationary state induced by resetting, the mean squared displacement, and the first-passage properties. The non-equilibrium stationary state is governed by modified Bessel functions and departs from the Laplace form found for homogeneous diffusion under resetting, while the mean squared displacement saturates at long times as $\langleρ^2\rangle\sim\bar{r}^{-1/2}$, $\bar{r}$ being the resetting rate. The mean first-passage time to an absorbing boundary exhibits an optimal resetting rate at which it is minimised. In addition, we incorporate memory effects through a subordination (continuous-time random walk) approach with a power-law kernel, deriving the fractional Fokker--Planck equation together with the non-equilibrium stationary state, the mean squared displacement expressed via the three-parameter Mittag-Leffler function, and the first-passage statistics; the optimal resetting rate increases as the memory exponent decreases.

</details>


### [176] [Non-equilibrium dynamics of drift-diffusion process under threshold resetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24438)
*Rahul Das,Satya N Majumdar,Arnab Pal*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究阈值重置（threshold resetting）下随机过程中非平衡稳态（NESS）的出现，提出NESS存在的一般条件，并证明稳态分布可由平均局部时间（MLT）与平均首次通过时间（MFPT）之比表达；以一维漂移扩散过程为例揭示异常弛豫与阻尼振荡等中间时间动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外部驱动重置（externally driven resetting）的稳态性质已被充分理解，而阈值重置这一事件驱动协议下的行为仍基本未被探索，因此需要建立其非平衡稳态存在性与弛豫动力学的普适认识。

Method: 采用理论解析方法：推导阈值重置下非平衡稳态存在的一般条件；证明稳态在位置 x 处可表示为该处平均局部时间（MLT）与到达阈值的平均首次通过时间（MFPT）之比；并以一维漂移扩散（drift-diffusion）过程作为示例进行分析。

Result: 对于含噪系统，有限的平均首次通过时间（MFPT）即可保证非平衡稳态存在。一维漂移扩散示例中，观察到丰富的中间时间动力学，包括空间分布中的异常弛豫（anomalous relaxation）、矩与均方位移（MSD）中的阻尼振荡，且这些行为受系统参数控制。

Conclusion: 研究给出了阈值重置下非平衡稳态出现与弛豫动力学的普遍理解，揭示诱导事件（induced events）如何塑造一大类随机过程的空间与时间性质。

Abstract: We study the emergence of non-equilibrium steady states (NESS) in stochastic processes under threshold resetting, an event-driven protocol in which the system resets to its initial configuration upon crossing a prescribed spatial boundary (threshold). In contrast to externally driven resetting, whose steady-state properties are well understood, the behavior under threshold resetting remains largely unexplored. We derive general conditions for the existence of a NESS and show that, whenever it exists, the steady state at a given position $x$ can be expressed as the ratio of two fundamental quantities: the mean local time (MLT) at $x$ and the mean first-passage time (MFPT) to hit the threshold. In particular, for noisy systems, a finite MFPT guarantees the existence of a NESS. As an illustrative example, we analyze a drift-diffusion process in one dimension and uncover rich intermediate-time dynamics, including anomalous relaxation in the spatial distribution, damped oscillations in the moments and in the mean-squared displacement (MSD), governed by system parameters. Our findings provide a general understanding of the emergence of non-equilibrium steady states and relaxation dynamics under threshold resetting, revealing how induced events shape the spatial and temporal properties of a broad class of stochastic processes.

</details>


### [177] [Effective Mass Approach to Dynamics of Non-Markovian Systems with Short Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24450)
*Mateusz Wiśniewski*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文提出“有效质量方法（effective mass approach）”，通过在马尔可夫近似框架内将短时记忆效应（short-memory effects）编码为有效质量，从而搭建连接马尔可夫（Markovian）与非马尔可夫（non-Markovian）动力学的桥梁，并论证短程时间相关性在微观系统中的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆（memory）是物理系统动力学的固有特征，通常在建模简化复杂结构时出现，并使部分底层行为被掩盖。对这类模型需同时了解当前与过去状态，导致非马尔可夫动力学（non-Markovian dynamics），而其历史依赖的相互作用引入了显著的数学复杂性。因此，当记忆时间（memory time）远短于动力学特征时间尺度时，通常被忽略，但代价是丧失对其在系统行为中作用的认识。

Method: 论文引入一种新方法，即有效质量方法（effective mass approach）。该方法将非马尔可夫系统近似为其马尔可夫对应物，并把短记忆效应封装在一个有效质量之中，从而为分析具有短记忆的系统提供可操作的框架，并在马尔可夫与非马尔可夫两个区间之间建立联系。

Result: 利用该方法，论文表明短记忆对物理动力学的影响可能非常显著。具体而言：在具有时间相关性（temporal correlations）的环境中，布朗粒子（Brownian particle）的定向输运（directed transport）可相对于其在无记忆介质中的行为发生反转；此外，论文提出一种设置，使记忆可作为生成随机信息比特（random information bits）的控制参数（control parameter）。

Conclusion: 总体而言，该论文的结果强调了微观系统中短时相关性（short-time correlations）的关键重要性，并显示短记忆并非可忽略的微小修正，而可能显著改变系统的动力学与功能表现。

Abstract: Memory is an inherent feature of the dynamics of physical systems. It typically emerges when the complex structure of the system is simplified during the modeling process, obscuring a part of its underlying behavior. Consequently, the correct description of such a physical model requires knowledge of not only its present, but also its past states, leading to non-Markovian dynamics. The resulting interactions depending on the system history, however, introduce a significant layer of mathematical complexity. For this reason, when the memory time is significantly shorter than the time scales characterizing the dynamics, it is typically neglected at the cost of losing information regarding its role in the system behavior. In this dissertation, I introduce a novel methodology, namely the effective mass approach, which bridges the Markovian and non-Markovian regimes and provides an accessible framework for analyzing systems with short memory. In this method, the non-Markovian system is approximated with its Markovian counterpart, in which the short-memory effects are encapsulated within an effective mass. I then utilize this approach to demonstrate that the impact of short memory on physical dynamics can be remarkably pronounced. In particular, I show that the directed transport of a Brownian particle in an environment exhibiting temporal correlations can be reversed relative to its behavior in a memoryless medium. Moreover, I propose a setup wherein memory serves as a~control parameter for the generation of random information bits. Collectively, the results of this dissertation underscore the critical importance of short-time correlations in microscopic systems.

</details>


### [178] [Avalanche Size and Interevent Time Statistics in Amorphous System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24462)
*Thomas Muhren,Roberto Benzi,Mauro Sbragaglia,Jeannot Trampert,Federico Toschi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文在剪切作用下软玻璃态系统（soft-glassy system）的场论模型中研究雪崩统计（avalanche statistics），发现雪崩大小（avalanche size）与事件间隔时间（interevent time）均服从幂律分布，且其标度指数（scaling exponent）满足精确关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无定形系统（amorphous systems）的间歇性黏滑流动（stick-slip flow）以雪崩为特征，其统计性质呈现稳健的幂律。雪崩大小已被广泛刻画，但事件间隔时间的统计仍基本未被探索，构成研究缺口。

Method: 采用剪切作用下软玻璃态系统的场论模型（field theoretical model），以流度（fluidity）为序参量（order parameter），其与软玻璃中局部塑性事件（plastic events）的速率相关。

Result: 雪崩大小 S 与事件间隔时间 t_i 均呈幂律分布，二者的标度指数满足一个精确关系；模型可解析推导标度指数，并重现广泛类别的无定形系统中所观察到的行为。

Conclusion: 该场论模型为无定形系统中雪崩大小与事件间隔时间的普适标度行为提供了统一解释，并给出可解析求解的指数关系。

Abstract: The intermittent, stick-slip flow of amorphous systems is characterized by avalanches whose statistical properties display robust power-laws. In this paper, we study avalanche statistics in a field theoretical model of a soft-glassy system under shear. The order parameter in our model is the fluidity, which is related to the local rate of plastic events in a soft-glass. While avalanche sizes $S$ have been extensively characterized, the statistics of interevent times $t_i$ have remained largely unexplored. Here, we find that both $S$ and $t_i$ are power-law distributed and that their scaling exponents satisfy a precise relation. The model enables the analytical derivation of the scaling exponents and the reproduction of the behavior observed across a wide class of amorphous systems.

</details>


### [179] [Stochastic optimisation method for estimating large deviations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24473)
*Daniël W. H. Cloete,Hugo Touchette*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文介绍并演示了一种名为 LDSTOP 的随机优化（stochastic optimisation）方法，用于高效计算大偏差函数（large deviation functions），并将其推广至离散时间马尔可夫链、连续时间跳跃过程与扩散过程三类马尔可夫过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平衡系统（nonequilibrium systems）的涨落刻画通常依赖大偏差函数，而既有方法（谱近似、重要性采样、克隆、分裂等）在效率与可扩展性上存在局限。LDSTOP 旨在提供一种简单、灵活且可扩展的替代方案，通过模拟被逐步引导的单条轨迹来近似"受驱过程"（driven process）。

Method: LDSTOP 结合控制理论（control theory）与机器学习（machine learning）的数值技术，迭代构造"受驱过程"，即马尔可夫过程的受控版本，以最优方式实现给定涨落或大偏差。文中针对三类马尔可夫过程分别定义待优化目标函数，说明受驱过程的多种表示选项（如神经网络），并给出简单应用的实现细节。

Result: 方法在离散时间马尔可夫链（discrete-time Markov chains）、连续时间跳跃过程（continuous-time jump processes）与扩散过程（diffusion processes）上均得到应用演示，展示了其效率以及结合 PyTorch、TensorFlow、JAX 等机器学习包进行随机过程模拟与高维优化问题求解时的易用性。

Conclusion: LDSTOP 因其简单、灵活与可扩展的特性，适用于应用中所考虑的全范围马尔可夫过程，并可借助现有机器学习框架便捷实现，从而支持高效计算大偏差函数与求解高维优化问题。

Abstract: A stochastic optimisation method was recently proposed to efficiently compute large deviation functions, used in statistical physics to characterise the fluctuations of nonequilibrium systems, modelled as Markov processes. The method, called LDSTOP, combines numerical techniques from control theory and machine learning to iteratively construct the "driven process", a controlled version of the Markov process used as a model of nonequilibrium system that realises a given fluctuation or large deviation of that system in an optimal way. Compared to other methods based on spectral approximations, importance sampling, cloning or splitting, LDSTOP is simple, flexible, and scalable, as it works by simulating single trajectories that are gradually guided towards the driven process. Here, we illustrate these advantages by applying the method on the full range of Markov processes considered in applications, namely, discrete-time Markov chains, continuous-time jump processes, and diffusion processes. For each type, we define the objective function to be optimised, explain different options available for representing the driven process (using, e.g., neural networks), and provide implementation details through simple applications. With these contributions, we aim to showcase the method's efficiency, as well as its ease of use when combined with available machine learning packages, such as PyTorch, TensorFlow and JAX, for simulating stochastic processes and solving high-dimensional optimisation problems.

</details>


### [180] [Random-matrix and transport frequencies in eigenstate spectral functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24602)
*Kadir Çeven,Rohit Patil,Marcos Rigol,Fabian Heidrich-Meisner*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文通过精确对角化（exact diagonalization）比较了本征态热化假说（eigenstate thermalization hypothesis, ETH）谱函数平台频率、谱形状因子频率与输运频率三类特征频率，发现前两者一致而输运频率系统偏大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孤立多体量子系统热化由本征态热化假说（ETH）解释，其谱函数低频谱段含长时动力学信息，但ETH相关特征频率与其他文献频率之间缺乏直接定量比较。

Method: 采用精确对角化（exact diagonalization），对具有扩散能量与自旋输运的干净及无序量子自旋梯子（quantum spin ladders）中的流算符（current operators）谱函数进行定量比较。

Result: 在ETH谱函数中观察到预期的低频平台；在所可达系统尺寸下，谱形状因子频率ω_SFF与平台范围一致，而输运频率（transport frequencies）系统性偏大，落在谱函数的非普适区（nonuniversal regime）。

Conclusion: 三类特征频率之间的定量差异表明，尚需更深入理解其来源。

Abstract: The fact that generic isolated many-body quantum systems thermalize is understood using the eigenstate thermalization hypothesis (ETH). In recent years, there has been much interest in the behavior of the ETH spectral functions, which characterize the smooth dependence of the variance of the off-diagonal matrix elements of observables on the associated energy and frequency, and whose low-frequency part contains information about the long-time dynamics. In finite systems described by the ETH, the spectral functions are expected to exhibit plateaus below a characteristic frequency $ω^{}_{\mathrm{ETH}}$. In this regime, the statistics of the matrix elements of observables are expected to be described by random matrix theory. Related frequencies that have been studied in the literature are $ω^{}_{\mathrm{SFF}}$, which controls the onset of random-matrix behavior in the spectral form factor, and the transport frequencies $ω^{}_{\mathrm{tr}}$, which are derived from transport coefficients. However, a direct quantitative comparison of these frequencies is lacking. Using exact diagonalization, we conduct such a comparison for the spectral functions of current operators in clean and disordered quantum spin ladders with diffusive energy and spin transport. We find clear evidence for the expected low-frequency plateaus in the ETH spectral functions. For the accessible system sizes, $ω^{}_{\mathrm{SFF}}$ is consistent with the extent of the plateaus, while the transport frequencies are systematically larger and lie in the nonuniversal regime of the spectral functions. Our findings highlight the need to better understand the origin of these quantitative differences.

</details>


### [181] [Optimal probing scale for current fluctuations in a Brownian gyrator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24748)
*Badr Farih*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究如何为受驱胶体转子（driven colloidal rotor）的循环电流涨落确定最优探测半径。作者以布朗回转器（Brownian gyrator）与高斯包络下的四极剪切（quadrupolar shear）为模型，选取使线性响应完全失效的探测方式，使信号全为二阶，并将响应分解到 m=3 角向扇区。结果表明最优半径由系统两个时标中较快者决定，交叉点位于 Ω=γ，且解析结果经数值模拟验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受驱胶体转子维持的循环电流（circulating current）其涨落需要被界定，而任何此类界都依赖电流对微扰的响应强度与该微扰带来的额外耗散（dissipation）。因此存在一个明确的问题：探测应在何处施加，因为单位 Onsager-Machlup 代价的响应取决探测半径并存在极值。

Method: 针对布朗回转器与高斯包络下的四极剪切，选取一种线性响应（linear response）在每个观测窗口都完全"看不见"的探测，使全部信号为二阶。问题可分离处理：代价为径向且不感知环流，响应位于 m=3 角向扇区，其中预解式（resolvent）化为 b=4 的 Kummer 方程，磁化率（susceptibility）为包络宽度的超几何函数（hypergeometric function）。通过极大化响应与代价之比求最优半径。

Result: 最优探测半径以闭式给出，由系统两个"时钟"中较快者决定：弱驱动下 r*=1.1264√(D/γ)，即陷阱的热半径（thermal radius of the trap）；强驱动下 r*=1.5563√(D/Ω)，即旋转一弧度所扩散的距离，此时陷阱刚度（trap stiffness）完全消失。交叉发生在 Ω=γ。两个极限及前因子（prefactors）均由直接模拟证实。

Conclusion: 循环电流涨落的界定问题可归结为最优探测半径的选取，该半径由驱动强度决定的时标竞争给出解析表达；线性响应盲区下的二阶信号方案使该优化问题得以干净分离并求解，且解析预测与模拟一致。摘要未给出结论性的更广泛推论。

Abstract: A driven colloidal rotor sustains a circulating current whose fluctuations one would like to bound. Any such bound rests on how strongly the current responds to a perturbation and on how much extra dissipation that perturbation costs, so there is a well-posed question of where to push: the response per unit Onsager-Machlup cost depends on the radius at which the probe acts, and is maximised at a definite one. We answer this for the Brownian gyrator and a quadrupolar shear under a Gaussian envelope, a probe chosen so that linear response is exactly blind to it at every observation window, so that the entire signal is second order. The problem separates: the cost is radial and does not see the circulation, while the response lives in the $m=3$ angular sector, where the resolvent reduces to Kummer's equation with $b=4$ and the susceptibility is a hypergeometric function of the envelope width. Maximising the ratio gives the optimal probing radius in closed form. It is set by whichever of the system's two clocks is faster: for weak driving $r^*=1.1264\sqrt{D/γ}$, the thermal radius of the trap, and for strong driving $r^*=1.5563\sqrt{D/Ω}$, the distance diffused in one radian of rotation, with the trap stiffness dropping out entirely. The crossover is at $Ω=γ$. Both limits, and the prefactors, are confirmed against direct simulation.

</details>


### [182] [Navier-Stokes hydrodynamics near the critical point with out-of-equilibrium modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24844)
*Md Hasanujjaman,Golam Sarwar,Mahfuzur Rahaman,Abhijit Bhattacharyya,Jane Alam*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究探讨慢非平衡模式（out-of-equilibrium modes, OEM）如何重塑临界点附近动力学密度涨落的功率谱，发现OEM通过约束耦合方式压低峰宽并导致Brillouin峰高度不对称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在临界点附近扩展流体力学（hydrodynamics）的有效适用范围，需要引入慢非平衡模式（OEM），但其对动力学密度涨落功率谱的影响尚不明确，故本文对此进行研究。

Method: 采用慢模式的运动方程（equation of motion），在引入OEM不改变热力学广延性（extensivity）的情形下进行分析；广延性条件对OEM与速度四维散度（four divergence of velocity）的耦合施加了额外约束。

Result: 无OEM时动态结构因子（dynamic structure factor）呈三个洛伦兹峰（Lorentzian peaks）：零频处的Rayleigh峰与对称分布于ω=0两侧的Brillouin峰。远离临界点时，引入OEM保持分布对称性，但所有峰宽减小、Brillouin峰高度不等；无论OEM是否为零，Brillouin峰在临界点附近均消失，且Rayleigh峰宽度在存在OEM时显著减小。

Conclusion: OEM存在时峰宽的减小表明涨落衰减率（decay rate）降低，即涨落衰减变慢（slowing down），这是临界点的显著特征。

Abstract: We investigate how slow out-of-equilibrium modes (OEM), introduced to extend the regime of validity of hydrodynamics near the critical point, fundamentally reshape the power spectrum of dynamical density fluctuations. We have used the equation of motion of slow modes for the situation when the extensive nature of thermodynamics is not altered due to the introduction of OEM. We find that the extensivity condition puts an extra constraint on the coupling of OEM with the four divergence of velocity. In the absence of OEM, the dynamic structure factor exhibits three Lorentzian peaks, one at zero frequency ($ω$), called Rayleigh peak and the other two called Brillouin peaks located symmetrically about $ω=0$. When the system is away from the critical point the inclusion of the OEM preserves the symmetry of the distributions but all the peaks acquire reduced widths and the Brillouin peaks adopt unequal heights. Irrespective of the value of OEM (zero or nonzero) the Brillouin peaks vanish near the critical point. The width of the Rayleigh peak substantially reduced in presence of OEM. Such reductions of the widths in presence of the OEM indicates the reduction in the decay rate of the fluctuation which leads to slowing down of decay of the fluctuations, a distinctive feature of the critical point.

</details>


### [183] [Channel concentration of critical quantum geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24905)
*Qian-Rui Lee,Daw-Wei Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 量子度规（quantum metric）只刻画基态沿参数方向的总响应，却无法分辨各激发通道如何分担该响应。本文定义通道集中度（channel concentration, CC），并借助精确有限尺寸定理与共形场论（CFT）导出多个模型的普适集中度值（如临界横场 Ising 模型 2/3、半填充 XX 配对响应 1/3）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有量子度规仅给出总响应强度，无法揭示响应在激发通道间的分布结构，因而可能掩盖不同物理情形下的本质差异。本文旨在引入可分辨通道分布的集中度指标，以刻画总度规标度所遗漏的响应区分。

Method: 将通道集中度定义为指定激发通道（如动量块）上归一化响应权重的平方和。通过精确有限尺寸定理计算临界横场 Ising 模型与半填充 XX 模型的集中度；在 XY Lifshitz 点分析场与各向异性微扰的集中度差异；并在 1+1 维幺正 CFT 圆环上，对由单个空间积分标量主算符微扰的非简并真空，推导含后代（descendants）的完整零动量能级响应权重。

Result: 精确结果给出临界横场 Ising 模型场响应热力学集中度为 2/3，半填充 XX 配对响应为 1/3，尽管两者主导度规标度相同。对 0<Δ<3/2 的标度维度，权重确定归一化响应分布与精确普适集中度函数，其复现 Ising 格点极限 2/3，并给出三态 Potts 热场约 0.8515（仅用指数近似约为 0.800）。有限尺寸相互作用计算进一步比较多体能级上的集中度与排序响应权重。

Conclusion: 这些精确基准揭示了总度规标度所遗漏的响应区分，并可作为与有限尺寸相互作用谱比较的参照，为识别不同激发通道响应结构提供判据。

Abstract: The quantum metric quantifies the total ground-state response along a parameter direction, but does not resolve how excitations share that response. We define channel concentration (CC) as the sum of squared normalized response weights over specified excitation channels, such as momentum blocks. An exact finite-size theorem yields thermodynamic CCs of 2/3 for the field response of the critical transverse-field Ising model and 1/3 for the half-filled XX pairing response, despite the same leading metric scaling. In finite-size approaches to the XY Lifshitz point, field and anisotropy perturbations yield different concentrations despite a common limiting Hamiltonian with quadratic dispersion. In a unitary 1+1-dimensional conformal field theory (CFT) on a circle, we consider a nondegenerate vacuum in a fixed sector perturbed by one spatially integrated scalar primary. We derive complete zero-momentum energy-level response weights, including descendants. For scaling dimension $0<Δ<3/2$, these weights determine the normalized response distribution and an exact universal concentration function. The expression reproduces the exact Ising lattice limit 2/3 and gives approximately 0.8515 for the three-state Potts thermal field, compared with approximately 0.800 from an exponent-only approximation. Finite-size interacting calculations compare concentrations and ranked response weights over many-body energy levels. These exact benchmarks show which response distinctions total metric scaling misses and guide comparisons with finite-size interacting spectra.

</details>


### [184] [Quantum Mpemba effect from Stark localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24993)
*Nico Albert,Masudul Haque,Shovan Dutta*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该工作提出利用斯塔克局域化（Stark localization）设计量子能量景观，并借助局域非相干跳跃（incoherent hopping）作为冷却机制，从而在量子系统中实现具有参量分离时间尺度的姆潘巴效应（Mpemba effect）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典系统中，崎岖势能景观（rugged potential energy landscape）为姆潘巴效应（更热初态冷却更快）提供了清晰机制，但该图像在量子系统中通常不成立。因此，作者旨在构造一种可设计量子能量景观以实现量子姆潘巴效应。

Method: 采用斯塔克局域化设计能量景观，以局域非相干跳跃（incoherent hopping）作为冷却机制；跳跃由稀少的“探测”事件（detection events）触发，其速率随能量增加。进一步考虑在位吸引相互作用（attractive on-site interactions）下束缚对（bound pairs）的集体跳跃。

Result: 更热的态冷却更快，且对局域初态产生超指数分离（super-exponentially separated）的冷却速率；在吸引在位相互作用存在时，束缚对的集体跳跃使该效应显著增强。该效应具有可在现有实验平台（present-day setups）中观测的清晰实验特征（experimental signatures）。

Conclusion: 通过斯塔克局域化与能量依赖的探测触发跳跃相结合，可在量子系统中实现时间尺度参数化分离的姆潘巴效应，且经束缚对集体跳跃增强，具备当前实验可验证性。

Abstract: In classical systems, rugged potential energy landscapes provide a transparent mechanism for the celebrated Mpemba effect, in which hotter initial states cool down faster. This picture generally does not survive in quantum systems. Here we show how to design a quantum energy landscape with local dissipation leading to an Mpemba effect with parametrically separated timescales. Our approach uses Stark localization to design an energy landscape and localized incoherent hopping as cooling mechanism. The hops are triggered by rare "detection" events whose rate grows with energy, allowing hotter states to cool faster and producing super-exponentially separated cooling rates for localized initial states. We further show that the effect is dramatically enhanced by collective hopping of bound pairs in the presence of attractive on-site interactions. These findings have clear experimental signatures accessible in present-day setups.

</details>


<div id='cs.SD'></div>

# cs.SD [[Back]](#toc)

### [185] [Causal limits and optimal allocation for passive frequency-selective hearing protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22395)
*Geonhwi Hwang,Jaewoo Lee,Hyunjun Kim,Hyeonwoo Na*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 将耳塞有限空腔体积视为设计资源，利用因果性给出的传输损耗守恒积分推导音频带预算 B_ad = (20/ln10)·π²V/(2cS)，并以危险加权注水法（water-filling）分配隔声预算，得到约 1.3 dB 防护换取 1 dB 语音代价的交换率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动式亥姆霍兹共振耳塞（Helmholtz-resonator earplugs）需在衰减危险噪声的同时保持语音可听，而耳廓尺寸壳体的空气体积有限，因此需要明确该体积作为设计资源如何被"花费"与分配。

Method: 针对零频透明、由无功侧支（reactive side branches）加载的带孔管道，由因果性（causality）确定传输损耗（transmission loss）的守恒积分，并以器件变量表达；随后加入黏性（viscous）与侧翼（flanking）约束，采用危险加权注水法（hazard-weighted water-filling）配合连续语音代价价格进行预算分配。

Result: 音频带预算 B_ad = (20/ln10)·π²V/(2cS) 由总腔体积 V 与管道面积 S 之比决定，全封闭（occluding）耳塞不受此约束；f² 价格信号偏向远高于声源峰值的频率；以语音代价价格替代严格透明后，模型给出约 1.3 dB 防护／1 dB 语音代价的交换率，预算在其饱和处确定；三种外形中低频选择性设计维持在约 1 dB，而危险匹配的中/高频设计可达 12 dB。

Conclusion: 制约设计的关键变量是管道面积与语音允许量（speech allowance），而非共振器数量（resonator count）。

Abstract: Passive Helmholtz-resonator earplugs aim to attenuate hazardous noise while keeping speech audible; the air volume of an ear-sized shell is finite, and we treat it as the resource a design spends. For a vented bore transparent at zero frequency and loaded by reactive side branches, causality fixes a conserved integral of transmission loss. We derive it in device variables, add viscous and flanking constraints, and allocate the budget by hazard-weighted water-filling with a continuous speech-cost price. The audio-band budget B_ad = (20/ln10) pi^2 V / 2cS is set by the ratio of total cavity volume V to bore area S; occluding plugs are exempt. The f^2 price signal favours frequencies well above the source peak. Replacing strict transparency by a speech-cost price gives an exchange rate of about 1.3 dB of protection per decibel of speech cost under the present model, with the budget setting where it saturates. Across three form factors, low-frequency-selective designs stay near a decibel while hazard-matched mid/high-frequency designs reach twelve. The binding variables are bore area and speech allowance, not resonator count.

</details>


<div id='physics.soc-ph'></div>

# physics.soc-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [186] [Inferring the microscopic mechanisms of opinion dynamics using a kinetic Ising model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23690)
*Ixandra Achitouv,David Chavalarias,Vincent Lahoche*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 该研究从为期一年的在线社交网络数据中推断观点更新的转移概率，验证其可由 kinetic Ising 热浴动力学准确描述，并将推断参数与社会学含义对应，同时揭示参数与网络拓扑的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kinetic Ising 模型被广泛用于描述二元观点动力学，但其微观层面的有效性鲜有实证检验，本文旨在填补这一实证空白。

Method: 从一年期在线社交网络中推断支配观点更新的转移概率，识别其符合 Ising heat-bath dynamics，并将推断参数解释为外在场（intrinsic bias）、耦合强度（social influence）与持续性项（temporal inertia）；随后在实证时序网络上进行 Monte Carlo 模拟。

Result: 推断的转移概率可被 Ising 热浴动力学准确描述；持续性（persistence）与节点度（node degree）正相关，且持续性与相互作用强度均与全局网络异质性（heterogeneity）和聚类（clustering）强相关；使用时变参数模拟可准确重现观测到的响应函数、翻转概率与宏观观点动力学。

Conclusion: 在 Twitter 气候辩论情境下，结果为在线观点形成的 kinetic Ising 描述提供直接实证支持，并建立微观社会行为与演化网络拓扑之间的定量联系。

Abstract: Kinetic Ising models are widely used to describe binary opinion dynamics, but their microscopic validity has rarely been tested empirically. Here, we infer the transition probabilities governing opinion updates from a year-long online social network and show that they are accurately described by an Ising heat-bath dynamics. The inferred parameters admit a direct sociological interpretation: the external field quantifies intrinsic bias, the coupling strength measures social influence, and a persistence term captures temporal inertia. We further show that persistence is positively correlated with node degree, while both persistence and interaction strength are strongly correlated with global network heterogeneity and clustering. Using the inferred time-dependent parameters in Monte Carlo simulations on the empirical temporal networks, we accurately reproduce the observed response functions, flip probabilities, and macroscopic opinion dynamics. Within the Twitter climate debate, our results provide direct empirical support for a kinetic Ising description of online opinion formation and establish a quantitative link between microscopic social behavior and evolving network topology.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [187] [Divergent strategies and convergent outcomes in autonomous materials discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23957)
*Jihan Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 科学智能体（scientific agents）的评估通常聚焦任务完成或结果复现，本研究转而考察开放式重复实验（open-ended campaigns）中的变异。16 个独立初始化的同配置会话在冻结数据库、固定协议与一周预算下探索金属有机框架（metal-organic frameworks）的甲烷存储目标，策略分化为四种路径，但最终收敛于相同材料前沿，同时暴露出一致性误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学智能体评估多关注是否完成任务或复现已知结果，而未考察其在重复开放式研究中的变异性与结论稳健性。该研究旨在通过多次独立运行，检验智能体结论的一致性及其潜在的系统性偏差来源。

Method: 单一模型-框架配置（model-harness configuration）被独立初始化 16 次，均获得含 12,499 个金属有机框架的冻结数据库、甲烷存储目标、固定协议（pinned protocol）与一周预算。随后对各会话策略、筛选结构数量及假设结构构建进行比较，并对半数智能体施加强制检查（enforced checks），对比结论有效性与复现情况。

Result: 策略分化为四种方法，筛选结构数量跨 100 至 5,000 不等，其中 8 次会话构建了 2,253 个假设结构。但智能体均发现约 200 cm³/cm³ 附近的相同材料前沿（materials frontier）；独立计算的数据库多孔区域前九优结构均出现在其报告中。强制检查使复现率由 8 次中的 1 次提升至 8 次中的 8 次（即全部复现），却未能可检测地改善结论有效性，因为 16 个智能体中有 15 个选择了同一被审计排除的条目——该不完整结构因缺失阴离子而产生了虚假孔体积。

Conclusion: 重复运行的智能体既表现出稳健的结论收敛，也因共享输入而产生共模误差（common-mode errors）。这表明仅凭结论一致性或复现性不足以证明结论有效性，评估需关注共享数据与协议引入的系统性偏差，而非仅考察任务完成或结果复现。

Abstract: Scientific agents are mostly evaluated on whether they complete tasks or recover known results; we instead study variation across repeated open-ended campaigns. Sixteen separately initialized sessions of one model-harness configuration received a frozen database of 12,499 metal-organic frameworks, a methane-storage objective, a pinned protocol and a one-week budget. Strategies diverged into four approaches spanning 100--5,000 screened structures, and eight built 2,253 hypothetical structures. Yet the agents recovered the same materials frontier near 200 cm^3/cm^3, and an independent calculation of the database's porous region found its nine best structures all among their reports. Enforced checks on half the agents raised fresh-run reproduction from one of eight to eight of eight but could not detectably improve conclusion validity, because fifteen of sixteen agents selected the same audit-excluded entry, an incomplete structure whose missing anions created artificial pore volume. Replicated agents thus reveal both robust conclusions and common-mode errors from shared inputs.

</details>


<div id='math.OA'></div>

# math.OA [[Back]](#toc)

### [188] [A complete characterization of eventually entanglement breaking unital quantum channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24168)
*B. V. Rajarama Bhat,Pankaj Dey,Biswarup Saha*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 该研究给出了幺正信道（unital channel）最终纠缠破缺（eventually entanglement breaking, EEB）的完整刻画，将其等价于若干特征向量的简单对易条件，并将结论推广至幺正量子信道单参数半群及幺正 EB 信道的凸集极值点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最终纠缠破缺信道的判定此前缺乏简单完备的刻画，作者旨在以特征向量的对易性质给出等价条件，并厘清 EEB 与最终正部分转置（eventually positive partial transpose, PPT）、最终混合扭曲退相位（eventually mixed twisted dephasing）之间的关系。

Method: 采用受 Watrous 结果启发的矩阵积分技巧（matrix integral technique），分析幺正信道特征向量的对易性质，并借助等角紧框架（equiangular tight frames, ETFs）理论研究凸集的极值点。

Result: 对幺正信道证明四条性质等价：(i) EEB；(ii) 最终 PPT；(iii) 最终混合扭曲退相位；(iv) 非零特征值对应特征向量与所有周缘特征向量对易。幺正量子信道单参数半群有类似结论，且连续性迫使此类半群成为本原的（primitive）。此外刻画了幺正 EB 信道凸集的极值点，并识别出扭曲退相位信道之外的若干极值点。

Conclusion: EEB 幺正信道可完全由其非零特征值特征向量的对易性质决定；该结论在单参数半群情形下因连续性而退化为本原性，并为幺正 EB 信道凸集的极值点分类提供了新工具与实例。

Abstract: We obtain a complete characterization of eventually entanglement breaking (EEB) unital channels in terms of some simple commutation properties of their eigenvectors. Specifically, the following properties are equivalent for a unital channel: (i) it is EEB; (ii) it is eventually positive partial transpose (PPT); (iii) it is eventually mixed twisted dephasing; and (iv) its eigenvectors corresponding to non-zero eigenvalues commute with all its peripheral eigenvectors. Similar results hold for one-parameter semigroups of unital quantum channels, where continuity forces such semigroups to become primitive. We employ a matrix integral technique inspired by a result of Watrous.
