We also characterize the extreme points of the convex set of unital EB channels and, using this characterization and the theory of equiangular tight frames (ETFs), identify several extreme points beyond the twisted dephasing channels.

</details>

### [189] [Matching Rules for a Three-Dimensional Strongly Aperiodic Monotile](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23783)
*Felix Flicker*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文研究三维强非周期单调铺砌（strongly aperiodic monotile）所依赖的面装饰（face decoration）条件，证明此类装饰要求可被大幅弱化，甚至仅凭方块颜色匹配规则即可强制产生同一铺砌结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期预印本（arXiv:2609.19214）在 Lee 与 Moody 提出的三维 Chair 铺砌基础上，通过面装饰强制非周期性，但原规则较为复杂，不利于寻找物理实现方式。本文旨在提炼面装饰的一般性要求，为物理系统实现强非周期单调铺砌提供便利。

Method: 在三维 Chair 瓦片（3D Chair tile）的面上引入基于三色箭头（three colours of arrow）的匹配规则（matching rules），该规则与原始规则不等价，但通过迫使 Chair 组合成线度加倍的低维“Superchair”实现同一铺砌；并进一步放宽约束，改用仅依赖方块颜色、与朝向无关的匹配规则。

Result: 发现面装饰的要求是极小的（minimal）；三色箭头匹配规则可强制生成 Superchair，且匹配规则自身唯一地复合（compose uniquely），构成本文及早期证明的核心；进一步放松后可仅用基于方块颜色、忽略朝向的单一匹配规则强制出相同结构。

Conclusion: 任何在几何或其他层面编码这些规则的物理系统，均可强制实现该强非周期单调铺砌；文中给出了简单示例。该结果为该单调瓦片的物理实现提供了更宽松、更易操作的设计条件。

Abstract: A recent pre-print [arXiv:2609.19214] proposed a three-dimensional (3D) strongly aperiodic monotile: a shape that tiles Euclidean space only aperiodically and which admits no symmetry of infinite order. The proof takes the 3D Chair tile identified previously by Lee and Moody, and adds geometric decorations to the faces so as to force aperiodicity (without these decorations The Chair also admits periodic tilings). Here we establish general requirements on face decorations to achieve the same end, in order to facilitate the search for physical realisations. We find that the requirements are minimal. We provide matching rules using three colours of arrow. They are not equivalent to the original rules, but force the same tiling by forcing Chairs to compose into `Superchairs' with doubled linear dimensions. In this process the matching rules themselves compose uniquely, which is the core of the earlier proof. Relaxing this constraint further we find that the same structure can be forced using only a matching rule based on the colours of squares, regardless of orientation. Any physical system encoding these rules (geometrically or otherwise) will force the strongly aperiodic monotiling. We provide simple examples.

</details>


### [190] [THz Spectroscopy of Urine Vapors from Patients with Prostate Cancer and Benign Prostatic Hyperplasia: A Pilot Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23814)
*V. A. Atduev,A. V. Maslennikova,V. L. Vaks,E. G. Domracheva,M. B. Chernyaeva,V. A. Anfertev,K. A. Atduev,Y. Tawalbeh,M. F. Pereira*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 一项探索性先导研究利用高分辨率太赫兹光谱分析尿液挥发性及热分解产物，在24例前列腺癌与14例良性前列腺增生患者间发现候选光谱特征差异，用于后续评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约13%男性终生会被诊断为前列腺癌，而血清前列腺特异性抗原（PSA）筛查虽广泛应用，其升高并非癌症特异，也可出现于前列腺炎与良性前列腺增生等良性病变，因此需要可提供互补分子信息的非侵入性手段。

Method: 采用高分辨率太赫兹光谱（terahertz spectroscopy）对尿液来源的挥发性及热分解产物进行检测，纳入24例前列腺癌患者与14例良性前列腺增生患者的尿液样本，比较其分子归属模式与候选光谱特征。研究定位为候选标志物识别与可行性评估。

Result: 在前列腺癌与良性前列腺增生两组样本间，观察到已报道的分子归属模式及候选光谱特征存在差异。研究未评估诊断准确性，也未评估其相对PSA的优越性。

Conclusion: 结果支持将太赫兹光谱作为与PSA及其他成熟临床评估并列的潜在互补分子信息来源加以进一步研究；同时指出在考虑常规临床应用前，尚需解决技术标准化（technical standardization）与临床验证（clinical validation）方面的要求。

Abstract: Approximately 13% of men will be diagnosed with prostate cancer (PC) during their lifetime. Serum prostate-specific antigen (PSA) is widely used for screening and risk assessment; however, PSA elevations are not cancer-specific and may also occur in benign conditions such as prostatitis and benign prostatic hyperplasia (BPH). Additional non-invasive approaches capable of provid- ing complementary molecular information are therefore needed. Here, we present an exploratory pilot study using high-resolution terahertz (THz) spectroscopy to examine urine-derived volatile and thermal-decomposition products. Analysis of urine samples from 24 patients with PC and 14 patients with BPH identified differences in the reported molecular-assignment patterns and candi- date spectral features for further evaluation. The study was designed for candidate identification and feasibility assessment and did not evaluate diagnostic accuracy or superiority to PSA. These findings support further investigation of THz spectroscopy as a potential source of complementary molecular information alongside PSA and other established clinical assessments. The study also outlines the technical standardization and clinical-validation requirements that must be addressed before this approach can be considered for routine clinical use.

</details>


<div id='math.DG'></div>

# math.DG [[Back]](#toc)

### [191] [Dirac-harmonic maps from manifolds with boundary via index theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24551)
*Volker Branding,Nicolas Ginoux*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文研究 Dirac-调和映射（Dirac-harmonic maps），即量子场论中超对称非线性 sigma 模型（supersymmetric non-linear sigma model）的数学版本；通过带边界流形的指标理论（index theory）建立一般存在性结果，并给出若干具体应用与基于扭转旋量（twistor spinors）的显式例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dirac-调和映射作为无界能量泛函（unbounded energy functional）的临界点出现，其一般存在性结果（general existence results）的建立具有相当难度。本文旨在为这一难题提供基于指标理论的解决途径。

Method: 主要手段是建立基于带边界流形指标理论的一般存在性结果。关键论据在于：边界的存在使 Atiyah-Patodi-Singer 指标公式（Atiyah-Patodi-Singer index formula）中出现额外贡献项，从而使指标（index）可以非零。此外，还给出基于曲面上扭转旋量（twistor spinors on surfaces）的显式例子。

Result: 证明了一个基于带边界流形指标理论的一般存在性结果，并将其应用于多种情形；同时构造了若干基于曲面扭转旋量的显式例子。

Conclusion: 借助边界项导致的非零指标，可有效处理 Dirac-调和映射的存在性问题；该框架在多种情形下适用，并可由扭转旋量给出具体实例。

Abstract: Dirac-harmonic maps are a mathematical version of the supersymmetric non-linear sigma model of quantum field theory. Since they arise as critical points of an unbounded energy functional, it is a challenging task to establish general existence results. Within this manuscript we mainly prove a general existence result based on index theory for manifolds with boundary and apply it in various situations. Due to the presence of a boundary there are additional contributions in the Atiyah-Patodi-Singer index formula allowing for a non-vanishing index, which is the key argument in our proofs. Further explicit examples based on twistor spinors on surfaces are also presented.

</details>


<div id='math.AP'></div>

# math.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [192] [Finite time singularity formation for a nonlocal Boussinesq system arising in cold-plasma dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22911)
*Diego Alonso-Orán,Rafael Granero-Belinchón*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文研究冷等离子体动力学（cold-plasma dynamics）中一类非局部 Boussinesq 系统（nonlocal Boussinesq system）的有限时间奇点形成（finite-time singularity formation）。证明在特定对称初值条件下会发生有限时间爆破（finite-time blow-up），并给出生命期（lifespan）的显式上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究对象来自冷等离子体动力学，属于非局部 Boussinesq 系统的奇点分析问题。目的是刻画何种初值条件会导致有限时间爆破，从而理解该模型的解的整体存在性与爆破机制。

Method: 采用解析分析手段处理该系统：考虑光滑且对称（smooth, symmetric）的初值，要求总密度严格为正（strictly positive total density）且原点处速度斜率充分为负（sufficiently negative velocity slope at the origin）。通过推导生命期的显式上界来完成论证。

Result: 在满足上述条件的初值下，解于有限时间内发生爆破。具体地，论文给出生命期（lifespan）的显式上界，并证明速度梯度（velocity gradient）变为无界。

Conclusion: 对称性、正的总密度与原点处充分负的速度斜率构成该非局部 Boussinesq 系统有限时间爆破的充分条件，爆破表现为速度梯度的无界增长。

Abstract: We study finite-time singularity formation for a nonlocal Boussinesq system arising in cold-plasma dynamics. We show that smooth, symmetric initial data with strictly positive total density and a sufficiently negative velocity slope at the origin lead to finite-time blow-up. More precisely, we give an explicit upper bound for the lifespan and show that the velocity gradient becomes unbounded.

</details>


### [193] [Directional Tangency and Anomalous Far-Field Decay for Schr{ö}dinger Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23472)
*Ruming Zhang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文识别出薛定谔方程（Schrödinger equations）中导致异常远场衰减（anomalous far-field decay）的方向切触机制（directional tangency mechanism）。通过基于费米面（Fermi surface）与观测方向的分解分析，表明几何对齐（geometric alignment）是该异常行为的关键，并在含势情形下通过首次玻恩修正（first Born correction）复现该机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在解释薛定谔方程远场衰减中出现的异常行为，并揭示其背后的几何机制，即所谓的方向切触机制。该问题的核心在于理解衰减阶数如何由源项奇异性与观测方向之间的几何关系所决定。

Method: 对常系数算子（constant-coefficient operators），基于费米面几何与观测方向引入极限预解式（limiting resolvent）的方向分解。在二维情形下，分别考察解析源、位于方向切触集上的横向非解析性（transverse non-analyticity），以及奇异点偏离切触集的情况；进一步构造有界、衰减的实值势，使同一机制通过有效源 \(Vu_0\) 出现在首次玻恩修正中。

Result: 二维情形下，解析源仅产生超代数小（super-algebraically small）的切触贡献；而位于方向切触集上的横向非解析性则产生阶为 \(R^{-(2\alpha+1)}\) 的代数贡献。将奇异性移离切触集可恢复超代数衰减，说明几何对齐至关重要。在含势情形中，所得切触贡献在合适的远程区域（long-range regime）内比常规二维远场项衰减更慢。

Conclusion: 异常远场衰减由源项奇异性与观测方向在费米面几何意义上的方向切触所驱动，几何对齐是其必要条件。该机制不仅适用于常系数算子，也可在势的首次玻恩修正中通过有效源 \(Vu_0\) 出现，并在远程区域内主导二维远场衰减。

Abstract: We identify a directional tangency mechanism responsible for anomalous far-field decay in Schrödinger equations. For constant-coefficient operators, we introduce a directional decomposition of the limiting resolvent based on the geometry of the Fermi surface and the observation direction. In two dimensions, we show that an analytic source produces a super-algebraically small tangency contribution, whereas a transverse non-analyticity located on the directional tangency set generates an algebraic contribution of order \(R^{-(2α+1)}\). Shifting the singularity away from the tangency set restores super-algebraic decay, showing that geometric alignment is essential to the anomalous behavior. We further construct a bounded, decaying real-valued potential for which the same mechanism occurs in the first Born correction through the effective source \(Vu_0\). In a suitable long-range regime, the resulting tangency contribution decays more slowly than the conventional two-dimensional far-field term.

</details>


### [194] [The uniform-in-time electrostatic limit of the linearised Vlasov-Maxwell system: the Poisson equilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23908)
*Marnie Smith*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该研究在线性化框架下量化了光速有限对 Vlasov-Maxwell 扰动的影响：其与线性化 Vlasov-Poisson 对应解之差在时间上一致有界，误差为初始偏差加上 \(c^{-1}\) 阶项，且该阶数是最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于理解光速 \(c\) 作为大参数时，Vlasov-Maxwell 系统与经典 Vlasov-Poisson 系统之间的定量差异，特别是横向场可能保持量级一时这种差异是否仍可控制。

Method: 方法为在 \(\mathbb{R}^3\) 上围绕 Poisson 平衡态对带 Newtonian 粒子输运的 Vlasov-Maxwell 系统作线性化，并将 \(c\) 视为大参数；通过沿自由输运控制横向场（Klein-Gordon 型色散）的累积效应，与线性化 Vlasov-Poisson 对应解进行比较。

Result: 结果表明，对一致受控的初值，Vlasov-Maxwell 扰动与其线性化 Vlasov-Poisson 对应解之差在时间上被初始偏差加上 \(c^{-1}\) 阶项一致界住；散射态也有相同估计，且 \(c\) 的收敛阶数是最优的。纵向动力学满足与线性化 Vlasov-Poisson 相同的 Volterra 方程并呈现 Landau 阻尼。

Conclusion: 结论是：在光速很大时，横向场不影响纵向动力学的 Landau 阻尼行为，仅对整体解引入 \(c^{-1}\) 阶的一致修正，且该修正不能改进。

Abstract: The Vlasov-Maxwell system with Newtonian particle transport is linearised about the Poisson equilibrium on $\mathbb{R}^3$, with the speed of light $c$ treated as a large parameter. For uniformly controlled initial data, with transverse fields that may remain of order one, the difference between the Vlasov-Maxwell perturbation and its linearised Vlasov-Poisson counterpart is bounded uniformly in time by their initial discrepancy plus a term of order $c^{-1}$. The same estimate holds for their scattering states, and the rate in $c$ is sharp. The longitudinal dynamics are governed by the same Volterra equation as in linearised Vlasov-Poisson and undergo Landau damping. The transverse fields exhibit Klein-Gordon-type dispersion, and control of their accumulated effect along free transport yields the uniform comparison.

</details>


### [195] [On rigidity of stationary black holes under a nontrapping assumption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23872)
*Alexandru F. Radu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 在无非束缚正交零测地线（non-trapped null geodesic）条件下，证明光滑、正则、渐近平直（asymptotically flat）的稳态真空（stationary vacuum）黑洞外部必为非常极端克尔（nonextremal Kerr）时空。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是刻画稳态真空黑洞的唯一性（uniqueness），将克尔（Kerr）解的特征从轴对称（axisymmetry）假设推广到由非束缚条件直接导出的情形。

Method: 选取递增区域以延展局部旋转对称性（rotational symmetry）；在无能层（ergoregion）中研究极限边界，其为稳态商度量（stationary quotient metric）的类时光子面（timelike photon surface）。由非束缚性导出沿完整旋转轨道的一致估计，并借助短零线段上的凸性控制边界；商空间零测地线可提升为与稳态基灵场（stationary Killing field）正交的时空零测地线。

Result: 所得边界几何无需预先的正则性假设；结合圆性（circularity），可将零测地线延续通过能量面接触处，其稳态投影限于紧集，由此与非束缚性矛盾，从而得到全局轴对称性（global axisymmetry）。

Conclusion: 非束缚条件蕴含全局轴对称性，进而推出外部区域为非常极端克尔时空，给出该情形下的黑洞唯一性结论。

Abstract: We prove that a smooth, regular, asymptotically flat stationary vacuum black hole has a nonextremal Kerr exterior if no null geodesic orthogonal to its stationary Killing field is trapped. We extend the local rotational symmetry by choosing successive domains whose limiting boundary in the ergoregion, if nonempty, is locally a timelike photon surface for the stationary quotient metric. To obtain this geometry without an initial regularity assumption on the boundary, we derive uniform estimates along complete rotational orbits from nontrapping and use convexity along short null segments to control the boundary. Its null geodesics lift to spacetime null geodesics orthogonal to the stationary field. Circularity allows us to continue them through ergosurface contact with stationary projection confined to a compact set, contradicting nontrapping and giving global axisymmetry.

</details>


### [196] [Strict ground-level gaps on long-pendant generalized star graphs for nonlinear Dirac equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24618)
*Zhipeng Yang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究非紧度量图（noncompact metric graphs）上自治非线性 Dirac 方程（autonomous nonlinear Dirac equations）的严格基态能级间隙（strict ground-level gaps）。证明在本质上约化（essential reduction）中存在足够长悬挂路径（pendant path）时，基态能级严格低于无穷远处的全直线能级；并给出一类不存在该间隙的图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究的动机在于刻画非紧度量图上非线性 Dirac 方程基态能级是否严格低于其无穷远处能级，即是否存在严格的基态能级间隙（ground-level gap）。由于沿半直线逃逸的集中序列在远离所有顶点重定位后所见为实直线，无穷远处能级自然由自治全直线能级 \(d_λ^\infty=d_{λ,\mathbb R}\) 给出。

Method: 证明使用了对称全直线基态（symmetric full-line ground state）的半直线限制、一致指数衰减（uniform exponential decay）、针对质量型 Dirac-Kirchhoff 谱投影（massive Dirac-Kirchhoff spectral projections）的预解式局域化（resolvent localization），以及广义 Nehari 纤维（generalized Nehari fibres）的紧性论证。结论在 \(λ\) 于紧子区间 \(I\Subset(-mc^2,mc^2)\) 上一致成立。

Result: 在本质上约化中存在足够长悬挂路径时，得到严格不等式 \(d_λ(\mathcal G)<d_λ^\infty\)，并且该间隙对 \(λ\in I\) 一致成立。此外，识别出一类无法存在此类间隙的初等图类：其本质约化与全直线等距的纯透射图（pure transmission graphs）满足 \(d_λ(\mathcal G)=d_λ^\infty\)。

Conclusion: 非紧度量图上自治非线性 Dirac 方程的基态能级间隙由图的本质上约化中足够长的悬挂路径所保证；当本质约化为全直线（如纯透射图）时该间隙消失，能级相等。

Abstract: We study strict ground-level gaps for autonomous nonlinear Dirac equations on noncompact metric graphs. The relevant level at infinity is the autonomous full-line level \(d_λ^\infty=d_{λ,\mathbb R}\), since a concentrating sequence escaping along a half-line sees the real line after recentering far from all vertices. We prove that, uniformly for \(λ\) in a compact subinterval \(I\Subset(-mc^2,mc^2)\), the presence of a sufficiently long pendant path in the essential reduction of the graph implies \[ d_λ(\mathcal G)<d_λ^\infty. \] The proof uses a half-line restriction of a symmetric full-line ground state, uniform exponential decay, resolvent localization for massive Dirac-Kirchhoff spectral projections, and a compactness argument for generalized Nehari fibres. We also identify elementary graph classes for which no such gap can hold: pure transmission graphs whose essential reduction is isometric to the full line satisfy \(d_λ(\mathcal G)=d_λ^\infty\).

</details>


### [197] [Counterexamples to Grad's conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24739)
*Javier Gómez-Serrano,Lukas Liehr,Mitchell A. Taylor*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该工作为每个充分大的 N 构造了嵌入实心环面上的光滑磁流体静力学方程解，其压力面为嵌套环面、磁场仅在圆形磁轴上消失，且（含反号等距）欧氏对称群恰为 N 阶循环群 C_N，并给出 Lean 4 形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁约束等离子体平衡的经典研究（如 Grad 猜想）预期特定对称性（平面反射、轴对称、螺旋对称），本文旨在构造偏离这些预期对称性的新型平衡解，以刻画平衡态模空间（moduli space）的丰富性。

Method: 对每个充分大的 N，直接构造嵌入实心环面（solid torus）上的光滑磁流体静力学（magnetohydrostatic）方程解：其正则压力层级为嵌套环面，磁场恰在圆形磁轴（magnetic axis）上为零；构造给出光滑单参数族，并在嵌入构型的模空间中局部非平凡；同时使用 Lean 4 对该结果进行形式化认证。

Result: 所得解的欧氏对称群（即使允许翻转磁场符号的等距）恰为绕 2π/N 整数倍旋转构成的循环群 C_N；这些平衡态构成光滑单参数族，在嵌入构型模空间中局部非平凡，且不具备 Grad 所猜想（conjectured）的平面反射、轴对称或螺旋对称性。

Conclusion: 结果表明存在一大类精确 C_N 对称的光滑磁流体静力学平衡解，其对称性显著弱于 Grad 猜想所预期的类型，说明相关模空间远比传统对称性假设所提示的更为丰富；结果的 Lean 4 形式化认证进一步提供了机器可检验的可靠性保障。

Abstract: For each sufficiently large $N$, we construct smooth solutions of the magnetohydrostatic equations on embedded solid tori whose regular pressure levels are nested tori, whose magnetic field vanishes exactly on a round magnetic axis, and whose group of Euclidean symmetries, even when isometries reversing the sign of the field are admitted, is exactly the cyclic group $C_N$ of rotations through multiples of $2π/N$. For each such $N$, these equilibria form a smooth one-parameter family which is locally nontrivial in the moduli space of embedded configurations and have none of the plane-reflection, axial, or helical symmetries conjectured by Grad. A Lean 4 certification of the above results is also provided.

</details>


<div id='physics.ins-det'></div>

# physics.ins-det [[Back]](#toc)

### [198] [Kinematic Fitting of electromagnetic calorimeter data - an improved method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22242)
*Nicolas Kolanus,Jonas Kohlen,Ulrike Thoma*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 针对运动学拟合（kinematic fitting）中高斯分布（Gaussian distribution）假设不成立的问题，提出将非高斯变量变换为高斯变量的改进方法，从而提升拟合的准确性与稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动学拟合广泛用于粒子物理实验以提高分辨率、压制本底并识别特定反应，但其通常假设被拟合量服从高斯分布，而该假设并非总成立，电磁量能器（electromagnetic calorimeter）的能量测量尤为明显。

Method: 通过对非高斯变量进行变换，使其转换为服从高斯分布的变量，并据此构建新的运动学拟合流程，以更好地处理量能器能量测量的非高斯特性。

Result: 改进后的拟合流程提升了运动学拟合的准确性与稳健性，改善了拟合的 pull 分布与置信水平（confidence-level）分布，同时优化了末态事件样本的不变质量（invariant mass）分布与信噪比（signal-to-background ratio）。

Conclusion: 将非高斯变量变换为高斯变量的运动学拟合方法，能够有效克服高斯假设的局限，为粒子物理实验提供更可靠的重建与筛选结果。

Abstract: Kinematic fitting is widely used in particle physics experiments as a powerful tool to improve experimental resolutions, to suppress background and to provide selection criteria for the identification of specific reactions. Kinematic fitting methods, however, typically assume that the fitted quantities follow Gaussian distributions; an assumption which does not always hold. This is particularly true for energy measurements from electromagnetic calorimeters. This issue can largely be overcome by performing a transformation of the non-Gaussian variables into variables which follow a Gaussian distribution. The new adapted kinematic fitting procedure allows to better account for the non-Gaussian nature of the measured calorimeter energies, at the same time improving the accuracy and robustness of the kinematic fit. This leads to improved pull and confidence-level distributions of the kinematic fit as well as improved invariant mass distributions and signal-to-background ratios in the final event sample.

</details>


### [199] [Real-Time SiPM Pulse Deconvolution for a High-granularity Dual-readout Calorimeter with Neutral Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22269)
*Spencer Allen,Matteo Cremonesi,Jordan Damgov,Erdem Yigit Ertorer,Janna Goodman,Giuseppe Di Guglielmo,James Hirschauer,Shuichi Kunori,Peter Meiring*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估用于高粒度双读出量能器（HG-DREAM）纵向分段的高量化一维卷积神经网络（1D CNN），从80采样、16 ns波形中直接预测脉冲位置。模型在ROC AUC上超过0.95，定点量化损失极小，ASIC实现吞吐量满足FCC-ee触发读出需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HG-DREAM面向FCC-ee，需通过精细三维簇射成像与切伦科夫（Cherenkov）、闪烁（scintillation）信号同时测量来提升能量分辨率。其关键使能技术是基于时间测量的纵向分段，需在光学纤维沿线以切伦科夫光子到达时间区分多个能量沉积。为此需研究可在前端电子学（front-end electronics）中实现的高效脉冲定位算法。

Method: 采用量化的一维卷积神经网络（quantized 1D CNN），直接以200 ps采样、80个采样点、16 ns宽度波形为输入预测脉冲位置。设计三种架构：紧凑基线及两种利用膨胀（dilation）与步幅（stride）达到约20采样感受野（receptive field，由SiPM脉冲宽度决定）的变体，每层使用4至8个滤波器，并做定点量化（fixed-point quantization）。同时在FPGA与ASIC上进行综合实现，并评估成对沉积的分离能力与吞吐量。

Result: 三种架构在ROC AUC上均超过0.95，每层仅需4至8个滤波器。<16,8>定点量化相对浮点参考的AUC损失小于10⁻³。应用于成对沉积时，最小模型在纵向间距20 cm及以上以优于95%的概率分离两个脉冲。400 MHz综合下，模型在Xilinx XCVU13P FPGA上使用9663 LUT，在TSMC 28 nm CMOS中约占18500 μm²并支持权重重配置。ASIC实现可持续4.8 MHz，超过0.5 MHz要求一个数量级；而可重配置权重FPGA实现无法满足该要求。

Conclusion: 在FCC-ee触发读出架构下，最严格的吞吐量需求来自约500 kHz的Z极点（Z-pole）触发率。ASIC实现满足该需求并留有大幅裕量，可重配置权重FPGA则不足。对于无触发（triggerless）架构下40 MHz的Z极点束团交叉频率，本文所开发模型与所针对硬件均无法实现。

Abstract: The High-Granularity Dual-Readout Calorimeter (HG-DREAM) designed for FCC-ee aims to achieve unprecedented energy resolution through fine three-dimensional shower imaging, simultaneous measurement of Cherenkov and scintillation signals, and machine learning capabilities. A key enabling technology is longitudinal segmentation via timing measurements, where multiple energy deposits along optical fibers are distinguished by the arrival times of Cherenkov photons. We present quantized one-dimensional convolutional neural networks that predict pulse locations directly from 80-sample, 16 ns waveforms digitized at 200 ps, and evaluate their implementation in front-end electronics. Three architectures, a compact baseline and two variants using dilation and stride to reach the $\sim$ 20-sample receptive field set by the SiPM pulse width, achieve ROC AUC above 0.95 with four to eight filters per layer. Fixed-point quantization to a $<$16,8$>$ baseline costs less than $10^{-3}$ in AUC relative to the floating-point reference. Applied to pairwise deposits, the smallest model separates two pulses with better than 95\% probability at longitudinal separations of 20 cm and above. Synthesized at 400 MHz, this model uses 9663 LUT on a Xilinx XCVU13P FPGA and occupies $\sim$ 18500 $μ$$m^2$ in TSMC 28 nm CMOS with reconfigurable weights. Assuming a triggered readout architecture for FCC-ee, the $\sim$ 500 kHz Z-pole trigger rate will be the most stringent throughput requirement across all FCC-ee operating modes. The ASIC implementation of the model sustains 4.8 MHz, exceeding the 0.5 MHz requirement by an order of magnitude, while the reconfigurable-weight FPGA implementation fails to do so. Sustaining the 40 MHz FCC-ee Z-pole bunch-crossing frequency in the triggerless architecture is not feasible with the models developed and the hardware targeted in this work.

</details>


### [200] [ARGUS: an experiment-agnostic framework for detector-health monitoring in particle physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23329)
*Kamal Benslama*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: ARGUS 是一个配置驱动（configuration-driven）的探测器健康监测框架，将通用部分（监测器、验证与报告）与探测器特有部分（数据模式与阈值）分离，新探测器只需数据适配器与配置文件即可部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粒子物理实验中探测器健康监测系统常因在调试（commissioning）压力下仓促编写而过于僵化、难以维护，导致许多实验被迫重复重建该系统。

Method: 框架由四个相互独立、仅比较而不合并的监测器构成：专家切割（expert cuts，权威判据）、默认关闭的附加式机器学习二次意见、可在阈值触发前预警的趋势预测（trend forecast），以及支持可插拔统计检验的参考直方图比较；上层告警层将各标志与警告转化为去重通知。在合成参考探测器上以植入病态（planted pathologies）和故障注入（fault-injection）方式构建真值进行验证。

Result: 专家切割以零误报找回全部植入病态；机器学习监测器在注入故障上达到 ROC AUC 0.896，并覆盖未编写切割的故障类别；趋势预测在切割触发前标记漂移通道；直方图比较捕获全部植入形状畸变且对干净样本对保持设定的误报率。第二次演示显示框架在文件布局重组时无需改动代码或配置即可适配。

Conclusion: 所有结果与图表均可仅由代码复现；框架已在两个粒子物理实验中实现并处于评估阶段，是否采用由各合作组决定。

Abstract: Every particle-physics experiment builds detector-health monitoring, and many end up rebuilding it, because a first system written under commissioning pressure often proves too rigid to maintain. ARGUS (Automated Anomaly-detection, Run-quality and General Unified Surveillance) is a configuration-driven framework that separates what is generic in this problem (the health monitors, their validation, and the reporting) from what is not (the data schema and the thresholds). Standing it up for a new detector requires a data adapter and a configuration file, not a rewrite. Detector health is judged by four independent monitors: expert cuts, which are authoritative; a machine-learning second opinion that is additive and off by default; a trend forecast that gives early warning before a threshold is crossed; and a reference-histogram comparison with pluggable statistical tests. The monitors are compared and never merged, and an alerting layer turns their flags and warnings into deduplicated notifications. We demonstrate the full chain on a synthetic reference detector with planted pathologies and a fault-injection campaign that manufactures ground truth: the cuts recover every planted pathology with no false positives, the machine-learning monitor reaches a ROC AUC of 0.896 on injected faults and extends coverage to fault classes no cut was written for, the forecast flags a drifting channel before its cut fires, and the histogram comparison catches every planted shape distortion while holding its configured false-positive rate on clean pairs. A second demonstration shows the framework adapting to a reorganized file layout with no change to code or configuration. Every result and figure in this paper regenerates from the code alone. The framework has been implemented for two particle-physics experiments, where it is under evaluation; adoption decisions rest with the collaborations.

</details>


### [201] [Leveraging Industrial Foundation Models at the Edge of Particle Physics Detectors via Distillation Learning and Hardware Co-design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23385)
*Gia Ancone,Qibin Liu,Liangyu Wu,Julia Gonski*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 该工作首次将工业级时间序列基础模型（foundation model）TimesFM 微调用于粒子物理数据采集（data acquisition, DAQ）任务，并通过蒸馏（distillation）与 FPGA 协同设计实现实时推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来粒子物理实验的 DAQ 系统可受益于 AI/ML 的两类极端发展：大规模基础模型提升特征提取性能，小规模探测器端部署实现实时智能数据处理。目前尚缺针对 DAQ 的工业基础模型微调研究。

Method: 以 Google Research 的 TimesFM（Time Series Foundation Model）骨干为起点，在漂移室径迹探测器（drift chamber tracker）与双读出量能器（dual-readout calorimeter）的实时回归任务上进行微调；随后将微调后的模型蒸馏为学生模型，并与 FPGA 实现协同设计以支持实时运行。

Result: 微调后的蒸馏模型在各任务上达到或超过此前已发表的 AI/ML 方案性能；从 TimesFM 出发的蒸馏与模型压缩流程具有通用性，可适配跨领域多种一维波形（1D waveform）任务。

Conclusion: 结果表明工业时间序列基础模型可有效迁移至粒子物理 DAQ，且“微调—蒸馏—FPGA 协同设计”的流程具备通用性与可移植性，为未来对撞机实时智能数据采集提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Data acquisition (DAQ) systems at future particle physics experiments stand to benefit from the extremes of AI/ML development: large-scale foundation models can enhance the performance of feature extraction algorithms, and small-scale on-detector deployments can enable real-time intelligent data handling. This work provides the first fine-tuning of an industrial foundation model for particle physics DAQ. Starting from the backbone of Google Research's TimesFM (Time Series Foundation Model), we demonstrate fine-tuning on real-time regression tasks for drift chamber trackers and dual-readout calorimeters. Furthermore, the fine-tuned TimesFM model is distilled into a student and co-designed with FPGA implementation to enable these models to run in real-time at future colliders. The fine-tuned distillations meet or exceed the performance of previously published AI/ML solutions for each task. Further, the pipeline of distillation and model compression from TimesFM is generic and can be easily adapted to a variety of 1D waveform tasks across domains.

</details>


### [202] [Retrofitting a commercial RF induction generator into a computer-controlled, vacuum and gas integrated annealing system for reactive-metal grain growth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22118)
*Sterling G. Baird,Ryan Weber,Christopher Nyborg,Ronald Guymon,Gage Erickson,Oliver K. Johnson*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 该工作将商用射频（RF）感应发生器改装为低成本高温真空退火系统，实现近熔点退火并可直接进行电子背散射衍射（EBSD）表征，无需样品制备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近熔点高温真空退火可驱动可控晶粒长大，但通常依赖昂贵的交钥匙真空感应炉，普通实验室难以负担。作者试图提供一种廉价替代方案，拓展此类退火与后续表征的可及性。

Method: 对裸机商用射频（RF）感应发生器进行改装：通过LabVIEW实现计算机功率控制、双波长光学温度反馈，并配备高真空石英管腔体与惰性气体回填。机加工石墨坩埚兼作感受器（susceptor）吸收射频并加热样品。改装仅需单调模拟功率控制输入，故可跨发生器迁移；参考配置采用6 kW固态发生器。另有改进样品组件用于不与射频场耦合的陶瓷。

Result: 镍样品无需任何制样即产生高质量EBSD花样，可直接从炉中送入电镜；EBSD图解析出数百个等轴晶，晶界为深热沟槽。改进组件在2500°下45分钟使钇稳定氧化锆（YSZ）晶粒粗化，而常规箱式炉在1600°需228小时。固定配置下1200–1400°范围内功率-温度标定呈线性（$R^2$=0.991）；8次1200°、12小时退火复现保温温度为1201.2±1.3°，1325°下40小时保温保持稳定。

Conclusion: 该改装为实验室提供了可负担的近熔点退火途径，并支撑“退火到EBSD”的直接表征流程；完整设计文件、物料清单（BOM）、控制软件与数据均已公开。

Abstract: High-temperature vacuum annealing near a metal's melting point drives controlled grain growth, but it normally requires an expensive turn-key vacuum induction furnace. We retrofit a bare commercial radio frequency (RF) induction generator with computer power control through LabVIEW, dual-wavelength optical temperature feedback, and a high-vacuum quartz-tube chamber with inert-gas backfill. A machined graphite crucible doubles as a susceptor, an RF-absorbing element that heats the specimen it encloses. The only requirement is a monotonic analog power-control input, so the retrofit transfers between generators. Our reference build uses a 6 kW solid-state generator. Surprisingly, nickel annealed in this system produced high-quality electron backscatter diffraction (EBSD) patterns with zero specimen preparation. Samples went directly from the furnace to the electron microscope without grinding, polishing, or etching. The EBSD maps indexed hundreds of equiaxed grains delineated by deep thermal grooves. A modified sample assembly extends the furnace to ceramics that do not couple to the RF field. It coarsened yttria-stabilized zirconia (YSZ) grains in 45 min at 2500°, compared with 228h at 1600° in a conventional box furnace. For a fixed configuration, the power-temperature calibration is linear ($R^2$ = 0.991) from 1200 to 1400°. Eight 12h nickel anneals at 1200° reproduced their soak temperature to 1201.2 $\pm$ 1.3°, and 40h soaks at 1325° remained stable. Complete design files, a bill of materials, control software, and data are openly available. This gives laboratories an affordable route to near-melting-point annealing with direct anneal-to-EBSD characterization.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [203] [SCALE: Simulation-Calibrated Amortized Learning for Energy Materials (A hybrid architecture connecting deterministic modeling, real-world data, and transformer-scale inference for accelerated energy-materials discovery)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22233)
*Kuan Huang,Bo Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SCALE（Simulation-Calibrated Amortized Learning for Energy Materials），一种以物理机制为基础、用真实实验数据校准的摊销学习架构，将确定性科学算子、实验校准、扩展标签生成与 transformer 级推理相连，并在固态金属氢化物储氢容量预测中完成验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能源系统在安全性、经济可负担性、韧性与可持续性方面面临多重压力，亟需加速可部署能源材料的发现。既有高成本机制计算与实验数据难以直接支撑大规模快速筛选、排序与逆向设计。

Method: SCALE 将选定的高成本机制计算与实测证据转化为可复用模型，用于快速筛选、排序、逆向设计与主动学习。框架包含十个基于方法的应用情形。在金属氢化物实现中，以 381 个实测 ML-HydPARK 容量锚点校准氢化物相平衡容量算子，生成 5,000 个候选-条件-原型教师标签；采用晶体学锚定的周期图表示保留原子位点、周期邻接与局域金属环境；并训练含 290 万参数的边偏置图 transformer（edge-biased graph transformer）。

Result: 该图 transformer 以五折代理保真度复现校准教师标签：MAE 0.0582 wt% H2、RMSE 0.0833 wt% H2、R2 = 0.9927、Pearson r = 0.9963。事后注意力分析显示，在无化学分组标签监督下，模型学到与既有氢化物化学一致的元素分组与金属-金属关系。训练完成后，评估由重复执行确定性工作流转为批量学习推理，单候选筛选成本降低约 10^7–10^8，同时保留与模拟和实验证据的关联。

Conclusion: SCALE 表明物理机制与实验校准相结合的摊销学习可在大规模候选评估中大幅降低计算成本，并保持与模拟及实验证据的可追溯关联，为可部署能源材料的快速发现提供可行路径。

Abstract: Energy systems face converging pressures for security, affordability, resilience, and sustainability, creating a need for faster discovery of deployable energy materials. Here we introduce SCALE (Simulation-Calibrated Amortized Learning for Energy Materials), a physics-grounded, real-world-data-calibrated learning architecture that connects deterministic scientific operators, experimental calibration, expanded calibrated label generation, and transformer-scale inference. SCALE converts selected high-cost mechanistic computation and measured evidence into reusable models for rapid screening, ranking, inverse design, and active learning. We formulate the framework, identify ten method-based application regimes, and demonstrate SCALE for solid-state metal-hydride hydrogen-storage capacity prediction. In this implementation, a hydride phase-equilibrium capacity operator is calibrated against 381 measured ML-HydPARK capacity anchors and used to generate 5,000 candidate-condition-prototype teacher labels. A crystallographically anchored periodic-graph representation preserves atomic sites, periodic neighbor relationships, and local metal environments absent from formula-only encodings. An edge-biased graph transformer with 2.90 million parameters reproduces calibrated teacher labels with five-fold surrogate fidelity of MAE 0.0582 wt% H2, RMSE 0.0833 wt% H2, R2 = 0.9927, and Pearson r = 0.9963. Post hoc attention analysis suggests that SCALE learns chemically organized element groupings and metal-metal relationships consistent with established hydride chemistry, without chemistry-group labels as supervision. Once trained, SCALE shifts million-candidate evaluation from repeated deterministic workflow execution to batched learned inference, reducing per-candidate screening cost by approximately 10^7-10^8 while retaining links to simulation and experimental evidence.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mtrl-sci'></div>

# cond-mat.mtrl-sci [[Back]](#toc)

### [204] [Efficient spectral Galerkin framework for nonlinear transient heat transfer in finite domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22268)
*Théo Andrieux,Andreas Ntinos,Manas V. Upadhyay*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出谱伽辽金（spectral Galerkin, SG）框架求解有限域内全非线性瞬态热方程，在保持高保真有限元（finite element, FE）精度的同时大幅降低计算成本，并应用于激光-金属相互作用与零件级激光扫描研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确模拟非均匀温度场的时间演化需解析热方程中的强非线性，这些非线性来源于温度相关的热物理性质（thermophysical properties）、相变潜热（latent heat）以及局部热源/热汇。高保真有限元模拟精度高但计算代价大，因此需要兼顾精度与效率的求解方法。

Method: 将热方程重构为常热物理性质的线性参考问题加残差强迫项（residual forcing terms），参考问题的解提供三维正交三角基（orthonormal trigonometric basis）。经伽辽金投影（Galerkin projection）将热方程降为关于时间的模态常微分方程（ordinary differential equations, ODEs）；参考算子在模态基下对角化，对固定非线性强迫可实现模态独立更新。这些ODE用指数时间差分（exponential time differencing）积分，并对非线性迭代校正。该方法避免了FE所需的全局求解，结构化网格便于GPU并行化。

Result: 应用于快速激光-金属相互作用时，SG求解器重现高保真FE温度场，相对误差小于1%，GPU运行时间快227倍。应用于零件级激光扫描研究[Ramani et al., Additive Manufacturing 52 (2022) 102643]时，计入蒸发与潜热使其所提出的加工指标降低超过一半。

Conclusion: SG框架能够以显著更低的计算成本达到高保真FE仿真的精度，并适用于激光-金属相互作用及零件级激光扫描等场景。SG热求解器源代码及示例以Apache 2.0许可发布于 https://github.com/manasvupadhyay/spectral_galerkin_heat 。

Abstract: Accurately modelling the temporal evolution of heterogeneous temperature fields requires resolving strong nonlinearities in the heat equation arising from temperature-dependent thermophysical properties, latent heats of transformation and any local heat sources/sinks. In this work, we present a spectral Galerkin (SG) framework to solve the fully nonlinear transient heat equation in finite domains to attain the accuracy of high-fidelity finite element (FE) simulations at considerably lower computational cost. The heat equation is reformulated into a linear reference problem with constant thermophysical properties and residual forcing terms. Solving the reference problem provides a complete three-dimensional orthonormal trigonometric basis, whose Galerkin projection reduces the heat equation to a set of modal ordinary differential equations (ODEs) in time; the reference operator is diagonal in the modal basis, and results in independent modal updates for a fixed nonlinear forcing. These ODEs can be integrated using exponential time differencing and iteratively corrected for nonlinearities. The SG method eliminates the global solve required by FE methods, and its use of structured grids allows efficient GPU parallelization. Applied to rapid laser-metal interactions, the SG solver reproduces high-fidelity FE temperature fields with less than 1% relative error while achieving 227-fold faster GPU runtimes. Applied to a part-scale laser scanning study [Ramani et al., Additive Manufacturing 52 (2022) 102643], the method shows that accounting for evaporation and latent heat more than halves their proposed processing metric. The source code of the SG heat solver and some worked examples are available at https://github.com/manasvupadhyay/spectral_galerkin_heat under the Apache 2.0 license.

</details>


### [205] [Predicting energy and structural response to force correction in molecular dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22287)
*Peng Kang,Da Wan,Shulin Bai,Pengfei Zhang,Peng Wang,Chenglong Wen,Zhen Li,Yu Liu,Lei Zheng,Li-Dong Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出通过在共享参考轨迹上测量领头响应系数（leading response coefficients），预测力校正（force correction）对能量交换与结构统计量的影响，并用独立模拟与非谐链、硅、硒化锡（SnSe）等体系加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估多聚焦于单个力评估的精度，缺乏对力校正如何经由原子运动与统计可观测量产生影响的物理刻画。论文旨在为参考信息（reference information）如何进入分子动力学（molecular dynamics）提供物理依据。

Method: 在共享参考轨迹上测量领头响应系数；引入残余功率（residual power）与位移维里（displacement virial）区分能量转移与恢复力变化，并考虑间歇性参考更新；以独立模拟检验预测的动能与构型位移，并设计匹配时间表实验考察参考时序作用。

Result: 非谐链中位移维里预测出仅靠功率描述所遗漏的结构位移；硅中局域力常数预测出方向性权衡，即标量校准修复光学运动却损害已准确的低频方向；SnSe 的独立有限温度积分支持由单独参考计算预测的构型响应方向。

Conclusion: 该框架通过力校正对原子运动与统计观测量的影响来评估其效果，超越单个力评估精度；结果为选择参考信息进入分子动力学的方式提供物理基础，并区分静态曲率校正与重复参考脉冲的收益。

Abstract: We predict how force correction changes energy exchange and structural statistics by measuring leading response coefficients on shared reference trajectories. Residual power and the displacement virial distinguish the transfer of energy from the change in restoring forces, including intermittent reference updates. Independent simulations then test the predicted kinetic and configurational shifts. A matched-timetable experiment shows that reference timing affects heating through its coupling to the evolving state. In an anharmonic chain, the displacement virial predicts a structural shift missed by a power-only description. Local force constants in silicon predict a complementary directional tradeoff: scalar calibration repairs optical motion while degrading an already accurate low-frequency direction. Full nonlinear trajectories confirm this tradeoff and distinguish the benefits of static curvature correction and repeated reference impulses. Independent finite-temperature integrals in orthorhombic tin selenide (SnSe) support the configurational-response direction predicted from separate reference calculations. These results provide a physical basis for choosing how reference information enters molecular dynamics. The framework assesses force correction through its effect on atomic motion and statistical observables, beyond the accuracy of individual force evaluations.

</details>


### [206] [NTBuilder: Commensurate Construction of Nanotubes from Arbitrary Two-Dimensional Crystals and a Catalog of 20 Million Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22341)
*Marcelo Lopes Pereira Junior*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文提出任意二维晶体卷曲成纳米管的通用理论，并实现开源工具 ntbuilder，揭示晶格类型决定管胞尺寸，构建了含 2033 万余个纳米管的公开目录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有数万种二维（2D）晶体被报道，但其纳米管（nanotube）通常仅针对单一材料、沿用源自石墨烯（graphene）的构建流程，缺乏普适理论。

Method: 建立任意二维晶体卷曲构建的通用理论并实现开源库 ntbuilder（含桌面与网页界面）；用由晶格度规（lattice metric）导出的单个有理数的约化形式表达管精确原胞（unit cell）尺寸，用第二个数的佳有理逼近（best rational approximations）构造最优近似胞；分析 17 个平面群（plane groups）在卷曲下退化为 7 种手性映射（chirality maps），并逐对检测弯曲引起的成键/断键。

Result: 三角与正方晶格总能以小胞闭合，而矩形、中心矩形与斜方晶格可能需大出多个数量级的胞；在联苯烯网络（biphenylene network）中近似胞比精确胞小八个数量级；框架还可组装共享同一轴向周期的多壁管、周期束及保持周期性的应变或扭转结构；应用于 46,403 个不同二维体系，产出 20,334,372 个纳米管的公开目录，其中 57.1% 卷曲时无键变化。

Conclusion: 该工作给出统一且可推广的卷曲构建框架，实现可控周期性偏离的近似胞选择，且所有结构可直接导出作为第一性原理（first-principles）与分子动力学（molecular dynamics）代码的输入。

Abstract: Although tens of thousands of two-dimensional (2D) crystals are now known, the nanotubes they can form are usually constructed for individual materials, using procedures derived for graphene. Here, we develop a general theory of the rolling construction for arbitrary 2D crystals and implement it in \ntbuilder{} (NanoTube Builder), an open-source library with desktop and web interfaces. We express the size of the exact unit cell of a tube through the reduced form of a single rational number built from the lattice metric, which explains why triangular and square lattices always close with small cells, whereas rectangular, centered rectangular, and oblique lattices can require cells larger by many orders of magnitude. The optimal approximate cells are the best rational approximations of a second number, so that a cell small enough for first-principles calculations can be chosen with a controlled departure from periodicity, as in the biphenylene network, where such a cell is eight orders of magnitude smaller than the exact one. The 17 plane groups reduce to seven distinct chirality maps once rolling is taken into account, the rolling sense of layers without a horizontal mirror defines distinct tubes, and bonds formed or broken by curvature are detected pair by pair against the flat layer. The same framework assembles multiwalled tubes whose walls share one axial period, periodic bundles, and strained or twisted structures that remain periodic. Applied to 46,403 distinct 2D systems, it produced a public catalog of 20,334,372 nanotubes, of which 57.1\% show no bond change upon rolling. All structures can be exported directly as input for first-principles and molecular dynamics codes.

</details>


### [207] [From Ferroionic Instability to Domain Patterns at an Exposed Surfaces of Multiaxial Ferroelectrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22366)
*Sergei V. Kalinin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作通过耦合矢量极化、表面电化学、静电、相容应变与竞争吸附，分析开放且电化学补偿表面多轴铁电体中畴结构的形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解开放、电化学补偿表面条件下多轴铁电体畴结构的形成机制，并将表面有序化与铁电弛豫体（ferroelectric relaxors）周期性表面调制联系起来。

Method: 建立包含矢量极化（vector polarization）、表面电化学（surface electrochemistry）、静电（electrostatics）、相容应变（compatible strain）与共同位点竞争吸附（competitive adsorption on common sites）的模型，构建均匀相图并分析极化不稳定性；先假设沿膜厚均匀的微扰，再引入深度依赖的极化剖面，并以非线性延拓（nonlinear continuation）追踪调制增长为成熟畴结构，同时优化周期并检验稳定性。

Result: 确定了初始不稳定性波长与已形成畴结构优选间距之间的关系；在稳定且弱各向异性体相之上考虑表面有序化，作为与铁电弛豫体相关的本构极限，为周期性表面调制来源提供线索；具有相同二次项的表面能可产生相同初始不稳定性，但偏好不同的演化态，包括周期性畴与均匀面内极化。

Conclusion: 该分析提供了将铁电调制初始发展同振幅选择（amplitude selection）、波长调整（wavelength adjustment）及畴结构后续演化相联系的流程。

Abstract: We analyze the formation of domain structures in multiaxial ferroelectrics with an open, electrochemically compensated surface. The model combines vector polarization, surface electrochemistry, electrostatics, compatible strain, and competitive adsorption on common sites. We construct homogeneous phase maps and analyze polarization instabilities, first assuming perturbations that are uniform through the film thickness and then allowing depth-dependent polarization profiles. Nonlinear continuation follows the growth of these modulations into developed domains. Optimization of their period and tests of their stability establish how the initial instability wavelength relates to the preferred spacing of an established domain structure. We also consider surface ordering above a stable, weakly anisotropic bulk as a constitutive limit relevant to ferroelectric relaxors, providing insight into possible origins of their periodic surface modulations. Surface energies with the same quadratic terms can produce the same initial instability but favor different developed states, including periodic domains and uniform in-plane polarization. This analysis provides a procedure for connecting the initial development of ferroelectric modulations to amplitude selection, wavelength adjustment, and the subsequent evolution of the resulting domain structures.

</details>


### [208] [A Hybrid Quantum Neural Network to Analyse Big Experimental Powder X-ray Diffraction Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22376)
*H. Dong,S. D. M. Jacques,M. Q. Hlatshwayo,E. Papoutsellis,K. Georgopoulos,A. M. Beale,A. Vamvakeros*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出混合量子神经网络（hybrid quantum neural network）框架，可从一维粉末衍射（powder diffraction）图谱中直接提取相权重分数与比例因子等定量参数，无需迭代精修；在IBM量子计算机上对约1万与约2万条实验X射线衍射断层（X-ray diffraction computed tomography, XRD-CT）数据进行验证，重建结果与经典Rietveld精修高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当评估复杂多相材料（multiphase materials）与噪声测量时，实验粉末X射线衍射（powder X-ray diffraction）数据的定量分析仍具挑战性，传统流程依赖迭代精修（iterative refinement），成本高且对噪声敏感。

Method: 构建混合量子神经网络（hybrid quantum neural network）框架，直接从一维粉末衍射图谱回归定量参数；训练采取噪声感知的经典模拟器预训练（noise-aware classical simulator pre-training），随后在量子处理单元（quantum processing unit）特征上进行快速下游微调（fine-tuning），以抵抗硬件退相干（hardware decoherence）。

Result: 将训练后的网络部署于IBM量子计算机，分析三相固体氧化物燃料电池（solid oxide fuel cell）约10,000条图谱与四相锂离子电池（lithium-ion battery）约20,000条图谱的实验X射线衍射计算断层数据集，成功重建定量空间相图，与经典Rietveld精修结果高度一致。

Conclusion: 该结果表明量子计算硬件可用于分析真实世界的材料表征（materials characterisation）数据，为无需迭代精修的定量衍射分析提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Quantitative analysis of experimental powder X-ray diffraction data remains challenging when evaluating complex multiphase materials and noisy measurements. We introduce a hybrid quantum neural network framework designed to extract quantitative parameters, such as phase weight fractions and scale factors, directly from one-dimensional powder diffraction patterns without iterative refinement. The model combines noise-aware classical simulator pre-training with fast downstream fine-tuning on quantum processing unit features, ensuring stability against hardware decoherence. We demonstrate the practical utility of this approach by deploying the trained network onto an IBM quantum computer to analyse experimental X-ray diffraction computed tomography datasets from a three-phase solid oxide fuel cell containing ca. 10,000 patterns and a four-phase lithium-ion battery containing ca. 20,000 patterns). The network successfully reconstructs quantitative spatial phase maps in strong agreement with classical Rietveld refinement, paving the way for using quantum computing hardware to analyse real-world materials characterisation data.

</details>


### [209] [A low-symmetry ground state of dense two-dimensional hydrogen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22440)
*Cesare Cozza,Chris J. Pickard,Guglielmo Mazzola*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究利用第一性原理（first-principles）结构搜索，发现致密二维氢（two-dimensional hydrogen）在中压区间可能不存在长程晶体序（long-range crystalline order），其无序结构焓低于所有候选晶相，暗示可能存在无序基态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维块体氢（bulk hydrogen）已被研究近一个世纪，涉及分子解离（molecular dissociation）、金属化（metallization）与反常熔化（anomalous melting）等复杂现象，但其二维对应体系尚未被探索。

Method: 采用第一性原理结构搜索与弛豫，在最多含512个原子的超胞（supercell）中进行；并在密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）框架下比较焓值，同时采用经典原子核（classical nuclei）与不同超胞尺寸。

Result: 在分子晶体与原子晶体之间的中间压力区间，所识别的无序结构的焓低于所有已考察的晶态候选；该偏好随超胞增大仍然存在，而主导结构因子（structure-factor）峰的增长显著慢于晶态参照相。

Conclusion: 虽无法严格排除具有超大原胞的晶体，但结果支持无序基态（disordered ground state）存在的可能性，并表明致密二维氢是研究晶化（crystallization）与结构无序（structural disorder）竞争的有前景体系。

Abstract: Hydrogen under pressure is an extremely complex system featuring molecular dissociation, metallization, and anomalous melting. While bulk hydrogen has been studied for nearly a century, its two-dimensional counterpart remains unexplored. Using first-principles structural searches and relaxations in supercells containing up to 512 atoms, we identify ground-state structures of dense two-dimensional hydrogen showing no evidence of long-range crystalline order over the simulated length scales. Within density functional theory, these structures have lower enthalpy than all crystalline candidates considered over an intermediate pressure interval between molecular and atomic crystals. This preference already emerges with classical nuclei and persists as the supercell size increases, while the dominant structure-factor peaks grow substantially more slowly than in the crystalline reference phases. Although crystals with very large primitive cells cannot be rigorously excluded, these findings support the possibility of a disordered ground state and identify dense two-dimensional hydrogen as a promising setting for investigating competition between crystallization and structural disorder.

</details>


### [210] [Laser-Ablated Au Electrodes with Preferred Orientation and Flat Interface on Oxides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22461)
*Ambrose Seo,Richard Lai,Sujan Shrestha,Tina Tong,Joseph W. Brill,Menglin Zhu,Chris Chae,Jinwoo Hwang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 脉冲激光沉积（pulsed laser deposition, PLD）制备的氧化物价金（Au）薄膜电极具有强[111]择优取向与原子级平整界面，其黏附性优于热蒸发Au薄膜，并可在同一沉积平台内实现电极与氧化物薄膜的集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热蒸发（thermal evaporation）Au薄膜在氧化物上通常呈随机取向的多晶结构，界面控制有限。论文旨在探索PLD生长Au薄膜的结构与界面特性，以改善氧化物电子与功能器件中的界面质量。

Method: 采用脉冲激光沉积在氧化物衬底上沉积Au薄膜，并与热蒸发Au薄膜进行结构对比；利用高分辨透射电子显微镜（high-resolution transmission electron microscopy, HRTEM）表征Au与外延氧化物（epitaxial oxide）薄膜的界面形貌。

Result: PLD生长的Au薄膜呈现垂直于衬底表面的强[111]择优取向，推测源于激光烧蚀（laser-ablated）Au羽流的高能特性；HRTEM显示Au电极与外延氧化物薄膜之间为原子级平整界面，并被纳米尺度间隙分隔，该间隙与范德华材料（van der Waals materials）的层间距相当。

Conclusion: 高度有序的微结构与平整界面形貌增强了Au薄膜对氧化物表面的黏附；PLD可在单一沉积平台内同时制备高质量Au电极与氧化物薄膜，提供更优的界面控制，对氧化物电子与功能器件具有潜在价值。

Abstract: Gold (Au) thin-film electrodes deposited on oxides by pulsed laser deposition (PLD) exhibit distinctive structural properties compared with thermally evaporated Au films. While thermally evaporated Au forms polycrystalline films with randomly oriented grains, PLD-grown Au films show a strong preferred [111] orientation perpendicular to the substrate surface, likely due to the energetic nature of the laser-ablated Au plume. High-resolution transmission electron microscopy reveals atomically flat interfaces between the PLD-grown Au electrodes and epitaxial oxide thin films, separated by nanometer-scale gaps comparable to interlayer spacings in van der Waals materials. The highly ordered microstructure and flat interfacial morphology result in enhanced adhesion of the Au films to oxide surfaces. These results demonstrate that PLD can produce both high-quality Au electrodes and oxide thin films within a single deposition platform, offering improved interfacial control and potential benefits for a wide range of oxide electronic and functional devices.

</details>


### [211] [Mobility-lifetime relation links photodegradation in spin-coated and gravure-printed organic solar cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22467)
*Maria Saladina,Paul Weitz,David Holzner,Chen Wang,Robert Eland,Svitlana Taranenko,Christopher Wöpke,Toni Seiler,Fabian Eller,Doyoung Sun,Alexander Ehm,Bekcy Joseph,Pelin Çiloğlu,Carmen Tretmans,Andreas Willert,Harald Kuhn,Roderick C. I. Mackenzie,Jan-Frederik Pietschmann,Martin Stoll,Yana Vaynzof,Dietrich R. T. Zahn,Safa Shoaee,Eva M. Herzig,Arved Carl Hübler,Thomas Heumüller,Christoph J. Brabec,Carsten Deibel*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究比较了旋涂（spin-coated）与全卷对卷（roll-to-roll）兼容的凹版印刷（gravure-printed）PM6:Y12 有机太阳能电池（organic solar cells, OSCs）在约1000小时连续光照下的退化行为，发现效率损失普遍遵循迁移率-寿命乘积（mobility-lifetime product, μτ）这一统一描述符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有OSCs退化机制研究多基于旋涂的实验室级器件，而可扩展加工会改变器件结构、活性层形貌以及电荷传输与复合特性；这些变化是否同时改变退化方式尚不明确。

Method: 对比旋涂与全卷对卷兼容凹版印刷的PM6:Y12太阳能电池，在约1000小时连续光照下进行老化测试，并结合暗态恢复（dark recovery）、形貌与复合相关表征分析退化机制。

Result: 两类器件虽初始性能与退化特征不同，但功率转换效率（power-conversion efficiency）损失均系统性地遵循μτ；印刷器件老化使复合寿命延长却强烈降低载流子迁移率，迁移率下降伴随PM6层状有序度（lamellar order）减弱，开路电压（open-circuit voltage）的额外损失主要源自非辐射复合（non-radiative recombination）增加。暗态恢复揭示出印刷结构特有的亚稳（metastable）贡献。

Conclusion: 迁移率-寿命乘积被确认为光降解的统一物理描述符，将老化引起的微观变化与旋涂及可扩展印刷OSCs的宏观性能损失联系起来，表明仅凭复合寿命延长不足以推断器件性能改善。

Abstract: The degradation mechanisms of organic solar cells (OSCs) have been studied primarily in spin-coated, laboratory-scale devices, whereas scalable processing modifies the device architecture, active-layer morphology, and underlying charge-transport and recombination properties. Whether these changes also alter how solar cells degrade remains unclear. Here, we compare spin-coated and fully roll-to-roll-compatible gravure-printed PM6:Y12 solar cells during $\sim$1000 h of continuous illumination. Despite distinct initial properties and degradation signatures, the loss of power-conversion efficiency systematically follows the mobility-lifetime product $μτ$. Remarkably, ageing of the printed devices increases the recombination lifetime while strongly reducing charge-carrier mobility, showing that a longer lifetime alone does not imply improved device performance. The mobility reduction is accompanied by decreased PM6 lamellar order, whereas the additional open-circuit voltage loss originates predominantly from increased non-radiative recombination. Dark recovery further reveals a metastable contribution specific to the printed architecture. These results identify the mobility-lifetime product as a unifying physical descriptor for photodegradation, linking ageing-induced microscopic changes to macroscopic performance loss across spin-coated and scalable printed OSCs.

</details>


### [212] [Revealing epitaxial relationships at Ga$_2$O$_3$ interfaces with p-type oxides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22472)
*Anna Sacchi,Krishna Acharya,Michelle A. Smeaton,Renae N. Gannon,Vladan Stevanovic,Steven R. Spurgeon,M. Brooks Tellekamp,Andriy Zakutayev*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究考察 p 型氧化物（p-type oxide）Cr$_2$O$_3$、NiO 与 n 型单斜 β-Ga$_2$O$_3$ 衬底的外延关系（epitaxial relationship），发现 (001) 取向衬底上外延关系出现在偏离衬底法向 χ = 22.5° 的非直觉角度，归因于界面氧亚晶格（oxygen sublattice）等价性主导的原子键合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cr$_2$O$_3$、NiO 等 p 型氧化物接触层与 n 型单斜 β-Ga$_2$O$_3$ 构成的 pn 异质结（pn-heterojunction）在高功率电子器件与极端环境应用中受到日益关注，但其外延关系的科学理解仍不完整。

Method: 针对 (001) 与 ($\bar{2}$01) 面外取向的 Ga$_2$O$_3$ 衬底，表征 Cr$_2$O$_3$ 与 NiO 层的外延关系；结合界面原子建模（interface modeling），分析界面能与原子键合来源，并确认面内取向与旋转畴（rotational domains）。

Result: 在商业相关性最高的 (001) 取向衬底上，Cr$_2$O$_3$ (0001) 与 NiO (111) 均平行于 Ga$_2$O$_3$ (101)，且相对衬底法向呈非直觉的 χ = 22.5°；该现象由 Cr$_2$O$_3$、NiO 极性表面取向与倾斜 Ga$_2$O$_3$ (101) 的氧亚晶格等价性主导的界面键合解释。($\bar{2}$01) 取向衬底上 Cr$_2$O$_3$ 的面内取向为 Cr$_2$O$_3$ $[12\bar{3}0]$ ∥ Ga$_2$O$_3$ $[010]$，含两个面内旋转畴。

Conclusion: 界面建模确认了 Cr$_2$O$_3$/($\bar{2}$01) Ga$_2$O$_3$ 的面内取向，并表明受应变的 O 终止 Ga$_2$O$_3$ ($\bar{2}$01) 表面与 Cr$_2$O$_3$ (0001) 具有最低界面能。除确定具体外延关系外，本工作为晶体对称性差异显著的异质界面提供了系统性、无歧义的结构表征方法。

Abstract: p-type oxide contact layers such as Cr$_2$O$_3$ and NiO are attracting increasing interest in pn-heterojunctions with n-type monoclinic $β$-Ga$_2$O$_3$ for high-power electronic devices and other extreme environment applications. However, scientific understanding of their epitaxial relationships remains incomplete. In this work we investigate the epitaxial relation of Cr$_2$O$_3$ and NiO layers to (001) and ($\bar{2}$01) out-of-plane oriented Ga$_2$O$_3$ substrates. Surprisingly, we find that, for the most commercially relevant (001)-orientation of the Ga$_2$O$_3$ substrate, the epitaxial relationships are Cr$_2$O$_3$ (0001) and NiO (111) $\parallel$ Ga$_2$O$_3$ (101), both at the non-intuitive $χ$ = 22.5 $^\circ$ angle with respect to the substrate normal. We explain this unusual discovery by the interfacial atomistic bonding dominated by oxygen sublattice equivalence of these Cr$_2$O$_3$ and NiO polar surface orientations to the tilted Ga$_2$O$_3$ (101), rather than Ga$_2$O$_3$ (001) substrate surface planes. Furthermore, we assign the in-plane orientation for Cr$_2$O$_3$ on ($\bar{2}$01)-oriented Ga$_2$O$_3$ as: Cr$_2$O$_3$ $[12\bar{3}0]$ $\parallel$ Ga$_2$O$_3$ $[010]$ with two in-plane rotational domains. Interface modeling confirms the in-plane orientation for Cr$_2$O$_3$/$(\bar{2}01)$ Ga$_2$O$_3$ and shows that strained O-terminated Ga$_2$O$_3$ $(\bar{2}01)$ surfaces have the lowest interfacial energy with Cr$_2$O$_3$ (0001). Beyond establishing the specific epitaxial relationships for Cr$_2$O$_3$ and NiO on Ga$_2$O$_3$, this work provides a systematic methodology for the unambiguous structural characterization of heterointerfaces involving materials with markedly different crystal symmetries.

</details>


### [213] [Experiments and Modeling of Defect Dynamics and BTI Behavior in Doped InO TFTs during $400^\circ$C Post-Processing Forming Gas Annealing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22509)
*Yu-Hsin Kuo,Chengyang Zhang,Priyankka Ravikumar,Sanghyun Kang,Taeyoung Song,Marco Villena,Luca Larcher,Hwan Kim,Minji Hong,Pilsang Yun,Gaurav Thareja,Shimeng Yu,Daewon Ha,Suman Datta,Julia Medvedeva,Asif Khan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究揭示单片三维（monolithic three-dimensional, M3D）集成中400 °C形成气体退火（forming gas anneal, FGA）对氧化物沟道薄膜晶体管（thin-film transistors, TFTs）性能与可靠性的非单调影响：短时退火致沟道致密化与浅缺陷态产生，长时退火致部分晶化并稳定氢于深局域态，从而恢复可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: M3D集成工艺中的高温后处理FGA会影响氧化物沟道TFT的电学性能与可靠性，但氢与缺陷在其中的交互机制尚不清晰。研究旨在阐明该过程中氢-缺陷相互作用的机理，并评估氢阻挡封装的有效性。

Method: 以铟钨氧化物（indium tungsten oxide, IWO）TFT为模型体系，采用3 nm Al₂O₃/3 nm HfO₂混合封装层抑制外部氢侵入；结合系统实验与基于密度泛函理论（density-functional-theory, DFT）的液态淬火分子动力学（liquid-quench molecular-dynamics, MD）模拟。

Result: FGA过程中器件行为呈非单调演变，由先致密化、后部分晶化主导。短时FGA（10 min）引起沟道致密化与浅的离域缺陷态，导致正偏压温度不稳定性（positive bias temperature instability, PBTI）显著退化及转移曲线出现特征“kink”。长时退火（>40 min）使氧化物沟道部分晶化，使氢稳定于深局域缺陷态、抑制氢迁移，2000 s应力后PBTI漂移降至10.4 mV，且kink完全消失。

Conclusion: 研究给出了高温后处理FGA中氢-缺陷相互作用的机理认识，并证明适当的氢阻挡封装可使氧化物沟道TFT集成在不损害电学性能与可靠性的前提下实现。

Abstract: We investigate the impact of a monolithic three-dimensional (M3D) integration process-critical $400^\circ$C post-processing forming gas anneal (FGA) on the electrical performance, reliability, and defect evolution of oxide-channel thin-film transistors (TFTs), combining systematic experiments with density-functional-theory (DFT)-based liquid-quench molecular-dynamics (MD) simulations. Indium tungsten oxide (IWO) TFTs are employed as a model system and encapsulated with a thin 3 nm Al$_2$O$_3$ / 3 nm HfO$_2$ hybrid layer that effectively suppresses external hydrogen ingress. We reveal a non-monotonic evolution of device behavior during FGA, governed by initial densification followed by partial crystallization. Short-duration FGA (10 min) induces channel densification and the formation of shallow, delocalized defect states, leading to pronounced positive bias temperature instability (PBTI) degradation and the emergence of a characteristic transfer-curve "kink." With prolonged annealing (>40 min), partial crystallization of the oxide channel occurs, stabilizing hydrogen in deep, localized defect states, suppressing hydrogen mobility, and restoring device reliability. As a result, the PBTI shift is reduced to 10.4 mV after 2000 seconds of stress, accompanied by complete elimination of the transfer-curve kink. These findings provide a mechanistic understanding of hydrogen-defect interactions during high-temperature post-processing FGA and demonstrate that appropriate hydrogen-blocking encapsulation enables oxide-channel TFT integration without compromising electrical performance or reliability.

</details>


### [214] [Compressive Splitting in Brittle Solids: The Inverse of Wrinkling in Sheets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22558)
*Maryam Khodadad,Francois Barthelat,John D. Clayton,George Gazonas,Kaushik Dayal*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究提出轴向劈裂（axial splitting）源于端部夹持约束下的边界诱导拉应力，是拉伸片材起皱（wrinkling）的压缩类比，并据此给出可预测的强度定律并经多材料验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴向劈裂是脆性固体（brittle solids）在压缩下的主导破坏模式，但其力学起源仍不明确。既有认识多将其视为随机缺陷（stochastic flaw）问题，缺乏可预测的力学机制。

Method: 从端部夹持加载压板（clamped loading platens）抑制横向泊松膨胀（Poisson expansion）出发，分析试样内部产生的边界诱导拉应力（boundary-induced tensile stresses），建立与拉伸片材起皱（wrinkling）相对应的压缩类比机制，并推导强度定律。

Result: 提出一个可预测的强度定律（strength law），将轴向劈裂与抗拉强度（tensile strength）、几何形状（geometry）及围压（confinement pressure）相关联；在岩石（rocks）到陶瓷（ceramics）等多种材料上得到验证。

Conclusion: 轴向劈裂应被理解为几何控制的弹性过程（geometry-controlled elastic process），而非随机缺陷问题。

Abstract: Axial splitting is the dominant failure mode of brittle solids under compression, yet its mechanical origin remains unclear. We show that clamped loading platens suppress lateral Poisson expansion, generating boundary-induced tensile stresses at the specimen interior -- the compressive analog of wrinkling in stretched sheets. This mechanism provides a predictive strength law, relating axial splitting to tensile strength, geometry, and confinement pressure. This is validated against diverse materials ranging from rocks to ceramics, establishing axial splitting as a geometry-controlled elastic process rather than a stochastic flaw problem.

</details>


### [215] [LiNiO2/NiO Phase Prediction Using Artificial Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22604)
*Jonas Scheunert,Shamail Ahmed,Thomas Demuth,Andreas Beyer,Kerstin Volz*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究利用神经网络对四维扫描透射电子显微镜（4DSTEM）衍射图样进行材料相识别，以区分LiNiO2与NiO。网络在合成图像上训练、在实验图样上测试，涵盖经典卷积神经网络（CNN）与多种视觉Transformer（ViT）架构，并借助GradCAM分析其决策依据，在高度变化的数据上表现稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在材料分析中，识别不同材料相具有关键意义；而4DSTEM数据采集会产生大量衍射图样，传统模板匹配（template-matching）方法在数据高度变化时往往表现不佳。区分LiNiO2与NiO在锂离子电池领域尤为重要且具挑战性，因为NiO的生成会限制电池容量。

Method: 训练多种网络架构以区分LiNiO2与NiO相的衍射图像：既测试经典卷积神经网络（CNN），也测试不同形式的视觉Transformer（ViT）。网络在合成图像（synthetic images）上训练，并在实验记录的衍射图样（diffraction patterns）上进行测试，同时使用GradCAM方法考察网络的决策过程。

Result: 网络在合成与实验衍射图样上均进行测试，结果十分稳健，尤其在处理高度变化的数据时表现突出——而这正是传统模板匹配方法通常难以应对的情形。

Conclusion: 神经网络（尤其是CNN与ViT等架构）为4DSTEM图像数据中的材料相识别提供了快速且训练后计算成本低廉的方法，并在LiNiO2与NiO区分任务上展现出优于传统模板匹配的鲁棒性。

Abstract: In the realm of material analysis, identifying different material phases is of key importance. Artificial intelligence in the form of neural networks provides a very fast and, once trained, computationally inexpensive method for analysing large amounts of image data, like the sets of diffraction patterns generated during four-dimensional scanning transmission electron microscopy (4DSTEM) dataset acquisition. In this work, we train multiple network architectures on images of this type to distinguish between the phases of LiNiO2 and NiO, an important and challenging distinction in the lithium-ion battery community, since the formation of NiO limits battery capacity. We test both classical convolutional neural networks (CNNs) and different forms of vision transformers (ViTs). Our networks are trained on synthetic images and tested on experimentally recorded diffraction patterns. Additionally, we also investigate the decision-making of our networks using the GradCAM method. We test our networks on both synthetic as well as experimental diffraction patterns. Our networks exhibit very robust results, especially when dealing with highly varying data, an area where traditional template-matching methods typically struggle.

</details>


### [216] [Highly Lattice-Mismatched Selective Area Epitaxy and Coalescence of PbSe Nanostructures on GaAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22650)
*Ashlee M. García,Hosni A. Kaissi,Jarod E. Meyer,Kira J. Martin,Maksim Gomanko,Laura A. Stern,Pooja D. Reddy,SeongJin Park,Wilson J. Yánez-Parreño,Sergey M. Frolov,Vlad S. Pribiag,Kunal Mukherjee*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究实现了 PbSe（硒化铅）的选择性区域生长（selective area growth, SAG），在 8% 晶格失配下获得高度定向、低缺陷的纳米结构，并展示其中红外发光与可栅控双端约瑟夫森结（Josephson junction）特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PbSe 为窄带隙（narrow bandgap）半导体，具有优异的（光）电子性质与缺陷容忍性（defect-tolerance），有望用于高密度、确定性排布的纳米结构网络，以构建可扩展量子平台（scalable quantum platform）。将其与选择性生长结合，可用于位点选择性量子发射器（site-selective quantum emitters）与低无序杂化纳米线网络。

Method: 采用选择性区域生长方法制备 PbSe 纳米结构，并通过结构与形貌表征、缺陷分析（穿透位错，threading dislocations）以及光学与电学输运测试进行评估；在此基础上，利用该生长条件制备可栅控的双端约瑟夫森结器件。

Result: 在 100 nm 开口内，>99% 的选择性生长岛为单一取向且呈立方对立方（cube-on-cube）取向，表面均方根粗糙度（root-mean-square surface roughness）低于 1 nm；74% 的岛及 86% 的掩模开口间合并区域无穿透位错。尽管比表面积高于平面对照样品，这些岛仍实现等效的中红外（mid-infrared）发光。同时制备出由该 PbSe 纳米线构成的可栅控双端约瑟夫森结。

Conclusion: 可及的选择性生长窗口、岛生长的形貌控制以及光学与电学输运性质的验证，表明 PbSe 选择性区域生长有望成为缺陷容忍的可扩展量子平台。

Abstract: Selective area growth of PbSe has the potential to realize deterministic placement of high-density, defect-tolerant nanostructure networks toward a scalable quantum platform. PbSe is a narrow bandgap semiconductor with advantageous (opto)electronic properties that has been shown to have a desirable defect-tolerance, enabling bright emission even when grown on highly-dissimilar platforms, and could be leveraged in combination with selective growth for site-selective quantum emitters and low-disorder hybrid nanowire networks. In this work, we achieve site-selective growth of well-faceted and ordered PbSe nanostructures, despite a large 8% lattice mismatch. Structural and morphological characterization reveal that $>$99% of selectively grown islands within 100 nm opening are single-orientation and cube-on-cube oriented with sub-nm root-mean-square surface roughness. Defect analysis showed that 74% of the islands and 86% of the coalesced regions between different mask openings were free of threading dislocations. These PbSe islands achieved equivalent emission in the mid-infrared despite having a higher surface-to-volume ratio than the planar control. Further, we present a gate-tunable two-terminal Josephson junction fabricated from the PbSe nanowires grown with conditions identified in this study. The combination of the accessible selective growth regime, morphological control of island growth and demonstrations of optical and electrical transport properties indicates promise for PbSe SAG as a defect-tolerant scalable quantum platform.

</details>


### [217] [GRAINSMITH: A Generator of Polycrystalline Models for Atomistic Simulations with Statistical and Grain-Boundary Morphology Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22666)
*Oguzhan Orhan,Soner Özgen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: GRAINSMITH 是一个开源 Python 包，用于生成统计可控、晶界形貌可选的多晶原子模型，支持分子动力学模拟与后续弛豫，并在固定版本与环境下实现跨并行度的字节级可复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶界工程（grain-boundary engineering）、晶粒尺寸效应（grain-size effects）与掺杂富集（dopant enrichment）的原子尺度研究需要具备预设微结构特征且可复现的模型，而现有工具在统计规格与晶界形貌控制方面存在不足。

Method: 该包通过单一配置指定晶粒尺寸与体积分布、晶体学织构（crystallographic texture）、以边界面积加权的取向差角分布（boundary-area-weighted disorientation-angle distributions）、相组成及晶界掺杂剂布置；晶体构建支持 230 个晶体学空间群（space groups），并使用周期性 Voronoi 与体积目标 Laguerre 镶嵌生成平面晶界，另设几何后端生成光滑弯曲与带限自仿射（band-limited self-affine）晶界。

Result: 提供包含二十六项检查的注册表（registry）以评估输入、构建属性与输出；每次运行导出 LAMMPS 数据与 Extended XYZ 文件，并附带结构与统计描述符及机器可读的来源信息（provenance）；在支持的 worker 数量范围内，配置与随机种子决定字节级可复现的原子构型与科学数据。

Conclusion: 通过结合统计规格、晶界形貌控制与可复现的原子输出，GRAINSMITH 支持微结构效应的系统性研究与跨生成模型的定量比较。

Abstract: Atomistic studies of grain-boundary engineering, grain-size effects and dopant enrichment require reproducible models with prescribed microstructural features. We present GRAINSMITH, an open-source Python package for generating statistically controlled polycrystalline models with selectable grain-boundary morphologies for molecular dynamics and subsequent relaxation. Within supported feature combinations, a single configuration specifies grain-size and volume distributions, crystallographic texture, boundary-area-weighted disorientation-angle distributions, phase composition and grain-boundary dopant placement. Crystal construction supports 230 crystallographic space groups. Periodic Voronoi and volume-targeted Laguerre tessellations provide planar boundaries, while distinct geometry backends generate smoothly curved and band-limited self-affine boundaries. A registry of twenty-six checks assesses applicable inputs, construction properties and outputs. Each run exports LAMMPS data and Extended XYZ files together with structural and statistical descriptors and machine-readable provenance. For a fixed software version and computational environment, the configuration and random seed determine byte-reproducible atomic configurations and scientific data across supported worker counts. By combining statistical specification, boundary-morphology control and reproducible atomistic output, GRAINSMITH supports systematic studies of microstructural effects and quantitative comparisons across generated models.

</details>


### [218] [Three-dimensional atom-by-atom measurement of interface diffusion dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22725)
*Doojin Park,Hyesung Jo,Yongmin Kwon,Seokjo Hong,Jaewhan Oh,Youngjoo Whang,Eunjik Lee,Gu-Gon Park,Sang Woo Han,Yongsoo Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作结合 MEMS 脉冲淬火加热与原子电子断层扫描（atomic electron tomography, AET），对同一 Pd@Pt 核壳纳米颗粒重建五个三维原子构型，实现身份校正后的逐原子扩散测量，并提出连接迁移率与物种、层、晶面分辨输运的三维框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 界面扩散通常以平滑浓度剖面与宏观输运系数表征，这对仅数个原子层厚的纳米界面而言过度粗粒化，掩盖了局部三维原子输运，且标量扩散系数无法描述物种、层与晶面分辨的再分布。因此，关键可观测量是原子本身的离散三维重分布。

Method: 将微机电系统（micro-electro-mechanical systems, MEMS）脉冲淬火加热与原子电子断层扫描（atomic electron tomography, AET）结合，重建同一共格 Pd@Pt 核壳纳米颗粒的五个三维原子构型，各对应明确热态；采用守恒物种的一对一匹配连接相邻构型，并以带标签的参考模拟校正同类原子不可分辨引起的位移偏置。

Result: 校正后的 Pd 扩散系数遵循阿伦尼乌斯（Arrhenius）行为，表观活化能为 1.10 eV，与迁移受限的空位介导输运一致；原子层跃迁统计显示三维迁移率升高可与净化学转移下降共存，并揭示被标量扩散系数掩盖的晶面依赖路径。

Conclusion: 上述测量建立了逐原子三维框架，将身份校正的迁移率与埋入式纳米界面处物种、层与晶面分辨的输运联系起来。

Abstract: Diffusion at interfaces governs how materials and devices form, evolve and function. It is usually measured through smooth concentration profiles and bulk transport coefficients, which average over the discrete atomic rearrangements. At nanoscale interfaces only a few atomic layers wide, however, such coarse-graining obscures the local three-dimensional (3D) atomic transport, and scalar diffusion coefficients cannot fully describe species-, layer- and facet-resolved redistribution. The essential observable is the discrete 3D redistribution of atoms themselves, beyond an averaged profile. Here we combine micro-electro-mechanical systems (MEMS) pulse-quench heating with atomic electron tomography (AET) to reconstruct five 3D atomic configurations of the same coherent Pd@Pt core-shell nanoparticle, each corresponding to a well-defined thermal state. Species-conserving one-to-one assignment links neighbouring configurations, and labelled reference simulations correct the displacement bias caused by indistinguishable same-species atoms. The corrected Pd diffusivities follow Arrhenius behaviour, giving an apparent activation energy of 1.10 eV, consistent with migration-limited vacancy-mediated transport. Atomic-layer transition statistics further show that increasing 3D mobility can coexist with declining net chemical transfer and reveal facet-dependent pathways hidden by the scalar diffusivity. Together, these measurements establish a 3D atom-by-atom framework that connects identity-corrected mobility to species-, layer- and facet-resolved transport at buried nanointerfaces.

</details>


### [219] [From Local Atomic Motifs to Thermodynamic State: An Interpretable Physics-Informed Framework for Cu-Zr Metallic Glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22992)
*Prashil S. Joshi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种多任务物理信息神经网络（physics-informed neural network, PINN），以 Voronoi 基元（Voronoi motif）分布直方图为输入，直接预测金属玻璃（metallic glasses）的温度并同步分类其热力学状态，在约束学习过程中嵌入物理梯度关系而非事后校验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有将局部原子结构与金属玻璃热力学状态关联的机器学习模型，通常仅在训练后评估物理一致性（physical consistency），而未在学习过程中施加约束；该工作旨在把物理约束直接嵌入基于结构基元的构效关系模型。

Method: 构建多任务 PINN：回归头由 Voronoi 基元分布直方图预测温度，并引入自动微分（autograd）得到的梯度约束，刻画结构基元、淬火速率（quench rate）与温度之间物理上合理的关系；辅助分类头将每个构型判别为液态、过冷/转变态或玻璃态。采用轨迹不相交（trajectory-disjoint）留出测试集、五折按轨迹分组交叉验证（trajectory-grouped cross-validation）、深度集成（deep ensemble）与蒙特卡洛 dropout（Monte Carlo dropout）评估稳健性与预测不确定性，并进行损失权重敏感性分析、与传统机器学习回归器对比以及 SHAP 分析。

Result: 分类任务测试准确率达 96.6%，宏平均 F1（macro-F1）为 0.95；回归头在轨迹不相交留出测试集上平均绝对误差（mean absolute error）为 23.4 K。敏感性分析表明，物理约束可在较宽的损失权重范围内引入而不损害预测精度，同时显著提升对指定物理趋势的符合度；基准对比显示其预测性能具有竞争力。SHAP 分析识别出耦合的近二十面体基元族（near-icosahedral motif family），尤以完整二十面体基元及其单原子扰动对应体为玻璃态的主导结构指纹。

Conclusion: 结果表明物理动机的约束可直接嵌入基于基元的结构-性能模型，为金属玻璃研究提供了一条从"事后可解释性"（post-hoc interpretability）走向物理约束机器学习（physically constrained machine learning）的路径。

Abstract: Machine-learning models that relate local atomic structure to the thermodynamic state of metallic glasses typically assess physical consistency after training rather than enforcing it during learning. Here, we develop a multi-task physics-informed neural network (PINN) that predicts temperature directly from Voronoi-motif population histograms while incorporating autograd-derived gradient constraints representing physically motivated relationships between structural motifs, quench rate, and temperature. The model simultaneously classifies each configuration as liquid, supercooled/transition, or glass through an auxiliary classification head. The classification task achieves 96.6% test accuracy with a macro-F1 score of 0.95, while the regression head yields a mean absolute error of 23.4 K on a trajectory-disjoint held-out test set. Five-fold trajectory-grouped cross-validation, deep-ensemble predictions, and Monte Carlo dropout are used to assess model robustness and predictive uncertainty. Sensitivity analyses demonstrate that physics constraints can be incorporated over a broad range of loss weights without compromising predictive accuracy, while substantially improving compliance with the prescribed physical trends. Benchmarking against conventional machine-learning regressors further demonstrates competitive predictive performance. SHAP analysis across the cross-validation ensemble identifies the coupled near-icosahedral motif family, particularly the full icosahedral motif and its single-atom-perturbed counterpart, as the dominant structural fingerprints of the glassy state. These results demonstrate that physically motivated constraints can be embedded directly into motif-based structure-property models, providing a pathway from post-hoc interpretability toward physically constrained machine learning for metallic glasses.

</details>


### [220] [Perspective: The HSE Screened Hybrid and the Band Gap Problem: Origins, Impact, and the Contenders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23225)
*Gustavo E. Scuseria*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文回顾了屏蔽杂化泛函（screened hybrid functional）HSE 自2000年代初提出以来的发展历程，包括其设计初衷、HSE03到HSE06的修订及对后续缺陷物理、光伏等领域的影响，并评估了近期半局域与杂化泛函在固体基准上的进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HSE 最初的动机并非显式预测带隙（band gap），而是使精确交换（exact exchange）在扩展体系中既经济可行又物理上可辩护；通过将精确交换限制在短程并屏蔽长程，逆转了为分子发展出的配方。

Method: 采用屏蔽杂化策略：将精确交换保留在短程（short range）并在长程（long range）屏蔽；HSE06相对HSE03的修订在保留半导体带隙精度的同时改善了分子热化学；交换分数四分之一沿用自分子无屏蔽PBE杂化，未对固体拟合。本文为Perspective，第一部分回顾诞生决策，第二部分以固体基准评估半局域与杂化泛函进展。

Result: HSE 对普通半导体（semiconductor）及部分金属氧化物（metal oxide）给出的带隙显著接近实验值，优于传统半局域近似（semilocal approximation）；近期 meta-GGA 在选定半导体上达到 HSE06 级精度，而介电相关（dielectric-dependent）与调节型杂化（tuned hybrid）针对固定屏蔽的局限作出改进。

Conclusion: HSE 确立了用于精确带隙计算的实用屏蔽杂化途径，拓展了缺陷物理（defect physics）、光伏（photovoltaics）、功率电子（power electronics）与量子信息（quantum information）等领域的从头算（first-principles）工作；与早期局域杂化（local hybrid）及局域范围分离（local range separation）共享同一目标——使精确交换适应电子环境；相关比较同时评估带隙、结构、能量学、计算成本与可迁移性（transferability）。

Abstract: The ideas behind the screened hybrid functional HSE took shape in the early 2000s, about a quarter century ago. HSE was not explicitly designed to predict band gaps, although at the time we did recognize that hybrids could help address the band-gap problem. It was designed to make exact exchange affordable and physically defensible in extended systems. Keeping exact exchange at short range and screening it away at long range inverted the prescription developed for molecules. When HSE band gaps for ordinary semiconductors and selected metal oxides came out substantially closer to experiment than those obtained with conventional semilocal approximations, we were pleasantly surprised and encouraged by the path we had taken. The revision from HSE03 to HSE06 improved molecular thermochemistry while preserving the good accuracy for semiconductor gaps. The one-quarter exchange fraction was inherited unchanged from the successful unscreened PBE hybrid for molecules, rather than fitted to solids. HSE established a practical screened-hybrid approach for accurate band-gap calculations, expanding first-principles work in defect physics, photovoltaics, power electronics, and quantum information, among other areas. The first part of this Perspective recounts the decisions made at HSE's birth and their influence on subsequent developments. The second part assesses recent advances in semilocal and hybrid functionals against benchmarks on sets of solids. Recent meta-GGAs achieve HSE06-level accuracy for selected semiconductors, while dielectric-dependent and tuned hybrids address the limitations of fixed screening. These hybrid developments share with our early work on local hybrids and local range separation the aim of adapting exact exchange to the electronic environment. The comparisons assess band gaps alongside structures, energetics, computational cost, and transferability.

</details>


### [221] [Trade-off between interface morphology and compositional homogeneity in AlGaAs/GaAs quantum wells revealed by multislice electron ptychography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23245)
*Yiwei Ju,Han-Hsuan Wu,Amberly Ricks,Levi Brown,Moaz Waqar,Rithvik Ramesh,Xingxu Yan,Toshihiro Aoki,Seth R. Bank,Xiaoqing Pan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究利用多层电子叠层成像（multislice electron ptychography）解析 AlGaAs/GaAs 非对称耦合量子阱（asymmetric coupled quantum wells, ACQWs）的三维界面形貌与组分分布，发现尖锐平坦的界面形貌主导二次谐波产生（second harmonic generation, SHG）响应，即便 AlGaAs 层内存在显著 Al/Ga 占位组分涨落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AlGaAs/GaAs 非对称耦合量子阱被视为增强二阶光学非线性（second-order optical nonlinearities）的潜力平台，其性能受异质结构（heterostructures）结构质量强烈影响；平坦界面与高组分均匀性通常被认为是优化器件功能的理想条件，但界面形貌与组分涨落对 SHG 的具体作用尚需厘清。

Method: 采用多层电子叠层成像（multislice electron ptychography），在不同生长中断（growth interruptions）条件下，对 AlGaAs/GaAs ACQWs 的三维界面形貌与组分分布进行解析。

Result: 更长的生长中断使 AlGaAs/GaAs 界面更尖锐、更平坦，但同时导致 AlGaAs 层内组分涨落更强；且尖锐平坦的界面形貌主导 SHG 响应，即便 AlGaAs 层内存在显著的 Al/Ga 占位组分涨落。

Conclusion: 研究阐明了生长中断如何调控量子阱结构，并为下一代光电子器件（optoelectronic devices）中的量子阱设计提供直接指导。

Abstract: AlGaAs/GaAs asymmetric coupled quantum wells (ACQWs) are promising platforms for enhanced second-order optical nonlinearities, with their performance strongly influenced by the structural quality of heterostructures. Flat interfaces, together with high compositional homogeneity, are generally desirable for optimizing device functionality. Here we show that sharp and flat AlGaAs/GaAs interface morphology dominates the second harmonic generation (SHG) response in AlGaAs/GaAs ACQWs, even when significant compositional fluctuations in Al/Ga occupancy are present within the AlGaAs layers. Using multislice electron ptychography, we resolve the three-dimensional (3D) interface morphology and compositional distribution in AlGaAs/GaAs ACQWs under distinct growth interruptions. Longer growth interruptions sharpen and flatten the AlGaAs/GaAs interfaces, but at the same time cause stronger compositional fluctuations in the AlGaAs layers. These findings clarify how growth interruption tunes quantum wells structures and provide direct guidance for quantum wells design in next-generation optoelectronic devices.

</details>


### [222] [Ultralow-Barrier Ion Transport in the Subnitride Electride Ba$_3$N for High-Rate Sodium Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23391)
*Seulbi Kim,Bo Gyu Jang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文利用第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）提出，准一维亚氮化物电子化合物（quasi-one-dimensional subnitride electride）Ba$_3$N 可作为钠离子电池（sodium-ion battery）负极宿主，以自发 Na 嵌入与间隙阴离子电子海实现低电位平坦平台与超快离子传输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子化合物（electrides）中过量电子离域于间隙空隙而非束缚于特定原子位点，具有奇特物理化学行为，但其作为电化学储能（electrochemical energy-storage）宿主的潜力尚未被充分探索。同时，钠离子电池负极长期存在性能权衡（performance trade-offs），如高电位斜坡损失与枝晶形成风险。

Method: 采用全面的第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations），以准一维亚氮化物电子化合物 Ba$_3$N 为对象，考察 Na 在开放链间通道（interchain channels）中的自发嵌入、相稳定性与离子迁移行为。

Result: Na 自发嵌入开放链间通道形成热力学稳定相（thermodynamically stable phases），产生异常平坦的低电位平台（flat low-potential plateau），既避免硬碳（hard carbon）的高电位斜坡损失，又保留防枝晶（dendrite）形成的安全裕度。游走的间隙阴离子电子海（itinerant interstitial anionic electron sea）通过抑制位点特异轨道相互作用动态平坦化电位景观，实现极低迁移势垒（migration barrier）的超快 Na 离子传输。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了利用电子化合物化学（electride chemistry）设计超快电池电极（ultrafast battery electrodes）的设计范式（design paradigm）。

Abstract: Electrides, ionic crystals in which excess electrons are delocalized within interstitial voids rather than bound to specific atomic sites, exhibit exotic physicochemical behaviors, yet their potential as electrochemical energy-storage hosts remains largely unexplored. Here, using comprehensive first-principles calculations, we demonstrate that the quasi-one-dimensional subnitride electride Ba$_3$N offers a compelling route to overcome the long-standing performance trade-offs in sodium-ion battery anodes.
