Spontaneous Na intercalation into the open interchain channels establishes thermodynamically stable phases, generating an exceptionally flat low-potential plateau that avoids the high-potential sloping losses of hard carbon while preserving an essential safety margin against dendrite formation. Crucially, the itinerant interstitial anionic electron sea dynamically flattens the potential landscape by suppressing site-specific orbital interactions, enabling ultrafast Na ion transport with an exceptionally low migration barrier. This work establishes a design paradigm for harnessing electride chemistry to achieve ultrafast battery electrodes.

</details>


### [223] [Sapphire-Side hBN Formation by Ni--Cr-Mediated Growth in a Stacked Ni--Cr/Boron/Sapphire Configuration: Effect of Boron Supply](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23447)
*Donghoi Kim,Aelim Ha,Soohyung Park,Young Duck Kim,Chinkyo Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究在垂直堆叠的 Ni–Cr/硼/蓝宝石（sapphire）体系中，于 1550 °C、常压下考察硼供给量对六方氮化硼（hexagonal boron nitride, hBN）形成位置的影响。结果显示合金侧 hBN 在所有合金:硼比例下均可生成，而蓝宝石侧 hBN 仅在较高硼供给（24:1 与 18:1）下出现，且硼供给增加会提升 c 轴相干长度。作者据此认定硼供给是从合金侧生长转向蓝宝石侧同步生长的关键参数，并首次报道了 Ni–Cr 介导的蓝宝石侧 hBN 生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属介导生长（metal-mediated growth）可获得高质量 hBN，但金属表面生成 hBN 并不意味着相邻介质衬底上也能生成 hBN。该研究即针对这一差异，探究金属介导生长能否延伸至相邻蓝宝石衬底。

Method: 采用垂直堆叠（vertically stacked）Ni–Cr/硼/蓝宝石构型，在 1550 °C、常压条件下运行，固定 Ni:Cr = 8:2 并改变相对硼供给量（Alloy:B）。通过拉曼光谱（Raman spectroscopy）检测 hBN，并辅以 X 射线衍射（XRD）确认蓝宝石侧 hBN。

Result: 拉曼光谱显示，在全部 Alloy:B 范围内，气体暴露的合金表面均可检测到 hBN；蓝宝石侧 hBN 仅在 Alloy:B = 24:1 与 18:1 的较高相对硼供给下出现，并由拉曼光谱与 XRD 共同确认。hBN (002) 反射随硼供给由 24:1 增至 18:1 而变窄，对应表观 c 轴相干长度（apparent c-axis coherent length）从约 86 nm 增大至 143 nm。额外的晶体反射也仅在这些高硼条件下出现。

Conclusion: 硼供给是控制从合金侧 hBN 生成过渡到蓝宝石侧 hBN 同步生长的关键参数。蓝宝石侧 hBN 一旦形成，是处于显著重构且化学改性的富 Al/O 蓝宝石环境中，而非完整衬底上的均匀薄膜。据作者所知，该工作首次展示了 Ni–Cr 介导生长成功实现蓝宝石侧 hBN 生成。

Abstract: Metal-mediated growth provides an effective route to high-quality hexagonal boron nitride (hBN), but hBN formation on the metal does not necessarily ensure hBN formation on an adjoining dielectric substrate. Here, we investigate this distinction using a vertically stacked Ni--Cr/boron/sapphire configuration operated at 1550~$^\circ$C under atmospheric pressure, with the relative boron supply varied at fixed Ni:Cr = 8:2. hBN is detected by Raman spectroscopy on the gas-exposed alloy surface throughout the investigated Alloy:B range, whereas sapphire-side hBN appears only at the higher relative boron supplies of Alloy:B = 24:1 and 18:1, as confirmed by both Raman spectroscopy and XRD. The hBN (002) reflection further narrows as the boron supply increases from 24:1 to 18:1, corresponding to an increase in the apparent c-axis coherent length from approximately 86 to 143~nm. Additional crystalline reflections also emerge only under these higher-boron conditions. These results identify boron supply as a key parameter governing the transition from alloy-side hBN formation to concurrent sapphire-side hBN growth. Once formed, sapphire-side hBN occurs within a substantially reconstructed and chemically modified Al/O-rich sapphire environment rather than as a uniform film on an intact substrate. To our knowledge, this study provides the first demonstration of successful sapphire-side hBN formation by Ni--Cr-mediated growth.

</details>


### [224] [Deterministic control of antiferromagnetic domain walls by circular phonons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23563)
*Vladislav Bilyk,Nikolai Khokhlov,Viktoriia Radovskaia,Peter Kim,Pietro Diona,Ravi Kaushik,Luca Maranzana,Takeshi Hayashida,Sergey Artyukhin,Edwin Hang Tong Teo,Apoorva Chaturvedi,Carl S. Davies,Andrei Kirilyuk,Alexey Kimel,Dmytro Afanasiev*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作利用圆偏振中红外光共振激发 Ni 掺杂 MnPS₃ 中一对近简并的正交红外活性声子模，实现对 180° 反铁磁畴的确定性重构，为反铁磁有序的动态晶格调控提供证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反铁磁体中不同取向的奈尔矢量（Néel vector）畴可共存，使其具备磁存储介质的功能潜力；但该潜力的实现关键取决于能否高效、确定性地控制反铁磁畴壁（domain wall）。

Method: 在范德华反铁磁体（van der Waals antiferromagnet）Ni 掺杂 MnPS₃ 中，通过晶格动力学工程（dynamic engineering of the crystal lattice）实现畴壁调控：以圆偏振中红外光共振激发一对近简并、正交的红外活性 A_u 与 B_u 声子模（phonon modes）。

Result: 该激发诱导了依赖螺旋性（helicity-dependent）的 180° 反铁磁畴重构，表明存在与奈尔序参量共轭的声子诱导有效场（effective field）；畴动力学呈现显著的螺旋性不对称性，由畴壁弹性（domain-wall elasticity）与缺陷介导钉扎（defect-mediated pinning）之间的相互作用所主导，使微小的声子驱动扰动得以累积为稳定的畴转变。

Conclusion: 研究确立了动态晶格调控与缺陷工程（defect engineering）作为确定性控制反铁磁有序的有效途径。

Abstract: In an antiferromagnet, areas with different orientations of the antiferromagnetic Néel vector, which plays the role of the order parameter, can coexist, thus forming antiferromagnetic domains separated by domain walls. This suggests that antiferromagnets can possess the functionalities of magnetic storage media. Whether one can benefit from these functionalities crucially depends on the availability of efficient means for deterministic control of antiferromagnetic domain walls. Here, we demonstrate an approach to deterministically control domain walls in the van der Waals antiferromagnet Ni-doped $MnPS_3$ via dynamic engineering of the crystal lattice. By resonantly exciting a pair of nearly degenerate orthogonal infrared-active $A_u$ and $B_u$ phonon modes with circularly polarized mid-infrared light, we induce helicity-dependent reconfiguration of 180° antiferromagnetic domains, providing evidence for a phonon-induced effective field conjugate to the Néel order parameter. The domain kinetics exhibit a pronounced helicity asymmetry governed by the interplay between domain-wall elasticity and defect-mediated pinning, enabling small phonon-driven perturbations to accumulate into stable domain transformations. Our results establish dynamic lattice control and defect engineering as means for deterministic control of antiferromagnetic order.

</details>


### [225] [Dual Role of Mobile Interstitials in Defect Kinetics: From Retardation to Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23564)
*Shihao Zhang,Shihao Zhu,Junping Du,Shuhei Shinzato,Ju Li,Shigenobu Ogata*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 论文在固定间隙原子化学势（chemical potential）的大正则系综（grand-canonical ensemble）框架下，构建了缺陷激活的自由能景观（free-energy landscape）$ΔG(Λ;T,μ)$ 与激活自由能（activation free energy）$ΔG^{\ddagger}$，并提出仅需采样初态与过渡态的双态化学势积分方法（TSCPI），用于快速预测含间隙原子的缺陷动力学随温度与化学势的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动的间隙原子（interstitial atoms）在缺陷迁移过程中发生再分布，使缺陷动力学（defect kinetics）预测复杂化，并导致关于加速与减速的矛盾报道；因此需要统一处理间隙原子再分布对缺陷激活自由能影响的框架。

Method: 在固定间隙化学势的大正则系综中定义路径自由能景观与激活自由能，并采用两种途径计算：基于平均力关系的超平面约束热力学积分（hyperplane-constrained thermodynamic integration），以及双态化学势积分（two-state chemical-potential integration, TSCPI）。TSCPI 仅需采样初态与过渡态，单次化学势积分即可给出宽 μ 范围内的 $ΔG^{\ddagger}(T,μ)$。

Result: 方法应用于含 H 的 FCC、BCC、HCP 金属（包括 Zr 的面内与跨面扩散）以及含 He 的 BCC W 中的空位扩散（vacancy diffusion），两种自由能途径均与分子动力学（molecular dynamics）均方位移给出的扩散系数一致。所得图谱揭示抑制与增强区域，包括随氢浓度增加由抑制向增强的转变（crossover）。

Conclusion: 位点占据分析（site occupancy analysis）将势垒变化（barrier shifts）与状态依赖的位点谱变化及可迁移的间隙相互作用项相联系；该框架可将矛盾报道统一为化学势依赖的抑制—增强区域，并为跨温度与化学势的快速映射提供工具。

Abstract: Mobile interstitial atoms redistribute while defects migrate, complicating prediction of defect kinetics and leading to contradictory reports of acceleration and retardation. We formulate defect activation in a grand-canonical ensemble at fixed interstitial chemical potential and define a pathway free-energy landscape $ΔG(Λ;T,μ)$ and the corresponding activation free energy $ΔG^{\ddagger}$. We compute these quantities using both hyperplane-constrained thermodynamic integration via a mean force relation and a two-state chemical-potential integration, denoted TSCPI. The latter requires sampling only the initial and transition states; a single chemical-potential integration then yields $ΔG^{\ddagger}(T,μ)$ across a wide $μ$ range, enabling rapid mapping over temperature and chemical potential. Applied to vacancy diffusion in FCC, BCC, and HCP metals with H (including in plane and cross plane diffusion in Zr) and in BCC W with He, both free energy routes agree with diffusion coefficients from molecular dynamics mean square displacements. The resulting maps reveal regimes of suppression and enhancement, including a crossover from suppression to enhancement with increasing hydrogen concentration. A site occupancy analysis links barrier shifts to state dependent site spectrum changes and transferable interstitial interaction terms.

</details>


### [226] [Phase field modelling of microstructure transformations in Zr-Sn alloy during irradiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23623)
*V. O. Kharchenko,D. O. Kharchenko,A. V. Dvornichenko*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作采用相场建模（phase field modelling）结合反应速率理论（reaction rate theory）与CALPHAD方法，在三维条件下研究Zr-Sn合金中溶质与空位浓度的空间重排，涵盖固溶体热处理与反应堆辐照两个阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目标在于揭示Zr-Sn合金在热处理与中子辐照条件下溶质与空位空间重排的特征，并考察第二相（secondary phase）在辐照下的稳定性及其统计性质对辐照剂量的依赖。

Method: 方法上构建了统一化的广义相场建模框架，将反应速率理论与CALPHAD方法相结合，并在三维条件下进行模拟，以获取块体（bulk）内溶质与缺陷重排的详细信息。

Result: 结果讨论了热处理阶段第二相的出现及其在中子辐照下的稳定性，并分析了辐照剂量对第二相颗粒统计性质的影响。

Conclusion: 结论表明，该广义相场方法可用于描述Zr-Sn合金在热处理与反应堆辐照条件下的溶质与缺陷演化，并刻画第二相颗粒统计特征随辐照剂量的变化。

Abstract: In this work we study peculiarities of spatial rearrangement of solute and vacancy concentrations in Zr-Sn alloy at stages of both thermal treatment of solid solution and irradiation of annealed alloy under reactor conditions. To this end, we exploit phase field modelling including reaction rate theory and CALPHAD method combined in one generalized approach. Simulations were done in 3D in order to get a detailed information about rearrangement of solutes and defects in a bulk. We discuss the emergence of the secondary phase at the stage of thermal treatment and its stability under neutron irradiation. We analyze an influence of the irradiation dose onto statistical properties of the secondary phase particles.

</details>


### [227] [Tailoring structural, electronic, optical, and magnetic properties of rare-earth gallates RGaO$_3$ (R = Ho, Er, Tm) via first-principles investigations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23636)
*T. Usman,K. Liaqat,S. Khan,M. Yar Khan,S. Ali Khan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 采用全势线性缀加平面波（FP-LAPW）方法在密度泛函理论（DFT）框架下对立方钙钛矿 RGaO₃（R = Ho, Er, Tm）进行第一性原理（ab initio）计算，系统研究其结构、电子、磁性与光学性质。ErGaO₃ 表现为半导体，HoGaO₃ 与 TmGaO₃ 表现为半金属（half-metallic），并呈现稳健的铁磁特征与自旋选择性光学响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在通过第一性原理计算，系统刻画 RGaO₃（R = Ho, Er, Tm）系列立方钙钛矿的结构、电子、磁性与光学性质，以理解其半金属行为与自旋相关光学响应的来源，并评估其在自旋电子学（spintronics）与光电子学（optoelectronics）中的应用潜力。

Method: 使用全势线性缀加平面波（FP-LAPW）方法，在密度泛函理论（DFT）框架下对立方钙钛矿 RGaO₃（R = Ho, Er, Tm）进行计算，并基于优化构型考察其结构、电子、磁性与光学性质。

Result: 优化后晶格常数处于 3.83–3.85 Å 区间。能带结构显示 ErGaO₃ 为半导体，而 HoGaO₃ 与 TmGaO₃ 呈半金属行为，可能源于自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）与费米能级附近电子态的相互作用。半金属化合物的光学响应受其自旋极化电子结构主导：半导体自旋通道吸收紫外光子，金属或小带隙通道与红外光相互作用。化合物具有高表面反射率，其中 TmGaO₃ 最大反射率达 69%；光学电导主要发生于较高能量，计算磁矩表明该系列具有稳健的铁磁特征。

Conclusion: RGaO₃（R = Ho, Er, Tm）系列中半金属化合物表现出由自旋极化电子结构主导的双通道光学行为，可实现自旋选择性光学激发，并具有高表面反射率与高能光学电导，适用于自旋电子学与高能光电子学应用；同时该系列整体呈稳健铁磁性。

Abstract: Ab initio calculations of cubic perovskites RGaO$_3$ (R = Ho, Er, Tm) were performed using the full-potential linearized augmented plane wave (FP-LAPW) method within the framework of density functional theory (DFT). We investigated their structural, electronic, magnetic, and optical properties in detail. In the optimized configurations, the lattice constants ranged from 3.83 to 3.85 A. The electronic band structures indicate that ErGaO$_3$ is semiconducting, whereas HoGaO$_3$ and TmGaO$_3$ exhibit half-metallic behavior, probably due to the interaction of spin-orbit coupling with the electronic states near the Fermi level. Optical response of half-metallic compounds (HoGaO$_3$,TmGaO$_3$) is strongly governed by their spin polarization electronic structure. The semiconducting spin channel absorbs ultraviolet photons, while metallic or small gap channel interacts with infrared light. This dual behavior enables spin selective optical excitation, relevant for spintronics and optoelectronics applications. These compounds show high surface reflectivity, with TmGaO$_3$ achieving a maximum reflectivity of 69%. Moreover, their optical conductivity mainly occurs at higher energies, indicating suitability for high-energy optoelectronic applications. The calculated magnetic moments suggest a robust ferromagnetic character across this series.

</details>


### [228] [Computational investigation of a perovskite LaBiO$_{3}$ for photovoltaic, thermoelectric, and optoelectronic applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23641)
*M. M. Woldemariam,N. G. Debelo,T. K. Tufa,E. M. Gurmesa,S. H. Didu,S. N. Asfaw,D. T. Keno*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究采用密度泛函理论（DFT）结合GGA+U方法，系统计算了三方相LaBiO$_3$钙钛矿氧化物的结构、电子、弹性、光学与热电性质，揭示其间接宽禁带（3.51 eV）与延展性特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在全面表征三方相（trigonal phase）LaBiO$_3$钙钛矿氧化物（perovskite oxide，空间群 R3c）的多物理性质，以评估其在光学与热电领域的应用潜力。

Method: 采用ONCVVPSP赝势（pseudopotential）与PBE泛函的密度泛函理论，通过状态方程（equation of state）确定基态参数，引入Hubbard校正（GGA+U）计算电子结构，并以Voigt-Reuss-Hill平均方案（Voigt-Reuss-Hill averaging scheme）获取力学性质，同时计算光学与半经典输运系数（semi-classical transport coefficients）。

Result: 计算得到晶格常数、体积、体模量（bulk modulus）及其压力导数；GGA+U给出3.51 eV的间接宽禁带；体模量与剪切模量之比表明该三方相具有延展性（ductile），并获得德拜温度（Debye temperature）与声速；光学性质在0-35 eV范围评估；热电方面获得电导率、塞贝克系数（Seebeck coefficient）与功率因子。

Conclusion: 三方相LaBiO$_3$为具间接宽禁带的延展性材料，其光学与热电参数的计算结果为该材料在相关功能器件中的潜在应用提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Using density functional theory (DFT) with the ONCVVPSP pseudopotential and PBE functional, this study investigates the structural, electronic, elastic, optical, and thermoelectric properties of the trigonal LaBiO$_{3}$ perovskite oxide (space group R3c). Ground-state parameters lattice constant, volume, bulk modulus, and its pressure derivative were determined using the equation of state. Applying the Hubbard correction (GGA+U) revealed an indirect, wide band gap of 3.51 eV. Mechanical properties, including the anisotropy factor, elastic modulus, and Poisson's ratio, were calculated via the Voigt-Reuss-Hill averaging scheme. The bulk-to-shear modulus ratio identifies the trigonal phase as ductile. Additionally, Debye temperatures and sound velocities were computed. Optical characteristics (absorption coefficient, refractive index, and electron energy loss function) were evaluated across a 0-35 eV spectral range. Finally, semi-classical transport coefficients, including electrical conductivity, Seebeck coefficient, and power factor, were calculated to assess the material's thermoelectric potential.

</details>


### [229] [Electronic energy structure and optical properties of In$_{4}$CdI$_{6}$ from $\textit{ab initio}$ calculations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23644)
*I. V. Semkiv,N. T. Pokladok,F. O. Ivashchyshyn,A. I. Kashuba*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作采用第一性原理（ab initio）方法计算 In₄CdI₆ 化合物的电子能谱，基于 DFT 框架并结合 LDA、GGA（PBE、PBEsol）与 HSE06 杂化泛函，确定最小带隙类型、分析能带色散、计算电子/空穴有效质量与态密度（DOS），并进一步得到介电函数的实部与虚部，以及折射率 n 与消光系数 k 等光学函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确阐述研究动机，仅表明目的在于表征 In₄CdI₆ 的电子能谱与光学性质。

Method: 在密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）框架下进行第一性原理（ab initio）计算，采用局域密度近似（LDA）与广义梯度近似（GGA）赝势，并引入 Heyd-Scuseria-Ernzerhof（HSE06）杂化泛函；GGA 部分使用 PBE 与 PBEsol 交换关联泛函。

Result: 基于电子能带结构确定了最小带隙（minimum band gap）类型，分析了价带与导带的能级色散（energy level dispersion），建立了 In₄CdI₆ 的电子有效质量（m_c）与空穴有效质量（m_v），并计算了态密度（DOS）。由电子能谱得到介电函数（dielectric function）的实部与虚部，并借助 Kramers-Kronig 关系导出折射率（n）与消光系数（k）。

Conclusion: 研究给出了 In₄CdI₆ 的电子能谱、有效质量、态密度及由介电函数导出的基本光学函数（n、k），构成对该化合物电子与光学性质的系统性计算表征。

Abstract: This work presents the $\textit{ab initio}$ calculations of the electronic energy spectrum of the In$_{4}$CdI$_{6}$ compound. The study was conducted within the framework of density functional theory (DFT), using local density approximation (LDA) and general gradient approximation (GGA) pseudopotentials, and the Heyd-Scuseria-Ernzerhof (HSE06) hybrid functional. The GGA approximation was implemented using the PBE and PBEsol exchange-correlation functional. Based on the electronic energy structure, the type of the minimum band gap was determined, and an analysis of the energy level dispersion for both the valence and conduction bands was performed. Furthermore, the effective masses of electrons ($\textit{m}_{c}$) and holes ($\textit{m}_{v}$) for In$_{4}$CdI$_{6}$ were established. The energy band analysis was complemented by the calculation of the density of states (DOS). Based on the electronic energy spectrum, the real and imaginary components of the dielectric function are calculated. Using the Kramers-Kronig relations, we also derive such fundamental optical functions of In$_{4}$CdI$_{6}$ as the refractive index $\textit{n}$, and the extinction coefficient $\textit{k}$.

</details>


### [230] [A small-distortion geometric model for elasto-plasticity in single crystals driven by the motion of dislocations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23728)
*Filip Rindler*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出"小畸变几何模型"（Small-Distortion Geometric Model），以两条假设——小畸变假设（Small-Distortion Hypothesis）与线张力近似（Line-Tension Approximation）——构建单晶几何非线性大应变弹塑性理论，使塑性流动由位错运动直接驱动，并支持从离散位错线到位错密度的均匀化（homogenization）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性弹塑性理论（nonlinear elasto-plasticity theory）的核心难题在于：如何建立几何非线性、大应变的单晶弹塑性模型，使塑性流动直接由位错运动驱动，并允许从离散位错线向位错密度的均匀化。该工作即针对这一未解问题提出新的建模框架。

Method: 基于两条假设进行建模：(1) 小畸变假设（Small-Distortion Hypothesis），允许总塑性畸变任意大，但其主导部分可表示为梯度，即位错的畸变效应相对试样尺寸很小，具体通过"乘性 Helmholtz 型分解"（multiplicative Helmholtz-type decomposition）实现，将任意矩阵场分解为梯度与受控旋度（curl）矩阵场之积；(2) 线张力近似（Line-Tension Approximation），将所有位错视为无穷细线，其局部应力场相对试样尺度应力可忽略，仅在聚合意义下起作用。模型还采用"时空框架"（Space-Time Framework）以精确描述位错线的平流（advection）。

Result: 由此得到的模型称为小畸变几何模型（Small-Distortion Geometric Model），在理想化介观（mesoscopic）设定下提供了晶体塑性（crystal plasticity）的具有物理依据的描述。

Conclusion: 该模型在介观尺度上弥合了位错力学（dislocation mechanics）与连续介质弹塑性（continuum elasto-plasticity）之间的鸿沟，为单晶几何非线性大应变弹塑性提供了统一的理论语言。

Abstract: A central problem in nonlinear elasto-plasticity theory is to formulate a geometrically nonlinear, large-strain model of elasto-plasticity in single crystals in which plastic flow is driven directly by the motion of dislocations and which allows for a homogenization procedure from discrete dislocation lines to dislocation densities. In this work, such a model is introduced based on two hypotheses: (1) The Small-Distortion Hypothesis posits that the total plastic distortion may be arbitrarily large but its dominant part admits a representation as a gradient, meaning that the distorting effect of dislocations is small relative to the specimen size. Concretely, this hypothesis is realized here through a "multiplicative Helmholtz-type decomposition", which splits an arbitrary matrix field into the product of a gradient and a matrix field with controlled curl. (2) The Line-Tension Approximation treats all dislocations as infinitesimally thin lines whose local stress fields are individually negligible compared to specimen-scale stresses and only matter in the aggregate. The resulting model, termed the Small-Distortion Geometric Model, furthermore employs the so-called Space-Time Framework, which furnishes a geometric language to precisely describe the advection of dislocation lines. As such, it provides a physically grounded account of crystal plasticity in an idealized mesoscopic setting that bridges dislocation mechanics and continuum elasto-plasticity.

</details>


### [231] [Experimentally validated process-microstructure-property relations of bainitic steels derived from phase-field simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23737)
*Dhanunjaya Kumar Nerella,Muhammad Adil Ali,Oguz Gulbay,Oleg Shchyglo,Ingo Steinbach*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究结合三维相场模拟（three-dimensional phase-field simulation）与屈服面（yield surface）分析，探讨热处理等加工条件对贝氏体钢（bainitic steel）微观组织与多轴载荷下力学性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在揭示加工条件—微观组织—力学性能之间的关联，为贝氏体钢的设计与应用以及预测模型开发提供依据。

Method: 采用三维相场模拟获取微观组织特征，包括贝氏体形貌（bainite morphology）、相分布（phase distribution）与残余奥氏体含量（retained austenite content），并将其与多轴载荷（multiaxial load）下屈服强度的变化进行关联，以屈服面表征。

Result: 结果表明，优化的加工路径可细化微观组织、提升力学性能，并显著改变屈服面特征。

Conclusion: 研究结论强调加工条件对贝氏体钢微观组织与屈服行为的显著调控作用，对贝氏体钢设计及工艺—组织—性能（process-structure-property）预测模型的构建具有参考价值。

Abstract: This study examines the impact of processing conditions, such as thermalprocessing, on the resulting microstructure and mechanical properties. Spe-cial emphasis is placed on microstructural features obtained from three-dimensional phase-field simulations, which provide detailed insights into bai-nite morphology, phase distribution and retained austenite content. Thesesimulated microstructures are correlated with changes in yield strength undermultiaxial load, as represented in the yield surface of the material. The re-sults demonstrate that optimized processing routes can refine the microstruc-ture, enhance mechanical properties and significantly alter the yield surfacecharacteristics. These findings provide valuable insights for the design andapplication of bainitic steels, as well as for the development of predictivemodels linking process-structure-property relationships.

</details>


### [232] [Transitions between bulk and interfacial fracture in diamond/$c$BN heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23963)
*Wei Qiu,Feiyu Zhou,Xiaonan Wang,Feng Xie,Yan Chen,Shengying Yue,Yilun Liu,Penghua Ying*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 结合密度泛函理论（DFT）与精调原子基础模型，揭示金刚石/立方氮化硼（cBN）异质结的拉伸断裂受界面终止、晶向与互混共同控制，并给出强度随扩散分数非线性下降及体相—界面断裂转变的三阶段规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无初始裂纹异质结究竟在界面还是相邻相内部断裂，取决于竞争原子面的相对内聚强度（cohesion），但界面化学、晶向与互混如何重塑这一竞争关系尚不清晰。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论（DFT）与经精调的原子尺度基础模型（atomistic foundation model）相结合，解析共格金刚石/立方氮化硼（cBN）异质结的拉伸断裂；所得势函数可重现独立 DFT 拉伸响应，包括未参与训练的 (001) 界面取向。分析手段包括原子分辨应力场、DFT 分离能（separation energetics）与电子局域化分析。

Result: C-N 键合的 (111) 与 C-B 键合的 (001) 界面在所研究的互混范围内均保持界面控制断裂；而洁净 C-B 键合 (111) 界面因界面结合更强，断裂发生在 cBN 内相邻的 B-N 面。扩散分数由 0 增至 50.0% 时，断裂强度由 50.3 GPa 非线性降至 15.8 GPa，并驱动体相到界面的转变，分为三个阶段：cBN 断裂（至 4.86%）、构型依赖的竞争（7.6%–10.1%）、界面断裂（12.5% 及以上）。

Conclusion: 该转变与应力重分布（stress relocation）及竞争面相对内聚强度的反转相关，电子局域化分析将内聚层级与终止类型和晶向依赖的键合联系起来；由此建立了调控强键合异质结断裂抗力与断裂路径的原子尺度框架。

Abstract: Whether an initially crack-free heterostructure fails at its interface or within an adjoining phase is controlled by the relative cohesion of competing atomic planes, but how interfacial chemistry, crystallographic orientation, and intermixing reshape this competition remains unclear. Here, we combine density functional theory (DFT) with a fine-tuned atomistic foundation model to resolve tensile fracture in coherent diamond/cubic boron nitride (cBN) heterostructures. The resulting potential reproduces independent DFT tensile responses, including an unseen (001) interface orientation. Interfacial termination, orientation, and diffusion-induced intermixing jointly determine fracture resistance and fracture-plane selection. C-N-bonded (111) and C-B-bonded (001) remain interface-controlled throughout the investigated intermixing range, whereas pristine C-B-bonded (111) fractures at a neighboring B-N plane inside cBN because the interface is more strongly bound. Increasing the diffusion fraction from 0 to 50.0% causes a nonlinear decrease in fracture strength from 50.3 to 15.8 GPa and drives a bulk-to-interface transition through three regimes: cBN fracture up to 4.86%, configuration-dependent competition between 7.6 and 10.1%, and interfacial fracture at 12.5% and above. Atom-resolved stress fields and DFT separation energetics show that this transition is associated with stress relocation and a reversal in the relative cohesion of competing planes, while electron localization analysis connects the cohesion hierarchy to termination- and orientation-dependent bonding. These results establish an atomistic framework for controlling fracture resistance and fracture pathways in strongly bonded heterostructures.

</details>


### [233] [Enhanced thermal conductivity of (010) (AlxGa1-x)2O3 epitaxial films utilizing indium-catalyzed molecular beam epitaxy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24081)
*Shivashree Gowda,Stephen Schaefer,Ethan A. Scott,Samreen Khan,Patrick E. Hopkins,M. Brooks Tellekamp*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作通过铟催化分子束外延（MBE）生长高质量(AlxGa1-x)2O3薄膜，抑制位错与相分离，使其热导率较此前报道提升2倍，并在0.01<x<0.20范围内观测到由合金散射主导的热导率下降趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: (AlxGa1-x)2O3/beta-Ga2O3晶体管是高压高频电子器件的潜在候选，但beta-Ga2O3及各向异性低热导率限制了其应用，而Al合金化会因合金散射与缺陷散射进一步降低热导率，因此需明确其热输运机制。

Method: 在beta-Ga2O3衬底上采用分子束外延（MBE），借助铟催化生长抑制位错形成与相分离，制备0.01<x<0.20的单相赝配（pseudomorphic）(010)取向薄膜，并用时域热反射（TDTR）测量热导率，结合虚拟晶体近似（VCA）与漫反射失配模型（DMM）进行分析。

Result: (AlxGa1-x)2O3热导率仅受合金散射限制而未观察到额外缺陷的不利散射，其数值较此前报道提高2倍；随Al组分x增大热导率持续下降，与VCA模型吻合；Al/(AlxGa1-x)2O3界面热边界导随x增大而降低，归因于声学模随x增加的硬化（stiffening）。

Conclusion: 铟催化MBE可有效抑制缺陷与相分离并获得高热导率(AlxGa1-x)2O3薄膜，其热学特征与合金散射及声学模硬化机制一致，为优化界面与组分的异质结构设计提供依据。

Abstract: (AlxGa1-x)2O3/beta-Ga2O3 transistors are an emerging candidate for high-power and high-frequency electronic devices. beta-Ga2O3 in particular is notably limited for anisotropic low thermal conductivity, which is further reduced in (AlxGa1-x)2O3 due to alloy and other defect-driven phonon scattering mechanisms. In this work we show that the thermal conductivity of (010) oriented (AlxGa1-x)2O3 thin films for 0.01 < x < 0.20, measured using time-domain thermoreflectance (TDTR), is limited by alloy scattering without observable adverse scattering by additional defects. This is enabled through the synthesis of the (AlxGa1-x)2O3 films using molecular beam epitaxy (MBE) on \b{eta}-Ga2O3 substrates, leveraging indium-catalyzed growth to suppress dislocation formation and phase separation to achieve single-phase pseudomorphic films up to x = 0.2. This growth process improved the thermal conductivity of (AlxGa1-x)2O3 by 2X as compared to previously reported values. For increasing Al composition (x), we observe a steady decline in (AlxGa1-x)2O3 thermal conductivity due to alloy scattering which is validated using virtual crystal approximation (VCA) model. We also show that the thermal boundary conductance across the Al/(AlxGa1-x)2O3 interface is reduced with increasing x, which we posit is due to the stiffening of the (AlxGa1-x)2O3 acoustic modes with increasing x by comparing experimental results with a diffuse mismatch model (DMM). Overall, these thermal characteristics provide valuable insights for designing heterostructures with optimized interfaces and composition.

</details>


### [234] [Polar vibrational excitations and glass-like response in relaxor PMN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24100)
*Kehan Cai,Pinchen Xie,Yifan Li,Roberto Car*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究利用基于第一性原理数据训练的原子尺度模型（atomistic models），揭示典型弛豫铁电体（relaxor ferroelectrics）PMN 中成分无序（compositional disorder）如何将弹性运动与电极化耦合，产生连续的极性振动激发谱，从而统一解释其介电、光学与热学异常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弛豫铁电体在保持晶体序（crystalline order）的同时表现出类玻璃的介电与热学响应，其微观机制尚待阐明。研究以典型体系 Pb(Mg_{1/3}Nb_{2/3})O_3（PMN）为对象，探索这类响应的起源。

Method: 采用基于第一性原理（first-principles）数据训练的原子尺度模型（atomistic models）对 PMN 进行模拟，考察成分无序对弹性与极化耦合的影响，并计算低频磁化率（susceptibility）、远红外光谱（far-infrared spectra）及相关激发谱。

Result: 成分无序使弹性运动与电极化耦合，产生连续的极性振动激发谱，区别于常规铁电体 PbTiO_3 的离散极性光学模（polar optical modes）。模拟定性再现了低频磁化率的温度依赖，远红外谱与实验吻合。低频下无序产生玻色峰（boson peak），包含过量类声子模与准局域激发（quasi-localized excitations）；后者态密度呈四次频率依赖并具显著非谐性（anharmonicity），类似结构玻璃。

Conclusion: 过量模式解释了 PMN 在高于 5 K 时比热（specific heat）的异常行为。该结果通过无序诱导的极性激发谱，将晶体弛豫铁电体的介电、光学与热学异常联系起来。

Abstract: Relaxor ferroelectrics retain crystalline order while exhibiting glass-like dielectric and thermal responses. Using atomistic models trained on first-principles data, we investigate these responses in the prototypical relaxor Pb(Mg_{1/3}Nb_{2/3})O_3 (PMN). Compositional disorder couples elastic motion to electric polarization, producing a continuum of polar vibrational excitations, unlike the discrete polar optical modes of conventional ferroelectric PbTiO_3. The simulations qualitatively reproduce the temperature dependence of the low-frequency susceptibility, while the calculated far-infrared spectra agree closely with experiments. At low frequencies, disorder in PMN generates a boson peak comprising excess phonon-like modes and quasi-localized excitations. The latter exhibit a quartic frequency dependence of their density of states and pronounced anharmonicity, as in structural glasses. These excess modes account for PMN's anomalous behavior in the specific heat for temperature higher than 5 K. These results connect the dielectric, optical, and thermal anomalies of a crystalline relaxor through its disorder-induced polar excitation spectrum.

</details>


### [235] [Ferroelectric switching of odd-parity magnon spin splitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24182)
*Yuhan Liang,Xingyu Yan,Bowen Hao,Ziye Zhu,Tianle Sui,Daniel Pharis,Xiaoxi Huang,Rakshit Jain,Tong Zhou,Di Yi,Wanjun Jiang,Pu Yu,Igor Žutić,Yuan-Hua Lin,Daniel C. Ralph,Tianxiang Nan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作报道了室温下多铁性BiFeO3中由铁电翻转调控的奇宇称磁振子自旋劈裂的输运证据，将奇宇称自旋劈裂从费米子电子态拓展至玻色子集体模式，并实现非易失电控与逻辑存内计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁对称性可在不产生净磁化的情况下解除动量空间的自旋简并，偶宇称自旋织构已在交替磁体（altermagnets）中确立，而奇宇称织构虽在补偿磁体的电子能带中出现，其在集体玻色激发中的对应物仍缺乏实验证据；此外，室温下以电压编程自旋劈裂仍是更广泛的挑战。

Method: 采用对称性分析与自旋波计算，结合室温输运测量，在BiFeO3中注入面内极化自旋流，观测其传播中产生的面外磁振子自旋分量及其晶向角度依赖，并通过铁电翻转检验其符号反转。

Result: 对称性分析与自旋波计算表明，摆线手性（cycloidal chirality）使相反自旋的磁振子支发生动量奇宇称的劈裂并决定其符号；实验中面外磁振子自旋分量呈现理论预言的晶向角度依赖，并在铁电翻转后反转，构成自旋劈裂磁振子态的输运指纹。

Conclusion: 该研究将奇宇称自旋劈裂从费米子电子态扩展至玻色子集体模式，建立了室温下的非易失电控，并利用编程的磁振子自旋驱动垂直铁磁体的确定性无场翻转，演示了XNOR逻辑存内计算。

Abstract: Magnetic symmetry can lift spin degeneracy in momentum space without producing net magnetization, generating spin textures classified by their parity under momentum reversal. Even-parity textures have well established in altermagnets. Odd-parity spin textures have recently emerged in electronic bands of compensated magnets, but their counterpart in collective bosonic excitations remains experimentally unresolved. Magnons provide a natural setting for this extension because they carry spin angular momentum through insulating magnets without accompanying charge flow. Beyond realizing this missing state, a broader challenge is to program spin splitting with voltage at room temperature. Here we report room-temperature transport evidence for ferroelectric switching odd-parity magnon spin splitting in multiferroic BiFeO3. Symmetry analysis and spin-wave calculations reveal that the cycloidal chirality splits opposite-spin magnon branches with an odd-in-momentum dependence and sets the sign. Experimentally, an injected in-plane-polarized spin current generates an out-of-plane magnon spin component during propagation. This component exhibits the crystalline angular dependence predicted by theory and reverses upon ferroelectric switching, providing a transport fingerprint of the spin-split magnon state. We use the programmed magnon spin to drive deterministic field-free switching of a perpendicular ferromagnet and demonstrate XNOR logic-in-memory. Our results extend odd-parity spin splitting from fermionic electronic states to bosonic collective modes and establish nonvolatile electrical control at room temperature.

</details>


### [236] [Ultrahigh Thermal Conductivity in Isotopically Ultrapure Single-Crystal Diamond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24191)
*Jinwen Liu,Junliang Fan,Zifeng Huang,Zhe Cheng*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 极低13C含量单晶金刚石实现约3100 W m⁻¹ K⁻¹的室温热导率，虽高于天然金刚石，但未超越高纯12C金刚石最佳值，提示同位素纯化极限外仍有其他声子散射机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金刚石是天然材料中热导率最高者，降低同位素散射有望进一步逼近其热导率上限。研究旨在通过对13C的极端同位素纯化来检验该极限。

Method: 制备ppm级13C含量的金刚石单晶，使用二次离子质谱（secondary ion mass spectrometry, SIMS）表征13C浓度及杂质，并采用时域热反射（time-domain thermoreflectance）测量室温热导率。

Result: SIMS显示13C浓度仅约0.00024%，其他杂质可忽略，为迄今报道同位素纯化程度最高的金刚石样品；室温热导率约3100 W m⁻¹ K⁻¹，明显高于天然金刚石，但与同位素较不纯的12C金刚石最佳报道值相当。

Conclusion: 在该极端同位素纯化水平下，除同位素散射外可能还存在其他声子散射机制，空位缺陷（vacancy defects）或为热导率残留抑制的贡献因素。

Abstract: Diamond has the highest thermal conductivity among natural materials. Reducing isotope scattering can further push the limit of thermal conductivity of diamond. Here, we report the room-temperature thermal conductivity of a diamond single crystal with an exceptionally low ppm-level 13C content. Secondary ion mass spectrometry (SIMS) reveals a 13C concentration of only ~0.00024% and negligible other impurities, placing this sample as the most isotopically purified diamond samples reported to date. Time-domain thermoreflectance measurements show a room-temperature thermal conductivity of ~3100 W m-1 K-1. Although this value is markedly higher than that of natural diamond, it remains comparable to the best reported values for less isotopically 12C pure diamond. This result suggests that additional phonon-scattering mechanisms beyond isotope scattering may exist at this extreme isotopic purification level. Vacancy defects may contribute to the remaining suppression of thermal conductivity.

</details>


### [237] [Electronic-Entropy-Driven Phase Transitions in Compressed Iron Oxides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24281)
*S. Azadi,S. M. Vinko,C. Crepisson,A. Principi,T. D. Kuehne,M. S. Bahramy*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作表明，在强电子激发（electronic excitation）条件下，电子熵（electronic entropy）可从次要修正项上升为主导热力学驱动力，驱动压缩铁氧化物（iron oxides）发生同质多象（polymorphic）与化学计量（stoichiometric）相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子熵通常仅被视为结构稳定性（structural stability）的次级修正，其在强电子激发下的主导作用尚未被系统认识，尤其对压缩铁氧化物相行为的调控缺乏定量研究。

Method: 采用有限温度密度泛函理论（finite-temperature density functional theory），计算 Fe₂O、FeO、Fe₄O₅、Fe₃O₄ 及多种 Fe₂O₃ 同质多象（α-、ι-、ζ-、η-、θ-Fe₂O₃）的电子温度依赖 Gibbs 自由能（Gibbs free energy），压力范围为 60–260 GPa。

Result: 60–140 GPa 下电子激发主要改变 Fe₂O₃ 多象相对稳定性，驱动 ι-Fe₂O₃ 向 η-Fe₂O₃ 转变；180 GPa 下自由能格局竞争激烈，FeO 在中等电子温度区间被稳定，更高温度则 η-Fe₂O₃ 有利；220–260 GPa 下低温最低自由能相为富铁的 Fe₂O，升温则稳定 FeO。

Conclusion: 电子熵既能控制固定成分（composition）下晶体结构的相对稳定性，也能调控不同铁氧化物化学计量之间的竞争；所预测的电子熵驱动相边界（phase boundaries）为超快（ultrafast）与高能量密度（high-energy-density）实验中的非热结构转变提供了途径。

Abstract: Electronic entropy is usually treated as a secondary correction to structural stability, but under strong electronic excitation it can become a primary thermodynamic driving force. Here we show that electronic entropy can drive both polymorphic and stoichiometric phase transformations in compressed iron oxides. Using finite-temperature density functional theory, we calculate the electronic-temperature-dependent Gibbs free energies of Fe$_2$O, FeO, Fe$_4$O$_5$, Fe$_3$O$_4$, and multiple Fe$_2$O$_3$ polymorphs, including $α$-, $ι$-, $ζ$-, $η$-, and $θ$-Fe$_2$O$_3$, over the pressure range 60--260 GPa. At 60-140 GPa, electronic excitation mainly reorganizes the relative stability of Fe$_2$O$_3$ polymorphs, driving transitions from $ι$-Fe$_2$O$_3$ to $η$-Fe$_2$O$_3$. At 180 GPa, the free-energy landscape becomes strongly competitive as FeO is stabilized over an intermediate range of electronic temperature, while $η$-Fe$_2$O$_3$ becomes favourable at higher T. At 220-260 GPa, the lowest-free-energy phase at low T is the Fe-rich compound Fe$_2$O, but increasing electronic temperature stabilizes FeO. These results demonstrate that electronic entropy can control not only the relative stability of crystal structures at fixed composition, but also the competition between different iron-oxide stoichiometries. The predicted electronic-entropy-driven phase boundaries provide a route to nonthermal structural transformations in ultrafast and high-energy-density experiments.

</details>


### [238] [Small-supercell and Small-dataset Training Strategy of Machine Learning Interatomic Potentials for Point Defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24293)
*Zhenxing Dai,Mingjue Ni,Xinpeng Li,Menglin Huang,Anderson Janotti,Shiyou Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出一种基于小超胞与少量 DFT 计算的高效机器学习原子间势（MLIP）训练方案，可用于中性及低电荷点缺陷，仅需四次 DFT 结构弛豫即可预测大超胞中的缺陷形成能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLIP 可兼顾大尺度与近第一性原理精度，但其训练依赖大量 DFT 数据；带电缺陷尤其需要大超胞以避免长程库仑相互作用与有限尺寸效应，导致数据构建成本高昂。

Method: 基于小于 100 原子的超胞与有限次 DFT 计算，仅通过四次 DFT 结构弛豫构建训练集；以 GaN、SiO₂ 和 Cu₂ZnSnS₄ 中的缺陷为代表性案例，考察该方案在不同超胞尺寸下预测缺陷总能量与结构弛豫的外推能力，并比较单一小超胞、多尺寸超胞及加入无缺陷体相超胞的效果。

Result: 训练所得缺陷专用 MLIP 可在超过 200 原子的大超胞中预测缺陷形成能，误差普遍小于 0.3 eV；仅用单个小缺陷超胞训练时，处理大超胞会产生严重误差；引入多个超胞尺寸的缺陷数据可提升预测性能，进一步加入无缺陷体相超胞则进一步提高精度。

Conclusion: 该工作为以低计算成本训练缺陷体系 MLIP 模型提供了实践指导，并表明多尺寸与体相数据的引入对提升外推精度具有重要作用。

Abstract: Machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) can treat large-scale material systems with near first-principles accuracy and have been widely used to accelerate point-defect simulations. However, the training of MLIPs usually relies on large amounts of DFT data. This issue is particularly pronounced for charged defects, for which DFT calculations of large supercells are required to avoid long-range Coulomb interactions and finite-size effects, making the construction of datasets computationally expensive. In this work, we propose an efficient MLIP training scheme for neutral and lowly charged point defects based on small supercells (less than 100 atoms) and limited number of DFT calculations. The scheme requires only four DFT structural relaxations to construct the training dataset and the trained MLIPs dedicated for the defect can predict the defect formation energies in larger supercells (over 200 atoms) with small errors (mostly smaller than 0.3 eV). Using defects in GaN, SiO$_2$, and $\mathrm{Cu}_2\mathrm{ZnSnS}_4$ as representative examples, we evaluate the extrapolation capability of this scheme for predicting defect total energies and structural relaxations across different supercell sizes. The results show that an MLIP trained only on single small defect supercell produces severe errors when treating large supercells. Incorporating defect data from multiple supercell sizes improves the predictive performance of the MLIP, while adding pristine defect-free bulk supercells further enhances the accuracy. These results provide practical guidance for training MLIP models of defect systems with low computational costs.

</details>


### [239] [Vacancy aggregation enhances NV- spin coherence in diamond: a cluster-correlation-expansion study of multi-vacancy spin baths in semiconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24295)
*Chikara Shinei*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究利用团簇关联展开（cluster-correlation-expansion）模拟表明，半导体中多空位复合体（multi-vacancy complexes）构成的顺磁浴（paramagnetic bath）在相同自旋密度下比孤立负空位浴产生更长的Hahn回波相干时间（2.7-4.4倍）。决定相干时间的并非零场分裂（zero-field splitting），而是浴中共享跃迁频率的自旋对比例；因此空位聚集实际上延长而非缩短NV-自旋相干。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既往观点将退火或辐照后金刚石中多空位复合体视为磁噪声来源，并归因于NV-退相干（decoherence），且有两项相干性纪录被归功于去除这些中心。本文旨在检验并反转这一归因。

Method: 采用团簇关联展开模拟（cluster-correlation-expansion simulations），以已发表的电子顺磁共振（electron-paramagnetic-resonance, EPR）参数作为输入，比较固定顺磁自旋密度下多空位浴与孤立负空位浴的Hahn回波相干时间，并分析跃迁频率匹配与精细结构分裂（fine-structure splitting）的作用。

Result: 在固定顺磁自旋密度下，多空位浴的Hahn回波相干时间为孤立负空位浴的2.7-4.4倍，且在三个数量级的浓度范围内保持恒定。若所有缺陷位于同一晶位，频率共同偏移仅带来1.3倍增益；实际缺陷占据对称等价但取向不同的格位，使近邻频率错开而停止交换，再增益1.9倍。

Conclusion: 相干时间由浴中共享跃迁频率的自旋对比例决定，而非零场分裂。空位聚集实际上延长而非缩短NV-自旋相干，反转了将多空位中心视为退相干主因的既有归因。

Abstract: Annealing a semiconductor makes its vacancies mobile; they aggregate into multi-vacancy complexes that often carry spin. Such centres are a magnetic-noise source for any spin qubit among them, in silicon and silicon carbide as well as in diamond, and they are accordingly blamed for NV- decoherence in irradiated and implanted diamond, with two coherence records credited to removing them. Cluster-correlation-expansion simulations driven by published electron-paramagnetic-resonance parameters invert that attribution. At a fixed paramagnetic spin density a multi-vacancy bath gives a Hahn-echo coherence time 2.7-4.4 times longer than a bath of isolated negative vacancies, constant over three decades of concentration. A bath dephases the qubit because its spins exchange spin projections with one another, and two can exchange only if their transition frequencies match. A fine-structure splitting shifts those frequencies. Were every defect on the same crystallographic site, all would shift alike and still match: worth only a factor 1.3. Real defects occupy symmetry-equivalent sites pointing in different directions, so neighbours land at different frequencies and stop exchanging: a further 1.9. What governs the coherence time is therefore the fraction of bath pairs sharing a transition frequency, not the zero-field splitting. Vacancy aggregation extends NV- spin coherence rather than shortening it.

</details>


### [240] [Amorphous insulating MoS$_x$ films deposited by magnetron sputtering as a low-index optical coating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24326)
*S. G. Martanov,E. V. Tarkaeva,A. V. Muratov,A. V. Lubenchenko,O. N. Pavlov,A. G. Vitukhnovsky,A. Yu. Kuntsevich*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过室温直流磁控溅射（DC magnetron sputtering）加压 MoS$_2$ 粉末靶材，可制备非晶 MoS$_x$ 薄膜，其光学性质与晶态 MoS$_2$ 根本不同，并具备集成光子学应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究多集中于晶态 MoS$_2$ 作为二维材料的探索，本文则关注非晶 MoS$_x$ 薄膜这一替代形式。

Method: 采用室温直流磁控溅射（DC magnetron sputtering），以加压的 MoS$_2$ 粉末为靶材制备非晶 MoS$_x$ 薄膜。

Result: 薄膜硫含量约为 $x=1.8$，呈硫缺陷；具光学各向同性，光学损耗较低，在可见与近红外光谱范围内折射率约为 2；且与剥离光刻（lift-off lithography）和干法刻蚀（dry etching）兼容。

Conclusion: 非晶 MoS$_x$ 因低光学损耗、适宜折射率及工艺兼容性，是集成光子学（integrated photonics）应用的有前景材料。

Abstract: While much effort are now concentrated on exploration of crystalline form of MoS$_2$ as a two-dimensional material, we show that room-temperature DC magnetron sputtering from pressurized MoS$_2$ powder target produces amorphous MoS$_x$ films with optical properties fundamentally different from those of crystalline MoS$_2$. The films were found to have a sulfur content of approximately $x=1.8$, indicating sulfur deficiency. They are optically isotropic and exhibit rather low optical losses, with a refractive index of about 2 over the visible and near-infrared spectral range. Their compatibility with lift-off lithography and dry etching makes amorphous MoS$_x$ a promising material for integrated photonic applications.

</details>


### [241] [Axial thermal expansion from free-energy minimization with temperature-dependent force constants in phonopy: a technical report, with $α$- and $ω$-Ti as the worked example](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24336)
*Atsushi Togo*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该报告介绍了在 phonopy 中通过最小化亥姆霍兹自由能（Helmholtz free energy）求六方晶体 $a$、$c$ 轴热膨胀系数（thermal expansion coefficients）的计算流程，并以钛的 $\alpha$、$\omega$ 相为算例，评估各设置对结果的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 六方晶体中 $a$、$c$ 两轴长度随温度变化速率不同，需用两个系数分别描述；而轴向系数因两轴在自由能中耦合，比体积系数更难计算，故需明确可复现的计算流程并评估其数值敏感性。

Method: 在给定的有限组 $a$、$c$ 取值上用谐振力常数（harmonic force constants）计算自由能，拟合 $a$–$c$ 自由能面；再由该面在各温度下最小化自由能得到平衡晶格参数，系数取其对温度的对数导数。变体中用自洽谐振近似（self-consistent harmonic approximation）的温度依赖力常数替代谐振力常数，其力由在各取值处拟合的机器学习势（machine-learning potential）计算。

Result: 两种计算均给出 $\alpha$-Ti 在低温下 $c$ 轴的负热膨胀（negative thermal expansion），而 $\omega$-Ti 无此现象；二者差异在 $\alpha$-Ti 的 $c$ 轴上最大。两种钛相中使 $a$ 伸长的热效应同时使 $c$ 缩短，故 $c$ 轴系数为两个反号项之和、部分抵消，任一项的微小误差都会导致系数的较大相对误差。

Conclusion: 轴向热膨胀系数比体积系数更难计算，其中以 $\alpha$-Ti 的 $c$ 轴最为敏感；报告量化了该流程中各设置对热膨胀系数的改变程度。

Abstract: In a hexagonal crystal the $a$ and $c$ axes change their lengths at different rates with increasing temperature, and the thermal expansion is described by two coefficients, one for each axis. The equilibrium lattice parameters at each temperature are found by minimizing the Helmholtz free energy over the lattice parameters, and the coefficients are their logarithmic derivatives with respect to temperature. This report describes a procedure for this minimization as implemented in phonopy. The free energy is computed with harmonic force constants at a finite set of values of $a$ and $c$, and a free-energy surface over $a$ and $c$ is fitted to these values. In a variant, the harmonic force constants are replaced by temperature-dependent force constants from a self-consistent harmonic approximation, with the forces computed by a machine-learning potential fitted at each of these values. The $α$ and $ω$ phases of titanium are the worked example. Both calculations give negative thermal expansion of the $c$ axis of $α$-Ti at low temperature and none in $ω$-Ti, and they differ most for the $c$ axis of $α$-Ti. The axial coefficients are harder to compute than the volumetric one, because the two axes are coupled in the free energy. In both phases of titanium the thermal effect that lengthens $a$ also shortens $c$. The coefficient of the $c$ axis is then the sum of two terms of opposite sign that partly cancel, and a small error in either term gives a large relative error in the coefficient. The report measures how much each setting of the procedure changes the thermal expansion coefficients, with the $c$ axis of $α$-Ti as the most sensitive case.

</details>


### [242] [Revealing tilt-driven structural heterogeneity in hybrid improper ferroelectrics by spatially-resolved crystallography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24383)
*Evie Ladbrook,Jon P. Wright,Mark S. Senn*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 利用扫描三维X射线衍射（scanning 3D X-ray diffraction）并结合对称性适配分析，在杂化非本征铁电体Ca$_{2.15}$Sr$_{0.85}$Ti$_{2}$O$_{7}$中，发现铁弹畴壁（ferroelastic domain walls）具有本征展宽特征，其倾转序参量经四方中间态连续旋转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁性氧化物（ferroic oxides）的畴壁可承载与体相本质不同的涌现功能（emergent functionalities），其微观结构对理解与调控宏观行为至关重要，因此需要获取体材料中单个畴壁的微观结构信息。

Method: 采用扫描三维X射线衍射（scanning three-dimensional X-ray diffraction）对Ca$_{2.15}$Sr$_{0.85}$Ti$_{2}$O$_{7}$中铁弹畴壁两侧耦合的八面体畸变模式（octahedral distortion modes）进行空间成像，并将层析重建（tomographic reconstruction）与对称性适配分析（symmetry-adapted analysis）相结合，分析八面体旋转（rotation）与倾转（tilt）及相关应变模式。

Result: 畴壁在本征上是展宽的，其宽度远超传统Ising型铁性界面（Ising-like ferroic interfaces）的预期值；壁结构表现为倾转序参量（tilt order parameter）经由一个四方中间态（tetragonal intermediate）发生连续旋转。

Conclusion: 该结果为铁弹壁形成、铁电翻转（ferroelectric switching）以及畴壁处涌现功能的结构机制提供了新的微观认识。

Abstract: Domain walls in ferroic oxides can host emergent functionalities that differ fundamentally from those of the surrounding bulk, making their microscopic structure central to understanding and controlling macroscopic behaviour. We use scanning three-dimensional X-ray diffraction to map the spatial evolution of coupled octahedral distortion modes across ferroelastic domain walls in the hybrid improper ferroelectric Ca$_{2.15}$Sr$_{0.85}$Ti$_{2}$O$_{7}$. By combining tomographic reconstruction with symmetry-adapted analysis of the octahedral rotation and tilt and associated strain modes, we map their spatial evolution across individual walls in the bulk crystal. We find that the walls are intrinsically extended, with widths far exceeding those expected for conventional Ising-like ferroic interfaces. The wall structure is characterised by a continuous rotation of the tilt order parameter through a tetragonal intermediate. These results provide new microscopic insight into the structural mechanisms governing ferroelastic wall formation, ferroelectric switching, and emergent functionality at domain walls.

</details>


### [243] [On the dual character of Zn impurity in SnTe: Tuning thermoelectric and topological properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24459)
*Kacper Pryga,Bartlomiej Wiendlocha*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 采用第一性原理方法研究 Zn 掺杂 SnTe 的电子结构与热电性能，揭示 Zn 同时调控热电输运与拓扑相变。研究表明 Zn 引入导带内类共振杂质态并诱导价带 L-Σ 能带收敛，从而提升 n 型与 p 型热电势并延缓双极效应，同时驱动能带由反转向平庸序转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SnTe 是 PbTe 的无铅类似物（lead-free analogue），是潜在热电材料，实验报道 Zn 掺杂可提升 p 型样品性能，但其微观机制尚不清晰。该研究旨在阐明 Zn 在 SnTe 中的作用并探索同时调控热电与拓扑性质的可能途径。

Method: 使用 Korringa-Kohn-Rostoker 方法结合相干势近似（coherent potential approximation, CPA）进行第一性原理计算，并辅以赝势（pseudopotential）计算，考察不同 Zn 浓度下 SnTe 的电子结构与热电性质。

Result: Zn 在导带内引入类共振杂质态（resonant-like impurity state），其随 Zn 浓度显著演化，显著增强 n 型 SnTe 的热电势（thermopower）。在价带中，Zn 掺杂诱导 L-Σ 能带收敛（band convergence），在较宽浓度范围内提高 p 型 Seebeck 系数并延缓双极效应（bipolar effect）的出现。此外，Zn 引起的能带结构改变驱动能带序由反转（inverted）向平庸（trivial）转变，表明存在可控的拓扑相变（topological phase transition）。

Conclusion: 研究阐明了 Zn 在 SnTe 中的微观作用，并确认掺杂可作为同时调控热电与拓扑性质的机制。

Abstract: We present a first-principles study of the electronic structure and thermoelectric properties of Zn-doped SnTe using the Korringa-Kohn-Rostoker method within the coherent potential approximation, complemented by pseudopotential calculations. SnTe is a lead-free analogue of PbTe and a candidate thermoelectric material in which Zn doping has been experimentally reported to enhance the performance of $p$-type samples. We show that Zn introduces a resonant-like impurity state, located within the conduction band, which evolves strongly depending on the Zn concentration. This feature leads to a significant enhancement of the thermopower in $n$-type SnTe. In the valence band, Zn doping induces L-$Σ$ band convergence, also resulting in an increased $p$-type Seebeck coefficient over a broad concentration range and delaying the onset of the bipolar effect. We further demonstrate that the Zn-induced band-structure modifications drive a transition from an inverted to a trivial band ordering, indicating a controllable topological phase transition. Our results clarify the microscopic role of Zn in SnTe and identify doping as a mechanism for simultaneously tuning thermoelectric and topological properties.

</details>


### [244] [III-V antiphase boundaries are not generated by Si or Ge substrate step edges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24792)
*Charles Cornet,Sreejith Pallikkara Chandrasekharan,Audrey Gilbert,Milan Silvestre,Rozenn Bernard,Pascal Turban,Gilles Patriarche,Eric Tournie,Laurent Pedesseau,Jean-Baptiste Rodriguez*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文综述了III-V族半导体在IV族衬底上价态与结构失配异质外延（heteroepitaxy）的最新进展，提出反相边界（antiphase boundaries, APBs）形成的修正描述，指出APBs并非在单原子台阶边缘产生，而由衬底台面重构（terrace reconstruction）主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: III-V族半导体与IV族平台的集成需求推动了价态失配异质外延研究，但界面形成机制尤其是反相边界（APBs）的起源尚存争议，需对浸润行为、界面原子构型、衬底钝化及APBs稳定性等关键问题作系统梳理。

Method: 采用批判性综述（critical review）方法，综合近期实验与理论进展及先驱研究，围绕浸润行为、异质界面原子构型、衬底钝化和APBs稳定性等关键方面进行分析与归纳。

Result: 分析表明，异质界面的形成由衬底台面重构（substrate terrace reconstruction）决定，APBs不在单原子台阶边缘（monoatomic step edges）产生，据此提出了APBs形成的修正描述。

Conclusion: 该推广的III-V/IV生长框架为III-V族半导体在IV族平台上的集成提供了新见解，并对价态失配异质外延（valence-mismatched heteroepitaxy）具有更广泛的参考价值。

Abstract: We critically review recent experimental and theoretical advances on the valence- and structure-mismatched heteroepitaxy of III-V semiconductors on group-IV substrates. We examine key aspects including wetting behavior, atomic configurations at the hetero-interface, substrate passivation, and the stability of antiphase boundaries (APBs). By synthesizing these findings with pioneering studies, we propose a refined description of antiphase boundaries formation. Our analysis shows that hetero-interface formation is dictated by substrate terrace reconstruction, demonstrating that APBs are not generated at monoatomic step edges. This generalized III-V/IV growth framework provides new insights for the integration of III-V semiconductors on group-IV platforms and, more broadly, for valence-mismatched heteroepitaxy.

</details>


### [245] [Scandium diboride: a semi-metallic, lattice, thermally matched substrate for vertical AlGaN power electronics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24856)
*MVS Chandrashekhar,Daniel Joel Harrison,Ahamed Raihan,Astrid D. Kengne,R. Shipra,Han Xie,Tasnia Jabin,Monte Hendrix,Ethan Scott,Roshan S. Annam,Sharad Mahatara,Evan N. Crites,Allana G. Iwanicki,Luke J. Meiler,Maxime Siegler,Renae N. Gannon,Steven R Spurgeon,Ashutosh Giri,Rajeswari Kolagani,Joshua A. Burrow,Stephan Lany,Patrick Hopkins,Tyrel M. McQueen,Michael Spencer,Satya Khushwaha*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究报道了采用激光二极管浮区法（laser diode floating zone method）在富硼（B-rich）条件下生长的六方二硼化钪（ScB₂，空间群 P6/mmm）单晶，其晶格与热膨胀性能可匹配 AlGaN 生长，并具备半金属导电性与竞争性热导率，有望用于超宽带隙（ultra-wide bandgap, UWBG）功率电子器件的衬底。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超宽带隙（UWBG）功率电子的潜力受限于衬底寄生电阻，且 AlGaN 厚层生长需要晶格与热膨胀匹配的衬底。研究旨在寻找兼具导电、导热与生长匹配性的衬底材料。

Method: 采用激光二极管浮区法，在富硼条件下以约 1mm/hr 的生长速率制备六方 ScB₂ 单晶，并通过摇摆曲线（rocking curve）、晶格膨胀测量、热容与电阻率测量进行表征，同时与第一性原理（first principles）计算对比。

Result: 晶体品质接近商用 SiC/GaN 衬底（(002) 摇摆曲线宽度 Δω=38''）。晶格可匹配 Al₀.₅₅Ga₀.₄₅N，AlGaN 生长温度下热膨胀系数约 5ppm/K。室温电阻率约 15μΩ·cm，773K 升至约 93μΩ·cm 且呈 T² 依赖。德拜温度（Debye temperature）约 850K，与理论匹配但低于富钪条件的 1100K。室温热导率 53W/mK，为第一性原理计算值的一半，电阻率为理论值约 2 倍；表面粗糙度约 2.5nm rms。

Conclusion: 德拜匹配（Debye matching）被视为衬底协同设计（codesign）的关键准则，可为 AlGaN 生长提供声子模式重叠以利于散热与热匹配。热导率与电阻率相对理论值的偏差归因于富硼生长产生的钪空位（Sc-vacancies），表明电子与声子对热输运起同等作用。光滑表面支持工程化声子桥（phonon bridges）与声子极化激元（phonon polaritons）等先进散热方式，可能使功率处理能力较现有 GaN/SiC 提升约 10-100 倍。

Abstract: We report the properties of hexagonal (space group P6/mmm) scandium diboride ($\mathrm{ScB}_2$) single crystals grown by a laser diode floating zone method at growth rates of ~1mm/hr under B-rich conditions with (002) rocking curve widths $Δω$=38'' approaching the quality of commercial SiC/GaN substrates. Lattice expansion measurements reveal matching to $\mathrm{Al_{0.55}Ga_{0.45}N}$ with a coefficient of thermal expansion ~5ppm/K at typical AlGaN growth temperatures, enabling thick AlGaN layers for ultra-wide bandgap (UWBG) power electronics >1kV. We measure semi-metallic room temperature resistivity ~15$μΩ$ cm, climbing to ~93$μΩ$ cm at 773K with a $T^2$ dependence effectively eliminating substrate parasitic resistance, the limiting factor in exploiting the full potential of UWBG. The Debye temperature $θ_{D,ScB_2}$ from heat capacity and lattice expansion is ~850K well matched to $θ_{D,ScB_2}$, but lower than the 1100K measured for Sc-rich growth conditions. We discuss Debye matching as a key substrate codesign criterion providing significant overlap in phonon modes for heat removal and thermal matching during AlGaN growth. The competitive thermal conductivity at room temperature 53W/mK is half that from full first principles calculations, a discrepancy we attribute to the presence of Sc-vacancies generated by B-rich growth. while the resistivity is ~2x the theoretical value, indicating that both electrons and phonons play equal role in thermal transport. The smooth ~2.5nm rms roughness surface enables advanced heat removal modalities through engineered phonon bridges and phonon polaritons in $\mathrm{ScB}_2$/AlGaN interfacial heterostructures, potentially allowing ~10-100x increase in power handling over state-of-the-art GaN/SiC.

</details>


### [246] [Face-to-face anneal temperature controls lattice parameter in Ta(C,N) virtual substrates for AlGaN power electronics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24893)
*Noah Zahn,Julia L. Martin,Michelle A. Smeaton,Renae Gannon,Henry Garland,M. Brooks Tellekamp*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过在N₂气氛中对TaC薄膜进行面对面退火（face-to-face annealing），实现了岩盐结构TaCₓN₁₋ₓ可调虚拟衬底，可支持x=0.5–1的AlₓGa₁₋ₓN外延，从而拓展垂直功率电子器件应用范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TaC薄膜虚拟衬底因晶格匹配、热膨胀匹配与金属导电性，是Al₀.₅Ga₀.₅N垂直功率器件的理想材料，但此前无法支持可变组分x的AlₓGa₁₋ₓN层，限制了器件应用范围。

Method: 采用面对面退火（face-to-face annealing）方法，在N₂气氛中对TaC薄膜进行退火处理，以实现岩盐结构TaCₓN₁₋ₓ虚拟衬底的可控制备。

Result: 退火温度低于1600 °C时，氮部分占据碳位与阴离子空位，形成具有中间阴离子组分的岩盐Ta(C,N)；温度≥1600 °C时，氮主要占据阴离子亚晶格，同时晶体质量与表面形貌退化并伴生第二相。

Conclusion: 该研究给出了制备可调晶格常数虚拟衬底所需的生长与工艺参数，使其适用于x=0.5–1的AlₓGa₁₋ₓN，可实现高Al含量下缺陷密度降低的垂直导电功率电子器件。

Abstract: Tantalum carbide (TaC) thin film ''virtual'' substrates are a highly desirable material for $\text{Al}_{0.5}\text{Ga}_{0.5}\text{N}$ vertical power electronics devices due to lattice matching, thermal expansion matching, and metallic conductivity. However, the material has not been demonstrated to support $\text{Al}_{x}\text{Ga}_{1-x}\text{N}$ layers of variable composition $x$, limiting the range of device applications. We present a method to achieve tunable rock salt $\text{Ta}\text{C}_{x}\text{N}_{1-x}$ virtual substrates via a face-to-face annealing of TaC thin films in an $\text{N}_2$ atmosphere. The results suggest that annealing temperatures below $1600\,^{\circ}\text{C}$ promote partial uptake of nitrogen onto carbon and anion vacancy sites to form rock salt $\text{Ta(C,N)}$ with intermediate anion compositions. At temperatures $\ge 1600\,^{\circ}\text{C}$, nitrogen primarily occupies the anion sublattice and the crystalline quality and surface morphology simultaneously degrade coincident with the formation of secondary phases. This study demonstrates the growth and processing parameters necessary to make tunable lattice constant virtual substrates for $\text{Al}_{x}\text{Ga}_{1-x}\text{N}$ from $x = 0.5\text{-}1$, enabling vertically conducting power electronic devices with reduced defect density at high Al-content.

</details>

### [247] [MadVfold: accelerating NLO event generation and reducing negative weights with SIMD vectorization and GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23500)
*Andrea Valassi*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出"向量化折叠"（vectorized folding）新思路，利用 SIMD 与 GPU 加速 NLO 模拟中昂贵的折叠（folding）计算，并给出基于 CUDACPP 的 MG5aMC 实现 MadVfold。初步结果显示，带折叠时整体加速约 6 倍至 9 倍，不带折叠时约 3 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NLO 模拟对 LHC 物理分析不可或缺，但计算昂贵：不仅速度慢，还会产生负权重（negative weights），导致必须模拟远大于所需的事件样本。折叠（folding）虽能有效减少负权重，但本身同样代价高昂，故需要进一步加速。

Method: 提出"向量化折叠"（vectorized folding）以加速此类计算，采用 SIMD 与 GPU 技术，并给出其面向 MG5aMC 的 CUDACPP 实现 MadVfold，同时将其扩展至未折叠的 NLO 事件生成（unfolded NLO event generation）。该工作基于以测试为中心、LLM 辅助的软件开发流程。

Result: 初步结果显示整体加速约为：带折叠时 6 倍至 9 倍，不带折叠时 3 倍。

Conclusion: 向量化折叠结合 SIMD 与 GPU 可显著加速 NLO 模拟中的折叠及相关计算，MadVfold 实现覆盖折叠与未折叠 NLO 事件生成两种场景；该实现依托以测试为中心、LLM 辅助的开发流程完成。

Abstract: NLO simulations are essential for LHC physics analyses but are expensive, as they are not only slow but also lead to negative weights, which imply the need to simulate much larger samples of events. Folding is a powerful technique to reduce negative weights but is itself expensive. In this paper I propose ``vectorized folding'' as a new idea to speed up these calculations using SIMD and GPUs, and I present its CUDACPP-based implementation for MG5aMC in MadVfold, including its extension for unfolded NLO event generation. Preliminary results show overall speedups around 6x to 9x with folding and 3x without it. This work is based on a test-centric, LLM-assisted software development process.

</details>


### [248] [Exact ballistic energy transport and emergent XXZ dynamics in an integrable three-state chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22817)
*Hanbing Liang,Fujun Liu*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究可积厄米三态链中弹道能量输运与涌现自旋动力学对耦合强度的依赖，借助正则 R-矩阵与微扰分析，严格给出热 Drude 权重系数及强耦合极限下等效 XXZ 模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者关注可积系统中弹道能量输运（ballistic energy transport）与涌现自旋动力学（emergent spin dynamics）如何随耦合强度演化，尤其考察连接时钟相互作用（clock interaction）与高度简并 flag 极限的模型中的普适行为。

Method: 通过构造正则 R-矩阵（regular R-matrix）建立全局守恒能量流，并解析计算其完全方差；同时在强耦合极限下分析退局域化的第三色旁观态（spectator state）导致的虚跃迁效应。

Result: 解析得到严格为正的高温主导热 Drude 权重系数（thermal Drude weight）与能量关联二阶矩的弹道增长率；强耦合极限下简并被解除，生成各向异性 Δ = -1/2 的有效自旋-1/2 XXZ 哈密顿量，并选定全活性双色扇区为真实基态。

Conclusion: 周期边界下旁观态虚运动引入正的长度变化 XXZ 超荷平方（supercharge squared）；对 L ≥ 4 该算符在有限且与长度无关的能量区间内严格湮灭所有态，由此证明完整周期有效理论精确复现低能 XXZ 谱（含全部态多重数）及宏观体自由能密度。

Abstract: We investigate the coupling dependence of ballistic energy transport and the emergent spin dynamics in an integrable Hermitian three-state chain that connects a clock interaction to a highly degenerate flag limit. By constructing a regular $R$-matrix to establish a globally conserved energy current, we analytically evaluate its full variance to obtain the exact, strictly positive leading high-temperature coefficient of the thermal Drude weight and the ballistic growth rate of the energy-correlation second moment. In the strong-coupling limit, the degeneracy is lifted by virtual transitions of a delocalized third-color spectator state, which generates an effective spin-$1/2$ XXZ Hamiltonian with anisotropy $Δ= -1/2$ and fundamentally selects the all-active two-color sector as the true ground state. For periodic boundaries, this virtual spectator motion introduces a positive length-changing XXZ supercharge squared that, for $L\ge4$, strictly annihilates all states within a finite, length-independent energy interval above the ground state. Consequently, we rigorously prove that the full periodic effective theory perfectly replicates the exact low-energy XXZ spectrum, including all state multiplicities, as well as its macroscopic bulk free-energy density.

</details>
