### [249] [Real-Time Doppler-Frequency Measurement and Frequency-Pulling Suppression in Arm-locking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24686)
*Zheng-Rong Xiang,Jun Ke,Tian-Chi Zhu,Hao-Xin Xu,Cheng-Gang Shao,Jie Luo*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于线性二次高斯（linear-quadratic-gaussian, LQG）控制器的实时多普勒频率测量与频率牵引抑制方法，通过时变控制策略在臂锁定（arm-locking）中兼顾频率牵引快速抑制与激光频率噪声抑制，从而缩短锁定捕获时间并降低系统失稳风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 臂锁定是空间引力波探测中抑制激光频率噪声的重要技术，但更强的噪声抑制会加大频率牵引（frequency pulling）幅度，导致锁定捕获时间延长与系统不稳定风险上升。因此需要在噪声抑制与牵引抑制之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出基于线性二次高斯（linear-quadratic-gaussian, LQG）控制器的实时多普勒频率测量与频率牵引抑制方案，并采用时变控制策略：初始阶段以快速限制频率牵引为首要目标，随后逐步放松牵引抑制水平、同时渐进增强激光频率噪声抑制能力，使残余频率噪声在过渡结束时满足时延干涉测量（time-delay interferometry, TDI）要求。针对多种臂锁定配置开展综合性能仿真。

Result: 仿真表明，所提抑制器可在约1000 s内降低频率牵引并将其幅度限制在100 MHz以下，从而无需传统多普勒频率预估计阶段。采用法布里-珀罗（Fabry-Perot）激光器时，闭环激光频率噪声的幅度谱密度（amplitude spectral density, ASD）在闭环运行2000 s与9200 s后分别满足第二代与第一代TDI要求。

Conclusion: 该方法可同时实现频率牵引的快速抑制与高水平的激光频率噪声抑制，为臂锁定的快速捕获与锁定提供了一条可行途径。

Abstract: Arm-locking is an important technique for suppressing laser frequency noise in space-based gravitational-wave detection. However, stronger noise suppression inevitably increases the magnitude of frequency pulling, thereby prolonging the lock-acquisition time and increasing the risk of system instability. To address this issue, this paper proposes a real-time Doppler-frequency measurement and frequency-pulling suppression method based on a linear-quadratic-gaussian controller. To achieve a balance between noise suppression and pulling suppression, a time-varying control strategy is adopted. At the initial stage of the time-varying control process, the primary objective is to rapidly limit frequency pulling. Subsequently, the level of pulling suppression is gradually relaxed while the capability for laser frequency-noise suppression is progressively enhanced, so that the residual frequency noise satisfies the time-delay interferometry (TDI) requirement by the end of the transition. Comprehensive performance simulations are conducted for several arm-locking configurations. The results show that the proposed suppressor can reduce frequency pulling within approximately 1000 s while limiting its magnitude to below 100 MHz, thereby eliminating the need for the conventional Doppler frequency pre-estimation stage. When a Fabry-Perot laser is used, the amplitude spectral density (ASD) of the closed-loop laser frequency noise meets the requirements of second-generation and first-generation TDI after 2000 s and 9200 s of closed-loop operation, respectively. These results demonstrate that the proposed suppressor provides both rapid frequency-pulling suppression and a high level of laser frequency-noise suppression. This new method provide a feasible way for rapid acquisition and locking of arm-locking.

</details>


### [250] [Non-Hermitian photonic time quasicrystal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24327)
*Shuhang Chen,Zhi Zheng,Qi Zhu*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作研究了非厄米 Aubry-André-Harper 模型的时间类比体系，发现将非公度时间调制延拓到复相位平面后，体放大率随复化坐标被组织为若干整数相，相位内斜率锁定为整数，即 Avila 加速度，从而把准周期算子理论的全局结构联系到可直接观测的光学传播响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子时间晶体（photonic time crystal）通过时间调制放行波，但其放大行为与准周期算子理论之间的联系尚不明确。该研究旨在将非厄米（non-Hermitian）准周期模型的全局整数不变量引入时间晶体框架，以获得可观测的量子化响应。

Method: 研究对象为非厄米 Aubry-André-Harper 模型的时间类比。方法是将非公度（incommensurate）时间调制延拓到复相位平面（complex phase plane），考察体放大率随复化坐标（complexification coordinate）的变化，并分析其在时间依赖介电常数调制中存在缺陷时的行为。

Result: 体放大率随复化坐标被组织为不同的整数相，每一相内斜率锁定为整数，该整数即 Avila 加速度（Avila's acceleration）。此量子化响应在保持底层解析相位结构的时间依赖介电常数调制缺陷下依然存在。作为波动层面的结果，属于不同整数相的两个输入频率获得指数分离的放大，产生频谱选择（spectral selection）并重塑时间拍频图案（temporal beat pattern）。

Conclusion: 结果表明，准周期算子理论中的一个全局整数可以支配非厄米光子时间准晶体（non-Hermitian photonic time quasicrystal）中可调、量子化的增长响应，建立了准周期算子理论与可观测光学传播响应之间的直接联系。

Abstract: Photonic time crystals amplify waves through temporal modulation. We study a temporal analogue of the non-Hermitian Aubry-André-Harper model. By continuing an incommensurate temporal modulation into the complex phase plane, we find that the bulk amplification rate as a function of the complexification coordinate is organized into distinct integer phases, with its slope locked to an integer within each phase. This integer is Avila's acceleration, connecting a global structure of quasiperiodic operator theory with a directly observable optical propagation response. The quantized response persists in the presence of defects in the time-dependent permittivity modulation that preserve the underlying analytic phase structure. As a wave-level consequence, two input frequencies belonging to different integer phases acquire exponentially separated amplification, producing spectral selection and reshaping the temporal beat pattern. Our results establish that a global integer of quasiperiodic operator theory can govern a tunable, quantized growth response in a non-Hermitian photonic time quasicrystal.

</details>


### [251] [Broadband Single-Particle Absorption Circular Dichroism Reveals Chiroptical Heterogeneity in Gold Helicoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24772)
*Rohit B. Raj,Susanna Bertuletti,Jeong Hyun Han,Sepin Cho,Debapriya Pal,Nick Feldman,Ki Tae Nam,Willem L. Noorduin,A. Femius Koenderink,Erik C. Garnett*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该研究报告了一种波长可调的积分球显微镜（integrating-sphere microscope），通过直接光学能量平衡（optical energy balance）在单颗粒水平测量宽带吸收圆二色性（absorption circular dichroism, CD），并据此揭示手性金螺旋体（gold helicoids）在吸收不对称因子（absorption dissymmetry factor, gabs）上的显著单颗粒异质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单颗粒水平的吸收圆二色性定量测量对理解单个手性纳米结构（chiral nanostructures）如何耗散光能至关重要，但宽带吸收率（absorptance）测量在实验上仍具挑战性。现有方法难以直接探测吸收手性光学响应，且系综平均（ensemble averaging）会掩盖颗粒特异性行为。

Method: 引入波长可调积分球显微镜，在右旋与左旋圆偏振（right- and left-circularly polarized）照明下，通过直接光学能量平衡测定单个纳米颗粒的吸收率，实现 gabs 的宽带测量。以非手性金纳米球（gold nanospheres）和强各向异性金纳米棒（gold nanorods）建立表观吸收 CD 基线与线性二色性到圆二色性（LD-to-CD）泄漏下限，并测量 87 个 L-谷胱甘肽合成（L-helicoids）与 96 个 D-谷胱甘肽合成（D-helicoids）的手性金螺旋体，结合相关性 SEM（correlative SEM）分析。

Result: L-与 D-螺旋体群体的平均颗粒级 gabs 符号相反且差异具有统计显著性。但单颗粒在响应符号、幅度、光谱位置与线形上呈现显著异质性，每个群体中超过三分之一的颗粒表现出与群体平均趋势相反的吸收 CD 响应。对 L-螺旋体的相关性 SEM 分析显示，具有典型投影螺旋体形貌的孤立颗粒仍存在反号响应，表明聚集与投影形貌的总体差异不足以解释该异质性。

Conclusion: 宽带单颗粒吸收 CD 光谱（broadband single-particle absorption CD spectroscopy）可作为吸收手性光学异质性（absorptive chiroptical heterogeneity）的直接探针，并揭示被系综平均所掩盖的颗粒特异性响应。

Abstract: Quantitative measurements of absorption circular dichroism (CD) at the single-particle level are essential for understanding how individual chiral nanostructures dissipate optical energy, yet broadband absorptance measurements remain experimentally challenging. Here, we introduce a wavelength-tunable integrating-sphere microscope that determines the absorptance of individual nanoparticles under right- and left-circularly polarized illumination through direct optical energy balance, enabling broadband measurement of the absorption dissymmetry factor, gabs. Measurements of achiral gold nanospheres and strongly anisotropic gold nanorods establish the apparent absorption CD baseline and demonstrate minimal detectable linear-dichroism-to-circular-dichroism (LD-to-CD) leakage under the experimental conditions. Applying this approach to 87 chiral gold helicoids synthesized using L-glutathione (L-helicoids) and 96 synthesized using D-glutathione (D-helicoids) reveals mean particle-level gabs values of opposite sign, with a statistically significant difference between the two populations. Individual particles nevertheless exhibit pronounced heterogeneity in response sign, magnitude, spectral position, and line shape. More than one-third of the particles in each population display opposite-sign absorption CD responses relative to their population-average tendency. Correlative SEM analysis of the L-helicoid population further shows that opposite-sign responses persist among isolated particles exhibiting the characteristic projected helicoid morphology, indicating that aggregation and gross differences in projected morphology are insufficient to explain the observed heterogeneity. These results establish broadband single-particle absorption CD spectroscopy as a direct probe of absorptive chiroptical heterogeneity and reveal particle-specific responses obscured by ensemble averaging.

</details>


<div id='physics.hist-ph'></div>

# physics.hist-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [252] [Pyroelectricity: A Brief History of its Discovery and Physical Principles - an Overview](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23736)
*Ruediger G. Ballas,Nataliya Koev*

Main category: physics.hist-ph

TL;DR: 本文以学生与工程师为读者，简要介绍热释电性（pyroelectricity）的两千余年历史沿革及其物理机制，涵盖从古代电气石（tourmaline）观察到现代红外探测器（infrared detector）与铁电材料（ferroelectric materials）应用的发展脉络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该文旨在为读者提供热释电性这一古老现象的简明入门介绍，兼顾历史发展与物理机制两个层面，以弥合早期类比描述与现代晶体学、电学解释之间的认知鸿沟。

Method: 采用历史梳理与物理阐释相结合的方式：一方面追溯从 Theophrastus 对加热电气石的描述，到 Aepinus、Canton 的电极化发现，再到 Haüy 与 Thomson 的现代晶体学描述；另一方面在物理部分给出热释电性的定义，并在微观层面用元胞永久偶极矩（permanent dipole moment）的矢量和加以表述，同时以数学方式推导宏观自发极化（spontaneous polarization）与表面电荷密度及热释电系数（pyroelectric coefficient）的关系。

Result: 研究区分了初级热释电效应（primary pyroelectric effect）与由晶体热形变引起的次级效应（secondary effect），并指出20世纪现代红外探测器与铁电材料的开发使该研究领域重新焕发生机（renaissance）。

Conclusion: 热释电性被界定为各向异性固体（anisotropic solids）中自发极化的温度依赖性，其历史从早期与磁性的类比演进至现代晶体学与物理描述，并在20世纪因红外探测与铁电材料的发展而复兴。

Abstract: This paper deals with the historical development and physical mechanisms of pyroelectricity, a phe-nomenon whose roots date back over 2000 years to ancient times. This paper is aimed at students and engineers as a concise introduction to the subject area. While the attractive effect of heated tourma-line was already described by Theophrastus, scientific systematization did not occur until the 18th century by researchers, such as Aepinus and Canton, who identified the effect of electrical polarization resulting from temperature changes. This essay highlights the path from early analogies to magnetism to the modern crystallographic description by Haüy and Thomson. In the physics section, pyroelectricity is defined as the temperature dependence of the spontaneous polarization in anisotropic solids. At the microscopic level, the permanent dipole moment of an elementary cell is described by the vector sum of individual moments. It is mathematically demonstrated that the macroscopic spontaneous polarization correlates with the surface charge density and is linked to the temperature change via the pyroelectric cofficient. A distinction was made between the primary pyroelectric effect and the secondary effect resulting from the thermal deformation of the crystal. Finally, the renaissance of this field of research through the development of modern infrared detectors and ferroelectric materials in the 20th century was highlighted.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [253] [Cohomology of $\mathfrak{psl}(n|n)$ in trivial and in adjoint representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22598)
*Thiago Oliveira Ferreira*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文通过Hochschild–Serre谱序列，在特征零域上完整计算了投射特殊线性李超代数（projective special linear Lie superalgebra）$\mathfrak{psl}(n|n)$ 在平凡系数与伴随系数下的上同调，并附带给出 $\mathfrak{sl}(m|n)$ 的平凡系数上同调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $\mathfrak{psl}(n|n)$ 的实形式即投射特殊酉李超代数（projective special unitary Lie superalgebra）$\mathfrak{psu}(n|n)$ 具有物理应用价值，尤其是 AdS/CFT 对应中的 $\mathfrak{psu}(2,2|4)$，因此需要明确其上同调结构。

Method: 采用与 $\mathfrak{psl}(n|n)$ 相关联的 Hochschild–Serre 谱序列（Hochschild–Serre spectral sequences），在特征零（characteristic zero）域上分别对平凡系数（trivial coefficients）与伴随系数（adjoint coefficients）进行完整计算。

Result: 得到 $\mathfrak{psl}(n|n)$ 在平凡系数与伴随系数下的上同调，并在过程中求得 $\mathfrak{sl}(m|n)$ 的平凡系数上同调，同时指出与既有文献中某陈述的不一致；还显式给出 $n\leqslant4$ 时低次上同调群。

Conclusion: 该工作系统刻画了 $\mathfrak{psl}(n|n)$ 的上同调，修正了文献中的一处分歧，并提供了低次上同调群的显式结果，可为相关物理应用提供代数结构依据。

Abstract: We compute the cohomology of the projective special linear Lie superalgebra $\mathfrak{psl}(n|n)$ over a field of characteristic zero in trivial and in adjoint coefficients in full detail through its associated Hochschild--Serre spectral sequences. Applications in physics exist for its real form, the projective special unitary Lie superalgebra $\mathfrak{psu}(n|n)$ --- in particular, $\mathfrak{psu}(2,2|4)$ for AdS/CFT. We also find the cohomology of $\mathfrak{sl}(m|n)$ in trivial coefficients along the way and we point out a discrepancy with a previous statement in the literature. We present the explicit cohomology groups of low degree for $n\leqslant4$.

</details>


### [254] [Distributional Fractional Taylor Series and Interpretation of the Fractal and Number-Theoretic Explicit Formulas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22759)
*Michel L. Lapidus,Matthew Overduin*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在广义分形弦（generalized fractal strings）框架下，建立了带误差项的分布型分形泰勒公式（distributional fractal Taylor's formula）及精确的分布型分形泰勒级数表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受 Lapidus 与 van Frankenhuisjen 关于广义分形弦分形显式公式（fractal explicit formulas）工作的启发，本文旨在将分形显式公式拓展为分形泰勒型展开。

Method: 在几何 zeta 函数 ζ_η 的 languidity（弱/强）增长假设下，将分数阶分布显式公式中的留数项表示为 Dirac δ 分布的分数阶 (-ω) 阶分布导数 Y_ω 作用于修正测试函数 φ(x)ln^{k-1}(x) 上。

Result: 系数由 ζ_η 在极点 ω 处 Laurent 展开主部的系数乘以在 ω 处取值的 gamma 函数给出；languidity 假设下存在误差项，强 languidity 假设下误差项消失，显式公式成为精确形式。

Conclusion: 所得结果推进了以分形泰勒级数展开（涉及底层分形复维度）刻画分形的更广泛研究纲领。

Abstract: Motivated by the work of M. L. Lapidus and M. van Frankenhuisjen on fractal explicit formulas for generalized fractal strings, we develop in this paper a distributional fractal Taylor's formula with error term and an exact distributional fractal Taylor series representation for generalized fractal strings. The explicit formulas that the aforementioned authors developed provide a way of expressing a generalized fractal string $η$ in terms of the underlying fractal complex dimensions, under suitable growth assumptions on its geometric zeta function, $ζ_η$, known as the languidity assumptions. This involves a sum indexed by the complex dimensions of the generalized fractal string, summing over all residues of $ζ_η$, multiplied by the Mellin transform of a tempered or Schwartz test function $φ$, denoted by $\widetildeφ$. Under languidity assumptions, there is an error term present. Under stronger assumptions, that is, in the case of strong languidity, no error term is present and the resulting fractal explicit formula is said to be exact. In this paper, we express the residue term in the fractional distributional explicit formulas as the fractional $(-ω)^{\mathrm{th}}$-ordered distributional derivative of the Dirac $δ$ distribution, denoted by $Y_ω$, applied to modified test functions of the form $φ(x) \ln^{k-1}(x)$, where $k$ runs from $1$ to the multiplicity of the pole $ω$ of $ζ_η$. The coefficients in this sum are written in terms of the coefficients in the principal part of the Laurent expansion at $ω$ of the geometric zeta function $ζ_η$, multiplied by the gamma function evaluated at $ω$. The results obtained in this paper contribute to the broader program of characterizing fractals in terms of fractal Taylor series expansions involving their underlying fractal complex dimensions.

</details>


### [255] [Holomorphic Conformal Cartan Geometry: Conformal Gravity, Twistors, and Ambitwistors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22861)
*Chen-Hsu Chien*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在全纯共形嘉当几何（holomorphic conformal Cartan geometry）的统一框架下综述了四维共形引力（four-dimensional conformal gravity）、扭量理论（twistor theory）与双扭量理论（ambitwistor theory），并以规范论视角建立了三者之间的直接联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形引力、扭量理论与双扭量理论通常各自独立发展，缺乏统一的几何表述。本文旨在以共形嘉当几何为共同基础，揭示三者在规范理论层面的内在关联。

Method: 从几何的旋量表示（spinorial representation）出发，系统构造取值于 \(\mathfrak{sl}(4,\mathbb{C})\) 的嘉当连接（Cartan connection）、其曲率2-形式（curvature 2-form）及相关的拖拉机丛（tractor bundles）；在实洛伦兹切片上固定外尔规范（Weyl gauge），并将局部扭量与对偶扭量丛构造为关联的自旋拖拉机丛；通过不变典则配对（invariant canonical pairing）联系局部扭量与对偶扭量。

Result: 二次曲率作用量在实洛伦兹切片上、固定外尔规范后重现四维外尔–巴赫共形引力（Weyl–Bach conformal gravity）；平行移动给出平坦空间中的经典关联关系（incidence relations）与弯曲背景下的手征可积性约束——\(\alpha\)-面对应反自对偶（anti-self-duality），\(\beta\)-面对应自对偶（self-duality）；双扭量空间可参数化复零测地线（complex null geodesics）并对称地容纳左右手外尔曲率。

Conclusion: 该框架建立了一座直接的规范论桥梁，将共形嘉当几何与扭量理论及双扭量理论相联结，为共形引力的统一几何描述提供了系统途径。

Abstract: Four-dimensional conformal gravity, twistor theory, and ambitwistor theory are reviewed within the unified framework of holomorphic conformal Cartan geometry. Starting from the spinorial representation of the geometry, the normal $\mathfrak{sl}(4,\mathbb{C})$-valued Cartan connection, its curvature $2$-form, and the associated tractor bundles are systematically formulated. Evaluated on the real Lorentzian slice, the quadratic curvature action is shown to reproduce four-dimensional Weyl--Bach conformal gravity upon fixing a Weyl gauge. Local twistor and dual twistor bundles are constructed as associated spin tractor bundles, illustrating how parallel transport yields the classical incidence relations in flat space and chiral integrability constraints in curved backgrounds: anti-self-duality for $α$-surfaces and self-duality for $β$-surfaces. Furthermore, by pairing local twistors and dual twistors via an invariant canonical pairing, ambitwistor space is shown to parameterize complex null geodesics and symmetrically accommodate both left- and right-handed Weyl curvatures. This framework establishes a direct gauge-theoretic bridge linking conformal Cartan geometry to twistor and ambitwistor theories.

</details>


### [256] [Higher-order and non-geometric symmetries and Noether's theorem in k-cosymplectic Hamiltonian field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23034)
*Manuel de León,Xuefeng Zhao*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在 \(k\)-余辛哈密顿场论框架下研究高阶对称性与非几何对称性，引入广义无穷小对称、高阶广义无穷小 Cartan 对称与良好非几何对称三类概念，并建立相应 Noether 型定理给出精确守恒量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有关于 \(k\)-余辛设定中标准 Cartan 对称性的工作存在局限，标准对称条件较为严格，且缺少对高阶与非几何对称性及其守恒律的系统几何处理。本文旨在放宽对称条件并统一推广至相关辛结构。

Method: 首先引入广义无穷小对称（generalized infinitesimal symmetry）以放宽标准对称条件，进而定义高阶广义无穷小 Cartan 对称（higher-order general infinitesimal Cartan symmetries）与良好非几何对称（good non-geometric symmetries）；针对每一类对称性建立 Noether 型定理，获取精确 \(k\)-余辛哈密顿系统的显式守恒量，并将结果平行推广至 \(k\)-辛流形（\(k\)-symplectic manifolds）上的哈密顿系统。

Result: 为所定义的两类新对称性分别建立了 Noether 型定理，并给出精确 \(k\)-余辛哈密顿系统的显式守恒量；结果扩展并推广了先前关于 \(k\)-余辛设定中标准 Cartan 对称性的工作，且所有结果均平行推广至 \(k\)-辛流形。

Conclusion: 该框架为经典场论中广义对称性及其关联守恒律提供了系统的几何处理方式，并通过具体示例说明了所提对称性的存在性与适用性，同时体现出方法在 \(k\)-余辛与 \(k\)-辛设定间的统一性。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate higher-order symmetries and non-geometric symmetries within the framework of $k$-cosymplectic Hamiltonian field theory. We introduce the concept of generalized infinitesimal symmetry, which relaxes the standard symmetry condition, and then define two new classes of symmetries: higher-order general infinitesimal Cartan symmetries and good non-geometric symmetries. For each of these symmetry classes, we establish a Noether-type theorem that provides explicit conserved quantities for exact $k$-cosymplectic Hamiltonian systems. These results extend and generalize previous work on standard Cartan symmetries in the $k$-cosymplectic setting. Furthermore, we extend all of these results in a parallel fashion to Hamiltonian systems on $k$-symplectic manifolds, demonstrating the unifying nature of our approach. Concrete examples are provided to illustrate the existence and applicability of the proposed symmetries. Our framework offers a systematic geometric treatment of generalized symmetries and their associated conservation laws in classical field theories.

</details>


### [257] [Long time stability of low--frequency packets of modes at finite total energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23119)
*Dario Bambusi,Antonio Ponno*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究初始总能量 E（与粒子数 N 无关）很小的 FPUT α-模型（Fermi–Pasta–Ulam–Tsingou α-model），证明当初始能量仅赋予波数 πk/N ≤ θ* 的傅里叶模式（Fourier modes）时，能量在长达 E^{-(1-ε/2)} 量级的时间内不会流向波数大于 θ* 的模式。证明基于 Toda 晶格（Toda lattice）与 FPUT 系统的接近性，并利用 Flaschka 积分（Flaschka integrals）构造合适的组合来控制该模式包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注 FPUT α-模型中的能量输运问题，即在总能量很小且与粒子数 N 无关的初始条件下，考察能量是否会在傅里叶模式间发生向高波数的流动。核心目标是给出能量不流向高波数模式的长时间尺度估计，说明 FPUT 系统在相关时间范围内表现出类似可积系统（integrable system）的稳定性特征。

Method: 证明依赖于 Toda 晶格与 FPUT 系统的接近性（closeness）。与既有方法不同，本文不使用 Toda 系统的作用-角变量（action-angle variables），而是利用后者的 Flaschka 积分。作者构造了 Flaschka 积分的一种合适重组，用其二次部分控制模式包（packet），并对其高阶部分给出良好估计。

Result: 若初始能量赋予波数 πk/N ≤ θ* 的傅里叶模式，则在直至 E^{-(1-ε/2)} 量级的长时间内，能量不会流向波数大于 θ* 的模式，其中 θ* 与 ε 均为小量。该结论在初始总能量 E 与粒子数 N 无关的前提下成立。

Conclusion: 本文给出了 FPUT α-模型中能量在傅里叶模式间不发生高频流动的长时间尺度结果，其证明路线为借助 Toda 晶格的可积结构（经由 Flaschka 积分而非作用-角变量）实现对该模式包的长期控制，从而确立所声明的能量局域化（energy localization）性质。

Abstract: We consider the FPUT $α$-model with $N$ particles and initial data with small total energy $E$, independent of $N$. In particular, we prove that if the energy is initially given to Fourier modes of wave number $πk/N\leq θ_*$, then it does not flow to modes of wave number larger than $θ_*$ up to long times of order $E^{-(1-ε/2)}$, $θ_*$ and $ε$ being small numbers. The proof relies on the closeness of the Toda lattice to the FPUT system. Unlike previous approaches, we do not make use of the action-angle variables of the Toda system, but we exploit the Flaschka integrals of the latter. Actually, we find a suitable recombination of the Flaschka integrals whose quadratic part is used to control the packet, the higher order part fulfilling very good estimates.

</details>


### [258] [Six-wave scattering in Hamiltonian Dysthe equations: Euler matching and integrability obstructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23175)
*Alex J. Sutherland,Solomon C. Yim*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该工作计算了空间与时间 Hamilton 型 Dysthe 方程中的有效六波相互作用，并与深水 Euler 哈密顿量的完整六次系数对比；结果表明，在一般二次色散共振集合上，六波散射系数由局部三次项与平均流系数之积主导，且 Zakharov–Schulman 条件将正则 $C^3$ 二次主符号限制在波作用、动量与线性能量的张成空间中，从而排除了物理 Dysthe 模型支持无穷多线性无关守恒律的逆散射层级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深水重力波的四波共振理论（Zakharov 方程）与 Dysthe 型包络方程是弱非线性水波的核心近似，但六波相互作用（six-wave interaction）在 Dysthe 截断中的精度及其与完整 Euler 哈密顿量的一致性尚不明确。该文动机在于通过显式计算与比较，判定 Dysthe 模型能否承载更高阶的共振结构，并厘清其可积性边界。

Method: 在二次色散共振补丁（quadratic-dispersion resonance patch）的紧子集 $K$ 上，对三次色散做小扰动并显式展开六次哈密顿量系数 $W_6$，得到均匀渐近公式 $W_6(\Phi_{\lambda,\varepsilon}(z))=\varepsilon\frac{4BD}{aL(z)}+O_K(\varepsilon^2)$；随后在 Craig–Guyenne–Sulem 时间系数（temporal coefficients）下推导对应的精确 Euler 共振表达式，并以 $\mu$ 为相对带宽（relative bandwidth）展开。进一步通过分类系数并应用 Zakharov–Schulman 条件分析正则 $C^3$ 二次主符号。

Result: 在 $BD\ne 0$ 时，相对开共振集合上出现非零散射；时间情形给出 $\frac{\mu}{4}W_6^{\rm E}(1+\mu\Psi_\mu(z))=-\frac{8}{L(z)}+O_K(\mu)$，其主项来自四次顶点（quartic vertices）的九次缩并（contractions），其余连通六次贡献一致有界。该文还分类了使生成六波系数恒为零的系数取值，在时间情形中同时给出每个非置换四波共振上的相消。相消集合包含已知的非线性 Schrödinger、混合 Chen–Lee–Liu、Calogero–Moser 导数非线性 Schrödinger 及 Hirota 代表元。

Conclusion: Zakharov–Schulman 条件将每个正则 $C^3$ 二次主符号限制在 $\operatorname{span}\{1,k,\omega(k)\}$ 内，即波作用、动量与线性能量符号的张成空间；因此物理 Dysthe 模型无法支持具有无穷多线性无关正则 $C^3$ 二次主符号的逆散射层级（inverse-scattering hierarchy）。

Abstract: We compute the effective six-wave interaction in spatial and temporal Hamiltonian Dysthe equations and compare it with the complete degree-six coefficient of the deep-water Euler Hamiltonian. On every compact subset $K$ of a generic quadratic-dispersion resonance patch, exact resonances persist under a small cubic correction to the dispersion, and \[ W_6\bigl(Φ_{λ,\varepsilon}(z)\bigr) =\varepsilon\frac{4BD}{aL(z)}+O_K(\varepsilon^2) \] uniformly in the resonance point $z$ and the deformation parameter $λ$. Here $L$ is the spectral diameter and $B,D$ are the local cubic and mean-flow coefficients. Thus $BD\ne0$ gives nonzero scattering on relatively open resonance sets. At the Craig--Guyenne--Sulem temporal coefficients, the corresponding exact Euler resonances satisfy \[ \fracμ{4}W_6^{\rm E}\bigl(1+μΨ_μ(z)\bigr) =-\frac{8}{L(z)}+O_K(μ), \] where $μ$ is the relative bandwidth. The leading term comes from nine contractions of quartic vertices; every other connected degree-six contribution is uniformly bounded. We classify the coefficient values for which the generic six-wave coefficient vanishes identically; in the temporal case, this also gives cancellation on every non-permutation four-wave resonance. Outside these sets, the Zakharov--Schulman condition restricts every regular $C^3$ quadratic leading symbol to $\operatorname{span}\{1,k,ω(k)\}$, the span of the symbols of wave action, momentum, and linear energy. Consequently the physical Dysthe models cannot support an inverse-scattering hierarchy with infinitely many linearly independent regular $C^3$ quadratic leading symbols. The cancellation sets contain known nonlinear Schrödinger, mixed Chen--Lee--Liu, Calogero--Moser derivative nonlinear Schrödinger, and Hirota representatives.

</details>


### [259] [Routing and memory in the three-dimensional mirror model on K-lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23244)
*Jian Gu,Paul Schaeffer,Qin Hao*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在三维曼哈顿镜面模型（Manhattan mirror model，又称 K-lattice）中开展数值实验，研究退局域化（delocalization）相关的可观测量。核心发现是：相对于一个保持所有透射与反射孔径集合的精确条件比较，物理透射存在统计显著的亏缺；同时邻近透射通道相对同基数均匀子集也受到抑制。结果提供关于反射路由（reflection routing）与自适应重访电荷（revisit charges）的估计，可能有助于严格证明，但未确立无限体积退局域相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于考察三维曼哈顿镜面模型（K-lattice）中与退局域化相关的数值可观测量，并试图通过受控实验分离透射边界通道（transmitting boundary channels）的位置与其间的路由（routing）。

Method: 主要实验将透射边界通道的位置与通道间的路由分离，构造一个保持所有透射与反射孔径集合（transmission and reflection aperture sets）的精确条件比较。第二个实验按返回年龄（return age）分解重复顶点（repeated vertices）的贡献；此外还开展了环形实验（annular experiments），并检验一项初看存在的历史依赖性（history dependence）能否独立复现。最后考察一个周期构型（periodic configuration）的辅助接触图（auxiliary contact graph）。

Result: 相对于精确条件比较，物理透射（physical transmission）出现统计可分辨的亏缺；邻近透射通道相对同基数（cardinality）的均匀子集亦被抑制。带符号旧记忆余项（signed old-memory remainder）的平方范数在测量范围内与反平方根衰减（inverse-square-root decay）一致。环形实验量化了递减的延续损失（continuation losses），而初看的历史依赖性未能独立复现。周期构型具有连通的辅助接触图，但仅有有限的路由环（routing cycles）。

Conclusion: 结果识别出关于反射路由（reflection routing）与自适应选择的重访电荷（revisit charges）的估计，可能有助于严格证明。但该研究并未确立无限体积退局域相（infinite-volume delocalized phase）。

Abstract: We study numerical observables relevant to delocalization in the three-dimensional Manhattan mirror model, also known as the K-lattice. Our principal experiment separates the locations of transmitting boundary channels from the routing between them. We derive an exact conditional comparison that preserves all transmission and reflection aperture sets, and find a statistically resolved deficit of physical transmission relative to this comparison. Nearby transmitting channels are themselves suppressed relative to a uniform subset with the same cardinality. A second experiment resolves the contribution of repeated vertices by their return age. The squared norm of the signed old-memory remainder is consistent with an inverse-square-root decay over the measured range. Annular experiments quantify decreasing continuation losses, while an initially apparent history dependence fails independent replication. Finally, a periodic configuration has a connected auxiliary contact graph but only finite routing cycles. These results identify estimates on reflection routing and adaptively selected revisit charges that may assist a rigorous proof. They do not establish an infinite-volume delocalized phase.

</details>


### [260] [Resurgence and Complex Geodesics for the Heisenberg Heat Kernel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23608)
*Xinxing Tang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究各向同性海森堡群（isotropic Heisenberg groups）上的复测地作用量（complex geodesic actions）与热核重涌现（heat-kernel resurgence），刻画了Borel奇点集合与复作用量差的一致性，并显式构造了方向Stokes算子与Stokes矩阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于将重涌现（resurgence）与Borel求和理论应用于海森堡群上的测地作用量与热核，以理解其大阶渐近行为、奇点结构与Stokes现象。摘要未给出更具体的背景陈述。

Method: 方法基于极小鞍点Borel芽（minimizing-saddle Borel germ）的解析延拓，构造非共振端点（nonresonant endpoints）下的行有限方向Stokes算子（row-finite directional Stokes operators），并显式计算正射线Stokes矩阵及其对数。针对垂直轴附近情形，由最近Borel奇点导出均匀两项大阶渐近（uniform two-term large-order asymptotics），并给出立方共振（cubic resonances）的均匀A_2合流公式。

Result: 对实端点 r>0, y≠0，解析延拓到达的非零有限奇点恰为有限复作用量差；非共振端点下原始alien支撑（primitive alien support）可严格小于完整Borel奇点支撑。垂直轴附近获得均匀两项大阶渐近；立方共振满足均匀A_2合流公式；并确定了原始Fourier围道所选取的重涌现扇区组合。正Borel求和在水平端点精确成立；非零各向同性复端点处不存在有限法向轨迹，但有符号垂直作用量格（signed vertical action lattice）构成完整极限谱。作用量与Stokes结构在各向同性H^n上与维数无关。

Conclusion: 结论指出海森堡群上热核重涌现的作用量与Stokes结构具有维数无关性，Borel奇点、alien支撑、Stokes扇区选择及极限谱均可被精确刻画，正Borel求和在特定端点下精确有效。

Abstract: We study complex geodesic actions and heat-kernel resurgence on isotropic Heisenberg groups. For real endpoints with $r>0,y\ne0$, the nonzero finite singularities reached by analytic continuation of the minimizing-saddle Borel germ are exactly the finite complex action differences. For nonresonant endpoints, we construct row-finite directional Stokes operators and compute the positive-ray Stokes matrix and its logarithm explicitly; the primitive alien support can be strictly smaller than the full Borel singular support.
  Near the vertical axis, we obtain uniform two-term large-order asymptotics from the nearest Borel singularity. Cubic resonances admit uniform $A_2$ confluence formulas. We also identify the combination of resurgent sectors selected by the original Fourier contour. Positive Borel summation is exact at horizontal endpoints. At nonzero isotropic complex endpoints, no finite normal trajectories exist, but the signed vertical action lattice is the complete limiting spectrum. The action and Stokes structures are independent of the dimension on isotropic $H^n$.

</details>


### [261] [Local conservation laws and symmetries of three-dimensional incompressible Euler flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23772)
*Alexey Shevyakov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文对三维均匀不可压缩 Euler 流（Euler flow）在速度与压力表述下、允许显式时间与位置依赖及任意有限阶导数的光滑局部守恒律（local conservation laws）进行完整分类；并分类其有限阶局部对称特征（symmetry characteristics）与伴随对称（adjoint symmetries）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望给出三维均匀不可压缩 Euler 方程局部守恒律与对称性的完整、无遗漏分类，涵盖速度、压力变量及显式时空依赖与任意有限阶导数，以厘清经典守恒量之外是否还存在其他局部守恒结构。

Method: 采用局部分析与对称性分类方法：先利用伴随对称的决定方程（determining equations）进行分类；再验证显式乘子恒等式（multiplier identities）；最后建立乘子限制与守恒流（current）类之间的对应。对最高阶导数响应（highest-derivative responses）作统一分析，将两类对称问题降至至多一阶。

Result: 在模去在解上为零的流（currents vanishing on solutions）与散度恒为零的流后，经典的质量（mass）、动量（momentum）、角动量（angular-momentum）、动能（kinetic-energy）与螺旋度（helicity）平衡，连同广义含时动量，构成完整分类。所有对称特征在解上等价于由点变换（point transformation）诱导者；每个伴随对称都有乘子代表，其与 Euler 方程之积恒为散度，这些代表穷尽了所有局部守恒律乘子在解上的限制。

Conclusion: 三维均匀不可压缩 Euler 流的局部守恒律与局部对称结构可被完整刻画：守恒律由经典平衡量与广义含时动量穷尽，对称特征均源于点变换，伴随对称可由乘子代表刻画，且证明归结为三阶段并可降至一阶分析。

Abstract: We classify all smooth local conservation laws of three-dimensional homogeneous incompressible Euler flow in velocity and pressure, allowing explicit time and position and derivatives of arbitrary finite order. Modulo currents vanishing on solutions and currents with identically zero divergence, the classical mass, momentum, angular-momentum, kinetic-energy and helicity balances, together with generalised time-dependent momentum, exhaust the classification. We also classify all smooth finite-order local symmetry characteristics and adjoint symmetries: differential expressions satisfying the linearised Euler equations and their formal adjoint on solutions, respectively. Every symmetry characteristic is equivalent on solutions to one induced by a point transformation. Every adjoint symmetry has a multiplier representative, whose product with the Euler equations is identically a divergence. These representatives exhaust the restrictions of all local conservation-law multipliers to solutions. The conservation-law proof has three stages: classification of adjoint symmetries using their determining equations, verification of explicit multiplier identities, and the correspondence between multiplier restrictions and current classes. A common analysis of highest-derivative responses reduces both symmetry problems to order at most one.

</details>


### [262] [Sum of Three Squares and Particle in a Cubic Box - Degeneracies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23794)
*S. Pratik Khastgir*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对立方盒中量子粒子的能级简并度（degeneracy）问题，即把正整数表示为三个自然数平方和的方式数，给出闭式精确解（closed-form, exact solution），并借助 Mathematica 对前五百万个数的简并度列表进行归纳与数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 立方盒中量子粒子的能级简并度等价于数论（number theory）中用三个自然数平方和表示正整数的表示数问题，属经典问题。作者希望为该问题提供一个可直接计算的闭式精确解。

Method: 从可由简并度列表推得答案的简单情形出发，逐步归纳构造公式；通过 Mathematica 生成前五百万个数的简并度列表并细致考察；同时以充足示例与暴力枚举法（brute-force method）数值计算的简并度进行对照。作者声明不提供证明，因未持有证明。

Result: 得到一个闭式、精确的简并度求解公式；文中给出的大量示例可与暴力枚举数值结果相互验证一致。

Conclusion: 立方盒中量子粒子能级简并度问题可由此闭式公式直接求解；该结果基于对大规模数值简并度列表的归纳与经验验证，而非严格数学证明，其正确性依赖示例与数值对照的支持。

Abstract: We address the problem of determining the degeneracy of a particular energy eigenvalue corresponding to a quantum particle trapped in a cubic box. In other words, we aim to find the number of ways a specific positive integer can be written as the sum of three natural number squares. This is a classic problem in number theory. We provide a closed-form, exact solution to the problem. We do not claim to provide proofs, as we have none; instead, we build up the formulae starting from simple cases where the answer could be deduced by studying the list of degeneracies. We present ample examples that could be verified against the degeneracy calculated numerically via the brute-force method. We arrived at the solution by meticulously studying the list of degeneracies for the first five million numbers, generated by Mathematica$^{\circledR}$.

</details>


### [263] [Quantum modularity in refined topological recursion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23864)
*Veronica Fantini,Kento Osuga*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究 Weber 型精化谱曲线（Weber-type refined spectral curve）上由精化拓扑递归（refined topological recursion）所得自由能的 Borel 可和性（Borel summability）与量子模性（quantum modularity）。验证了 Stokes 射线成对出现、Borel-Laplace 和等于 Barnes 双伽马函数（Barnes double gamma function）；并出人意料地证明每条射线上 Stokes 常数的生成函数为 Jacobi 量子模函数（Jacobi quantum modular function）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于考察精化拓扑递归所生成自由能在 Borel 求和下的预期性质，并探究其与量子模性之间的潜在联系。摘要指出，除验证既有预期外，还试图揭示该框架可能具有的更普遍结构特征。

Method: 在 Weber 型精化谱曲线上计算精化拓扑递归给出的自由能，并分析其 Borel 可和性。具体包括考察 Stokes 射线的配对结构、Borel-Laplace 求和与 Barnes 双伽马函数的关系，以及沿各射线 Stokes 常数生成函数的模性性质。

Result: 一方面，验证了预期性质：Stokes 射线成对出现，且自由能的 Borel-Laplace 和与 Barnes 双伽马函数一致。另一方面，出人意料地证明沿每条射线的 Stokes 常数生成函数是一个 Jacobi 量子模函数。

Conclusion: 证明过程提示量子模性可能是精化拓扑递归的一个普遍特征，其适用范围可超出 Weber 型精化谱曲线。

Abstract: In this paper we study the Borel summability and quantum modularity of the free energy computed by refined topological recursion on the Weber-type refined spectral curve. On the one hand, we verify expected properties, namely, Stokes rays come in a pair and the Borel-Laplace sum of the free energy coincides with the Barnes double gamma function. Somewhat unexpectedly, on the other hand, we prove that the generating function of the Stokes constants along each ray is a Jacobi quantum modular function. Our proof suggests that quantum modularity may be a general feature of refined topological recursion, applicable beyond the Weber-type refined spectral curve.

</details>


### [264] [Recursive construction of spectral basis and its application to matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24418)
*A. Acus,A. Dargys*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文给出广义谱基（generalized spectral basis）构造的递推公式（recursive formula），并以各向异性介质（anisotropic media）中电磁波传播的不可对角化矩阵（non-diagonalizable matrix）符号计算为例进行展示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对不可对角化矩阵（non-diagonalizable matrix）难以构造谱基的问题，需要一种具有普适性的构造方法。摘要未对此做更详细说明。

Method: 提出广义谱基构造的递推公式（recursive formula），并将其用于描述各向异性介质（anisotropic media）中电磁波传播的不可对角化矩阵的符号计算（symbolic computation）。

Result: 给出了电磁波传播矩阵的示例应用，说明该方法可用于该类不可对角化矩阵的谱基构造。摘要未报告具体数值或性能指标。

Conclusion: 该方法具有足够的一般性（general），例如可应用于克利福德代数（Clifford algebra）的多向量（multivectors）。

Abstract: We prove a recursive formula for construction of generalized spectral basis. Example of application is presented for symbolic computation with non-diagonalizable matrix that describes electromagnetic wave propagation in anisotropic media. The method is general enough and can be applied, for example, to multivectors of the Clifford algebra.

</details>


### [265] [The Hadamard parametrix on globally hyperbolic spacetimes with Robin boundary conditions: Fundamental solutions and Hadamard states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24465)
*Beatrice Costeri,Claudio Dappiaggi,Benito Alberto Juárez-Aubry*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 在有时间型边界（timelike boundary）的全局双曲时空（globally hyperbolic spacetime）上，研究了带 Robin 边界条件的 Klein-Gordon 方程：证明前向与后向基本解的存在唯一性并刻画其波前集（wavefront set），随后引入 Robin-Hadamard 两点关联函数（Robin-Hadamard two-point correlation function）并证明其与波前集条件的等价性（Radzikowski 定理的类比），最后借助静态背景基态与形变论证证明该类关联函数的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典量子场论中 Hadamard 两点函数的刻画（Radzikowski 定理）通常在无边界的全局双曲时空上建立，而带时间型边界与 Robin 边界条件（Robin boundary conditions）的情形缺乏相应理论框架，需明确基本解性质、奇异性传播及关联函数的允准条件。

Method: 首先证明前向与后向基本解的存在唯一性及其结构性质；在 ∂M 无穷小凸性（infinitesimal convexity）与破碎零双特征线（broken null bicharacteristics）边界反射局部有限性假设下，用 Melrose-Sjöstrand 奇异性传播定理（propagation of singularities theorem）刻画内部波前集。其次定义 Robin-Hadamard 两点函数为具有指定反对称部分与奇异结构的正分布，并给出局部 Hadamard 形式：除光滑余项外由标准定向 Hadamard 拟基本解（directed Hadamard parametrix）加上反射零测地线贡献组成，系数由输运方程（transport equations）与边界 Robin 匹配条件确定，适用于任意有限次反射。最后从合适静态背景的基态出发，用形变论证（deformation argument）证明存在性。

Result: 建立了前向与后向基本解的存在唯一性及内部波前集刻画；给出了含有限次反射贡献的局部 Robin-Hadamard 形式，并证明该局部表述与波前集条件等价，即得到带时间型边界情形下 Radzikowski 定理的对应版本；进一步证明在所考虑的时空类上存在 Robin-Hadamard 两点关联函数。

Conclusion: 在带时间型边界的全局双曲时空上，Robin 边界条件下的 Klein-Gordon 理论可建立与无边界情形平行的 Hadamard 框架：基本解、波前集刻画、局部拟基本解展开与 Radzikowski 型等价定理均成立，并可由静态背景通过形变论证推广至更一般的时空。

Abstract: On a $d$-dimensional, $d\geq 2$, globally hyperbolic spacetime with a timelike boundary $(\mathcal{M},g)$, we investigate the Klein-Gordon equation with Robin boundary conditions. First of all, we prove existence and uniqueness of the advanced and retarded fundamental solutions, discussing in addition their structural properties. Secondly, under the hypothesis of infinitesimal convexity of $\partial\mathcal{M}$ and of local finiteness of boundary reflections for broken null bicharacteristics, we characterize their wavefront set in the interior $\mathring{\mathcal{M}}$, using the propagation of singularities theorem of Melrose and Sjöstrand.
  In the second part of the paper, we introduce a notion of Robin-Hadamard two-point correlation function as a positive distribution on $\mathcal{M}\times\mathcal{M}$ with prescribed antisymmetric part and singular structure, the latter being characterized in terms of its wavefront set. We complement this definition with a local Hadamard form. Up to a smooth remainder, this consists of the standard directed Hadamard parametrix together with additional contributions associated with the reflected null rays. Their coefficients are determined by transport equations supplemented by Robin matching conditions at the boundary. The construction applies to an arbitrary, but finite number of reflections. We prove that this local formulation is equivalent to the wavefront set condition, thereby establishing a counterpart of Radzikowski's theorem in the presence of a timelike boundary. Finally, starting from the ground state on suitable static backgrounds and using a deformation argument, we prove existence of Robin-Hadamard two-point correlation functions on the class of spacetimes considered.

</details>


### [266] [Payoffs and perception mediate environmental feedback in an N-player trust game with Q-learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24493)
*Ruqiang Guo,Zhaoyi Hu,Fangfang Wang,Linjie Liu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该研究将固定角色的双群体层级信任博弈（hierarchical trust game）与内生环境耦合，使用异质表格型 Q 学习（tabular Q-learning）智能体，考察物质激励与环境反馈如何共同塑造信任动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习模型擅长刻画适应过程，生态演化模型擅长刻画行为-环境反馈，但内生环境如何通过物质激励与感知状态改变信任尚不清晰。该研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构建固定角色、双群体的层级信任博弈，智能体为异质表格型 Q 学习主体，并耦合中心化内生环境（centered endogenous environment）。主要反馈对比中，固定收益基线（fixed-payoff baseline）与反馈分支从同一学习状态初始化，以控制先前学习的影响；随后比较收益参数或观测环境层级变化时的响应。

Result: 随收益乘数（payoff multiplication factor）增大，固定收益博弈中的成功信任从低水平跨越到较高的有限时域（finite-horizon）水平，且由风险偏好型投资者（risk-seeking investors）主导。环境反馈在感知层级边界（perception-tier boundaries）附近促进信任，并在高回报运行点强化信任下降。在高回报运行点，收益反馈强化了学习驱动的信任下降；更强的驱动（drive）与更长时域（horizon）加深这一负效应。

Conclusion: 相较于固定收益信任博弈，环境反馈将既往集体结果带入未来收益与观测状态。内生环境通过物质激励与感知状态共同影响信任演化，并可能放大信任的衰减趋势。

Abstract: Trust develops through learning, while collective behavior can alter the environment in which later decisions are made. Reinforcement-learning models describe adaptation, and eco-evolutionary models describe behavior-environment feedback, but how an endogenous environment changes trust through material incentives and perceived states remains unclear. We couple a fixed-role, two-population hierarchical trust game populated by heterogeneous tabular $Q$-learning agents to a centered endogenous environment. For the main feedback comparisons, we initialize the fixed-payoff baseline and feedback branches from the same learned state to control for prior learning. We then compare responses when payoff parameters or the observed environmental tier vary. As the payoff multiplication factor increased, successful trust in the fixed-payoff game crossed from a low to a higher finite-horizon level, led by risk-seeking investors. Environmental feedback promoted trust near perception-tier boundaries and reinforced its decline at the high-return operating point. At the high-return operating point, payoff feedback reinforced the learning-driven decline in trust. Stronger drive and longer horizons deepened this negative effect. Compared with a fixed-payoff trust game, environmental feedback carries past collective outcomes into future payoffs and observed states.

</details>


### [267] [Proactive Incentive Regulation in Multi-Agent Systems with Environmental Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24506)
*Xinyang Cao,Shijia Hua,Linjie Liu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文构建了资源状态与激励强度耦合的多智能体环境反馈博弈框架，比较资源充裕与资源稀缺条件下惩罚机制的实施效果，指出在资源充裕阶段实施惩罚可将公地悲剧转化为支持完全合作的双稳态，而在资源稀缺阶段实施惩罚则引发内点稳定、异宿环与Hopf分岔等复杂动力学并阻碍稳定合作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境反馈系统中理性主体的自利行为常削弱合作与环境可持续性。惩罚性激励机制虽被广泛认为可缓解此类社会困境，但其在不同环境条件下的实施时机尚缺乏充分理解。

Method: 建立多智能体环境反馈博弈框架（multi-agent environmental feedback game framework），其中激励强度与资源状态相互耦合；并在资源充裕（resource-abundant）与资源稀缺（resource-scarce）两种条件下考察系统动力学随激励强度的变化，结合理论分析刻画其动力学机制。

Result: 理论分析表明，在资源充裕状态下施加惩罚可使系统由公地悲剧（tragedy of the commons）转变为支持完全合作的双稳态（bistable regime）；而在资源稀缺状态下施加惩罚则产生复杂动力学行为，包括内点稳定（interior stability）、异宿环（heteroclinic cycles）与Hopf分岔（Hopf bifurcations），从而阻碍稳定合作的出现。

Conclusion: 激励监管中的时机至关重要：在有利环境条件下进行前瞻性干预（proactive intervention），比在环境退化后延迟执行惩罚更有利于维持合作。

Abstract: In environmental feedback systems, self-interested behaviors of rational agents often undermine cooperation and environmental sustainability. Although punitive incentive mechanisms are widely recognized as effective in addressing such social dilemmas, the timing of their implementation under different environmental conditions remains insufficiently understood. In this paper, we develop a multi-agent environmental feedback game framework with coupled incentive intensities across resource states. We investigate how system dynamics vary with incentive intensity under both resource-abundant and resource-scarce conditions. Theoretical analysis shows that imposing penalties in resource-abundant states can transform the system from a tragedy of the commons into a bistable regime that supports full cooperation. In contrast, imposing penalties in resource-scarce states leads to complex dynamical behaviors, including interior stability, heteroclinic cycles, and Hopf bifurcations, which hinder the emergence of stable cooperation. These results highlight the importance of timing in incentive regulation. Proactive intervention under favorable environmental conditions is more effective in sustaining cooperation than delayed enforcement after environmental degradation.

</details>


### [268] [Batalin-Vilkovisky algebras from Ševera bicomplexes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24781)
*Eugenia Boffo,Domenico Fiorenza*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文引入 Ševera 双复形（Ševera bicomplex）概念，其为微分算子阶数受限的双复形，并结合收缩同伦（contracting homotopy）条件，通过谱序列第二页导出 Batalin-Vilkovisky 代数结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于统一并刻画 Batalin-Vilkovisky 代数结构的来源，尤其是 Ševera 关于奇辛流形（odd symplectic manifold）光滑函数代数上的 BV 代数结构这一原型例子，并通过 Cartan 演算（Cartan calculus）视角加以审视。

Method: 方法基于分级交换代数（graded commutative algebra）A 上的 A-模结构，构造双复形 (M, d₀, d₁)，要求 dᵢ 为 Grothendieck 微分算子且阶数不超过 i，并要求 (M, d₀) 存在与 d₀ 同阶微分算子的收缩同伦 K；进而分析相应谱序列（spectral sequence）第二页的微分 d₂。

Result: 在适当假设下，谱序列第二页上的 d₂ 是 E₂ 上的 Batalin-Vilkovisky 算子；当 E₂ 为自由秩 1 的 A-模时，A 的分级交换代数结构被提升为 Gerstenhaber 代数（Gerstenhaber algebra）结构，选取 d₂-闭基元素后进一步提升为 Batalin-Vilkovisky 代数结构。

Conclusion: 全文以 Cartan 演算视角考察 Batalin-Vilkovisky 代数，并在结论部分简要讨论了向导出 Cartan 演算（derived Cartan calculus）推广的迹象。

Abstract: A Ševera bicomplex is a bicomplex $(M,d_{0},d_{1})$ whose differentials $d_{i}$ are Grothendieck differential operators of order $\leq i$ with respect to a given $A$-module structure on $M$, where $A$ is a graded commutative algebra. One also requires that $(M,d_{0})$ admits a contracting homotopy $K$ that is a differential operator of the same order as $d_{0}$. Under suitable assumptions, the differential $d_{2}$ of the second page of the spectral sequence associated with $(M,d_{0},d_{1})$ is a Batalin-Vilkovisky operator on $E_{2}$, so that when $E_{2}$ is a free rank 1 $A$-module, the graded commutative algebra structure of $A$ is enhanced to a Gerstenhaber algebra structure, and the choice of a $d_{2}$-closed basis element for $E_{2}$ further enhances this to a Batalin-Vilkovisky algebra structure. The prototypical example of this construction is Ševera's description of the Batalin--Vilkovisky algebra structure on the algebra of smooth functions of an odd symplectic manifold. Throughout the whole article we look at Batalin-Vilkovisky algebras through the lenses of Cartan calculus. Indications of a generalization to derived Cartan calculus are briefly discussed in the concluding section.

</details>


### [269] [Constraints for Physical Gauge Groups Coming from the Causal Action Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24888)
*Felix Finster,Niels Gresnigt*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本工作综述因果费米子系统（causal fermion systems）的因果作用量原理（causal action principle）对有效局域规范群（effective local gauge groups）施加的约束，并表明规范势的二次约束在李代数（Lie algebras）与克利福德代数（Clifford algebras）结构之间建立联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标在于澄清因果作用量原理如何限制可能出现的有效局域规范群，尤其是双线性对数项（bilinear logarithmic terms）所导致的规范势二次约束的代数含义。

Method: 从对应于规范群 U(N) × U(N) 的一般手征势（chiral potentials）出发，系统推导二次约束，将其表述为李代数与克利福德代数结构之间的关系，并以若干示例加以说明。

Result: 二次约束（源自双线性对数项）确实在李代数结构与克利福德代数结构之间建立了联系；该联系可由一般手征势出发系统化地得到，并在多个实例中得以展示。

Conclusion: 因果作用量原理对有效局域规范群给出可分析的代数约束，其中李代数与克利福德代数的关联构成理解这些约束的关键框架，并通过具体例子得到印证。

Abstract: The constraints for the effective local gauge groups stemming from the causal action principle for causal fermion systems are reviewed. The constraint which are quadratic in the gauge potentials (coming from the so-called bilinear logarithmic terms) are shown to make a connection between the structures of Lie algebras and Clifford algebras. This connection is worked out systematically starting from general chiral potentials corresponding to the gauge group $\mathrm{U}(N) \times \mathrm{U}(N)$. The general results are illustrated in various examples.

</details>


### [270] [Don't mind the gap: Absolutely Continuous Edge Spectrum in a Mobility Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25000)
*Simon Becker,Mengxuan Yang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究二维格点哈密顿量与磁薛定谔算子（magnetic Schrödinger operators）在迁移率隙（mobility gap）中的边缘谱（edge spectrum），证明非零体拓扑指数（bulk topological index）可强制整个区间落入半空间算子的绝对连续谱（absolutely continuous spectrum），并给出绝对连续谱重数（multiplicity）下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迁移率隙指体能谱可能局域化而非必然缺失的能量区间，此前的体-边对应（bulk-edge correspondence）多关注谱隙内的边缘态，本文旨在将该结论推广至迁移率隙这一更一般的情形。

Method: 在适当的局域化（localization）与边界假设下，针对二维格点哈密顿量与磁薛定谔算子，采用拓扑指数分析及辅助空间切割构造（auxiliary-space cut construction，见第2.3节）进行论证。

Result: 常数非零体拓扑指数蕴含该区间整体属于相应半空间算子（half-space operator）的绝对连续谱；在区间内几乎处处能量处，绝对连续谱重数不低于该指数绝对值。

Conclusion: 结果表明体拓扑非平凡性在迁移率隙内同样产生绝对连续的边缘谱，为体-边对应提供了超出谱隙范畴的更普适刻画。

Abstract: We study the edge spectrum of two-dimensional lattice Hamiltonians and magnetic Schrödinger operators in a mobility gap, an energy interval in which the bulk spectrum is potentially localized rather than necessarily absent. Under suitable localization and boundary assumptions, we prove that a constant nonzero bulk topological index implies that the entire interval belongs to the absolutely continuous spectrum of the corresponding half-space operator. The absolutely continuous spectral multiplicity is bounded below by the absolute value of the index at almost every energy in the interval. The first version of the auxiliary-space cut construction in Section 2.3 was generated using ChatGPT 6.

</details>

### [271] [Interaction renormalization and the plasma staircase](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22915)
*Alexander V. Milovanov,Alexander Iomin,Jens Juul Rasmussen*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 该文解决了磁约束聚变等离子体中 E×B 阶梯（E×B staircase）动力学与结构稳定性的悬而未决问题，将 Hasegawa-Wakatani 静电漂移波湍流模型（Hasegawa-Wakatani model of electrostatic drift-wave turbulence）与相互作用重整化（interaction renormalization）思想结合，得出稳定性由非绝热参数 δ 的临界值 δc = π 决定的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: E×B 阶梯的动力学与结构稳定性问题因兼具科学与实用意义而在文献中引发广泛关注，此前尚存未解之处。该问题的实际意义在于对托卡马克（tokamak）中半透输运屏障阵列（arrays of semipermeable transport barriers）的部署具有重要影响。

Method: 作者提出新方法，将静电漂移波湍流的典型 Hasegawa-Wakatani 模型与源自量子场论（quantum field theory）的相互作用重整化思想相结合；并基于带有重整化相互作用常数的薛定谔-牛顿方程（Schrödinger-Newton equation）进行分析。

Result: 结果表明，一旦非绝热参数 δ 超过某一临界值，等离子体阶梯即表现出鲁棒与稳定，否则不稳定；该临界值为相互作用模型的精确结果，即 δc = π。当 δ < δc 时，不稳定系统沿径向膨胀，其径向二阶矩（radial second moment）随时间 t 以 t^(2/3) 增长。

Conclusion: 所得结果对托卡马克中半透输运屏障阵列的实现具有重要意义，并为其生成提出了一个阈值条件。

Abstract: We resolve an existing question concerning dynamical and structural stability of the E {\times} B staircases in magnetically confined fusion plasma. The problem has stirred considerable interest in the literature due to a unique combination of its scientific and practical aspects. Here, we report a new approach to the problem by combining the paradigmatic Hasegawa-Wakatani model of electrostatic drift-wave turbulence with an idea of interaction renormalization, imported from quantum field theory. It is shown based on the Schrödinger-Newton equation with renormalized interaction constant that the plasma staircase is robust and stable as soon as the nonadiabaticity parameter, δ, goes above a certain critical value, and is unstable otherwise. This critical value is an exact result of the interaction model and is found to be δc = π. For δ < δc , the unstable system expands in the radial direction, resulting in a radial second moment that grows with time t as t^(2/3). The results obtained bear important implications with regard to the implementation of arrays of semipermeable transport barriers in tokamaks, suggesting a threshold condition for their generation.

</details>


### [272] [On the statistical theory of strong electrolytes and high-temperature plasmas: New applications of the work by Yukhnovskii and Kelbg II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23621)
*W. Ebeling,M. Holovko*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 该文在Kramers、Hellman、Glauberman、Yukhnovskii与Kelbg等人使用指数势（exponential potentials）的历史基础上，进一步发展该理论并给出新结果，重点涉及量子统计理论，推导指数相互作用的Kelbg量子势，讨论屏蔽（screening）与热力学稳定性（thermodynamic stability）问题，并应用于碱金属等离子体（alkali plasmas）与聚变等离子体（fusion plasmas）的定性理论，表明指数势与早期解析结果可对电解质及碱金属等离子体中的相变（phase transitions）作定性解析处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指数势自Kramers等人以来已被用于求解量子化学（quantum chemistry）、离子溶液（ionic solutions）与等离子体（plasmas）问题，但其量子统计理论仍有待拓展。因此，本文旨在进一步发展该理论并补充新结果，尤其是量子统计层面的结果。

Method: 在既有指数势理论框架上作理论推广：推导指数相互作用（exponential interactions）对应的Kelbg量子势（Kelbg quantum potential），并围绕屏蔽效应与热力学稳定性展开讨论；同时将指数势及Yukhnovskii与Kelbg的早期解析结果用于碱金属等离子体与聚变等离子体的定性分析。

Result: 得到指数相互作用的Kelbg量子势，并对屏蔽与热力学稳定性问题作出讨论；进一步给出碱金属等离子体与聚变等离子体定性理论的新应用。

Conclusion: 指数势与Yukhnovskii、Kelbg的早期解析结果，可对电解质（electrolytes）和碱金属等离子体中的相变等困难问题实现定性解析处理，说明该理论框架具有更广的适用性。

Abstract: Exponential potentials were used since Kramers, Hellman, Glauberman, Yukhnovskii and Kelbg for solving problems of quantum chemistry, ionic solutions and plasmas. Here we develop the theory further and add new results, in particular with respect to the quantum-statistical theory. In particular, we derive the Kelbg quantum potential for the exponential interactions and discuss screening as well as the problem of thermodynamic stability. Further we give new applications to a qualitative theory of alkali plasmas and fusion plasmas. We show that the exponential potential and the early analytical results of Yukhnovskii and Kelbg allow a qualtative analytical treatment of such difficult problems as the phase transitions in electrolytes and alkali plasmas.

</details>

### [273] [Jet-like di-hadron correlations in pO, OO, and Ne--Ne collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24237)
*Suraj Prasad for the ALICE Collaboration*

Main category: nucl-ex

TL;DR: 基于 ALICE 于 2025 年首次开展的轻离子碰撞（pO、OO、Ne–Ne）测量，通过双强子关联（di-hadron correlations）近侧峰宽度（near-side peak width）随多重数的变化，搜寻小系统中的介质效应并检验流体力学描述的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重离子碰撞主要用于研究夸克–胶子等离子体（quark–gluon plasma, QGP），但 LHC 上 pp 与 p–Pb 碰撞中观测到的类流体力学流行为暗示小系统中也可能形成 QGP 液滴。为填补小系统与大系统之间的多重数间隙并检验流体力学描述是否适用，需引入中间尺度的轻离子碰撞。

Method: 利用 ALICE 探测器，首次在 LHC 上对 pO、OO 与 Ne–Ne 轻离子碰撞开展双强子关联测量，提取近侧关联峰宽度，并将其作为中快度平均带电粒子多重数的函数进行研究。

Result: 在低横动量（transverse momentum, $p_{\rm T}$）下，OO 与 Ne–Ne 碰撞的近侧峰比 pp 与 pO 碰撞更宽；在中心碰撞中，其宽度接近 Pb–Pb 碰撞的测量值。在高 $p_{\rm T}$ 下，峰宽随多重数增加而略有收窄。与 PYTHIA、AMPT 和 JETSCAPE 计算对比，三者在全运动学范围内均无法重现所测宽度。

Conclusion: 小系统与轻离子碰撞中近侧峰宽度随多重数及碰撞系统增大而展宽，并在中心轻离子碰撞中趋近 Pb–Pb 水平，支持介质效应随系统尺寸增强的图像；现有 PYTHIA、AMPT 与 JETSCAPE 模型尚不能完整描述该观测。

Abstract: Heavy-ion collisions are primarily studied to investigate the properties of hot and dense nuclear matter, known as the quark--gluon plasma (QGP). However, recent observations of hydrodynamic flow-like behaviour in pp and p--Pb collisions at the LHC suggest the possible formation of QGP droplets even in small collision systems. To bridge the multiplicity gap between small and large systems and to examine the applicability of hydrodynamic descriptions, light-ion collisions were performed for the first time at the LHC in 2025. This contribution presents measurements of the near-side peak width of di-hadron correlations in pO, OO, and Ne--Ne collisions with ALICE, used to search for medium effects. The width of the near-side correlation peak is studied as a function of the average charged-particle multiplicity at midrapidity. At low transverse momentum ($p_{\rm T}$), the near-side peak is broader in OO and Ne--Ne collisions than in pp and pO collisions, and in central collisions its width approaches that measured in Pb--Pb collisions. At higher $p_{\rm T}$ the peak narrows modestly with increasing multiplicity. The results are compared with PYTHIA, AMPT, and JETSCAPE calculations, none of which reproduces the measured widths over the full kinematic range.

</details>


<div id='physics.flu-dyn'></div>

# physics.flu-dyn [[Back]](#toc)

### [274] [An Exactly Solvable Ekman Layer with a Fractional-Order Stress Closure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23207)
*Sandy Hardian Susanto Herho,Rizki Dimas Permana,Iwan Pramesti Anwar,Rusmawan Suwarman,Deny Juanda Puradimaja,Dasapta Erwin Irawan*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该研究放宽经典风驱海洋表层动量平衡中的局地性（locality）假设，得到分数阶（fractional order）应力闭合律；其解表明表面偏转角小于45度、深度积分输运仍严格垂直于风、远场按幂律（power law）衰减，且局地极限为奇异极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典旋转海洋风驱表层理论以局地通量-梯度律（local flux-gradient law）闭合动量平衡，预测表面流相对风偏转45度，该值高于多数直接测量结果，因此需要检验放宽局地性假设后的后果。

Method: 从湍流应力与平均切变的精确积分关系出发，要求记忆核（memory kernel）不携带任何优先垂直尺度，从而导出幂律核与分数阶应力律；由于分数阶导数作用于有界速度剖面时在表面为零、无法施加风应力，而另一定义又使表面流无界，故改以应力为求解变量，在0到1之间的各阶用Mittag-Leffler函数闭式求解，并以三种独立算法交叉验证，未使用观测或模型数据。

Result: 表面偏转角仅依赖闭合阶数，且在全部阶数下均小于45度；深度积分输运仍严格垂直于风，因该约束来自动量平衡而非闭合方式。远场按幂律而非指数衰减，其振幅在局地极限下消失，说明该极限为奇异极限；在突加应力下表面瞬态按代数方式衰减。三种独立算法结果高度一致。

Conclusion: 放宽局地性可得到自洽的分数阶应力闭合框架，其预测表面偏转小于经典理论的45度，同时保留由动量平衡决定的垂直输运约束，并揭示了局地极限的奇异性与代数型瞬态衰减。

Abstract: The classical theory of the wind-driven surface layer of a rotating ocean closes the momentum balance with a local flux-gradient law, in which the turbulent stress at a given depth is proportional to the shear at that depth. It predicts a surface current deflected forty-five degrees from the wind, which exceeds most direct measurements. This study asks what follows when locality is relaxed. Beginning from the exact integral relation between turbulent stress and mean shear, and requiring the memory kernel to carry no preferred vertical scale, we obtain a power-law kernel and therefore a stress law of fractional order. The resulting equation cannot be posed on the velocity, because the fractional derivative of a bounded profile vanishes at the surface, so the wind stress cannot be applied, while the alternative definition of the derivative leaves the surface current unbounded. Posed on the stress instead, the problem is solvable in closed form in Mittag-Leffler functions at every order between zero and one. The surface deflection then depends on the closure order alone and is smaller than forty-five degrees throughout, whereas the depth-integrated transport stays exactly normal to the wind, because that constraint follows from the momentum balance and not from the closure. The far field decays as a power law rather than exponentially, and its amplitude vanishes in the local limit, so that limit is singular. Under a suddenly applied stress the surface transient decays algebraically. Three independent algorithms agree closely, and no observational or model data are used.

</details>


### [275] [The Positive Defect Problem: Target and Admissibility Criteria for a Programmatic Search for Unforced Navier-Stokes Blowup](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23868)
*Jarret Petrillo,James Glimm*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文为无强迫 Navier-Stokes 问题的爆破（blowup）搜索设定了一个明确目标：正缺陷问题（positive defect problem），并将其等价转化为 Littlewood-Paley 壳层能量通量的下界条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2026 年 9 月的程序化搜索已得到强迫情形的奇点，但无强迫问题（Clay 问题陈述 (A)、(B)）仍开放，缺乏搜索目标。本文旨在固定这一目标，即正缺陷问题。

Method: 将正缺陷问题证明为与「窗口平均后穿过 Littlewood-Paley 壳层的能量通量下界」等价，后者对应 Onsager 湍流理论中粗粒化通量的傅里叶侧形式；并给出候选解的必要条件与不可用判据，辅以 128^3 与 256^3 的伪谱搜索，形式化部分在 Lean 4 与 Mathlib 上完成。

Result: 正缺陷蕴含爆破，从而对陈述 (B) 给出否定回答，但逆命题未知。必要条件包括：速度上的 Type II 奇异时刻、能量集中于零长度集合、压力不在 L^2、速度不在 Onsager 临界类（Onsager-critical class）L^3_t B^{1/3}_{3,c_0}，以及无法塌缩到固定稳态 Euler 剖面。伪谱搜索显示细壳层通量下界在三分之一翻转时间内达到上限的 1%–6% 以内，但在 Kolmogorov 波数处尺度上失效。

Conclusion: 候选解必须区别于一般湍流之处在于其级联深度而非时间特征。除特别标注外，所有蕴含关系均为 Lean 4 上关于 Mathlib 的定理；该库不含 Navier-Stokes 对象，方程仅通过假设进入。

Abstract: On 7 and 8 September 2026 programmatic search produced singularities: a forced Navier-Stokes singularity at every fixed viscosity, statements (C) and (D) of the Clay problem, and two Euler singularities. The unforced problem, statements (A) and (B), stands open, and a search for it needs a target. This paper fixes one: the positive defect problem, that a Leray-Hopf solution from smooth data on the periodic cube loses energy on a finite window, at fixed viscosity, beyond what viscosity removes. A positive defect implies blowup and so a negative answer to statement (B); the converse is not known. The paper proves the target equivalent to a floor on the energy flux through the Littlewood-Paley shells, the Fourier-side form of the coarse-grained flux of the Onsager theory of turbulence, averaged over the window; states necessary conditions on a candidate: a singular time of Type II in velocity, energy concentrating on a set of zero length, a pressure outside L^2, a velocity outside the Onsager-critical class L^3_t B^{1/3}_{3,c_0}, an obstruction to collapse onto a fixed steady Euler profile; and states what cannot certify one: no finite computation witnesses a Galerkin-uniform ceiling, and selection and forcing return the question to a positive defect. A pseudo-spectral search at 128^3 and 256^3 shows which condition of the reduction binds: the fine-shell flux floor holds to within 1 to 6 percent of the ceiling for a third of a turnover time, and fails in scale at the Kolmogorov wavenumber, so a candidate must differ from generic turbulence in the depth of its cascade, not in its timing. Every implication not marked otherwise is a theorem in Lean 4 over Mathlib; the library contains no Navier-Stokes object, and the equation enters only through hypotheses.

</details>


### [276] [Bubble detachment from circular cavities and flat surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24342)
*Ianto Cannon,Stefan Endres,Lutz Mädler,Marc Avila*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该研究通过数值求解杨-拉普拉斯方程（Young-Laplace equation），确定了附着于平面上的气泡在给定接触半径、接触角与毛细长度（capillary length）下的最大稳定体积及其三种脱离模式，结果与多类实验数据吻合，并可对称适用于悬挂液滴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气泡在固体表面上的最大稳定体积与其脱离条件对电解、沸腾、碳酸化等过程具有重要意义，但此前多依赖解析估计，缺乏统一的定量描述。

Method: 采用打靶法（shooting method）求解杨-拉普拉斯方程，计算气泡的平衡形状，并据此判断其失稳脱离的临界条件。

Result: 识别出三种脱离模式：腔口颈缩（necking at the cavity）、侧向失稳（sideways instability）以及铺展后在周围表面上颈缩；预测的脱离体积与电解气泡、沸腾气泡、碳酸化气泡以及悬挂液滴（pendant drops）的实验测量吻合良好。

Conclusion: 该结果整合并推广了既有解析估计，且依据对称性可直接应用于悬挂液滴。

Abstract: We determine the maximum stable volume of bubbles attached to flat surfaces as a function of contact radius, contact angle, and capillary length. By solving the Young-Laplace equation using a shooting method, we calculate equilibrium bubble shapes and determine when bubbles detach through three distinct modes: necking at the cavity, sideways instability, and spreading followed by necking on the surrounding surface. Our predicted detachment volumes agree well with experimental measurements of electrolytic, boiling, and carbonated bubbles, as well as pendant drops. These results bring together and extend previous analytical estimates and, by symmetry, also apply directly to pendant drops.

</details>


### [277] [Fluid Flow as Transport of Probability: Entropy, Compressibility, and Irreversibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23850)
*Ankit Bhattacharjee*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该论文将流体密度视为空间概率密度函数（spatial probability density function），把宏观流动解释为不确定性的连续输运，推导出一般流动条件下香农熵（Shannon entropy）的时间演化，并将微观扩散（microscopic diffusion）严格识别为由局部费舍尔信息（Fisher Information）控制的熵源。基于局部佩克莱特数（Peclet number）为 1 的条件，给出压缩混合层平衡厚度的代数标度律，并通过数值模拟验证，平均相对误差为 0.001。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续性方程（continuity equation）虽在数学结构上与概率输运及刘维尔方程（Liouville equation）相似，但在工程语境中，对宏观流体流动的信息论解释较少被探讨。该研究旨在为宏观流动提供一种信息论与热力学视角的补充分析框架。

Method: 将流体密度建模为空间概率密度函数，把宏观运动视为不确定性的连续输运；推导一般流动条件下香农熵的时间演化，并分析熵产生与宏观可压缩性（compressibility）及微观扩散的关系；据此建立压缩混合层平衡厚度的代数标度律（scaling law）。

Result: 熵产生依赖于宏观可压缩性与微观扩散；微观扩散表现为严格正的熵源，且其大小精确由局部费舍尔信息决定；在局部佩克莱特数为 1 时，推导出压缩混合层平衡厚度的显式代数标度律。通过一维典型流动的有限差分模拟以及三维空间变化 Arnold-Beltrami-Childress（ABC）流中的对流-扩散模拟进行验证，平均相对误差为 0.001。

Conclusion: 该框架为热力学不可逆性（thermodynamic irreversibility）提供了解析视角，并可能应用于湍流建模（turbulence modeling）、换热器中的热熵产生、内燃机缸内混合以及空气动力学流动等方向。

Abstract: The continuity equation serves as a fundamental principle for mass transport in continuous media. While its mathematical structure mirrors that of probability transport and the Liouville equation, an informational interpretation of macroscopic fluid flow is less commonly explored in engineering contexts. This paper treats fluid density as a spatial probability density function, modeling macroscopic motion as the continuous transport of uncertainty. I derive the temporal evolution of Shannon entropy under general flow conditions, establishing how entropy generation depends on macroscopic compressibility and microscopic diffusion. Microscopic diffusion is shown to act as a strictly positive entropy source governed exactly by local Fisher Information. Furthermore, the framework yields an explicit algebraic scaling law characterizing the equilibrium thickness of compressive mixing layers at a local Peclet number of unity. The theoretical model is computationally validated through finite-difference simulations of one-dimensional canonical flows and advection-diffusion within a three-dimensional, spatially varying Arnold-Beltrami-Childress (ABC) flow, yielding a mean relative error of $0.001$. The framework provides an analytical perspective on thermodynamic irreversibility, with potential applications to turbulence modeling, thermal entropy generation in heat exchangers, in-cylinder mixing in internal combustion engines, and aerodynamic flows.

</details>


<div id='hep-lat'></div>

# hep-lat [[Back]](#toc)

### [278] [First-Principles Determination of the QCD Contribution to the Axion-Photon Coupling Using Domain-Wall Fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22698)
*Tian Lin,Hao-Yang Cheng,Xu Feng,Lu-Chang Jin,Chuan Liu,Qi-Yuan Luo*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 该工作通过格点量子色动力学（lattice QCD）独立计算轴子-光子耦合（axion-photon coupling）中由 QCD 动力学贡献的模型无关项 $\mathcal C_{\rm QCD}$，得到 $\mathcal C_{\rm QCD}=1.965(35)$，为第一性原理基准并调和了此前手征微扰理论（ChPT）的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴子-光子耦合对实验轴子搜寻及强 CP（strong CP）解检验至关重要，其中 $\mathcal C_{\rm QCD}$ 为模型无关贡献。不同框架下的次领头阶手征微扰理论（NLO ChPT）给出高达约 8% 的分歧，需精确的第一性原理计算予以澄清。

Method: 采用与近期背景场计算互补的独立格点 QCD 方法：计算赝标量到双光子的三点函数，并利用反常 Ward 恒等式（anomalous Ward identities）将 $\mathcal C_{\rm QCD}$ 分离为精确反常贡献与轻夸克质量压低的修正项。修正项用畴壁费米子（domain-wall fermions）计算，其良好手征对称性显著抑制离散化效应；使用两个近物理的 $N_f=2+1$ 系综并进行连续极限外推。

Result: 得到同位旋对称部分 $\mathcal C_{\rm QCD}^{\rm IS}=1.619(30)$、同位旋破缺部分 $\mathcal C_{\rm QCD}^{\rm IB}=0.347(22)$，总计 $\mathcal C_{\rm QCD}=1.965(35)$。与已发表 NLO ChPT 预测的直接比较显示表面张力，但作者识别了其来源并表明 ChPT 结果可与格点结果相协调。

Conclusion: 该结果为轴子-光子耦合的 QCD 贡献提供了第一性原理基准，并对相互竞争的手征微扰理论描述构成定量检验。

Abstract: The axion-photon coupling, crucial for experimental axion searches and tests of the strong CP solution, receives a substantial model-independent contribution $\mathcal C_{\rm QCD}$ from QCD dynamics. Next-to-leading-order chiral perturbation theory (NLO ChPT) in different frameworks has yielded puzzling discrepancies of up to $8\%$, motivating precise first-principles calculations. We present an independent lattice QCD determination using a method complementary to the recent background-field calculation. Computing pseudoscalar-to-two-photon three-point functions and exploiting anomalous Ward identities, we separate $\mathcal C_{\rm QCD}$ into an exact anomaly contribution and a light-quark-mass-suppressed correction. The latter is computed using domain-wall fermions, whose excellent chiral symmetry strongly suppresses discretization effects. Working on two near-physical $N_f=2+1$ ensembles with continuum extrapolation, we obtain $\mathcal C_{\rm QCD}^{\rm IS}=1.619(30)$ (isospin-symmetric), $\mathcal C_{\rm QCD}^{\rm IB}=0.347(22)$ (isospin-breaking), and $\mathcal C_{\rm QCD}=1.965(35)$ in total. While direct comparisons with published NLO ChPT predictions reveal apparent tensions, we identify their sources and show that the ChPT results can be reconciled with our lattice determination. Our result provides a first-principles benchmark for the QCD contribution to the axion-photon coupling and a quantitative test of competing ChPT descriptions.

</details>


<div id='physics.chem-ph'></div>

# physics.chem-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [279] [From Heuristics to Machine Learning: The Performance Ceiling for Single-Ion Magnets and Its Electronic Origin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24038)
*Federico Zahariev,Regina Pereyra,Vassiliki-Alexandra Glezakou,Durga Paudyal*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该研究检验了合成前可获得的结构信息能否预测单分子磁体（SIM），发现三类几何描述均仅达约76%准确率，揭示几何方法无法获取识别SIM所需的电子结构与连接性信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习被期望加速单分子磁体（single-ion magnets, SIMs）的发现，但合成前可用的结构信息是否足以支持此类预测尚不明确，需系统评估几何描述能力的上限及其失效原因。

Method: 针对SIMDAVIS 1.2.1数据库中1215个镧系配合物，比较三个递进层次的结构描述：配位点的表格化特征、配位多面体的连续对称性度量（continuous symmetry measures）以及完整的三维原子排布；并结合多参考从头算（multireference ab initio）计算与对高置信度错误背后结构的检视来分析失效案例。

Result: 三种描述方法的准确率均收敛于约76%，仅略高于"对Dy3+预测为SIM"这一单规则的71%。被几何模型漏检的SIM为场诱导弛豫体（field-induced relaxers），其基态Kramers双重态易发生隧穿，该性质对几何描述符不可见；大量假阳性样本含多个镧系中心或自由基，其弛豫为集体行为，超出单离子图像。将筛选限制于高预测置信度化合物时，准确率提升至88%，同时保留48%的数据集。

Conclusion: 识别SIM所需的电子结构与连接性信息无法仅由几何方法获得；几何模型仍具实用价值（高置信度筛选）。基于失效分析，提出结合核数（nuclearity）与自由基的简单过滤器以及从头算配体场（ligand-field）描述符的策略。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) is expected to speed up the discovery of single-ion magnets (SIMs), but does the structural information available before synthesis allow such predictions? For 1215 lanthanide complexes from the SIMDAVIS 1.2.1 database we compared three increasing levels of structural description: tabular features of the coordination site, continuous symmetry measures of the coordination polyhedron, and the complete 3D arrangement of atoms. All three converge to an accuracy near 76%, only slightly above the 71% of the single rule "predict SIM for Dy3+". To explain the failures, we combined multireference ab initio calculations with an inspection of the structures behind the high-confidence errors. The SIMs missed by the geometric models are field-induced relaxers whose ground Kramers doublets are prone to tunnelling, a property invisible to geometric descriptors. Many false positives contain several lanthanide centers or radicals, so their relaxation is collective and outside the single-ion picture. The electronic-structure and connectivity information needed to identify SIMs is therefore not accessible to geometric methods alone. Geometric models remain useful: restricting the screening to compounds with high prediction confidence raises the accuracy to 88% while retaining 48% of the dataset. Building on the analysis of the failures, we propose a strategy that combines simple filters for nuclearity and for radicals with ligand-field descriptors from ab initio calculations.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.supr-con'></div>

# cond-mat.supr-con [[Back]](#toc)

### [280] [Raman scattering in cuprate oxychlorides high-temperature superconductors single-crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22400)
*Chafic Fawaz,Yingzheng Gao,Luca Laveder,Lorenzo Menon,Owen Moulding,Rolf Heid,Blair W Lebert,Christophe Bellin,Keevin Beneut,David Santos-Cottin,Ikuya Yamada,Yuichi Okazaki,Hajime Yamamoto,Masaki Azuma,M. -A. Méasson,Matteo d'Astuto*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究系统测量了钠掺杂铜基氧氯化物超导体 Na$_x$Ca$_{2-x}$CuO$_2$Cl$_2$ 在全相图（从反铁磁区到超导区）的拉曼响应，发现除预期声子模外还存在由强电子-声子耦合（electron–phonon coupling）共振过程激发的额外模式，并在反铁磁相高能量下观测到可解释为双磁振子（bimagnon）的 $B_{1g}$ 模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在通过拉曼散射（Raman scattering）刻画该类铜基超导体（cuprate superconductor）从反铁磁（antiferromagnetic）到超导（superconducting）区域的完整相图行为，并澄清额外拉曼模式的激发机制及其与磁激发的关联。

Method: 采用拉曼光谱手段测量 Na$_x$Ca$_{2-x}$CuO$_2$Cl$_2$ 样品，并在不同入射光子能量下重复测量以验证共振效应；同时追踪 $B_{1g}$ 模的温度与掺杂依赖性。

Result: 除预期拉曼活性声子模（Raman-active phonon modes）外，检测到若干额外模式，可解释为强电子-声子耦合所支持的共振过程激发；在高能量下，反铁磁相中出现明确的 $B_{1g}$ 模，可解释为双磁振子（bimagnon）。

Conclusion: 该 $B_{1g}$ 模的温度与掺杂依赖为铜基超导体中的多磁振子激发（multimagnon excitation）提供了信息，并可在理论上与交换相互作用（exchange interaction）相联系。

Abstract: We investigate the Raman response of sodium-doped cuprate oxychloride Na$_x$Ca$_{2-x}$CuO$_2$Cl$_2$ high-temperature superconductors across their entire phase diagram, from the antiferromagnetic to the superconducting region. In addition to the expected Raman-active phonon modes, we detect several additional modes that can be interpreted as being excited via a resonance process enabled by strong electron--phonon coupling. To verify the resonance effect, the Raman response at different incident photon energies was measured. At high energies, there is a well-defined $B_{1g}$ mode in the antiferromagnetic phase, which can be interpreted as a bimagnon We follow its temperature and doping dependence, providing information on the multimagnon excitation in these cuprates, which can be theoretically linked to the exchange interaction.

</details>


### [281] [Comment on "Topography of Fermi arcs in t-PtBi$_2$ using high-resolution angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy" arXiv:2503.08841 (cond-mat)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22776)
*Sergey Borisenko*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 通过重新分析 O'Leary 等人的完整沉积数据，仅使用实验测定的动量分布曲线（MDC）极大值、能量分布曲线（EDC）峰位与前缘，发现强各向异性的低能能隙与色散回弯，与 Changdar 等人独立报道的结果一致；表明 O'Leary 等人的数据实际支持而非否定 t-PtBi$_2$ 的表面超导性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ARPES 与扫描隧道谱（scanning tunneling spectroscopy）已在 Weyl 半金属（Weyl semimetal）t-PtBi$_2$ 中确立表面超导性，但 O'Leary 等人基于独立 ARPES 实验得出 3 K 以上不存在超导特征的相反结论。本文旨在解决这一分歧，检验两方数据是否真正矛盾。

Method: 重新分析 O'Leary 等人的完整沉积数据，仅采用实验测定的 MDC 极大值、EDC 峰位与前缘；并使用金属体态费米截止（metallic bulk Fermi cutoff）对原始温度依赖数据集进行配准（registering）。同时指出其温度依赖数据源自另一表面终止（surface termination），且先前以温度依赖的能量平移、动量平移与动量重标度（energy/momentum translations and momentum rescalings）方式呈现。

Result: 分析得到强各向异性的低能能隙与色散回弯（dispersion back-bending），其幅度与角依赖关系紧密再现 Changdar 等人独立报道的结果。以金属体态费米截止配准后，在四个费米交叉处分别得到约 2、2、1 和 0.8 meV 的有限能隙；各向异性的低能压制（low-energy suppression）可持续至 19 K。

Conclusion: O'Leary 等人独立获得的数据（不同样品、不同光子能量）确认而非反驳此前报道的各向异性超导能隙，并额外显示各向异性低能压制可延伸至 19 K。

Abstract: Angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) and scanning tunneling spectroscopy have established surface superconductivity in the Weyl semimetal t-PtBi$_2$, whereas O'Leary et al. concluded from an independent ARPES experiment that superconducting signatures are absent above 3 K. Here we reanalyze the complete deposited data of O'Leary et al. Using only experimentally determined momentum-distribution-curve (MDC) maxima, energy-distribution-curve (EDC) peak positions, and leading edges, we find a strongly anisotropic low-energy gap and dispersion back-bending that closely reproduce the magnitude and angular dependence reported independently by Changdar et al. We further show that the temperature-dependent data of Ref.~\cite{Oleary} originate from the other surface termination and were presented using temperature-dependent energy translations, momentum translations, and momentum rescalings. Registering the original temperature-dependent datasets using the metallic bulk Fermi cutoff reveals finite gaps of approximately 2, 2, 1, and 0.8 meV at the four Fermi crossings. Thus the independently acquired data of O'Leary et al., obtained on different samples and with a different photon energy, confirm rather than contradict the previously reported anisotropic superconducting gap and additionally show that an anisotropic low-energy suppression persists to 19 K.

</details>


### [282] [Pressure-induced unconventional charge-density-wave states in kagome metal AV3Sb5 (A = K, Rb, Cs)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22794)
*Zhimian Wu,Linpeng Nie,Ye Yang,Kuanglv Sun,Huachen Rao,Dan Zhao,Zhongjun Li,Tao Wu,Xianhui Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文通过压力依赖的 51V 核磁共振（NMR）测量，在 KV3Sb5 与 RbV3Sb5 中识别出压力诱导的非常规电荷密度波（CDW）相，并将其归因于可能的非公度三Q（incommensurate triple-Q）CDW，指出该现象是 AV3Sb5 家族的共同特征，且由范霍夫奇点（van Hove singularities, vHSs）驱动的电子涨落所调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AV3Sb5（A = K, Rb, Cs）为探索新奇多体量子现象提供了新平台；CsV3Sb5 在中等压力下出现 q = 3/8 的条纹状 CDW 及超导双穹顶（double-dome）行为，而既有密度泛函理论（DFT）计算表明该压力诱导 CDW 超出常规声子软化图像，电子关联作用不明，其微观机制尚缺乏深入理解。

Method: 对 KV3Sb5 与 RbV3Sb5 开展压力依赖的 51V 核磁共振（nuclear magnetic resonance, NMR）测量，通过 NMR 谱识别压力诱导 CDW 相，并系统分析奈特移位（Knight shift）与核自旋-晶格弛豫率之间的 Korringa 关系。

Result: 尽管加压 KV3Sb5 与 RbV3Sb5 中未出现超导双穹顶行为，NMR 谱在两种材料中均识别出压力诱导 CDW 相，可能为非公度三Q CDW，说明超出 DFT 的压力诱导非常规 CDW 是 AV3Sb5 的共性。与条纹状 CDW 不同，该非公度三Q CDW 并不强烈抑制超导转变温度（Tc），而是与中等压力区间的近平台行为相吻合。Korringa 关系分析表明，范霍夫奇点（vHSs）驱动的电子涨落是压力诱导非常规 CDW 的有效调控因素。

Conclusion: 研究将压力诱导的非常规 CDW 确立为 kagome 金属 AV3Sb5 中的一种新颖关联量子态，并强调 vHSs 驱动的电子涨落在其中的关键作用。

Abstract: Since the discovery of charge density wave (CDW) and superconductivity, kagome metal AV3Sb5 (A = K, Rb, Cs) provides a new platform for exploring novel many-body quantum phenomena. In CsV3Sb5, a stripe-like CDW with commensurate wave vector q = 3/8 was observed under moderate pressures, which leads to a peculiar superconducting double-dome behavior in pressure-dependent phase diagram. Previous density functional theory (DFT) calculations indicate that the pressure-induced stripe-like CDW is beyond conventional phonon softening scenario, suggesting a nontrivial role of electronic correlations. However, an in-depth understanding for the pressure-induced unconventional CDW remains elusive. Here, we performed pressure-dependent 51V nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR) measurements on KV3Sb5 and RbV3Sb5. Although the superconducting double-dome behavior is absent in pressurized KV3Sb5 and RbV3Sb5, a pressure-induced CDW phase, ascribed to a possible incommensurate triple-Q CDW, is identified by NMR spectra in both materials, indicating that the pressure-induced unconventional CDW beyond DFT calculations is a common feature for kagome metal AV3Sb5. In contrast to the stripe-like CDW, the pressure-induced incommensurate triple-Q CDW does not strongly suppress the superconducting temperature (Tc) but coincide with an almost plateau behavior at intermediate pressure regime in the pressure-dependent superconducting phase diagram. Furthermore, by systematically analyzing the Korringa relation between Knight shift and nuclear spin-lattice relaxation rate in AV3Sb5, van Hove singularities (vHSs) driven electronic fluctuations are revealed as an effective knob for the pressure-induced unconventional CDW. Finally, our present findings underscore the pressure-induced unconventional CDW as a novel correlated quantum state in kagome metal AV3Sb5.

</details>


### [283] [Bulk Superconductivity in Rocksalt LaN$_{1-x}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23213)
*Caeli Benyacko,Lin-Ding Yuan,Josiah A. Turner,Laila Reimanis,Siyuan Ji,Cheng Li,Erick A. Lawrence,Hanna Z. Porter,James M. Rondinelli,Stephen D. Wilson*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 通过激光浮区炉中镧金属与高压氮气直接反应合成氮化镧（LaN），证实其为岩盐结构、化学计量比 LaN₀.₉₄，并在 5.95 K 呈现超导转变，成为稀土单氮化物家族首个体超导成员。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀土单氮化物（rare earth mononitride）家族中尚未有体超导成员被确立，且第一性原理预测化学计量比的 LaN 在动力学上不稳定，因此需要探索非化学计量组成下的合成与物性。

Method: 在激光浮区炉（laser floating-zone furnace）中使镧金属与高压氮气直接反应合成样品，并结合同步辐射 X 射线与中子衍射（neutron diffraction）进行结构表征，辅以电输运、热容与磁化测量。

Result: 衍射结果验证为岩盐结构（rocksalt structure），化学计量比为 LaN₀.₉₄，与第一性原理预测的化学计量极限下动力学不稳定相符；输运、热容与磁性测量表明 LaN₀.₉₄ 呈金属性，并在 T_c = 5.95 K 发生超导转变。

Conclusion: LaN 被确立为稀土单氮化物家族中首个体超导成员，并提示可利用高压光学反应器（high-pressure optical reactor）提供的独特加工空间对该系列开展后续探索。

Abstract: We report the synthesis and characterization of lanthanum nitride by direct reaction of lanthanum metal with high pressure nitrogen gas in a laser floating-zone furnace. Combined synchrotron X-ray and neutron diffraction measurement verifies a rocksalt structure with stoichiometry LaN$_{0.94}$, consistent with first principles prediction that LaN is dynamically unstable in the stoichiometric limit. Electrical transport, heat capacity, and magnetization measurements show that LaN$_{0.94}$ is metallic with a superconducting transition at $T_c=5.95$ K. Our data establish LaN as the first bulk superconducting member of the rare earth mononitride family and motivates future exploration of the series using the unique processing space afforded by high-pressure optical reactors.

</details>


### [284] [Coexistence and Interconversion of Multiple-Order Majorana Modes in Topological Superconductor Films with Varying Thickness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24311)
*Zhizhong Ding,Chuanbao Zhang,Shunhong Zhang,Xiaoyu Zhu,Wei Qin,Ping Cui,Zhenyu Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作从理论上研究 $C_{2h}$ 对称拓扑超导薄膜（如 2M-WS$_2$）与磁性绝缘体近邻耦合时，马约拉纳模（Majorana modes）随厚度的演化，揭示单一材料平台中一阶、二阶、三阶马约拉纳模的共存及其电场调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究多关注单一阶次的马约拉纳模，而不同阶次马约拉纳模在单一平台中的共存与相互转换尚不明确，且晶体对称性在其中的作用有待厘清。该工作旨在探索厚度与交换场穿透深度对马约拉纳模演化的调控机制。
