Method: 采用理论分析方法，考察 $C_{2h}$ 对称拓扑超导薄膜与磁性绝缘体近邻耦合体系；通过改变薄膜厚度与近邻诱导交换场，分析涡旋线上端模、界面手性马约拉纳边模、铰链模及角模的演化，并讨论超薄膜极限下的二维 $Z_2$ 拓扑。

Result: 厚膜中涡旋线上同时存在表面与界面两个马约拉纳束缚态（Majorana bound states），界面模随交换场增强演化为手性马约拉纳边模；$C_{2h}$ 对称性使两个手性模沿四个侧面中两个的铰链传播。当交换场穿透深度足够浅时，两模局域于界面附近但演化不同：一个坍缩为两个马约拉纳角模，另一个与界面手性模合并；角模与界面手性模解耦良好。此共存现象在超薄膜极限下依然保持，并可借助电场控制的二维 $Z_2$ 拓扑实现多阶马约拉纳模的相互转换。

Conclusion: 合适的晶体对称性对马约拉纳模的产生、调控及潜在编织（braiding）具有关键作用，有助于验证非阿贝尔统计（non-Abelian statistics）并推动容错量子计算（fault-tolerant quantum computation）。

Abstract: We theoretically investigate the thickness-dependent evolution of Majorana modes in $C_{2h}$-symmetric topological superconductor films (such as the recently discovered 2M-WS$_2$) proximity coupled with magnetic insulators. For sufficiently thick films, two Majorana bound states coexist as end modes at the surface and interface along a vortex line, with the interfacial mode evolving into a chiral Majorana edge mode upon increasing the proximity-induced exchange field. The intrinsic $C_{2h}$ crystalline symmetry selects two chiral Majorana modes circulating along the hinges on two of the four side surfaces of the film. When the penetration depth of the exchange field is sufficiently shallow, the two circulating modes are localized near the interface, but with qualitatively different subsequent evolutions. One of them further collapses to form two Majorana corner modes, while the other merges with the chiral Majorana mode circulating around the interface. Importantly, the corner modes are well decoupled from the interfacial chiral mode, thereby enabling an unprecedented coexistence of first-, second-, and third-order Majorana modes within a single material platform. We further show that such coexistence persists even in the ultrathin-film limit, where the electric-field-controlled two-dimensional $Z_2$ topology offers an extra advantage to readily interconvert the multiple-order Majorana modes. These findings highlight the pivotal role of the proper crystalline symmetry in enabling emergence, manipulation, and potential braiding of Majorana modes for demonstrating non-Abelian statistics and fault-tolerant quantum computation.

</details>


### [285] [Parallel Simulation of Josephson Junctions With Multiplicative Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24461)
*Vincenzo Pierro,Luigi Troiano,Elena Mejuto Villa,Sergio Pagano,Giovanni Filatrella*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作利用并行图形处理器（GPU）加速约瑟夫森结（Josephson junction）在临界电流涨落下的开关动力学数值模拟，针对缺乏解析结果的非平衡洗衣板模型（washboard model with multiplicative noise）实现高效大规模计算，从而在真实偏置电流扫描速率下完整刻画噪声对结开关电流分布的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 所研究的系统对应含乘性噪声（multiplicative noise）的非平衡洗衣板模型，该模型缺乏解析结果，因此需要依赖大规模数值模拟。同时，真实偏置电流扫描速率（bias current sweep rates）下的模拟计算量庞大，传统计算资源难以在合理时间内完成。

Method: 采用并行图形处理器（parallel graphic processing units, GPU）进行加速仿真，对受临界电流涨落影响的约瑟夫森结开关动力学开展大规模数值模拟。

Result: 所提方法可在短时间内、以相对低廉的资源完成大规模数值模拟，并能在真实偏置电流扫描速率下完整刻画噪声对结开关电流分布（switching current distributions）的影响。

Conclusion: GPU加速方案有效克服了该非平衡乘性噪声模型缺乏解析解与计算成本高的限制，为在全参数范围（尤其真实扫描速率条件）下系统研究噪声效应提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Parallel graphic processing units have been employed for fast simulations of the switching dynam- ics of Josephson junctions subject to critical current fluctuations. Such a system is modeled by a nonequilibrium washboard model with multiplicative noise, for which analytical results are lacking. The proposed approach allows us to execute extensive numerical simulation in short time and with relatively inexpensive resources. This allows us to fully characterize the effect of the noise on the junction switching current distributions at realistic bias current sweeprates.

</details>


### [286] [Detection of signals in presence of noise through Josephson junction switching currents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24533)
*O. V. Pountougnigni,R. Yamapi,C. Tchawoua,V. Pierro,G. Filatrella*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该论文利用约瑟夫森结（Josephson junction）在存在高斯噪声（Gaussian noise）条件下检测正弦信号，通过线性扫描偏置电流并分析开关电流（switching current）判断谐波驱动是否存在。结果表明检测效率随扫描速率（ramp rate）变化且在较快速度下更有利，说明收集开关电流是一种稳健的技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为模拟真实实验装置，需在有限时间与噪声环境下检验微弱谐波信号的可检测性，因此采用线性扫描偏置电流直至跃迁到有限电压的方式来检测正弦信号。研究旨在评估不同初始相位条件与扫描速率对检测效率的影响。

Method: 在约瑟夫森结上施加正弦信号，分两种条件进行：初始相位未知（非相干策略，incoherent strategy）与初始相位已知（相干策略，coherent strategy）。检测通过线性扫描偏置电流直至发生向有限电压的跃迁，并收集开关电流；检测效率由信噪比（signal-to-noise ratio）评估，具体以 Kumar-Carrol 指数（Kumar-Carrol index）估计。

Result: 在两种条件下，Kumar-Carrol 指数估计的信噪比分析均显示，检测效率对约瑟夫森结扫描速率（ramp rate）存在有益依赖，尤其在相对较快的速度下更为有利。

Conclusion: 可以认为，开关电流的收集是一种稳健（robust）的技术，因此能够利用预先设定的有限时间来采集数据所带来的优势。

Abstract: Josephson junctions can be employed to reveal a sinusoidal signal in presence of Gaussian noise. To mimic realistic setups, the detection is performed linearly ramping the bias current until a switch to the finite voltage occurs; the analysis of the resulting switching currents can be exploited to decide about the presence of the harmonic drive. The signal is applied in two conditions: with an unknown initial phase (incoherent strategy) and with a known initial phase (coherent strategy). In both conditions, the analysis of the efficiency of the detection, performed through the signal-to-noise ratio, as estimated by the Kumar-Carrol index, shows that the dependence upon the Josephson junction ramp rate is beneficial, especially for relatively fast speed. One can conclude that the collection of the switching currents is a robust technique, and thus it is possible to exploit the advantages of a predetermined finite time to collect the data.

</details>


### [287] [New theoretical model of the dynamic anomalies in HTc superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24607)
*J. Sosnowski*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该论文针对高温超导体（HTc superconductors）在缓变磁场下动态电流-电压（current-voltage）特性异常，提出了一种新的理论模型，并与既有实验数据进行了对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高温超导体在缓变磁场中呈现动态电流-电压特性异常，其机理尚需理论解释。该研究旨在通过对磁通穿透（magnetic flux penetration）过程的细致分析，建立可描述此类异常的理论模型。

Method: 模型基于对高温超导平板（superconducting slab）中磁通穿透过程的详细分析。理论分析包含两部分：一是基于扩散方程（diffusion equation）新解的结果，二是基于唯象临界态模型（phenomenological critical state model）的结果。

Result: 论文给出了新扩散方程解与唯象临界态模型两种理论分析，并将模型与先前的实验数据进行了比较。

Conclusion: 所提出的理论模型可用于解释高温超导体在缓变磁场下动态电流-电压特性的异常，并得到与先前实验数据比较的支持（具体结论细节摘要未给出）。

Abstract: New theoretical model of the dynamic current-voltage characteristics anomalies in HTc superconductors in slowly varying magnetic field has been proposed. The model is based on an analysis in details of the magnetic flux penetration into HTc superconducting slab in such case. The comparison of model with previous experimental data has been presented. Theoretical analysis resulting from a new solution of diffusion equation is given as well as other one based on phenomenological critical state model.

</details>


### [288] [Interface engineering of spin triplet Cooper pairs for spin-valve implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24803)
*Debashree Nayak,Abhisek Sahoo,Pratap Kumar Sahoo,Kartik Senapati*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在有电流通过时，自旋单态（spin singlet）库珀对可在超导体/重金属界面部分转化为等自旋三重态（equal-spin triplet）对，进而在重金属中产生非平衡自旋矩；该自旋矩与铁磁层磁矩共同作用，使 Nb/Pt/Ni/Pt/Nb 垂直纳米器件呈现磁自旋阀（spin-valve）响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已预测超电流自旋霍尔效应（supercurrent spin-Hall effect）可在重金属中产生非平衡自旋矩，但如何将其直接用于超导自旋电子学（superconducting spintronics）中的器件功能尚不清楚。该工作旨在验证这种电流诱导的三重态自旋矩能否与铁磁层磁矩耦合，实现可调控的自旋阀响应。

Method: 采用 Nb/Pt/Ni/Pt/Nb 垂直纳米器件结构，在 Nb/Pt 界面故意引入界面粗糙度，以产生有限的 Rashba 自旋轨道耦合（Rashba spin-orbit coupling），并在 Ni 界面引入面外磁矩分量，从而促进单态-三重态库珀对转换。对比测量了粗糙界面与光滑界面器件的磁自旋阀响应。

Result: 粗糙界面器件表现出可观测的磁自旋阀效应，其幅度取决于 Nb/Pt 界面单态-三重态库珀对转换效率；在测量分辨率范围内，光滑界面器件未观察到可辨识的自旋阀响应。由于 Ni 层磁矩与 Pt 层中电流诱导的三重态自旋矩可分别通过磁场和偏置电流独立切换，该自旋阀可经由任一参数调控。

Conclusion: 结果表明，界面粗糙度与 Rashba 自旋轨道耦合是促进单态-三重态转换、实现自旋阀响应的关键因素。该工作展示了一种直接利用自旋极化三重态库珀对（spin polarized triplet Cooper pairs）的超导自旋电子学新途径。

Abstract: Spin singlet Cooper pairs can be converted into equal-spin triplet pairs at a superconductor/heavy-metal interface under suitable conditions. Under current carrying conditions, these triplet correlations can give rise to a non equilibrium spin moment in the heavy metal through the predicted supercurrent spin-Hall effect. Here we demonstrate that this current induced triplet spin moment, in conjunction with the magnetic moment of a ferromagnetic layer, can enable a magnetic spin-valve response in Nb/Pt/Ni/Pt/Nb vertical nano-devices. The magnitude of this spin-valve effect depends on the efficiency of singlet-triplet Cooper pair conversion at the Nb/Pt interface. In order to facilitate efficient triplet generation, we deliberately introduced interfacial roughness to introduce finite Rashba spin-orbit coupling at the Nb/Pt interface and an out-of-plane component of magnetic moment at the Ni interface. In contrast, no discernible spin-valve response was observed in devices with smooth interfaces within measurement resolutions. Since the magnetic moment of the Ni layer and the current-induced triplet spin moment in the Pt layer are independently switchable using a magnetic field and bias current, respectively, the spin valve can be controlled via either parameters. These results demonstrates a novel approach to directly utilizing the spin polarized triplet Copper pairs in superconducting spintronic applications.

</details>


### [289] [Generalized conditions for odd-frequency pairing in superconducting systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24827)
*Florian Kayatz,Annica M. Black-Schaffer,Jorge Cayao*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作给出了任意超导系统中奇频配对（odd-frequency pairing）出现所满足的广义充要条件，将此前多带体超导体（multiband bulk superconductor）中的一阶表达式推广至所有超导体系及任意阶（在序参量与频率两方面），并应用于若干具体体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇频超导配对已被预测在多种系统中出现，并可导致顺磁迈斯纳响应（paramagnetic Meissner response）与长程超导邻近效应（long-range superconducting proximity effect）等现象，因此需要普适的判定条件。已有的一阶表达式仅适用于多带体超导体，范围有限。

Method: 通过理论推导，建立适用于从体超导体到超导杂化结构（superconducting hybrid structures）的广义充要条件，并将其在序参量和频率上推广至所有阶。随后将该条件应用于超导-铁磁体（superconducting-ferromagnet）与超导约瑟夫森结（superconducting Josephson junctions），以及由常规超导体邻近化（proximitized）的过渡金属硫族化合物单层（transition metal dichalcogenide monolayer）。

Result: 所导出的广义条件可统一刻画奇频配对的出现；在若干体系中，该条件被用于理解相应超导态的性质。

Conclusion: 奇频配对的判定条件被推广为适用于所有超导体系、所有阶的普适形式，为分析各类超导及杂化结构的超导态提供了统一工具。

Abstract: Odd-frequency superconducting pairing has been predicted to arise in many systems and is known to lead to phenomena such as paramagnetic Meissner response and long-range superconducting proximity effect. Here, we provide generalized necessary and sufficient conditions for odd-frequency pairing appearing in any superconducting system, from bulk superconductors to superconducting hybrid structures. This generalizes an earlier first-order expression in multiband bulk superconductors to all superconducting systems and to all orders, in both order parameter and frequency. We then apply the derived conditions to several systems, including superconducting-ferromagnet and superconducting Josephson junctions, as well as a transition metal dichalcogenide monolayer proximitized by a conventional superconductor, where the generalized conditions are used to understand the properties of the superconducting state.

</details>


### [290] [Electronic Reconstruction Towards Topological Superconductivity in FeTe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24843)
*Hongtao Rong,Yang Ge,Zi-Jie Yan,Haoran Lin,Bing Xia,Xiaoda Liu,Zihao Wang,Pu Xiao,Lok-Kan Lai,Stephen Paplini,Jiatao Song,Jiangang Yang,Peter J. Hirschfeld,Shuolong Yang,Jiabin Yu,Cui-Zu Chang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作通过分子束外延、角分辨光电子能谱、输运测量与理论计算相结合，追踪20个单胞FeTe薄膜在Te退火过程中由反铁磁金属向超导体的电子重构，确立了化学计量比FeTe的本征低能电子结构并揭示其拓扑表面态与超导共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学计量比FeTe薄膜中本征超导（intrinsic superconductivity）的发现重新激发了对其非常规与拓扑超导（topological superconductivity）的研究兴趣，但其本征电子结构尚未被解析。

Method: 结合分子束外延（molecular beam epitaxy）、角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）、电输运测量、密度泛函理论（DFT）与嵌入式动力学平均场理论（embedded dynamical mean-field theory），追踪Te退火逐步移除过量间隙Fe过程中薄膜的电子重构，并采用高分辨偏振依赖激光ARPES测量拓扑表面态。

Result: 演化过程中伴随准粒子相干性恢复、电子关联减弱、Lifshitz转变与拓扑相变（topological phase transition），形成以dxy为主的空穴与电子口袋；费米能级下约2 meV处存在浅的dxz/dyz派生空穴带及M点两电子口袋间散射等潜在配对通道；激光ARPES显示拓扑表面态的圆二色性与化学计量比FeTe预期的轨道角动量织构一致。

Conclusion: 结果确立了超导FeTe的本征低能电子结构并识别出与超导最相关的电子态；本征超导与拓扑表面态的共存使化学计量比FeTe成为探索拓扑超导及可能马约拉纳束缚态（Majorana bound states）的候选平台。

Abstract: The recent discovery of intrinsic superconductivity in stoichiometric FeTe films has renewed interest in the Te-rich end member of the iron chalcogenides for studies of unconventional and topological superconductivity, yet its intrinsic electronic structure remains unresolved. In this work, we combine molecular beam epitaxy, angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES), electrical transport measurements, density functional theory, and embedded dynamical mean-field theory to track the electronic reconstruction of 20-unit-cell FeTe films as Te annealing progressively removes excess interstitial Fe and drives the system from an antiferromagnetic metal to a superconductor. We find that this evolution is accompanied by recovered quasiparticle coherence, reduced electronic correlations, a Lifshitz transition, and a topological phase transition, yielding dxy-dominated hole and electron pockets that favor inter-pocket scattering. In addition, a shallow dxz/dyz-derived hole band located about 2 meV below the Fermi level may provide an incipient-band pairing channel, while scattering between the two electron pockets at M may offer additional pairing channels. High-resolution polarization-dependent laser ARPES measurements further reveal a topological surface state whose circular dichroism is consistent with the expected orbital-angular-momentum texture of stoichiometric FeTe. These results establish the intrinsic low-energy electronic structure of superconducting FeTe and identify the electronic states most relevant to superconductivity. The coexistence of intrinsic superconductivity and a topological surface state establishes stoichiometric FeTe as a promising platform for exploring topological superconductivity and possible Majorana bound states.

</details>


<div id='hep-th'></div>

# hep-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [291] [Microcanonical Operator Spectrum, BPS Chaos and Free Probability Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22422)
*Alexandre Belin,Yichao Fu,Federico La Rocca*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文研究简单算子（simple operators）投影至微正则能量窗口后所得特征谱的统计性质。在假设简单算子本征基相对哈密顿量本征基为 Haar 随机取向的条件下，证明该谱服从高斯随机矩阵统计，并讨论其对 BPS 混沌与本征态热化的意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 简单算子的矩阵元是混沌量子多体系统热化的核心，其各阶矩通常由本征态热化假说（Eigenstate Thermalization Hypothesis）统计描述。本文旨在给出更精细的刻画：投影到微正则能量窗口后简单算子的本征谱，该谱同时编码矩阵元的全部矩，且在哈密顿量谱存在简并时尤为relevant。

Method: 假设简单算子的本征基相对于哈密顿量本征基为 Haar 随机取向，由此推导该随机取向所诱导的概率分布，并借助自由概率论（Free Probability Theory）工具进行分析。

Result: 证明投影到小微正则窗口的算子谱服从高斯随机矩阵统计（Gaussian random matrix statistics）。

Conclusion: 论文讨论了上述结果对 BPS 混沌（BPS chaos）以及更一般的本征态热化（eigenstate thermalization）的启示。

Abstract: Matrix elements of simple operators are central to thermalization in chaotic quantum many-body systems. Their moments are often described statistically through the Eigenstate Thermalization Hypothesis. In this paper, we study a more refined characterization of these matrix elements: the eigenspectrum of simple operators once projected to microcanonical energy windows. The spectrum encodes all moments of the matrix elements at once, and is particularly relevant when the spectrum of the Hamiltonian is degenerate. Assuming that the eigenbasis of the simple operator is Haar-randomly oriented with respect to that of the Hamiltonian, we prove that the spectrum of an operator projected to a small microcanonical window obeys Gaussian random matrix statistics. We do this directly by studying the probability distribution inherited from the random orientation, as well as using tools from Free Probability Theory. We discuss the implications for BPS chaos and more generally for eigenstate thermalization.

</details>


### [292] [Tripartite Form Universality in Holographic Entropy Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22504)
*Veronika E. Hubeny,Yu Liu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文研究全息熵不等式（holographic entropy inequalities）的"三分形式"（tripartite form）是否具有普适性，通过证明两类已知的无穷族不等式均可改写为该形式，给出了支持性证据并推导出若干有用恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全息熵不等式刻画了全息（holography）中几何态的纠缠结构，但超加性（subadditivity）之外的不等式含义尚不清晰。arXiv:2309.06296 提出的三分形式虽提供了紧凑表述，但其普适性（即是否所有不等式都能如此改写）此前并无先验保证。

Method: 作者提出猜想：所有全息熵不等式均可改写为三分形式，并通过显式构造 arXiv:2309.15145 中两类无穷族不等式的三分形式表达式来提供证据；同时详细说明推导过程，并导出若干有用的恒等式作为进一步重新表述的铺垫。

Result: 证明了 arXiv:2309.15145 中两类无穷族全息熵不等式的每个成员都可写成三分形式（由三分信息与条件三分信息项以单位系数构成），并给出其显式表达式，尽管对这两族而言改写过程尤为非平凡。

Conclusion: 三分形式对这两类无穷族不等式的适用性支持了其普适性猜想，所导出的恒等式有助于发展更多信息量的重新表述方式；同时通过证明任意全息熵不等式均满足的若干结构性质，展示了三分形式的效力。

Abstract: To elucidate the meaning of holographic entropy inequalities (beyond subadditivity) which characterize the entanglement structure of geometric states in holography, arXiv:2309.06296 proposed the "tripartite form" for these inequalities, consisting of tripartite information and conditional tripartite information terms with unit coefficients. While this provides a compact and useful packaging of the inequalities, it is not a priori guaranteed that all inequalities can be recast in this form. Here we conjecture that they can, and present substantial evidence, by proving that the two known infinite families of holographic entropy inequalities found in arXiv:2309.15145 can indeed be written in the tripartite form. This is significant because such recasting is particularly nontrivial for these families. Apart from providing the explicit tripartite form expressions for every member of these two infinite families, we detail how we arrived at them, in the process deriving several useful identities which may serve as stepping stones to formulate further repackaging of the corresponding information quantities. We also illustrate the power of the tripartite form by proving a number of structural properties satisfied by any holographic entropy inequality.

</details>


### [293] [External Sources in Pseudo-Quantum Electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22526)
*L. H. C. Borges,A. A. Nogueira,F. A. Barone*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在赝量子电动力学（Pseudo-Quantum Electrodynamics, PQED）框架下研究静止点状外源之间的相互作用，涵盖普通电荷、Dirac点及广义拓扑源，并揭示非局域性带来的新型各向异性扭矩效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探究PQED非局域特性是否能在点状外源相互作用中产生传统局域平面理论（ordinary planar Maxwell electrodynamics）所不具备的物理效应，并拓展对Dirac点与广义拓扑源相互作用的理解。

Method: 在PQED框架内考虑静止点状外源，包括普通电荷、Dirac点及广义拓扑源等构型，计算相应的相互作用能、力、扭矩与电磁场构型，并与普通平面麦克斯韦电动力学（planar Maxwell electrodynamics）的结果进行对比。

Result: PQED的非局域性（nonlocal character）产生传统局域平面理论所没有的物理效应；拓扑源诱导出内禀各向异性（anisotropic）且非中心（non-central）的相互作用，导致相互作用物体之间出现非平凡的扭矩（torques）。

Conclusion: 这些相互作用的方向性特征提示其可应用于平面凝聚态系统（planar condensed matter systems）中拓扑缺陷（topological defects）与各向异性现象（anisotropic phenomena）的有效描述。

Abstract: In this letter, we investigate the interaction between stationary point-like external sources within the framework of Pseudo-Quantum Electrodynamics (PQED). Besides ordinary electric charges, we consider configurations involving Dirac points and generalized topological sources. For the configurations analyzed, we compute the corresponding interaction energies, forces, torques, and electromagnetic field configurations. Furthermore, we compare the results obtained in PQED with those arising in ordinary planar Maxwell electrodynamics, showing that the nonlocal character of PQED gives rise to physical effects with no counterpart in the conventional local planar theory. In particular, our results show that topological sources induce intrinsically anisotropic and non-central interactions, leading to the emergence of nontrivial torques between the interacting objects. The directional character of these interactions suggests possible applications in the effective description of topological defects and anisotropic phenomena in planar condensed matter systems.

</details>


### [294] [Phase Transitions at More Unusual Values of Theta](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22613)
*Dhruv Aldas,Hitoshi Murayama,Bea Noether,Digvijay Roy Varier*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文在真空结构可精确求解的一类 QCD 类似理论中计算真空能量的 $\\theta$ 依赖，采用更简洁的 $\\mathcal{N}=1$ 方法复现既有结果，并发现相变可出现在 $\\theta/\\pi$ 的有理分数值与无理数值处。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QCD 的 $\\theta$ 依赖（$\\theta$ dependence）因真空结构复杂而难以精确分析，作者借助一类可精确求解的 QCD 类似理论（cousins of QCD）来研究该问题，并希望摆脱以往依赖完整 $\\mathcal{N}=2$ 计算框架的限制。

Method: 研究对象为 $\\mathcal{N}=1$ 有效场论（effective field theory, EFT），其来源是含 $N_f$ 个基本超多重态（fundamental hypermultiplets）的 $\\mathcal{N}=2$ $SU(N_c)$ 规范理论，随后经反常介导超对称破缺（anomaly mediated supersymmetry breaking, AMSB）形变。作者使用 $\\mathcal{N}=1$ 方法而非完整 $\\mathcal{N}=2$ 计算机制来求解。

Result: 新方法复现了此前使用完整 $\\mathcal{N}=2$ 机制得到的结果；同时可推广到完整 $\\mathcal{N}=2$ 方法难以处理的无穷多理论。研究确定了理论发生相变（phase transition）的 $\\theta$ 取值，发现相变可发生在 $\\theta/\\pi$ 的有理分数值与无理数值处。

Conclusion: $\\mathcal{N}=1$ 方法为分析 $\\theta$ 依赖与相变提供了更简便且适用范围更广的途径，并揭示相变位置在 $\\theta/\\pi$ 上并不限于有理分数值，亦包含无理数值。

Abstract: We calculate the $θ$ dependence of the vacuum energy in an infinite family of cousins of QCD, where the vacuum structure can be analyzed exactly. These theories are $\mathcal{N}=1$ EFTs that descend from $\mathcal{N}=2$ $SU(N_c)$ gauge theory with $N_f$ flavors of fundamental hypermultiplets, that are then deformed by anomaly mediated supersymmetry breaking (AMSB). We reproduce results found in previous work that used the full $\mathcal{N}=2$ machinery but via a much simpler $\mathcal{N}=1$ method. Our method easily extends to infinitely many more theories for which the full $\mathcal{N}=2$ approach becomes intractable. We identify values of $θ$ for which the theory undergoes a phase transition, and find that they can occur at both fractional and irrational values of $θ/π$.

</details>


### [295] [Semiclassical reconstruction of the Toda pole lattice via analytic continuation of the Probabilistic Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22717)
*Jacques Distler,Bahaa Elshimy*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将 Toda 场论（Toda field theory）的关联函数（correlators）重构为相对于高斯测度（Gaussian measure）的随机变量 w-矩，从而给出 Coulomb 气体表示的概率论构造，并导出对 Toda 动量与耦合常数的约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为 Toda 关联函数及 Seiberg 界（Seiberg bounds）提供系统的概率论解释，并刻画其收敛区域与可积性边界。

Method: 将原初场的 n 点函数重新解释为随机变量的 w-矩；推导 L²、L^p 收敛条件、平凡性约束与交叉矩的局部可积条件；再借助 Mellin-Barnes 表示对矩指标作解析延拓。

Result: 所得约束定义了 Toda 关联函数的扩展收敛区域；局部收敛边界成为亚纯的局部可积除子（divisors），在半经典 b→0 极限下重构 Liouville 电荷极点格（charge pole lattice）；对 A_n Toda 理论，W_n-闭合条件选出秩一 Virasoro 型子模，其上标量可积条件退化为 Liouville 型除子并复原相应电荷极点格。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了 Toda 场论关联函数的概率论刻画，统一了 Seiberg 界与收敛/可积性结构，并揭示半经典极限下 Liouville 型子模的出现。

Abstract: We develop a probabilistic construction of Toda field theory correlators from the Coulomb gas representation. Reinterpreting $n$-point functions of primaries as $w$-moments of random variables with respect to a Gaussian measure, we derive constraints on the Toda momenta and coupling constant. $L^2$ and $L^p$ convergence conditions for the associated random variables, triviality constraints on the correlators, and local integrability conditions for the cross-moments are derived. These constraints define an extended region of convergence for the Toda correlators and provide a systematic, probabilistic interpretation of the Seiberg bounds. We proceed to analytically continue the moment indices by employing a Mellin-Barnes representation of the random variables. The local convergence boundaries become meromorphic local integrability divisors, and, in the semiclassical $b\to0$ limit, these divisors reconstruct the Liouville charge pole lattice. For $A_n$ Toda theory, we show that natural $W_n$-closure conditions in this semiclassical limit select rank-one, Virasoro-like submodules inside the full Toda theory. On these slices, the scalar integrability conditions reduce to Liouville-like divisors and recover the corresponding Toda charge pole lattices.

</details>


### [296] [Toroidal Charged AdS Branes and Deformed Toda Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23079)
*Sangheon Yun*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 论文证明带电 p-膜（p-brane）在 AdS 中的运动方程可约化为耦合一维 Toda 型方程，并据此构造具有 T^n 对称性的静态环面黑膜（black brane）。纯 Maxwell 情形给出精确 sinh 解，其膨胀子（dilaton）推广含跑动环面模量与 $O(a^2)$ 反作用；热力学量满足第一定律，但膨胀子源将 Stefan-Boltzmann 指数修正为 $p_{\rm eff}=p+a^2/2$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于利用可积结构（Toda 型方程）来系统构造高维 AdS 中的静态环面黑膜，并考察膨胀子耦合对解与全息热力学（holographic thermodynamics）的修正，特别是对偶场论中标度行为的偏离。

Method: 将 $(n+p+2)$ 维 AdS 中磁荷 p-膜运动方程约化为耦合一维 Toda 型方程，并构造 $T^n$ 对称、含 p 个平移不变方向的静态环面黑膜；对膨胀子耦合 $e^{a\phi}$ 情形做微扰展开，将线性化系统化为两个 Pöschl-Teller 方程，其一为初等方程、另一由非整数阶连带 Legendre 函数求解，并在弱耦合 $\epsilon=a^2$ 下显式构造 $O(a^2)$ 反作用。

Result: 纯 Maxwell 情形获得精确 $\sinh$ 解，即 $(p+2)$ 维黑膜与 n-环面的乘积，渐近为 $\mathrm{AdS}_{p+2}\times T^n$；其膨胀子类比具跑动环面模量和非平凡膨胀子，一般解非初等。由表面引力（surface gravity）、视界面积与全息重整化（holographic renormalization）得到 Hawking 温度、熵密度与质量密度，验证第一定律 $d\mathcal{M}=T_H\,ds$ 及对偶 $(p+1)$ 维理论的无迹共形应力张量。在 $O(a^2)$ 阶第一定律仍成立，但膨胀子源将 Stefan-Boltzmann 指数移至 $p_{\rm eff}=p+a^2/2$，破坏 $s\propto T_H^p$ 的干净幂律，同时可归一化膨胀子毛（dilaton hair）与熵解耦。

Conclusion: Toda 型约化为构造高维 AdS 环面黑膜提供了有效途径，且精确解与其膨胀子推广均可解析处理。膨胀子耦合在全息热力学上引入对数式修正，修正了对偶理论的有效维数指数，而膨胀子毛不贡献熵；第一定律在微扰阶的持续成立支持该解的全息解释。

Abstract: We show that the equations of motion for magnetically charged $p$-branes in $(n+p+2)$-dimensional AdS reduce to coupled one-dimensional Toda-type equations, and use this structure to construct static toroidal black branes with $T^n$ symmetry and $p$ translation-invariant directions. In the pure-Maxwell case we obtain an exact $\sinh$ solution--a $(p+2)$-dimensional black brane times an $n$-torus, asymptotically $\mathrm{AdS}_{p+2}\times T^n$--whose dilatonic analogue, with a scalar coupled through $e^{aφ}$, has a running torus modulus and a non-trivial dilaton. A perturbative expansion reduces the linearized system to a pair of Pöschl-Teller equations, one elementary and one solved by associated Legendre functions of non-integer degree, so that the general solution is non-elementary; at weak coupling $ε=a^2$ we construct the $O(a^2)$ backreaction explicitly. From the surface gravity, horizon area, and holographic renormalization we obtain the Hawking temperature, entropy density, and mass density, verifying the first law $d\mathcal{M}=T_H\,ds$ and the traceless conformal stress tensor of the dual $(p+1)$-dimensional theory. At $O(a^2)$ the first law persists, but the dilaton source shifts the Stefan-Boltzmann exponent to $p_{\rm eff}=p+a^2/2$, breaking the clean power law $s\propto T_H^p$ while the normalizable dilaton hair decouples from the entropy.

</details>


### [297] [Homogeneous Non Symmetric Special Kähler Geometries as Broken Isometry Metrics on Symmetric CV {K}ähler Manifolds:a new tool for $r=2$ CaNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23395)
*Pietro Fré,Mario Trigiante,Antoine Van Proeyen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 论文完整证明可解群 $\mathcal{S}_{2,2+p}$（度量等价于 Calabi-Vesentini 对称空间）与支撑 L$(-1,p)$ 全纯 Kähler 度量的可解群 $\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{L}(-1,p)}$ 之间的同构，并将 L$(-1,p)$ 刻画为陪集流形 $\mathrm{Iso}_{\mathrm{L}(-1,p)}/\mathrm{H}$，进而提出其在 Cartan 神经网络中的潜在应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于厘清 Calabi-Vesentini 对称空间与齐性非对称 special manifold L$(-1,p)$ 之间在可解群层面的精确等价关系，并从 Alekseevskyan 视角比较同一可解李代数上的两种二次型。

Method: 方法上，通过给出并比较同一可解李代数（solvable Lie algebra）上的两个不同二次型（quadratic forms），证明两可解群的同构，并考察 L$(-1,p)$ 的全等距群 $\mathrm{Iso}_{\mathrm{L}(-1,p)}$，分析其作为 $\mathrm{SO}(2,2+p)$ 的非半单子群结构，直接构造其在 CV 流形可解坐标上的作用。

Result: 结果表明，$\mathrm{Iso}_{\mathrm{L}(-1,p)}\subset \mathrm{SO}(2,2+p)$ 是简单等距群的非半单子群；L$(-1,p)$ 可表示为 $\mathrm{Iso}_{\mathrm{L}(-1,p)}/\mathrm{H}$，其中 $\mathrm{H}=\mathrm{U(1)}_L\times \mathrm{SO(p)}$，且 $\mathrm{U(1)}_L$ 的生成元位于 $\so(2,2+p)$ 中但不属于规范的 $\so(2)\oplus\so(2+p)$ 子代数。

Conclusion: 该同构识别为 Cartan 神经网络（Cartan Neural Networks）提供了工具，可在基于 CV Tits Satake 普适类的 $r=2$ 神经网络逐层映射中引入额外非线性变换，预期能提升其表达能力（expressivity）。

Abstract: In this paper we prove in full detail the isomorphism between the solvable group $\mathcal{S}_{2,2+p}$, metric equivalent to the Calabi Vesentini symmetric space $\mathrm{SO(2,2+p)/SO(2)\times SO(2+p)}$, and the solvable group $\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{L}(-1,p)}$ supporting the Kähler metric of the homogeneous non symmetric special manifold L$(-1,p)$. From an Alekseevskyan point of view the two spaces simply correspond to two different quadratic forms on the same solvable Lie algebra that we present and compare in detail. Furthermore considering the full group of isometries $\mathrm{Iso}_{\mathrm{L}(-1,p)}$ of L$(-1,p)$ we show that $\mathrm{Iso_{L(-1,p)}}\subset \mathrm{SO}(2,2+p)$ is a non-semisimple subgroup of the simple isometry group of Calabi-Vesentini manifolds. Altogether the Special Kähler manifold L$(-1,p)$ can be seen as a coset manifold $\mathrm{Iso_{L(-1,p)}}/\mathrm{H}$ where $\mathrm{H}=\mathrm{U(1)}_L\times \mathrm{SO(p)}$, the generator of $\mathrm{U(1)}_L$ being in $\so(2,2+p)$, yet not in the canonical $\so(2)\oplus\so(2+p)$ subalgebra. The action of $\mathrm{Iso}_{\mathrm{L}(-1,p)}$ on the solvable coordinates of the CV manifold can be constructed directly. This identification provides a valuable tool for Cartan Neural Networks, introducing additional non linear transformations in every map from one layer to the next one of an $r=2$ Neural Network based on the CV Tits Satake universality class; in perspective, this new tool increases expressivity.

</details>


### [298] [Spin density from second order transport in fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23399)
*Domingo Gallegos,Roi Klein,Amos Yarom*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究含自旋自由度（spin degrees of freedom）的带电流体（charged fluid）的流体力学（hydrodynamics），指出在3+1维共形场论（conformal field theory）中自旋密度（spin density）与标准二阶输运系数（second-order transport coefficients）的线性组合及规范与混合规范-引力反常系数（gauge- and mixed gauge-gravitational anomaly coefficients）的二次项相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在厘清带自旋带电流体的流体力学描述中自旋密度的理论来源，特别是其与输运系数及反常效应的关系。

Method: 在3+1维共形场论框架下进行理论论证，推导自旋密度与二阶输运系数线性组合及反常系数二次项的联系；并在若干自由理论（free theories）与全息理论（holographic theories）中计算自旋密度。

Result: 自旋密度可表示为标准二阶输运系数的线性组合，加上一个与规范及混合规范-引力反常系数二次相关的附加项；该结果在多种自由与全息理论的计算中得到体现并加以比较。

Conclusion: 含自旋带电流体的流体力学中，自旋密度受输运系数与反常效应的共同支配，不同自由与全息理论的计算对比为相关理论结构提供了检验依据。

Abstract: We study the hydrodynamics of a charged fluid with spin degrees of freedom. We argue that, in a conformal field theory in 3+1 dimensions, the spin density is related to a linear combination of standard second-order transport coefficients, and an additional term quadratic in the gauge- and mixed gauge-gravitational anomaly coefficients. We compute the spin density in several free and holographic theories and compare the results.

</details>


### [299] [Gauge Origami of the Conifold $\times$ $\mathbb{C}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23556)
*Taro Kimura,Go Noshita*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文在环面 Calabi–Yau 四维流形 $\mathcal{C}\times\mathbb{C}$ 上，从其胚砖模型（brane brick model）构造规范折纸（gauge origami）系统，并用 Jeffrey–Kirwan 留数定义配分函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是在一般环面 Calabi–Yau 四维流形上建立规范折纸（gauge origami）构造，本文选取 $\mathcal{C}\times\mathbb{C}$ 作为迈向该目标的第一步。

Method: 从胚砖模型出发，以 brick matching 确定基本 D6-胚（D6-brane）框架、以相边界（phase boundary）确定 D4-胚框架；用 Jeffrey–Kirwan 留数定义配分函数，并以有色 BPS 晶体（colored BPS crystal）描述不动点。

Result: 在统一的框架化箭图量子力学（framed quiver quantum mechanics）中实现了 magnificent-four、tetrahedron-instanton 与 spiked-instanton 三个扇区；通过其共有的分数 D0-胚箭图（fractional D0-brane quiver）组合这些基本扇区，可得到一般规范折纸构型。

Conclusion: 该构造为在一般环面 Calabi–Yau 四维流形上研究规范折纸提供了第一步。

Abstract: We construct gauge-origami systems on the toric Calabi--Yau fourfold $\mathcal{C}\times\mathbb{C}$ from its brane brick model. Brick matchings and phase boundaries determine elementary D6- and D4-brane framings, respectively, realizing the magnificent-four, tetrahedron-instanton, and spiked-instanton sectors in a unified framed quiver quantum mechanics. We define their partition functions by Jeffrey--Kirwan residues and describe the fixed points in terms of colored BPS crystals. General gauge-origami configurations are obtained by combining these elementary sectors through their common fractional D0-brane quiver. This construction provides a first step toward gauge origami on general toric Calabi--Yau fourfolds.

</details>


### [300] [Where's Kasner? OPE Signature of Kasner Exponents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23776)
*Nejc Čeplak,Samuel Valach*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文分析全息热关联函数（holographic thermal correlation functions）的解析结构，针对在径向坐标逆幂次下有限阶截断的 black brane 几何，建立反弹奇点（bouncing singularities）幂律指数与黑洞奇点附近时间方向 Kasner 指数（Kasner exponent）之间的直接关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大 $N$ 极限下，全息热关联函数的复时间奇点（complex-time singularities）可对应探测黑洞内部并反弹于奇点的测地线（geodesics），从而携带黑洞近奇点区域的信息。本文旨在刻画全息 OPE 数据（holographic OPE data）的渐近行为，并厘清其与奇点附近几何的定量联系。

Method: 选取 blackening factor 在径向坐标逆幂次下有限阶截断的 black brane 几何，分析其态中标量两点函数（scalar two-point functions）所涉及的全息 OPE 数据的渐近行为，并据此推导反弹奇点幂律指数与时间方向 Kasner 指数的关系。

Result: 对该类几何，发现反弹奇点幂律指数与黑洞奇点附近时间方向的 Kasner 指数之间存在直接关系；文中进一步讨论了该关系的适用范围（regime of validity）。

Conclusion: 全息 OPE 数据的渐近行为可作为探针，将复时间奇点的幂律特征与近奇点几何的 Kasner 指数相关联；同时讨论了向横向空间方向（transverse spatial directions）推广的可能性。

Abstract: At large $N$, the analytic structure of holographic thermal correlation functions contains non-trivial information about the near-singularity region of dual black holes. In particular, complex-time singularities can be related to geodesics that probe the black hole interior and bounce off the singularity. We analyse the asymptotic behaviour of holographic OPE data entering scalar two-point functions in states dual to black branes whose blackening factors truncate at finite order in inverse powers of the radial coordinate. For this class of geometries, we find a direct relation between the power-law exponent of these bouncing singularities and the Kasner exponent associated with the time direction near the black hole singularity. We discuss the regime of validity of this relation and comment on possible extensions to the transverse spatial directions.

</details>


### [301] [Projective geometry of second-order supersymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23938)
*Stevan Cebrian,Mikhail S. Plyushchay*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文指出，量子力学中无反常的二阶超对称（second-order supersymmetry, SUSY）可用射影几何（projective geometry）表述：由射影联络（projective connection）与保持它的向量场刻画。固定物理坐标与能量原点后，这些数据决定配对哈密顿量与超荷，并给出超势零点处算符光滑延拓的判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为无反常二阶超对称提供统一的几何框架，澄清超势（superpotential）零点处量子算符的延拓条件，并揭示超对称辅助几何与相关代数结构之间的内在联系。

Method: 采用射影几何方法，引入射影联络与保持该联络的向量场；利用场的二次不变量确定中心参数与候选单态能量；通过经典虚构的相似变换（similarity transformation）选取仿射数据，并由黎曼模型（Riemannian model）的维度约化与适配坐标实现耦合常数变形（coupling-constant metamorphosis）。

Result: 场的定点结构给出算符光滑延拓的显式判据；协变因子生成 Schwarzian 量子修正；维度约化通过体积测度再现仿射数据。单能隙 Lamé 对（one-gap Lamé pair）示例说明了 SUSY 辅助几何与 Gelfand-Dikii-Virasoro 结构的关系；其 Lax-Novikov 积分通过射影稳定子（projective stabilizer）的一阶作用作用于薛定谔解，并在无反射极限下于物理坐标中正比于辅助的三阶 Bol 算符（third Bol operator）。

Conclusion: 无反常二阶超对称可纳入射影几何框架，射影数据完整编码配对哈密顿量、超荷与相关代数结构，并将超对称辅助几何与 Gelfand-Dikii-Virasoro 及 Bol 算符结构联系起来。

Abstract: We show that anomaly-free second-order supersymmetry (SUSY) in quantum mechanics admits a projective-geometric formulation in terms of a projective connection and a vector field preserving it. Once the physical coordinate and energy origin are fixed, these data determine the partner Hamiltonians and supercharges; the field's quadratic invariant fixes the central parameter and candidate singlet energies. The fixed-point structure of this field yields explicit criteria for smooth continuation of the quantum operators through superpotential zeros. The classically fictitious similarity transformation selects affine data whose covariant factors generate the Schwarzian quantum correction. Dimensional reduction of a Riemannian model reproduces these affine data through the volume measure, while a coordinate adapted to the selected field implements coupling-constant metamorphosis. A one-gap Lame pair illustrates the relation between the SUSY auxiliary geometry and the Gelfand-Dikii-Virasoro structure governing physical solution products. Its Lax-Novikov integral acts on Schrodinger solutions through the first-order action of a projective stabilizer; in the reflectionless limit it becomes proportional, in the physical coordinate, to the auxiliary third Bol operator.

</details>


### [302] [Cassini Ansatz in the Skyrme Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23991)
*N. V. Gerasimeniuk,A. A. Korneev,A. V. Molochkov,O. G. Tkachev*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文研究 Skyrme 模型中球对称解的拉伸问题，采用三维 Cassini 卵形线的推广作为延展试探解（ansatz），发现静态能量随纵向形变参数线性增长，斜率约为 0.14 GeV²，与多种禁闭模型中的弦张力一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球对称解在形变下的行为是理解 Skyrme 模型非微扰结构的重要切入点，同时其能量-形变关系可能与禁闭（confinement）机制中的弦张力（string tension）存在关联。

Method: 将三维推广的 Cassini 卵形线（Cassini ovals）作为延展试探解，用于描述球对称解的拉伸，并计算静态能量对纵向形变参数的依赖关系。

Result: 静态能量（static energy）随纵向形变参数呈线性依赖，斜率 σ ≈ 0.14 GeV²，该数值与多种禁闭模型给出的弦张力吻合良好。

Conclusion: 结果表明 Skyrme 模型中的拉伸解可自然给出与禁闭弦张力相符的能量斜率，暗示该模型与禁闭现象之间存在潜在联系。

Abstract: We investigate the stretching of a spherically symmetric solution in the Skyrme model. The three-dimensional generalization of the Cassini ovals is considered as ansatz of extension. The linear dependence of static energy on the longitudinal deformation parameter is obtained with a slope $σ\approx 0.14\ \mathrm{ GeV}^2$, which is in good agreement with the string tension known from various models of confinement.

</details>


### [303] [Critical Kasner scaling from Cauchy-horizon collapse near holographic phase transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24227)
*Ling-Long Gao,Yan Liu,Hong-Da Lyu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该研究揭示，非极端柯西视界（Cauchy horizon）上标量毛（scalar hair）分岔的对数分支系数决定爱因斯坦-罗森桥（Einstein-Rosen bridge）的坍缩速率，并给出临界指数与振幅的定量预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有对黑洞标量毛分岔的研究多聚焦外部时空形变，而外部形变在临界点消失，内部结构（柯西视界、桥坍缩）如何受临界模控制尚不清晰。本文旨在建立零模（zero mode）与黑洞内部晚期几何之间的定量联系。

Method: 采用解析的零模匹配（zero-mode matching）：分析标量扰动在参考柯西视界处的对数分支系数，结合局部柯西视界数据确定桥坍缩指数速率；并以旋转 BTZ 到带毛旋转黑洞的转变为例说明机制，再用标量化 RN-AdS₄ 黑膜（black brane）的全非线性数值解检验 Kasner 标度。

Result: 零模匹配可在不拟合非线性内部的情况下同时确定临界指数与其振幅；若坍缩后几何进入 Kasner 区，则 1−p_t ∝ |λ−λ_c|^{2β}。标量化 RN-AdS₄ 黑膜的数值解定量验证了该 Kasner 标度；低温下两个具相同线性临界数据的带电模型却分别收敛到幂律与反对数 Kasner 标度。

Conclusion: 有限温度分岔附近的内部几何由零模透射（zero-mode transmission）支配，而低温标度由非线性红外完成（infrared completion）决定，说明内部命运并非仅由线性临界数据确定。

Abstract: We study continuous scalar-hair bifurcations from stationary black holes with a nonextremal Cauchy horizon. Although the exterior deformation vanishes at criticality, the critical zero mode generically develops a logarithmic branch at the reference Cauchy horizon, producing a nonuniform critical limit. We show that the coefficient of this logarithm in the scalar perturbation, together with local Cauchy-horizon data, determines the exponential collapse rate of the Einstein-Rosen bridge. If the post-collapse geometry evolves into a Kasner regime, the boundary order-parameter scaling $O\propto|λ-λ_c|^β$ further implies $
  1-p_t\propto|λ-λ_c|^{2β}. $ Crucially, zero-mode matching fixes both the critical exponents and the associated amplitudes, without any fit to the nonlinear interior. We illustrate this mechanism in the transition from rotating BTZ to hairy rotating black holes. For scalarized RN-AdS$_4$ black branes, we quantitatively verify the predicted Kasner scaling using fully nonlinear numerical solutions. At low temperature, two charged models with identical linear critical data instead approach different infrared endpoints, yielding respectively power-law and inverse-logarithmic Kasner scaling. Thus interiors near a finite-temperature bifurcation are controlled by zero-mode transmission, whereas low temperature scaling is governed by the nonlinear infrared completion.

</details>


### [304] [Saddle-Order Universality in Ensemble-Averaged Black Hole Thermodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24344)
*Ankit Anand,Peng Cheng*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文在约化欧几里得黑洞系综中为系综平均广义自由能建立了鞍点阶命题，指出主导鞍点附近首个稳定项的阶数决定涨落贡献；普通临界点由四次软模支配，给出四分之一温度而非高斯半温度结果，并在四维RN-AdS与Kerr-AdS黑洞中解析验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有黑洞系综涨落分析多依赖高斯（Gaussian）近似，预期半温度贡献；但在临界点附近软模（soft mode）行为可能偏离高斯图像，需要一个统一的鞍点阶判据来刻画约化系综中的涨落结构。

Method: 在正则性与稳定性假设下，建立约化欧几里得黑洞系综中系综平均广义自由能的鞍点阶命题（saddle-order proposition），以主导鞍点周围首个稳定项的阶数确定领头涨落贡献，并将其推广至具有加权齐次领头势的耦合集体模。

Result: 普通临界点由四次软模支配，产生四分之一温度贡献而非半温度高斯结果；四维RN-AdS与Kerr-AdS黑洞解析验证该命题，其热力学控制量可映射至共同四次范式（quartic normal form），其标度函数沿零场单阱方向连接临界区与高斯区。

Conclusion: 该命题通过黑洞临界性为约化系综涨落提供了统一描述，将临界与高斯区域纳入同一四次范式框架。

Abstract: We establish a saddle-order proposition for the ensemble-averaged generalized free energy in reduced Euclidean black hole ensembles. Under regularity and stability assumptions, the leading fluctuation contribution is determined by the order of the first stabilizing term around the dominant saddle. An ordinary critical point is governed by a quartic soft mode, giving a quarter-temperature contribution instead of the familiar half-temperature Gaussian result. We verify this analytically for four-dimensional RN-AdS and Kerr-AdS black holes and map their thermodynamic controls to a common quartic normal form, whose scaling function connects the critical and Gaussian regimes along the zero-field one-well direction. The proposition also extends to coupled collective modes with weighted-homogeneous leading potentials. These results provide a unified description of reduced-ensemble fluctuations through black hole criticality.

</details>


### [305] [Miwa deformation of WLZZ matrix models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24360)
*T. Diudin,A. Popolitov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一种普适方法，用于为 WLZZ 矩阵模型（matrix models）正分支定义多元正交多项式（multivariate orthogonal polynomials），并借此将严格超可积性（strict superintegrability）与 Miwa 形变公式（Miwa deformation formulas）从已有文献中的高斯情形（Gaussian case）推广至更一般情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有文献仅在高斯情形下处理多元正交多项式，其严格超可积性与 Miwa 形变公式的适用范围受限。本文旨在突破该限制，为 WLZZ 矩阵模型正分支建立普适的多项式定义框架。

Method: 提出一种普适方式（universal way）来定义 WLZZ 矩阵模型正分支的多元正交多项式，以此作为推广的构造基础。

Result: 该定义方式使得严格超可积性与 Miwa 形变公式得以从高斯情形推广到 WLZZ 矩阵模型正分支的更一般设定中。

Conclusion: 多元正交多项式的普适定义为 WLZZ 矩阵模型正分支提供了统一框架，拓展了严格超可积性与 Miwa 形变公式的适用范围，超越此前仅限高斯情形的结果。

Abstract: In this paper we propose a universal way to define multivariate orthogonal polynomials for the positive branch of WLZZ matrix models. This allows us to generalize from the Gaussian case, previously considered in the literature, the strict superintegrability property and the Miwa deformation formulas.

</details>


### [306] [Spin-curvature effects in slowly rotating black hole spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24364)
*Arpita Jana,Subhajit Mazumdar,Sunandan Gangopadhyay*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 研究慢速旋转黑洞时空中大质量自旋-1/2量子粒子的自旋-曲率相互作用，采用WKB近似（WKB approximation）与Gordon分解（Gordon decomposition）计算量子力，发现旋转参数（rotation parameter $a$）引入随径向距离衰减更快的修正项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在平直Minkowski时空中不存在自旋-曲率耦合，而弯曲时空因自旋联络（spin connection）使Dirac方程出现非平庸结构；该耦合产生的力使粒子偏离测地线（geodesic），需在旋转黑洞背景下量化其效应。

Method: 先在慢速旋转黑洞背景中计算粒子随动系（co-moving frame）的标架（tetrads），再基于WKB近似与Dirac概率四流（probability four current）的Gordon分解，求解自旋-曲率相互作用对应的相对论量子力分量，计算精确到旋转参数（$a$）的线性阶。

Result: 旋转参数改变力的量级，通过引入一个附加项实现；该附加项随径向距离的衰减比静态球对称Schwarzschild黑洞时空中的力量级更快。

Conclusion: 在慢速旋转黑洞时空中，自旋-曲率耦合导致的量子力相对Schwarzschild情形受到旋转参数修正，且其径向衰减更迅速，表明黑洞转动对自旋粒子的偏离测地线行为具有可辨识的修正效应。

Abstract: We investigate the spin-curvature interaction for a massive spin-1/2 quantum particle in a slowly rotating black hole spacetime. In contrast to the flat Minkowski space, the curved spacetime introduces non-trivial spin connections into the Dirac equation due to the direct curvature-spin interaction. The force arising from this non-trivial spin-curvature coupling makes the particle deviate from its geodesics. We first calculate the tetrads in the co-moving frame of the particle in the slowly rotating black hole background. We then compute the components of the relativistic quantum force, arising from the spin-curvature interaction, using these co-moving tetrads up to linear order in the rotation parameter ($a$) of the black hole. The procedure of computing the force is based on the WKB approximation and the Gordon decomposition method for the Dirac probability four current. Our results show that the rotation parameter modifies the magnitude of the force by introducing a term that decays more rapidly with radial distance than the force magnitude in a static spherically symmetric Schwarzschild black hole spacetime.

</details>


### [307] [T-duality of string charge density/bit threads correspondence in type II supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24366)
*Houwen Wu,Shuxuan Ying*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在十维 Type II 超引力（supergravity）框架下检验弦电荷密度/比特线程（string charge density/bit threads）对应关系在阿贝尔 T-对偶（Abelian T-duality）下的行为，发现尽管局部几何量改变，对偶不变量（如最大流量）仍复现黑洞熵（black hole entropy）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比特线程对应关系此前主要建立于特定背景的弦电流上，其在对偶变换下的稳健性尚待验证。本文旨在通过阿贝尔 T-对偶检验该对应的非平凡性，并将其适用范围从弦电流拓展到卷绕膜（wrapped-brane）电流。

Method: 在十维 Type II 超引力中沿共同紧致圆（compact circle）实施 T-对偶：将 Type IIB 的 F1-NS5-P 背景映射为 Type IIA 的 P-NS5-F1 背景（卷绕与动量电荷互换），并将 D1-D5-P 映射为 D0-D4-F1。对径向 D1 源被对偶圆卷绕后形成的 D2 源，将其空间双矢量与归一化闭一形式缩并，构造守恒的有效径向流。

Result: 径向 F1 源仍为 F1 源，其投影电荷密度给出的比特线程流最大流量复现黑洞熵；在 D1-D5-P 情形中，缩并所得守恒有效径向流的最大流量同样复现熵。虽然局部度规、膨胀子（dilaton）、源电流与流范数均改变，但膨胀子加权的横向面积密度（dilaton-weighted transverse area density）与积分后的最大流量保持不变。

Conclusion: 该结果为比特线程对应关系提供了非平凡的 T-对偶检验，并将该对应从弦电流推广至可约化为类弦流的卷绕膜电流。

Abstract: In this paper, we study Abelian T-duality of the string charge density/bit threads correspondence in ten-dimensional Type II supergravity. T-duality along the common compact circle maps the Type IIB F1-NS5-P background to a Type IIA P-NS5-F1 background with the winding and momentum charges exchanged. The radial F1 source remains an F1 source, and its projected charge density gives a bit-thread flow whose maximal flux reproduces the black hole entropy. The same duality maps D1-D5-P to D0-D4-F1. In this case, a radial D1 source becomes a D2 source wrapped on the dual circle. Contracting the resulting spatial bivector with the normalized closed one-form on that circle gives a conserved effective radial flow whose maximal flux again reproduces the entropy. Although the local metrics, dilatons, source currents, and flow norms change, the dilaton-weighted transverse area density and the integrated maximal flux are invariant. These results provide a nontrivial T-duality test of the correspondence and extend it from string currents to wrapped-brane currents that reduce to string-like flows.

</details>


### [308] [Dynamical Schwinger Production of Dipole--Antidipole Pairs in a Fractonic Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24415)
*Celio R. Muniz,R. N. Costa Filho*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究一维偶极守恒玻色-哈伯德链（dipole-conserving Bose–Hubbard chain）在周期性二次势驱动的含时二秩电场（rank-two electric field）下，从满填充莫特背景（Mott background）产生激发的过程，揭示了由精确偶极守恒主导的多光子动力学施温格产生（dynamical Schwinger production）的凝聚态类比机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有方案多针对已有的偶极或分形子激发（fractonic excitations）进行操作，而本文关注的是从初始莫特态出发、在未预先制备这些激发的条件下实现其产生。该问题为探索精确偶极守恒下的激发产生机制提供了新切入点。

Method: 采用精确对角化（exact diagonalization）与二阶微扰处理相结合的方法，分析单次关联跃迁对|030⟩紧凑构型（能量3U）及源自2U的低能流形（含空间可分辨的偶极-反偶极构型）的生成，并考察|1111⟩↔|0220⟩通道的自能与共振位移。

Result: 有限跃迁使低能流形变为色散能带，其有限链边缘显著低于紧凑的3U能标；但归一化奇次谐波谱权重被微扰抑制为(J/U)²，反映初末本征态的虚杂化。二阶处理预测的弱场共振位移与精确时间演化一致，长链动力学显示真正的偶极-反偶极分离及向高能多体扇区的再分布。

Conclusion: 该机制构成多光子动力学施温格产生在凝聚态体系中的类比：精确偶极守恒以可移动的反向偶极取代可移动的异号电荷，而孤立分形子电荷保持不可动。

Abstract: We investigate field-induced excitation production from the unit-filled Mott background of a one-dimensional dipole-conserving Bose--Hubbard chain driven by a periodic quadratic potential, realizing a time-dependent rank-two electric field. Unlike protocols that manipulate pre-existing dipolar or fractonic excitations, we address their production from an initially Mott-like state without prepared dipolar or fractonic excitations. A single correlated hop directly nucleates the compact $|030\rangle$ configuration at the atomic energy $3U$, while a lower manifold derived from $2U$ contains spatially resolved dipole--antidipole configurations. Exact diagonalization shows that finite hopping turns this lower manifold into a dispersive band whose finite-chain edge lies substantially below the compact $3U$ scale. Despite this lower energetic edge, its normalized odd-harmonic spectral weight is perturbatively suppressed as $(J/U)^2$, reflecting virtual hybridization of the initial and final eigenstates. A consistent second-order treatment of the $|1111\rangle\leftrightarrow|0220\rangle$ channel includes diagonal self-energy shifts and predicts a weak-field resonance displacement confirmed by exact time evolution. Extended-chain dynamics shows genuine dipole--antidipole separation together with redistribution into higher-energy many-body sectors. The resulting mechanism provides a condensed-matter analogue of multiphoton dynamical Schwinger production in which exact dipole conservation replaces mobile opposite charges by mobile opposite dipoles, while isolated fractonic charges remain immobile.

</details>


### [309] [Unistochastic reduction of multi-matrix coherent-state kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24483)
*Shannon Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文重新审视多矩阵BPS关联子中出现的有限-N相干态重叠积分，指出其可自然地表示为单随机集（unistochastic set）上Haar前推测度的Laplace变换，并在N=3时给出紧致的三维Bessel积分表示，对一般有限N给出低阶矩展开与采样方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多矩阵BPS关联子（multi-matrix BPS correlators）中有限-N相干态重叠积分是待解决的问题，其结构此前缺乏统一的概率论刻画，尤其是如何高效计算或近似该类积分尚不明确。

Method: 将积分 I_N(C)=∫_{U(N)}dμ_H(U) exp(∑_{ij}C_{ij}|U_{ij}|^2) 识别为单随机集上Haar前推测度（Haar-pushforward measure）的Laplace变换；利用行列随机性（row and column stochasticity）将源变量由N^2降至(N-1)^2；N=3时借助已知的单随机体（unistochastic body）上的Haar诱导测度构造三维Bessel积分表示；一般N情形下结合Weingarten calculus给出精确低阶矩展开，并提出直接的Haar前推采样（Haar-pushforward sampling）方案。

Result: 对交换相干态参数，I_N(C)被证明是单随机集上Haar前推测度的Laplace变换；源变量维数由N^2约化为(N-1)^2；N=3时获得紧致的三维Bessel积分表示；对一般有限N得到基于Weingarten calculus的精确低阶矩展开及可行的前推采样方案。

Conclusion: 该工作以Laplace变换与单随机体上的Haar前推测度为统一框架，重新表述有限-N相干态重叠积分，既给出N=3的紧凑解析表示，也为一般N提供矩展开与采样计算途径。

Abstract: We revisit the finite-$N$ coherent-state overlap integrals appearing in multi-matrix BPS correlators. For commuting coherent-state parameters, these integrals take the form $I_N(C)=\int_{U(N)}dμ_H(U)\, \exp\!\left(\sum_{ij}C_{ij}|U_{ij}|^2\right)$. We show that $I_N(C)$ is naturally the Laplace transform of the Haar-pushforward measure on the unistochastic set. Row and column stochasticity reduce the source variables from $N^2$ to $(N-1)^2$. For $N=3$, using the known Haar-induced measure on the unistochastic body, we obtain a compact three-dimensional Bessel-integral representation. For general finite $N$, the same formulation gives an exact low-order moment expansion via Weingarten calculus and a direct Haar-pushforward sampling program.

</details>


### [310] [Braneworld tidal charge through the classical double copy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24521)
*Juan C. La Cruz,Jesús A. Rodríguez*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究 Randall-Sundrum II 场景下膜上旋转黑洞几何的经典双拷贝（classical double copy），发现潮汐荷（tidal charge）在正则源中占主导，并与 Kerr-Newman 的电荷平方存在形式对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究额外维（extra dimension）在膜上诱导的有效四维几何，其经典双拷贝结构如何反映体（bulk）与膜（brane）的物理贡献，以及这些贡献在双拷贝框架下是否可区分。

Method: 利用 Kerr-Schild 表示（Kerr-Schild representation）于平直背景上构建该有效几何的单拷贝（single copy）与零拷贝（zeroth copy），并与 Kerr-Newman 几何进行对比分析；进一步在膜上引入电磁场，考察低能 Kerr-Schild 几何及其双拷贝的结构。

Result: 单拷贝与零拷贝的正则源（regular source）完全正比于潮汐荷，而质量相关贡献在正则区域为无源。潮汐荷与电磁荷平方在诱导几何及其双拷贝层面存在形式对应，尽管二者物理起源不同且潮汐荷可取正负号。当膜上包含电磁场时，两类贡献仅通过组合 $Q^2+\beta$ 进入低能几何及双拷贝。

Conclusion: 结果表明，物理上截然不同的膜与体贡献在有效几何的经典双拷贝中可以变得不可区分，揭示了双拷贝对应关系在有效描述中的等效性与潜在局限。

Abstract: We investigate the classical double copy of the rotating black-hole geometry induced on the brane in the Randall-Sundrum II scenario. The influence of the five-dimensional bulk is encoded in the effective four-dimensional geometry through a tidal charge, while the metric admits a Kerr-Schild representation over a flat background. We construct the corresponding single and zeroth copies and find that their regular sources are entirely proportional to the tidal charge, whereas the mass-dependent contribution is source-free in the regular region. Comparison with Kerr-Newman reveals a formal correspondence between the tidal charge and the squared electromagnetic charge at the level of the induced geometry and its double copies, despite their different physical origins and the fact that the tidal charge may take either sign. When an electromagnetic field is included on the brane, both contributions enter the low-energy Kerr-Schild geometry and its double copies only through the combination $Q^2+β$. These results illustrate how physically distinct brane and bulk contributions can become indistinguishable in the classical double copy of an effective geometry.

</details>


### [311] [On the (In)equivalence of de Sitter and Holographic Entanglement Entropies in Lovelock Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24557)
*Takamasa Kanai*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文研究高曲率引力（higher-curvature gravity）中德西特熵（de Sitter entropy）与全息纠缠熵（holographic entanglement entropy）的关系，并在膜世界（braneworld）实现中加以检验。结果表明：在 RS II 模型的静态渐近德西特膜世界时空中，两者一致；而在稳态时空中，由于外在曲率（extrinsic-curvature）贡献，两者一般不同。该结论在 Gauss-Bonnet 与一般 Lovelock 引力中均成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在厘清高曲率引力中引力熵与全息熵之间的关系，并借助膜世界实现给出更广泛的全息对应（holographic correspondence）视角。研究动机源于德西特熵与全息纠缠熵在更高阶曲率修正下是否仍然一致这一问题。

Method: 采用引力作用量的正则表述（canonical formulation）与欧几里得引力路径积分（Euclidean gravitational path integral），推导包含高曲率表面项贡献的德西特熵，并与相应熵泛函（entropy functional）给出的全息纠缠熵进行比较。分析先在 Gauss-Bonnet 引力中进行，随后推广至一般 Lovelock 引力。

Result: 在 RS II 模型的静态渐近德西特膜世界时空中，德西特熵与全息纠缠熵一致；但对稳态时空两者一般不同。差异来源于与等时面相关的外在曲率贡献，该贡献在静态情形下为零，而在稳态几何中一般非零。对一般 Lovelock 引力，静态情形的一致性在 Gauss-Bonnet 之外仍然成立。膜世界黑洞（braneworld black holes）亦呈现相同区分：静态时黑洞熵与全息纠缠熵一致，稳态时一般不同。

Conclusion: 结果澄清了高曲率引力中引力熵与全息熵的关系，并为 Lovelock 理论及其膜世界实现中的全息对应提供了更广泛的视角。核心结论是静态与稳态构型在熵一致性上存在本质区分。

Abstract: We investigate the relation between de Sitter entropy and holographic entanglement entropy in higher-curvature gravity and its braneworld realization. Using the canonical formulation of the gravitational action and the Euclidean gravitational path integral, we derive the de Sitter entropy, including contributions from higher-curvature surface terms, and compare it with the holographic entanglement entropy obtained from the corresponding entropy functional.
  We first consider Gauss-Bonnet gravity and show that the two entropies coincide for static asymptotically de Sitter braneworld spacetimes on the RS II model, while they generally differ for stationary spacetimes. The mismatch in the stationary case arises from an extrinsic-curvature contribution associated with the constant-time surface, which vanishes for static configurations but is generally nonzero for stationary geometries.
  We then extend the analysis to general Lovelock gravity and show that the agreement between the de Sitter entropy and holographic entanglement entropy persists for static asymptotically de Sitter braneworld spacetimes in the RS II model beyond the Gauss-Bonnet case. We also comment that the same distinction between static and stationary configurations applies to braneworld black holes: the black hole entropy agrees with the holographic entanglement entropy in the static case, while the two generally differ for stationary braneworld black holes.
  Our results clarify the relation between gravitational and holographic entropies in higher-curvature gravity and provide a broader perspective on holographic correspondence in Lovelock theories and their braneworld realizations.

</details>


### [312] [The Gaiotto-Kim Trace Formula and Line Defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24582)
*Sungjoon Kim,Spencer Stubbs*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文研究将 4d 半 BPS 线缺陷（half-BPS line defects）插入 Gaiotto–Kim 提出的迹公式（trace formula）中，该公式计算由 4d $\mathcal{N}=2$ 超共形场论导出的 3d 拓扑量子场论（TQFT）的椭球分割函数。由此得到到 3d Wilson 环的同态，并保持 4d 缺陷的融合代数。文中还给出确定 3d TQFT 单对象的系统算法，并借助超对称局域化刻画其模 $S$、$T$ 矩阵，应用于具有高次单值性的 $(A_1,G)$ Argyres–Douglas 理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究的出发点是 Gaiotto–Kim 的迹公式，其计算由 4d $\mathcal{N}=2$ 超共形场论导出的 3d TQFT 的椭球分割函数。作者希望在该框架中引入 4d 半 BPS 线缺陷，以建立与 3d Wilson 环的联系并研究其融合结构。

Method: 方法包括将 4d 半 BPS 线缺陷插入迹公式，从而构造到 3d Wilson 环的同态；同时提出系统性算法，用 UV 3d $\mathcal{N}=2$ 阿贝尔 Chern–Simons-物质理论中的 Wilson 环确定 3d TQFT 的单对象。此外利用超对称局域化（supersymmetric localization）刻画 TQFT 的模 $S$- 与 $T$- 矩阵。

Result: 所得对应关系保持 4d 缺陷的融合代数（fusion algebra）；算法可确定 3d TQFT 的单对象，并通过超对称局域化得到模 $S$- 与 $T$- 矩阵。该框架被应用于由迹公式产生的多种 3d TQFT，涉及 $(A_1,G)$ Argyres–Douglas 理论的高次单值性。

Conclusion: 该工作将 4d 半 BPS 线缺陷与 3d Wilson 环联系起来，并提供了从 UV 阿贝尔 Chern–Simons-物质理论出发确定 3d TQFT 单对象及模数据的算法，展示了其在 $(A_1,G)$ Argyres–Douglas 理论相关 3d TQFT 中的应用。

Abstract: We study the insertion of 4d half-BPS line defects into the trace formula proposed by Gaiotto and Kim, which computes the ellipsoid partition function of the 3d topological quantum field theory (TQFT) arising from a 4d $\mathcal{N}=2$ superconformal field theory. This yields a homomorphism to 3d Wilson loops, preserving the fusion algebra of the 4d defects. We also present a systematic algorithm for determining the simple objects of a 3d TQFT in terms of Wilson loops in the UV 3d $\mathcal{N}=2$ abelian Chern-Simons-matter theory. Utilizing supersymmetric localization, we characterize the modular $S$- and $T$-matrices of the TQFT. We apply our algorithm to various 3d TQFTs arising from the trace formula with higher-power monodromy of the $(A_1,G)$ Argyres-Douglas theories.

</details>


### [313] [ABJM to BMN in a Double Scale Limit: Indices and Bubbling Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24649)
*Zhengyuan Du*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在 \(N,k\to\infty\) 且 \(N/k^2\to\nu\in(0,\infty)\) 的双标度极限（double scale limit）下，固定正单极荷 \(q\) 的 ABJM 理论对应 rank-\(q\) BMN 矩阵模型，该关系由引力侧与超对称指标（supersymmetric index）两侧分别证实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目的在于刻画 ABJM 理论双标度极限中紧致零圆（compact null circle）保持有限半径的扇区，并厘清该扇区与 BMN 矩阵模型之间的对应关系。该极限下 \(\nu\) 固定模型的无量纲耦合，为连接两种理论提供了自然框架。

Method: 引力侧对 Donos–Simón 半 BPS 几何的 Hopf 商取双标度极限：承载增长背景通量的圆盘趋于无限大导电平面的同时，其余有限圆盘复现 Lin–Maldacena 静电问题，其量子化通量映射为标记 BMN 真空的划分数据。场论侧则由有限 \(N\)、有限 \(k\) 的 ABJM 局域化公式（localization formula）出发进行证明。

Result: 在固定正单极荷 \(q\) 下，ABJM 超共形指标在横向逸度（transverse fugacities）中逐系数分解为一个普适中性贡献与 rank-\(q\) BMN 矩阵模型的精化 Witten 指标（refined Witten index），并附带纵向动量权重。该中性贡献恰为零单极荷指标的极限。

Conclusion: 除以该普适因子可隔离对所有 BMN 超对称真空求和的 BMN 指标，从而在引力与场论两侧同时确立了 ABJM 双标度极限与 rank-\(q\) BMN 矩阵模型的对应关系。

Abstract: We study a double scale limit of $U(N)_k\times U(N)_{-k}$ ABJM theory, with $N,k\to\infty$ and $N/k^2\toν\in(0,\infty)$, at fixed positive total monopole charge $q$. In this limit, the Penrose geometry retains a compact null circle of finite radius, and the sector of monopole charge $q$ carries $q$ units of longitudinal momentum. It is therefore naturally associated with the rank-$q$ BMN matrix model, while $ν$ fixes its dimensionless coupling. We establish this relation from both gravity and the supersymmetric index. On the gravity side, we take the double scale limit of Hopf quotients of the Donos--Simón half-BPS geometries. The disk carrying the growing background flux becomes an infinite conducting plane, while the remaining finite disks reproduce the Lin--Maldacena electrostatic problem. Their quantized fluxes map directly to the partition data labeling BMN vacua. On the field-theory side, starting from the finite-$N$, finite-$k$ ABJM localization formula, we prove that, at fixed positive monopole charge $q$, the ABJM superconformal index factorizes coefficientwise in the transverse fugacities into a universal neutral contribution and the refined Witten index of the rank-$q$ BMN matrix model, together with the longitudinal momentum weight. The neutral contribution is precisely the limiting zero-monopole-charge index. Dividing by this universal factor therefore isolates the BMN index summed over all its supersymmetric vacua.

</details>


### [314] [Finite-Time Doppler Covariance Spectroscopy in the AdS/CFT correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24806)
*Feiyi Liu,Shiyang Chen,Yang Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在全息（holographic）视角下提出一个精确的有限时长协方差（finite-duration covariance）框架，用于在 AdS/CFT 对应中解析手性（chirality）。在旋转 BTZ 黑洞时空中，边界控制间的相对运动将和频匹配转化为由标量场角动量标记的离散速度共振，并通过双设置正交循环重构共振对贡献，进而给出本征浴手性的有限线宽估计量，并可用于反演 BTZ 旋转参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在解决 AdS/CFT 对应中的手性分辨问题，特别是如何在有限时长、有限时间协方差数据下区分控制诱导的共振运动学与浴的本征手性。选择旋转 BTZ 黑洞时空作为具体场景，以在标量场角动量标记下研究共振行为。

Method: 构建精确的有限时长协方差框架：利用边界控制间相对运动，把紧致边界柱面上的和频匹配转化为离散速度共振。由于完整实控制协方差包含差频扇区，采用双设置正交循环（two-setting quadrature cycle）将其抵消，并从完全半正定协方差矩阵重构共振对贡献。对孤立角模，利用相反共振速度下的重构对响应采样相反角动量与共同控制选择频率，以归一化对比度消去反射对称控制因子与态无关算符归一化，得到本征浴手性的有限线宽估计量。

Result: 对平滑浴谱，由共同居中偶窄化窗口引入的偏差从线宽的二阶项开始，从而把保护扩展到高斯采样之外；给出显式实投影泄漏界（real-projection leakage bound）以控制邻近模污染。基于精确 BTZ 谱的长脉冲计算验证了相位循环重构与二次线宽标度；基于精确截断转移幅度的浴加权计算确立了向该基线的收敛。在默认控制下，前两个非零角模支持从完整角和中提取信息，并以亚百分比的确定性偏差重构 BTZ 旋转参数。

Conclusion: 该框架将控制诱导的共振运动学与本征浴手性分离，并把有限时间协方差数据转化为对旋转 BTZ 黑洞的定量估计，为 AdS/CFT 中的手性解析提供了可操作的协方差方案。

Abstract: From a holographic perspective, we formulate an exact finite-duration covariance framework that resolves chirality in the AdS/CFT correspondence. In a rotating BTZ black hole spacetime, relative motion between two boundary controls converts sum-frequency matching on the compact boundary cylinder into discrete velocity resonances indexed by the angular momentum of the scalar field. Because the complete real-control covariance also contains difference-frequency sectors, a two-setting quadrature cycle cancels them and reconstructs the resonant pair contribution from complete positive-semidefinite covariance matrices. For an isolated angular mode, reconstructed pair responses at opposite resonance velocities sample the boundary spectrum at opposite angular momentum and a common control-selected frequency. Their normalized contrast cancels reflection-symmetric control factors and the state-independent operator normalization, yielding a finite-linewidth estimator of intrinsic bath chirality. For a smooth bath spectrum, the bias from common centered even narrowing windows begins at quadratic order in linewidth, extending the protection beyond Gaussian sampling. An explicit real-projection leakage bound controls contamination from neighboring modes. Long-pulse calculations with the exact BTZ spectrum verify phase-cycle reconstruction and quadratic linewidth scaling. Bath-weighted calculations with exact truncated transfer amplitudes establish convergence to this baseline. Under the default controls, the first two nonzero angular modes support extraction from the complete angular sum and reconstruct the BTZ rotation parameter with subpercent deterministic bias. The framework thus separates control-induced resonance kinematics from intrinsic bath chirality and converts finite-time covariance data into a quantitative estimate of the rotating BTZ black hole.

</details>


### [315] [The Generalized Uncertainty Principle. Massless QED Renormalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24817)
*Ezequiel Valero,Héctor Gisbert,Victor Ilisie*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本研究在无质量GUP形变量子电动力学中考察树图算符内容在重整化下是否闭合，发现一阶形变、单圈层次上，消除冗余算符后仅需一个新的物理抵消项——轴矢-轴矢四费米相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义不确定性原理（generalized uncertainty principle, GUP）形变的有效场论解释本身并不能确定其树图层次算符内容是否在重整化下闭合，本文旨在检验这一问题。

Method: 在无质量GUP形变量子电动力学（quantum electrodynamics, QED）中，于单圈（one loop）层次并展开至形变参数一阶，计算相关格林函数（Green functions）的紫外发散并确定抵消项结构。

Result: 消除冗余算符（redundant operators）后，除树图理论原有的抵消项外，重整化仅需一个独立物理抵消项，即轴矢-轴矢四费米相互作用（axial-axial four-fermion interaction）；原形变在此阶允许一个最小辐射完成，并导出诱导威尔逊系数（Wilson coefficient）的首个对数跑动。

Conclusion: GUP形变在单圈、一阶层次上具有最小辐射完成；所诱导算符与爱因斯坦-嘉当引力（Einstein-Cartan gravity）中出现的接触相互作用在算符层次上存在对应关系。

Abstract: The effective-field-theory interpretation of generalized uncertainty principle (GUP) deformations does not by itself establish whether their tree-level operator content is closed under renormalization. We investigate this question in massless GUP-deformed quantum electrodynamics at one loop and to first order in the deformation parameter. We compute the ultraviolet divergences of the relevant Green functions and determine the counterterm structure. Our central result is that, after eliminating redundant operators, renormalization requires only one independent physical counterterm beyond those associated with the tree-level theory: an axial-axial four-fermion interaction. The original deformation therefore admits a minimal radiative completion at this order. We derive the leading logarithmic running of the induced Wilson coefficient and discuss its operator-level correspondence with the contact interaction arising in Einstein-Cartan gravity.

</details>


### [316] [Quantum inversion of the vacuum hierarchy and multi-kink unbinding in the non-degenerate double sine-Gordon model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24861)
*Jonathan Lozano-Mayo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该研究考察非简并双正弦-戈尔登模型（non-degenerate double sine-Gordon model）中多扭结（multi-kink）的真空能量量子修正与结合能。单圈计算（one-loop calculation）预测经典真空层级发生反转，矩阵乘积态（matrix-product-state）数值计算证实该反转并在连续极限下持续存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典层面非简并双正弦-戈尔登模型存在多个真空，其层级由经典真空劈裂（vacuum splitting）决定；然而量子涨落是否改变该层级、以及多扭结结合（binding）如何受此影响，尚需系统研究。该工作旨在定量刻画真空能量的量子修正及其对多扭结稳定性的影响。

Method: 采用以主真空的介子质量（meson mass）定义正规序（normal ordering）的单圈计算；并以矩阵乘积态方法对 n=4 进行数值模拟，通过格点间距外推（extrapolation in the lattice spacing）推断连续极限行为；辅以有限链计算与高斯有效势（Gaussian effective potential）对照，并使用集体坐标（collective-coordinate）描述分析扭结结构。

Result: 单圈计算预测经典真空层级反转：量子涨落使中央次级真空（central secondary vacuum）在超过由经典真空劈裂与介子质量比设定的耦合阈值后占据优势。n=4 的矩阵乘积态结果证实该反转，且体能量密度外推支持其在连续极限下持续。交越点（crossing）低于单圈预测，在 α=0.3 处低约 22%，并可由高斯有效势很好地再现。α=0.3 的有限链计算显示，拓扑荷（topological charge）Q=4 的多扭结中央区域随真空能量差减小而扩张，交越之后则随系统尺寸增长。

Conclusion: 量子修正可导致真空层级反转，并驱动多扭结结合能趋于丧失；该过程伴随的复合体总质量修正仍低于经典值的十分之一，表明结合丧失发生时质量修正幅度有限。

Abstract: We study the quantum corrections to the vacuum energies and to the binding of the multi-kinks of the non-degenerate double sine-Gordon model. With normal ordering defined at the meson mass of the primary vacuum, the one-loop calculation predicts an inversion of the classical vacuum hierarchy, in which quantum fluctuations favour the central secondary vacuum above a coupling set by the classical splitting of the vacua and their meson mass ratio. Matrix-product-state calculations for $n=4$ confirm the inversion, and extrapolation of the bulk energy densities in the lattice spacing supports its persistence in the continuum. The crossing lies below the one-loop prediction, by $22$ percent at $α=0.3$, and is reproduced closely by the Gaussian effective potential. Finite-chain calculations at $α=0.3$ show that the central region of the multi-kink of topological charge $Q=4$ expands as the difference of the vacuum energies falls, as a collective-coordinate description predicts, and that beyond the crossing it grows with the size of the system. The composite therefore approaches the loss of its binding with a correction to its total mass still under a tenth of the classical value.

</details>


### [317] [Measuring correlations using local and nonlocal quenches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24873)
*Alexey G. Mikhaylenko,Andrew G. Semenov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于量子淬火（quantum quench）测量量子场系统关联函数（correlation functions）的理论方案，并以有质量与无质量标量场理论（scalar field theories）为例说明其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目标在于提供一种测量量子场系统中关联函数的理论方法，并进一步用于研究非高斯关联（non-Gaussian correlations）。

Method: 方法核心是施加短时扰动（量子淬火），利用系统进一步演化中平均场（average field）对初始态关联函数的依赖关系；并区分局域淬火（local quench）与非局域淬火（nonlocal quench）两类扰动。论证依托有质量与无质量标量场理论。

Result: 结果表明，对系统的短时扰动会使平均场的后续演化依赖于初始态的关联函数；在局域与非局域两类淬火情形下，可分别测量不同的关联函数。

Conclusion: 所提方案可用于测量关联函数，并可扩展应用于非高斯关联的研究。

Abstract: We present a theoretical proposal for measuring correlation functions in a quantum field system based on the use of quantum quenches. Using the examples of massive and massless scalar field theories, we show that a short-term perturbation of the system leads to a dependence of the further evolution of the average field on the initial state's correlation functions. Considering two types of perturbations, namely local and nonlocal quenches, we show which correlation functions can be measured in each of these cases. The proposed procedure can be applied to study non-Gaussian correlations.

</details>


### [318] [Gauss--Bonnet running and the de Sitter saddle of quadratic gravity inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24912)
*Ruolin Liu,Niayesh Afshordi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 纯由二次引力（quadratic gravity）曲率平方耦合的量子跑动（quantum running）驱动的暴胀（inflation）模型，在一圈跑动中保留通常被忽略的高斯-博内（Gauss-Bonnet）系数后，可恢复此前缺失的 de Sitter 态。由此得到方案独立（scheme independent）的标量倾斜（scalar tilt）与可由物质场数目调控的张量-标量比（tensor-to-scalar ratio）预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前用纯量子跑动驱动的暴胀虽能容纳近期宇宙微波背景（cosmic microwave background, CMB）观测偏好的高标量倾斜，但其起始的欧几里得四球面（Euclidean four-sphere）极大值只存在于单一重整化方案（renormalization scheme）中，且一旦纳入欧拉迹反常（Euler trace anomaly）便不再稳定。本文旨在恢复这一缺失的 de Sitter 态。

Method: 采用标准一圈跑动（standard one-loop running），并保留通常被忽略的高斯-博内系数（Gauss-Bonnet coefficient），使欧拉跑动（Euler running）在唯一耦合比值处平衡 R² 项的标度依赖。据此求取同时满足引力约束与紧致欧几里得作用量（compact Euclidean action）驻定条件的 de Sitter 解，并分析其标量子（scalaron）势。

Result: 所得 de Sitter 态具有极平坦的山顶势（hilltop，m²/H²≃-5×10⁻¹⁰），并连接到一个反线性暴胀平台（inverse-linear inflationary plateau）。最后 N*≃50–60 个 e-折（e-folds）为方案独立，标量倾斜 n_s≃1-4/(3N*)；张量-标量比 r 由增强跑动的物质场数目决定。当前张量上限排除纯引力（pure gravity）并给出最小物质含量：约 4×10⁶ 个共形耦合标量（conformally coupled scalars）或 3×10⁵ 个矢量（vectors），比动量诱导方案所需少 2.6 倍；而一圈可控性要求给出约十倍高的上限。

Conclusion: 在该参数窗口内，模型预测 r≳0.008 与 0.973≲n_s≲0.978，处于即将开展的 CMB 偏振巡天（CMB polarization surveys）的探测范围内。

Abstract: Inflation driven purely by the quantum running of the curvature-squared couplings of quadratic gravity was shown to accommodate the high scalar tilt favored by recent cosmic microwave background observations, starting from a Euclidean four-sphere at a maximum of the running $R^2$ coupling. That maximum exists only in one renormalization scheme, and is not stationary once the Euler trace anomaly is included. We show that the standard one-loop running, with the usually neglected Gauss--Bonnet coefficient retained, recovers the missing de Sitter state: the Euler running balances the scale dependence of the $R^2$ term at a unique coupling ratio, giving a de Sitter solution that is stationary for the gravitational constraint and for the compact Euclidean action alike, and whose scalaron potential is an extremely flat hilltop ($m^2/H^2\simeq-5\times10^{-10}$) joined to an inverse-linear inflationary plateau. The last $N_*\simeq50$--$60$ $e$-folds are scheme independent, with scalar tilt $n_s\simeq1-4/(3N_*)$, while the tensor-to-scalar ratio, $r$, is set by the number of matter fields that enhance the running. The current tensor bound excludes pure gravity and sets a minimum matter content, some $4\times10^6$ conformally coupled scalars or $3\times10^5$ vectors---$2.6$ times fewer than the momentum-induced scheme requires---while one-loop control to the end of inflation sets a maximum about ten times higher. Across that window the model predicts $r\gtrsim0.008$ with $0.973\lesssim n_s\lesssim0.978$, within reach of upcoming CMB polarization surveys.

</details>


### [319] [Chiral Carroll string near Schwarzschild black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24939)
*Kedar S. Kolekar*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文通过对相对论性闭合玻色弦作用量按光速 $c$ 的幂次做 Carroll 展开，得到弯曲弦-Carroll 靶空间中手性 Carroll 弦的作用量，并将其用于研究趋近4维 Schwarzschild 黑洞的经典闭合玻色弦动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在建立手性 Carroll 弦（chiral Carroll string）在弯曲靶空间中的作用量表述，并将 Carroll 极限（Carroll limit）视角应用于黑洞视界附近的弦动力学问题。

Method: 方法为对相对论性闭合玻色弦（closed bosonic string）作用量做以光速 $c$ 为幂次的 Carroll 展开，导出在弯曲的弦-Carroll 靶时空中的手性 Carroll 弦作用量；随后将该框架应用于趋近4维 Schwarzschild 黑洞的经典闭合玻色弦，取近视界弦-Carroll 展开几何作为靶时空。

Result: 结果显示，手性 Carroll 弦在视界上可以具有左行或右行涨落（left-moving or right-moving fluctuations），同时在 Rindler 时空中表现出非平凡运动，且该运动依赖于这些视界涨落。

Conclusion: 手性 Carroll 弦为描述近视界弦动力学提供了有效框架：视界上的手性与 Rindler 时空中的非平凡运动相互关联，表明 Carroll 展开可用于刻画黑洞视界附近的弦行为。

Abstract: We obtain the action for the chiral Carroll string moving in a curved string-Carroll target spacetime from a Carroll expansion, in powers of the speed of light $c$, of the action for the relativistic closed bosonic string. Applying this setup to a classical closed bosonic string approaching the $4$-dimensional Schwarzschild black hole, we study the dynamics of the string when it behaves as a chiral Carroll string with the near-horizon string-Carroll expanded geometry as the target spacetime. We find that the chiral Carroll string can have either left-moving or right-moving fluctuations on the horizon, while exhibiting a non-trivial motion in the Rindler spacetime which depends on these horizon fluctuations.

</details>


### [320] [Towards All-Order Locality in the Higher-Spin Holomorphic Zero-Form Sector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24956)
*D. A. Valerev*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本工作在高自旋理论（higher-spin theory）中构造全纯零形式扇区（holomorphic zero-form sector）的局域相互作用顶点，给出保证时空自旋局域性（space-time spin-locality）的充要条件，并计算出自旋局域的 $\\eta^2$ 零形式顶点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vasiliev 方程导出的全纯零形式扇区中，局域相互作用顶点的构造尚缺系统性判据；已有工作仅以较弱形式提出相关要求，且仅确立旋量自旋局域性（spinor spin-locality），不足以保证时空自旋局域性。

Method: 聚焦零形式顶点的一项要求（此前在 arXiv:2208.02004 中以较弱形式提出），该要求与旋量自旋局域性结合可保证时空自旋局域性；据此推导全纯一体形式扇区（holomorphic one-form sector）顶点上确保此类零形式顶点存在的充要条件，并将该方案应用于具体计算。

Result: 得到全纯一体形式扇区顶点的充要条件，并计算出全纯自旋局域的 $\\eta^2$ 零形式扇区顶点，其局域性程度超出此前仅确立旋量自旋局域性的结果。

Conclusion: 该结果为高自旋理论中局域顶点的系统性全阶（all-order）构造奠定了基础。

Abstract: This work addresses the construction of local interaction vertices in the holomorphic zero-form sector obtained from Vasiliev equations. We focus on a requirement on zero-form vertices, previously proposed in a less restrictive form in arXiv:2208.02004, which, together with spinor spin-locality, guarantees space-time spin-locality. Necessary and sufficient conditions on the holomorphic one-form sector vertices are derived that ensure the existence of holomorphic zero-form vertices satisfying this requirement. As a concrete demonstration, the proposed scheme is applied to compute the spin-local holomorphic $η^2$ zero-form sector vertex, a result that goes beyond previous work where only spinor spin-locality was established. These findings lay the groundwork for a systematic all-order construction of local vertices in higher-spin theory.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.quant-gas'></div>

# cond-mat.quant-gas [[Back]](#toc)

### [321] [Onset of a Fold Cascade in a Hopf Texture Driven by a Navier--Stokes Blow-Up Analog](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24490)
*Antti J. Niemi*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 在双组分玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体（two-component Bose–Einstein condensate）的赝自旋（pseudospin）Hopf纹理与黏性 Navier–Stokes 热云耦合框架下，以 Madelung–Schrödinger 理论与 Faxén 型平流修正驱动该纹理，观测到守恒 Hopf 荷下的折痕（fold）介导前像对产生与湮灭，并首次确认一→三转变遵循 Thom A₂ 的平方根开折。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机源于双组分玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体可同时承载赝自旋 Hopf 纹理（pseudospin Hopf texture）与遵循黏性 Navier–Stokes 动力学（viscous Navier–Stokes dynamics）的热云，并受 OpenAI 所报道的有限时间奇异解（finite-time singular solution）启发，尝试以坍缩核的替代物驱动该纹理。

Method: 采用含 Faxén 型平流修正（Faxén-like advective correction）的有效 Madelung–Schrödinger 理论（effective Madelung–Schrödinger theory），以坍缩核的替代物（surrogate of the collapsing core）驱动 Hopf 纹理，并追踪其拓扑演化。

Result: 纹理经历折痕介导（fold-mediated）的前像对（preimage pairs）产生与湮灭，过程中 Hopf 荷（Hopf charge）守恒；并确认首个一→三转变（one-to-three transition）服从 Thom A₂ 的平方根开折（square-root opening）。

Conclusion: 结果表明双组分玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体中的赝自旋 Hopf 纹理在黏性热云驱动下可展现拓扑荷守恒的折痕介导前像对动力学，且其一→三转变与 Thom A₂ 灾变（catastrophe）的平方根标度一致。

Abstract: A two-component Bose--Einstein condensate can host a pseudospin Hopf texture while its thermal cloud obeys viscous Navier--Stokes dynamics. Motivated by OpenAI's reported finite-time singular solution, we drive such a texture with a surrogate of the collapsing core, using an effective Madelung--Schrödinger theory with a Faxén-like advective correction. The texture undergoes fold-mediated creation and annihilation of preimage pairs at conserved Hopf charge with the first one-to-three transition confirmed to obey the square-root opening of a Thom $A_2$

</details>


### [322] [Robust Spin Universality Across a Ballistic-to-Diffusive Transport Crossover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23811)
*Cătălin Paşcu Moca,Ovidiu I. Pâţu,Balázs Dóra*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该工作研究开放量子系统中计数统计分布在相干—非相干跨界（coherent-to-incoherent crossover）时是否保持其普适类，并以无穷大相互作用哈伯德链（infinite-$U$ Hubbard chain）给出肯定答案：输运指数改变，但非高斯 M-Wright 自旋涨落始终存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是：完整的计数统计分布（full counting statistical distribution）在相干到非相干的跨界过程中，其普适类（universality class）能否保持不变。这对开放量子系统（open quantum systems）是基础性问题，此前未有定论。

Method: 针对无穷大相互作用哈伯德链，利用不可穿透动力学（impenetrable dynamics）所固有的精确自旋—电荷组合定律（spin-charge composition law），追踪电荷部门（charge sector）从弹道输运（ballistic flow）到扩散流体力学（diffusive hydrodynamics）的演化；并引入局域密度退相位（local density dephasing）。结合解析（analytics）与数值（numerics）方法验证结果。

Result: 局域密度退相位改变了输运指数（transport exponents），但未破坏底层的非高斯 M-Wright 自旋涨落（non-Gaussian M-Wright spin fluctuations）。该耗散跨界（dissipative crossover）将特征自旋转移宽度（spin-transfer width）的标度从 $t^{1/4}$ 移至 $t^{1/8}$。尽管标度时间被改变，中心自旋特征函数（central spin characteristic functions）仍一致坍缩到单一的 M-Wright 标度函数（M-Wright scaling function）上。

Conclusion: 在相干—非相干跨界下，计数统计的普适类并未被破坏：标度时间指数可变，但坍缩所对应的奇异 M-Wright 标度函数不变，表明底层非高斯自旋涨落具有跨界稳健性。

Abstract: Whether a full counting statistical distribution preserves its universality class across a coherent-to-incoherent crossover is a fundamental question in open quantum systems. We resolve this for the infinite-$U$ Hubbard chain, showing that local density dephasing changes transport exponents without destroying the underlying non-Gaussian M-Wright spin fluctuations. Utilizing the exact spin-charge composition law inherent to impenetrable dynamics, we track the charge sector from ballistic flow to diffusive hydrodynamics. This dissipative crossover shifts the characteristic spin-transfer width from $t^{1/4}$ to $t^{1/8}$. Despite this altered scaling time, the central spin characteristic functions uniformly collapse onto a singular M-Wright scaling function, what we confirm using analytics as well as numerics.

</details>


### [323] [Universal Dynamics of a Spin-$3/2$ Fermi Gas in Traps with Distinct Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24211)
*Shuyi Li,Qiang Gu*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 在自旋-3/2费米气体中观察到的自旋混合振荡幂律衰减（power-law decay），被推广检验至能级间距递增与递减的势阱（无限深方势阱与Pöschl–Teller势）。所有系统均呈现 $A(t)=A_0-\gamma t^\alpha$ 形式的幂律衰减，表明该行为不限于谐振子阱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谐振子阱中自旋-3/2费米气体的等间距谱是高度特殊的情形，其自旋混合振荡的普适幂律衰减起源尚不清晰。为探究该行为的来源，需考察能级间距不同的势阱中的动力学。

Method: 以能级间距递增的无限深方势阱（infinite square well）与递减的Pöschl–Teller势为对象，研究其中自旋-3/2费米气体的动力学，并与谐振子阱对比，分析单粒子谱结构对衰减行为的影响。

Result: 尽管单粒子谱（single-particle spectra）差异显著，所有系统均表现出相干振荡（coherent oscillations）的幂律衰减 $A(t)=A_0-\gamma t^\alpha$。指数 $\alpha$ 对粒子数、相互作用强度及整体能量尺度不敏感，但在不同谱结构的势阱间呈现系统性差异；衰减参数 $\gamma$ 则同时受谱结构与整体能量尺度强烈影响。

Conclusion: 幂律衰减并非谐振子阱所独有，而是在性质迥异的谱结构中普遍存在。$\alpha$ 在不同势阱间的系统性变化表明，底层单粒子谱在塑造衰减动力学中起重要作用。

Abstract: Universal power-law decay of spin-mixing oscillations has been observed in a harmonically trapped spin-$3/2$ Fermi gas, whose exactly equally spaced spectrum represents a highly special case. To gain insight into the origin of this behavior, we investigate the dynamics in traps with increasing and decreasing level spacings, represented by the infinite square well and the Pöschl--Teller potential, respectively. Despite pronounced differences in their single-particle spectra, all systems exhibit power-law decay of coherent oscillations, described by $A(t) = A_{0} - γt^α$.
