The exponent $α$ remains insensitive to particle number, interaction strength, and the overall energy scale, but exhibits systematic differences among traps with distinct spectral structures. In contrast, the decay parameter $γ$ is strongly influenced by both the spectral structure and the overall energy scale. These findings demonstrate that power-law decay is not restricted to the harmonic trap but persists across qualitatively distinct spectral structures. The observed variation of $α$ among different traps further suggests that the underlying single-particle spectrum plays an important role in shaping the decay dynamics.

</details>


### [324] [Thermal Evolution and Disorder Dependence of the Bose-glass: Spatial, Spectral, and Localization Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24247)
*Madhumita Kabiraj,Raka Dasgupta*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文借助有限温谱函数、空间非均匀性与逆参与率三种诊断手段，刻画无序Bose-Hubbard模型中Bose-glass相（玻色玻璃相）的玻璃态特征，揭示无序驱动玻璃态形成而热涨落逐渐抹除其信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bose-glass相的性质难以由单一观测量完整描述，需要多种诊断手段互补以刻画其玻璃态（glassy character）本质。

Method: 采用有限温Green函数与随机相近似（random phase approximation）计算谱函数，用Gutzwiller拟设（Gutzwiller ansatz）计算空间非均匀性，并对小尺寸体系做精确对角化（exact diagonalization）以获取逆参与率（inverse participation ratio, IPR）。

Result: 无序在Mott能隙（Mott gap）内引入低能局域态，并在足够强无序下使能隙闭合；空间非均匀性随无序急剧上升并在中等无序处饱和；IPR显示局域化随无序增强。升温抑制上述特征：低能谱权重减弱、非均匀性变化更平滑、IPR下降。

Conclusion: 三种诊断共同表明无序驱动玻璃态特征发展，而热涨落逐渐削弱其信号；空间非均匀性的早期饱和与低能激发及IPR的持续演化不一致，说明任何单一诊断都无法完整刻画Bose-glass态的演化，需采用组合表征。

Abstract: In this work, we characterize the glassy character of the Bose-glass phase in a disordered Bose-Hubbard model using three complementary diagnostics: the finite-temperature spectral function, spatial inhomogeneity, and the inverse participation ratio. Spectral analysis, obtained from finite-temperature Green's function and random phase approximation, shows that disorder introduces localized low-energy states within the Mott gap, eventually closing the gap at sufficiently strong disorder. Spatial inhomogeneity, calculated using the Gutzwiller ansatz, increases sharply with disorder and then saturates at moderate disorder strengths. The inverse participation ratio has been calculated from the exact diagonalization of a small system, and it reveals enhanced localization with stronger disorder. Increasing temperature suppresses these disorder-induced features: the low-energy spectral weight diminishes, the spatial inhomogeneity varies more smoothly, and the IPR decreases. Taken together, these diagnostics show that disorder drives the development of glassy character, while thermal fluctuations gradually wash out its signatures. The early saturation of spatial inhomogeneity compared with the continued evolution of low-energy excitations and the inverse participation ratio demonstrates that no single diagnostic fully captures the evolution of the Bose-glass state, highlighting the need for a combined characterization.

</details>


### [325] [From harmonic to sound-like oscillations in a quantum gas confined in a gravity compensated shell trap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24738)
*Matthieu Cassus,Rishabh Sharma,Maxime Pesche,Laurent Longchambon,Thomas Badr,Romain Dubessy,Hélène Perrin*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本研究考察壳形势阱（shell-shaped trap）中量子气体（quantum gas）在竖直反向力抵消重力条件下的质心振荡（center of mass oscillations），分析谐波频率、四次修正及声波主导的阻尼行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于考察重力被部分或近乎抵消时，壳形势阱几何曲率对量子气体质心运动的修正，以及由此引发的内部激发与弛豫效应。

Method: 测量壳底部质心振荡的谐波频率，并与包含超越旋转波近似（rotating wave approximation）修正的势阱解析公式进行对比；在重力部分补偿时引入四次修正，在重力近乎抵消时考虑声波（sound waves）控制的内部激发。

Result: 测得的壳底谐波频率与解析公式相符；部分补偿重力时四次修正不可忽略；重力近乎抵消时量子气体占据下半球很大比例，驱动质心会激发内部模式，其时间演化由声波主导。

Conclusion: 在该极限下，弛豫过程导致质心振荡出现强烈阻尼（strong damping），表明内部激发与声波机制对质心动力学具有显著影响。

Abstract: We study the center of mass oscillations of a quantum gas in a shell shaped trap in the presence of a vertical force opposed to gravity. The measured harmonic frequency at the bottom of the shell is compared with an analytical formula for the trap potential including corrections beyond the rotating wave approximation. When gravity is partially compensated, a quartic correction to the harmonic motion has to be included due to the shell curvature. As gravity is nearly canceled, the quantum gas occupies a large fraction of the lower hemisphere. Driving the center of mass induces internal excitations in the quantum gas, whose time evolution is governed by the presence of sound waves. Relaxation processes induce a strong damping of the center of mass oscillation in this limit.

</details>


<div id='math.CA'></div>

### [326] [Rectifiability of the straight visible boundary and the limits of segment-based Hardy criteria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22407)
*H. Abbas,A. Azzouz*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究定义在区域 Ω⊂ℝⁿ 上的加权 Hardy 不等式（weighted Hardy inequality），指出用直线段刻画可见边界以推导该类不等式的思路存在内在障碍。若边界点沿满足一致厚度条件的线段可见，则区域包含该点为顶点的截锥，从而量化直线可见边界是有限个 Lipschitz 图像之并且为 (n-1)-可求长的，不含 λ>n-1 的 Hausdorff 内容。由此，基于线段的标准要么不可满足、要么冗余。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加权 Hardy 不等式可由边界合适可见部分的 Hausdorff 内容下界推出，而用直线段描述的可见集比 Koskela–Lehrbäck 基于曲线的视觉边界更易验证。作者因此考察这一简化思路是否可行，并揭示其固有的结构性限制。

Method: 通过几何论证建立关键蕴含：若边界点 ξ 从 x 沿满足一致厚度条件（uniform thickness condition）的线段可见，则 Ω 含有以 ξ 为顶点的截锥（truncated cone）。据此分析量化直线可见边界 ∂^{str}_{x,α}Ω 与球 B(x,C₀d_Ω(x)) 交集的结构与测度性质，并结合内容条件与 Hardy 不等式已有结果进行比较。

Result: 量化直线可见边界是有限个 Lipschitz 图像之并，是 (n-1)-可求长的（(n-1)-rectifiable），且对任意 λ>n-1 不携带 λ-Hausdorff 内容。由于 λ≤n-1 的内容条件在无任何可见性假设下已推出 (p,β)-Hardy 不等式（β<p-n+λ），基于线段的标准要么无法满足、要么冗余，始终达不到真正需要可见性的 β≥p-1 区间。指数 n-1 可取到，说明可求长性定理是尖锐的；文中给出一个属于 Koskela–Lehrbäck 视觉边界但不属于任何 ∂^{str}_{x,α}Ω 的边界点。

Conclusion: 直线段可见性方法受制于 n-1 这一内在阈值，无法触及可见性真正必要的参数范围。此外，不含厚度条件的原始直线可见性（raw straight visibility）可摆脱该障碍，对 λ≤n-1 已充分，而 λ>n-1 情形仍为未决问题（open）。

Abstract: Weighted Hardy inequalities on a domain $Ω\subset\R^n$ can be deduced from lower Hausdorff-content bounds on suitable visible parts of the boundary, and it is natural to look for visible sets described by straight segments, which are easier to verify than the curve-based visual boundary of Koskela and Lehrbäck. We show that this approach is subject to an intrinsic obstruction. If a boundary point $ξ$ is seen from $x\inΩ$ along a segment satisfying a uniform thickness condition, then $Ω$ contains a truncated cone with vertex $ξ$; consequently the quantitatively straight visible boundary $\partial^{\mathrm{str}}_{x,α}Ω\cap B(x,C_0d_Ω(x))$ is a finite union of Lipschitz graphs, is $(n-1)$-rectifiable, and carries no $λ$-Hausdorff content for any $λ>n-1$. Since content conditions with $λ\le n-1$ already imply the $(p,β)$-Hardy inequality for $β<p-n+λ$ without any visibility assumption, segment-based criteria are either unsatisfiable or redundant: they never reach the regime $β\ge p-1$ in which visibility is genuinely needed. We show that the exponent $n-1$ is attained, so that the rectifiability theorem is sharp; we exhibit a boundary point lying in the visual boundary of Koskela and Lehrbäck but in no $\partial^{\mathrm{str}}_{x,α}Ω$; and we observe that raw straight visibility, without the thickness condition, escapes the obstruction, is sufficient for $λ\le n-1$, and remains open for $λ>n-1$.

</details>


<div id='nlin.AO'></div>

# nlin.AO [[Back]](#toc)

### [327] [Deviations from global coupling in adaptive oscillator networks: a mean-field theory for the variance of coupling weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22597)
*Richard Gast,Shotaro Takasu,Juergen Kurths,Ann Kennedy*

Main category: nlin.AO

TL;DR: 该文针对自适应网络（adaptive networks）中耦合强度均值无法刻画连接异质性的问题，提出二阶矩封闭（second-order moment closure）方法，推导耦合权重方差的平均场方程，并揭示权重方差对振子异质性（oscillator heterogeneity）的非线性非单调依赖及其受相位相干（phase coherence）中介的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理与生物系统普遍为自适应网络，其中节点动力学与连边动力学协同演化（co-evolve）。既有平均场约化仅追踪平均耦合强度，无法判断耦合何时保持有效均匀、何时涌现结构化连接。因此需要能刻画耦合权重涨落的理论工具。

Method: 针对具有自适应耦合（adaptive coupling）的异质相位振子（phase oscillators）网络，从均匀耦合权重出发，引入二阶矩封闭，推导耦合权重方差（coupling-weight variance）的平均场方程。

Result: 相对权重方差对振子异质性呈非线性、非单调依赖，且由相位相干中介；偏离全局耦合的程度取决于振子异质性与适应规则（adaptation rule）之间的相互作用。对称适应产生强耦合的相干振子核心并形成无适应时不存在的双稳区（bistable regime）；反对称适应在同一核心内产生反对称耦合，从而使其失稳。

Conclusion: 所推导的方程可区分自适应网络表现为全局耦合系统的参数区间与形成更复杂耦合模式的区间，为判定自适应网络的行为类型提供判据。

Abstract: A wide range of physical and biological systems are adaptive networks, in which the dynamics of the nodes and of the edges connecting them co-evolve. Mean-field reductions of such systems typically track only the average coupling strength, and therefore cannot determine when the coupling stays effectively homogeneous and when structured connectivity emerges. Here, we present a second-order moment closure that allows us to derive mean-field equations for the coupling-weight variance in networks of heterogeneous phase oscillators with adaptive coupling, starting from uniform coupling weights. In agreement with network simulations, we find a nonlinear, non-monotonic dependence of the relative weight variance on the oscillator heterogeneity that is mediated by the phase coherence. Moreover, we find that deviations from global coupling strongly depend on an interaction between the oscillator heterogeneity and the adaptation rule. Whereas symmetric adaptation causes a strongly coupled core of coherent oscillators to emerge and creates a bistable regime that is absent without adaptation, antisymmetric adaptation leads to antisymmetric coupling within the same core, thereby destabilizing it. Our equations therefore delineate the regimes in which adaptive networks behave like globally coupled systems from those in which more complex coupling patterns form.

</details>


### [328] [Discrete-time Kuramoto model with phase lag: Linear stability analysis and onset of synchronization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22715)
*Prashant M. Gade,Shamik Gupta*

Main category: nlin.AO

TL;DR: 本文研究含相位滞后（phase lag）的离散时间（discrete-time）Kuramoto 模型，在无穷多振子连续极限下推导精确的 Frobenius-Perron 方程，解析给出非相干态失稳阈值，并揭示离散时间系统特有的周期与混沌（chaos）状态以及 Ott-Antonsen 拟设（ansatz）的失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的连续时间 Kuramoto 模型已被广泛用于同步研究，但其离散时间版本在稳定性机制上存在本质差异，且相关理论与阈值结果尚不明确。本文旨在刻画离散时间含相位滞后模型的同步转变及其非线性动力学行为。

Method: 考虑全局耦合、频率分布非均匀的相位振子，其演化由非线性映射描述。在 N→∞ 的连续极限下，推导单振子概率密度时间演化的精确 Frobenius-Perron 方程；通过研究非相干态（incoherent state）的线性稳定性（linear stability）确定同步起始，并对洛伦兹频率分布（Lorentzian distribution）解析求解同步阈值。

Result: 对洛伦兹频率分布得到同步阈值的解析表达式，其数值与连续时间 Kuramoto 模型的阈值不同，反映离散时间映射与连续时间流在稳定性条件上的根本差异。超过同步阈值后，系统呈现周期（periodic）与混沌（chaotic）状态，这是经典 Kuramoto 模型所不具备的。有限 N 系统的数值模拟验证了阈值的解析预测。

Conclusion: 离散时间 Kuramoto 模型在同步阈值以上展现出周期和混沌等丰富非线性现象，同时表明常用于连续时间情形的 Ott-Antonsen 拟设（ansatz）在离散时间框架下失效，低维描述不再适用。

Abstract: We investigate the discrete-time version of the Kuramoto model with phase lag, which comprises globally-coupled phase oscillators of distributed frequencies that are evolving under a nonlinear map. In the continuum limit of an infinite number of oscillators ($N\to \infty$), we derive the exact Frobenius-Perron equation for the time evolution of the single-oscillator probability density, and study linear stability of the incoherent state. Instability signals onset of synchronization. The corresponding synchronization threshold is obtained analytically for the case of a Lorentzian distribution of the oscillator frequencies. The threshold differs from that of the continuous-time Kuramoto model, reflecting the fundamentally different stability conditions for discrete-time maps and continuous-time flows. Beyond synchronization threshold, we observe several interesting nonlinear phenomena: Unlike the classical Kuramoto model, the discrete-time version exhibits periodic and chaotic states. Numerical simulations of the finite-$N$ system confirm the analytical prediction for the synchronization threshold, while highlighting breakdown of the celebrated Ott-Antonsen ansatz invoked to conveniently study the continuous-time Kuramoto model in terms of a low-dimensional description.

</details>
